# Untangle log

## Scope

- **Target:** the whole LevitateOS superproject and its submodules, **except `tools/ginux` and `tools/recipe`** (links from in-scope code to them still count as findings). Ignored as non-code: `docs/ginux*`, `docs/history`, `node_modules`, `target`.
  - In: `distro/{builder,contract,elf,variants}`, `xtask`, `testing/{install-tests,rootfs-tests,hardware-compat,fsdbg}`, `tools/{recab,recinit,reciso,recpart,recqemu,recshuttle,recstrap,recuki}`, `tui`, `docs/content`, `docs/website`, root config (`Cargo.toml`, `package.json`, `justfile`, `turbo.json`, `deny.toml`) and the design docs that state facts the code also states.
- **Product goal that shapes the audit (user, 2026-11-07):** LevitateOS and AcornOS must be true siblings **with the same features and capabilities**, and the **install experience must be as close to identical as possible**. The only intended user-facing differences:

  | | LevitateOS | AcornOS |
  |---|---|---|
  | privilege tool | sudo | doas |
  | desktop | KDE Plasma | LXQt |
  | kernel | LTS (6.12.x) | latest stable (7.x) |

  The bases underneath differ (Fedora RPM + systemd + glibc vs Alpine APK + OpenRC + musl) and must not leak into the experience beyond what is unavoidable. Every other divergence is logged as a `[parity]` problem; per-distro copies of one capability as `[hidden]`; code branching on the distro name as `[special]`.
- **Additions chosen:** none. Asked; the answer restated the scope rather than picking Comments or Tests, so neither is done. (AGENTS.md bans unit tests outside ginux anyway, so "Tests" would only touch integration/scenario checks.) Can be added before the refactor if wanted.
- **Repo rules that bind the refactor:** commit straight to each repo's one branch (no branches), submodules first then superproject pointers, no unit tests, every VM through libvirt (`qemu:///session`). Other sessions edit and stage hunks in this checkout concurrently: check `git diff --cached` before `git add` and commit only our own hunks.
- **Previous untangles:** rec* tools (`docs/history/UNTANGLE.md`) and install UI (`docs/history/UNTANGLE-install-ui.md`, archived at the start of this audit, superproject `61085b8`). Both finished; their leftovers and regressions count here.
- **Tooling for later phases:** `.artifacts/untangle-repo/` (gitignored)
  - `scripts/skeleton.sh` → `skeleton/` (Rust via `skeleton_rs.py`, TS via `skeleton_ts.ts`; variants and builder recipes copied whole). ~90k lines → 26k.
  - `scripts/rust_deps.py` → `map/crates.txt`, `map/modules-<crate>.txt`, `map/mod-fanin.txt`.
  - `scripts/depgraph.py` → TS import graph for `tui/` in `map/`.
  - `scripts/history.sh` → `map/churn-<repo>.txt`, `map/cochange-<repo>.txt` for the superproject and each in-scope submodule.
  - `scripts/variant_diff.sh` → `map/variant-diff.txt` (levitate vs acorn variant trees, file by file).
  - `BRIEF.md` (the checklist and entry format given to every auditor), `findings/<area>.md` (raw area findings), `baseline/`.

- **`cargo xtask check`** (`baseline/check.txt`): exit 1. `cargo check --workspace --all-targets` ok; guest scripts `sh -n` ok; docs content builds; `bun install --frozen-lockfile` ok; typecheck ok for docs/content and all seven tui packages. **FAIL:** "generated files current" — `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/generated/recpart.ts` is out of date (recpart moved on without `cargo xtask gen`).
- **`cargo xtask check tui`** (`baseline/check-tui.txt`): exit 0. Typecheck, lint, format, renders at 120×36, install-docs as each distro's docs pane at 80×24 (38×21) and 240×67 (118×64), snapshots, and every story of disk-plan, install-hub (4), install-docs (6) and recab-tui (9) pass.
- **Scenario status** (`cargo xtask status <distro>`, `baseline/status-*.txt`), identical for both distros: `live-boot` and `live-tools` STALE; `build-preflight`, `install`, `installed-boot`, `automated-login`, `runtime`, `ab-update` never run. So no install or A/B scenario has a recorded pass on either distro.
- **`cargo xtask doctor`** (`baseline/doctor.txt`): exit 0. Repo QEMU tools not installed (host QEMU used); libvirt, passt, OVMF, SSH key, bun, xorriso ok; LevitateOS Fedora 45 base lock (2026-11-07), kernels `6.12.116-levitate` and `7.3.3-acorn`, and live-boot/live-tools ISOs for both distros present.
- **Built artifacts present** (`.artifacts/out/<distro>/releases/`, root-owned): both distros have base-rootfs, live-boot and live-tools ISOs from 2026-11-06/07. Note: both `base-rootfs` `filesystem.erofs` images are 4096 bytes (empty), while live-boot/live-tools images are 31–94 MB. LevitateOS live-tools ISO 298 MB, AcornOS 232 MB.
- **After the baseline**, other sessions ran scenarios (`.scenarios/*.json`, 21:09–22:23): LevitateOS live-boot and install passed, live-tools failed (vi missing); AcornOS live-boot, live-tools, install and ab-update passed. Compare against these, not only the `status` snapshot above.
- **Not run at baseline:** VM scenarios (`cargo xtask test all <distro>`, `test ab-update <distro>`) — long-running libvirt boots; run them at the start of the refactor phase if a behavioral reference is needed, and compare against `status` output above.

**Hotspots** (last year; superproject history includes an old layout — `leviso/`, `AcornOS/`, `IuppiterOS/`, `distro-spec/` — since moved into submodules):
- distro/builder: `src/lib.rs` (41), `pipeline/live_tools.rs` (31), `pipeline/products.rs` (30), `bin/workflows/artifacts.rs` (30), `artifact/live_overlay.rs` (30), `pipeline/config.rs` (25).
- distro/contract: `variant.rs` (58), `validate.rs` (38), `schema.rs` (37), `runtime.rs` (31).
- install-tests: `preflight.rs` (39), `scenarios/mod.rs` (35), `distro/{mod,levitate,acorn}.rs` (27/26/26), `distro/iuppiter.rs` (21).
- superproject (last 2 months): `xtask/src/cli/types.rs` (26), `distro/variants/acorn/ring2/products.toml` (13 — vs 7 for levitate's: Acorn is being caught up by hand), `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs` (13).

**Co-change pairs that live apart** (hidden-link suspects):
- `install-tests distro/acorn.rs` ↔ `distro/levitate.rs` (22), ↔ `iuppiter.rs` (16/12): per-distro copies that move together.
- `builder artifact/live_overlay.rs` ↔ `pipeline/live_tools.rs` (17) ↔ `pipeline/overlay.rs` (12/11): one feature (the live system's UX) split across artifact/ and pipeline/.
- `builder bin/workflows/*` ↔ `pipeline/source.rs`, `pipeline/products.rs`: the CLI layer changes whenever the pipeline does.
- Superproject: `distro/contract` ↔ `tui` (14), `tui` ↔ `xtask/src/cli/types.rs` (14), `tools/recpart` ↔ `tui` (13), `distro/builder` ↔ `acorn/ring2/products.toml` (11), acorn ↔ levitate `products.toml` (7), `install-tests` ↔ `xtask/.../scenarios.rs` (9).

**Dependency graph.** Crate graph has no cycles. Worst parts: `xtask` and `install-tests` each depend on nearly every crate (both are the integration points, and they overlap — two orchestrators of the same scenarios); `distro-builder` (volatile) is a dependency of `install-tests` (a test harness that should depend on outputs, not the builder's internals); `distro-builder` depends on `tools/recipe` by path. Highest module fan-in: `xtask::util` (54), `builder::workflows` (38), `builder::outputs` (15), `recpart::types` (12).

**Variant diff.** Only file present in one variant: `levitate/ring3/keys/RPM-GPG-KEY-fedora-45-primary`. Every other file exists in both and differs (`map/variant-diff.txt`, 730 lines): mostly the same file with distro names, paths, kernel pins and comments swapped — near-copies.

**Where leverage looks highest:** (1) the per-distro parallel paths (builder Fedora vs Alpine, install-tests per-distro modules, variant near-copies) — the parity goal and the hidden-link count both live here; (2) the live-system UX split across builder `artifact/` and `pipeline/`; (3) the two orchestrators (`xtask` and `distro-builder` CLI / `install-tests` runner); (4) leftovers of a third distro (IuppiterOS) and the old layout.

## Problems

Totals: **2065 entries — 168 parity, 613 special, 931 hidden, 67 needless, 286 surplus**.
The special-case count, **613**, is the number the refactor works to bring down; the parity count, **168**, is the number of sibling gaps between LevitateOS and AcornOS.

### Biggest clusters

1. **Two siblings built by two parallel stacks.** The Fedora and Alpine rootfs producers share no code (`fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai` vs `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai`). LevitateOS's package list is variant TOML with `xtask base lock`; AcornOS's is a string in Rhai, hand-pinned, with no update path. The live overlay and the installed-system recipe each have a systemd half and an OpenRC half that drifted apart: hard-coded `eth0` vs NetworkManager, autologin on tty2–6 only on LevitateOS, shutdown cleanup only on systemd, different allowed consoles, an OpenRC-only service whitelist. The init system and libc are decided about six ways: by distro name (`live_tools.rs` "acorn"→openrc), by probing files (runtime checker, `build-release.sh`, `system-setup.sh`, recsetup), or by three separate `InitSystem` enums. (BR, BP044–052, Z001–011, P, C)
2. **Capability gaps between the siblings** (168 parity entries; matrix below):
   - **AcornOS has no installed initramfs.** It therefore gets no root fsck, LUKS or LVM, and no emergency reboot in A/B trials. Whether there is one is decided in about 8 places that don't read each other.
   - **Package manager.** LevitateOS ships none, though its docs advertise `recipe`. AcornOS ships `apk` with a repository that points at a build-host path.
   - **WiFi.** Only AcornOS has WiFi tools; the WiFi steps in LevitateOS's docs fail on its own ISO.
   - **Tools and services.** Diagnostic tools (vi, htop, smartctl, hdparm) are only on AcornOS; auditd is only on LevitateOS.
   - **A/B carry-over.** It drops `sudoers.d` on LevitateOS and the WiFi config on AcornOS.
   - **Shell.** bash on LevitateOS, ash on AcornOS, both on the install screen and for new users.
   - **Kernel.** About 40 kconfig differences beyond the LTS-vs-latest line (preemption, HZ, LIVEPATCH, ZRAM, …).
   - **Smaller gaps.** Different boot command lines (Fedora's `inst.sshd=0` lands on AcornOS), locales and stated hardware requirements.
3. **The install experience is written twice.** All 8 getting-started pages exist as `acorn-*` copies, paired only by a file-name prefix. The install commands are copied 4–5 times (install-hub, disk-plan, docs, website) and already disagree (`myhost`/`myhostname`, `initramfs.img`/`initramfs-installed.img`). install-docs picks the distro by reading `/etc/os-release` itself, which regresses the previous untangle's F13. "LevitateOS" branding shows on AcornOS: in disk-plan, `/run/levitate-install`, `LEVITATE_INSTALL_*` and `recstrap --help`. (U001–017, U)
4. **Facts shared by string across tools, builder and tests:**
   - the installed initramfs name, spelt in 7+ places;
   - boot entry, `loader.conf` and ESP layout, with 4–5 writers;
   - partition labels and the `/var`-on-STATE layout;
   - live mount paths;
   - recab-trial service paths;
   - the privilege tool, which no variant declares (it is detected by probing in recsetup and special-cased in tests);
   - kernel version and sha, written 3–4 times per distro;
   - the trial boot options, defined 3 times.
   (Z018–024, TL012–040, TI, C001–003)
5. **Three orchestrators with their own vocabulary.** xtask, the `distro-builder` CLI and the install-tests runner each define the scenario and product names and the scenario→product mapping. `cargo xtask run` re-implements install-tests' VM launch, SSH and injection code. Work passes between crates as env vars set with `unsafe set_var` and as argv strings matched by slice patterns. install-tests links the image builder, and the builder links the VM harness to learn one marker string. (X, BP074–082, BR, Z025, Z033–034)
6. **The contract is written three times, and much of it is never read.** Every manifest field exists as a schema struct, a serde mirror and a hand converter. About 300 lines handle a legacy flat layout that no variant uses. Many declared fields are never read: release metadata, `installed_uki`, `default_username`, `auth_mode`, runtime-policy path lists, seven `ViolationCode`s. The validator and the builder apply different rules to the same field. Six per-variant hook wrappers pass about 30 env vars to a shell hook that calls the builder back. (C)
7. **Dead code that still has to compile.**
   - In the builder: `alpine/`, `artifact_store/` (1.2k lines), `artifact/disk/`, `artifact/iso.rs` (`unimplemented!`), `contracts/`, `component/`, `executor/`, `preflight/`, `cache`, `timing`, and its three crates used only by them.
   - `testing/hardware-compat`: no caller; 786 hand-copied kconfig rules and no notion of 6.12 vs 7.x.
   - `testing/fsdbg`: most of it, including the binary, the rootfs/auth-audit/qcow2 checklists and `oracle/`. Its oracles are LevitateOS-only.
   - `testing/rootfs-tests` and `testing/ginux-boot`: no caller. (BP001–043, BR, TF, Z029)
8. **Tests that check the wrong thing or one distro only.**
   - The shared `live-tools.sh` expects AcornOS's tool set, so LevitateOS fails on vi.
   - Some checks are gated by distro name (`preflight.rs` `distro_id == "levitate"`).
   - the builder's preseed contract test covers only AcornOS.
   - Waits are fixed timeouts (180 s for AcornOS vs 60 s), and readiness is detected by matching console strings.
   - The ab-update scenario stages slot B with the live initramfs, which recsetup refuses.
   - Scenarios boot the install disk read-write, so they change it for later scenarios.
   - Tool checks are defined three times, and the three disagree.
   (TI, TF, P, Z026)

**Not dry.** The three completeness rounds found 456, 350 and 252 new entries. The parity tracer was close to dry (7, 6, 7), so the sibling gaps are well covered. The hidden-link and special-case tail is not: each round still found more, mostly at finer grain (local robustness, error handling, temp-file naming, drifted copies inside one crate). Another round would add entries of that kind.

### Capability matrix (LevitateOS vs AcornOS)

From the end-to-end parity trace (`findings/P.md`). Rows where the two columns differ beyond sudo/doas, Plasma/LXQt and the kernel line are parity problems; each has an entry below.

| Capability | LevitateOS | AcornOS | Where implemented |
|---|---|---|---|
| Base package set declared | variant TOML `ring3/sources.toml` `rpm_base.packages` (≈150 names) | hard-coded string in recipe `_profile_packages` + `extra_apks` in `sources/alpine.rhai` | `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:21`; `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:47`, `recipes/sources/alpine.rhai:15` |
| Package lock / refresh tool | `cargo xtask base lock levitate` (release+updates, sig-checked) | none (hand-pinned ISO 3.24.4 + 13 extra APKs) | `xtask/src/tasks/base.rs:50` |
| Security updates in base | Fedora updates repo via lock | frozen at ISO point release except hand-pinned extras | as above |
| Rootfs completeness check | 45 required paths | ~15 checks incl. doas.conf contents | `fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:54`; `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:100` |
| Init / PID 1 | systemd | busybox init + OpenRC (`inittab`) | `installed-system.rhai:36` / `:94` |
| Live autologin, tty1 | yes (agetty drop-in, launcher) | yes (getty -n -l launcher) | `live-overlay.rhai:41` / `:23` |
| Live autologin, tty2-6 | yes, every getty@ autologins root | no, plain login prompt | `live-overlay.rhai:45` / `:27` |
| Live serial console (ttyS0) | autologin, install screen on request | autologin, install screen on request | `live-overlay.rhai:44,55` / `:29`; `guest/install-ux/install-ux.sh:21` |
| Installed serial console | only if `console=ttyS0` passed to recsetup | always (inittab) | systemd generator / `installed-system.rhai:100` |
| Live SSH | sshd enabled (scenario list) | sshd enabled (scenario list) | `live-overlay.rhai:77` via `pipeline/config.rs:49` |
| Installed SSH | sshd not enabled | sshd not enabled | `installed-system.rhai` (neither enables) |
| Live networking | NetworkManager + `live-net-setup` retry service | ifupdown static `eth0 dhcp` + dhcpcd | `live-overlay.rhai:57` / `:39` |
| Installed networking | NetworkManager enabled | dhcpcd + networking enabled | `installed-system.rhai:62` / `:111` |
| WiFi | no (no wpa_supplicant / NM-wifi in lock) | wpa_supplicant + iw shipped, not enabled | lock / `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:61` |
| Firmware (linux-firmware) | none | none (dead copy code only) | `distro/builder/src/alpine/firmware.rs` (unused) |
| Audit daemon | auditd shipped and live-enabled | none (but `audit=1` on cmdline) | lock; `levitate/scenarios/scenarios.toml:58` |
| Docs viewer (install-docs) | yes, page `installation`, product auto-detected | yes, page `acorn-installation`, `--product acorn` | `ring2/products.toml:9` both |
| Install docs content | 8 pages | 8 parallel `acorn-*` pages | `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/` |
| Install screen shell | `/bin/bash` | `/bin/sh` (ash) | `ring2/products.toml:8` both |
| Install TUI (install-hub) | yes | yes (docs slugs mapped to `acorn-*`) | `tui/apps/install/install-hub`, `install-docs/src/docs.ts:70` |
| Disk planner (disk-plan) | yes | yes | `ring2/products.toml:11` both |
| A/B layout (recpart) | yes, default (`immutable`) | yes, default | `scenarios.toml` `runtime_policy` both |
| A/B trial + rollback (recab) | yes; trial opts `rd.emergency=reboot systemd.crash_action=reboot` | yes; no trial opts (relies on `panic=`) | `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:109` |
| A/B carried config | etc/systemd/system, NM connections (not sudoers.d) | interfaces, inittab, doas.conf/doas.d, runlevels, conf.d (not wpa_supplicant.conf) | `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:118` |
| recab-tui on installed system | not shipped | not shipped | (only `cargo xtask tui`) |
| Initramfs for installed system | yes (recinit build-install, systemd) | none, kernel boots root directly | `_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:268` |
| Installed UKI (`*.efi`, recovery) | declared, never built/installed | declared, never built/installed | `ring1/transforms.toml` `installed_uki` both |
| Secure Boot | unsigned | unsigned | docs only |
| Locale setup | glibc precompiled, English only (`glibc-langpack-en`) | musl-locales (many languages, LANG only) | `recsetup/target.rs:288`; lock / `sources/alpine.rhai:21` |
| Keymap setup | `/etc/vconsole.conf` | loadkmap bmap or keymaps service | `guest/installed/system-setup.sh:54` |
| Timezone setup | tzdata, symlink | tzdata, symlink | `system-setup.sh:26` |
| User creation | useradd | busybox adduser/addgroup | `system-setup.sh:86` |
| Default login shell | bash | /bin/sh (ash) | `recsetup/target.rs:331` |
| Privilege tool (intended) | sudo, `%wheel ALL` 0440 | doas, `permit persist :wheel` 0600 | `fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:173`; `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:156` |
| Package manager on installed system | none (no rpm/dnf; docs advertise `recipe`, which is not shipped) | apk + alpine-conf `setup-*`; `/etc/apk/repositories` lists a build-host path | `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:233` |
| Live diagnostics tools | no vim/vi, htop, smartctl, hdparm | vim, htop, smartctl, hdparm | lock; `sources/alpine.rhai:15` |
| Kernel pin | 6.12.116 (3 copies) | 7.3.3 (3 copies) | `build-host.toml:25`, `recipes/kernel/metadata.rhai:11`, `recipes/linux-prebuilt.rhai:28` |
| Kernel prebuilt source | GH `LevitateOS/leviso` | GH `LevitateOS/AcornOS` | `linux-prebuilt.rhai:31,44` |
| Kernel policy beyond version | PREEMPT_VOLUNTARY, HZ 250, LIVEPATCH, CHECKPOINT_RESTORE | PREEMPT, HZ 1000, NO_HZ_FULL, ZRAM | `build-host/kernel/kconfig` both |
| Live kernel cmdline | quiet (`loglevel=3`, `systemd.show_status=0`), `selinux=0` | verbose | `scenarios.toml` `required_kernel_cmdline` both |
| Kernel update path on installed system | full A/B image only (`recab stage --kernel --initrd`) | full A/B image only (`recab stage --kernel`) | `docs/.../07-post-installation.ts:69` / `16-acorn-post-installation.ts:49` |
| Desktop ISO | Plasma prototype outside pipeline (Wayland) | LXQt prototype outside pipeline (X11, sddm marked failed) | `docs/desktop.md:17` |
| Scenarios run | same 8 ids | same 8 ids | `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:63` |
| live-tools scenario | fails (shared script wants Acorn's tool set; contract wants `vi`) | passes (P079: the recorded split-pane smoke failure is stale; `.scenarios/acorn.json` now has live-boot, live-tools and install passed) | `.scenarios/*.json`, `test-scripts/live-tools.sh:78` |
| Live-boot evidence checks | sshd enabled/active, `inst.sshd=0`, tmpfiles | networking/dhcpcd running, default route, sshd | `test-scripts/live-boot.sh:39` / `:45` |
| Build-preflight installed-initramfs check | yes | skipped by name | `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:383` |
| rootfs-tests / hardware-compat / fsdbg rootfs checklist | targeted at Levitate (default paths, Rocky/systemd oracles) | not targeted | `testing/rootfs-tests/src/main.rs:107`, `testing/hardware-compat/src/main.rs:15`, `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/rootfs.rs:263` |
| Release hooks | 3 wrapper scripts + evidence script | 3 wrapper copies + evidence copy | `ring0/hooks/*.sh`, `build-host/evidence/build-capability.sh` both |
| Release supporting artifacts | erofs, live initramfs, installed initramfs | erofs, live initramfs | `ring0/release.toml:16` both |
| Hardware requirements (docs) | x86-64-v3, 16 GB RAM, NVMe | x86-64, none stated | `01-getting-started.ts:15` / `11-acorn-installation.ts:30` |

### How the list was built

Nine area auditors and one end-to-end parity tracer (`findings/*.md`, IDs `C`, `BP`, `BR`, `X`, `TI`, `TL`, `TF`, `U`, `P`) plus the lead's cross-area pass (`Z`). A per-bucket merge dropped duplicates and opened the code for every parity and special entry and a third of the rest (`merge/dedup-*.md`): of 1162 entries, 147 were dropped (7 as false) and 84 corrected. Then three completeness rounds: fresh auditors who had not seen the list re-audited each area (`merge/fresh-*-r*.md`) and a comparer kept only new, verified entries (`merge/new-*-r*.md`, IDs `<area><round>NNN`, e.g. `TO3021`). Entries can cite each other by ID.

### Where the entries went

The redesign moved every entry from here to its fix under `## Fixes` (or to `### Leave as is`). The audit-time list, grouped by area, is kept in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/merge/problems.md` and `merge/final.jsonl`.

## Fixes

32 fixes for the 2,065 problems: 2,000 sit under a fix and 65 under Leave as is. Each problem sits in exactly one place, and its text is unchanged from the audit.

Built by 10 area designers (`.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-*.md`). Each read its area's full problem list, derived requirements from the special cases and proposed fixes; their 165 area fixes are merged here into 32. Every global fix names the area designs it merges, and their detail is in those files. Where a parity problem was placed by both an area designer and the parity designer, the area designer's placement is used; the parity designer's alternative is in `area-parity-all.json`.

**Reviewed with the user 2026-11-07.** They confirmed auditd on both (D3) and one shared busybox initramfs (D5). They chose **Recipe as the package manager on both systems** (D7). They accepted the rest as planned, but want to inspect the plan and keep room to change their mind. So the refactor applies the structural fixes first, and re-confirms each user-visible decision (D1, D2, D4, D6, D8–D16) with the user right before the fix that applies it.

### Requirements (from the special cases)

The special cases reduce to these needs, which the code never had a place for:

1. **The base differences are declared facts:** package backend, init, libc, privilege tool, desktop, kernel line. Every layer needs them at build time and on the running image; today they are probed, defaulted or matched on the distro name. → G04
2. **Every name built from a variant id is computed, not spelt** (hostname, ISO label, artifact and UKI names, prompts, localversion, targets). → G04, G05
3. **Products and scenarios are a fixed vocabulary named once.** Their mapping, inputs and `all` selection are derived from it. → G05
4. **The contract schema exists once and is validated once.** It holds only what really varies; values that can't vary are their owner's code. → G03
5. **One shared capability set** that both images provide, which the build checks and the scenarios test. → G07
6. **Each system feature is stated once,** with only the on-disk mechanism per init system. This covers live and installed, including the reproduced package-script effects. → G12, G13
7. **Both package sets come from one lock tool,** with the trust check recorded as data. → G08
8. **The kernel has one pin per variant, one exact release check and one shared policy.** The kconfig is enforced on the kernel that ships. → G11
9. **There is one installable system rootfs.** Every product is it plus layers, and what is installed is what boots. → G14
10. **The system image is a complete boot payload** (modules, firmware, installed initramfs) on both siblings. → G15
11. **Variants contain no code.** Release assembly is typed builder code, and the boot command line is a build fact. → G16
12. **Each on-disk convention has one owner that exports it:** disk layout, ESP and boot entries, live protocol, initramfs names. → G17
13. **An A/B trial has one verdict function and a declared carry list,** and is checked by outcome on both siblings. → G19
14. **A tool's machine-read output, rules and messages live in the tool.** Consumers get them generated, and checks run the real tool. → G20
15. **The install steps exist once, valid on both live userlands.** Docs, TUIs and the install scenario all render them. → G22, G23
16. **A page or app knows its distro only from what it is given.** There is no default distro and no "levitate" on AcornOS. → G23
17. **Every subprocess runs under one policy.** Host prerequisites are declared once and checked once. → G09
18. **Data between crates passes as typed parameters, not env vars or argv strings.** A missing prerequisite is a typed value that only the front door renders. → G10, G25
19. **One VM launcher and one scenario runner.** Waits end on structured signals under one time budget, and scenario inputs are never modified. → G25, G26
20. **Every check runs on both distros, or it is deleted.** Expectations about an artifact are checked by its producer when it writes it. → G02, G26, G27
21. **Runs have one record format** with atomic allocation and a prune that keeps what is referenced. → G06
22. **Dead code is deleted, not fixed;** special cases inside it go with it. → G01, G02

### Decisions for the user

The refactor applies the default unless the user picks otherwise. Every one is user-visible except where noted.

| # | Decision | Default | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | Login and install shell | bash on both | G07 |
| D2 | Live and installed tool set | the union on both: LevitateOS gains vim, htop, smartctl, hdparm, tar, gzip | G07 |
| D3 | auditd (designers disagreed) | **decided: on both** | G07 |
| D4 | Network stack | NetworkManager + wpa_supplicant on both, live and installed; AcornOS drops ifupdown/dhcpcd | G28 |
| D5 | Installed initramfs | **decided: one shared busybox recinit initramfs for both**, replacing LevitateOS's systemd initramfs | G15 |
| D6 | Firmware | a curated firmware set in both rootfs trees | G15 |
| D7 | Package manager on the installed system | **decided: Recipe on both** ("the heart of the OS"); the base system still updates by A/B image; AcornOS drops apk | G29 |
| D8 | Kernel policy | one shared kconfig (PREEMPT_DYNAMIC, HZ=1000, ZRAM on both); only the version line differs | G11 |
| D9 | Live session | autologin on tty1–6, sysrq and quiet boot on both; drop `inst.sshd=0` and `selinux=0` | G13 |
| D10 | Installed services | chrony on both; sshd installed but off; serial console only when asked for | G07 |
| D11 | Mutable (non-A/B) install path (designers disagreed) | keep it and test it on both | G26 |
| D12 | Install tools | static musl binaries on both; merge the recpart and recab repos into one install-tools repo | G18 |
| D13 | AcornOS package lock | yes: a lock and refresh like LevitateOS's | G08 |
| D14 | CI | both distros on every push (about double the CI time) | G27 |
| D15 | Docs | one guide; AcornOS slugs lose `acorn-` (old links break); same hardware baseline on both; state path `/run/install/` | G23 |
| D16 | Desktop display server | X11 on AcornOS until Alpine ships `lxqt-wayland-session` | G30 |
| D17 | Dev-facing (not user-visible) | distro argument required everywhere; `test all` runs every scenario without stopping at the first failure; drop `cargo xtask env` and the repo QEMU download; fw_cfg-only injection; archive the hardware-compat, rootfs-tests and leviso-elf repos; uniform `-base-rootfs.iso` naming | G05, G25, G02 |

### Order

Deletions come first (G01, G02): they remove 238 problems at no risk and shrink what later fixes must touch. Next come the foundations everything reads: the contract shape (G03), the distro profile (G04), the registries (G05, G06), the capability set (G07) and package locks (G08), and the host-command layer (G09, G10). Then the build: kernel, rootfs, system policies, products, boot payload, release assembly (G11–G16). Then the on-target tools (G17–G20), the install screen and guide (G21–G24), the test runner and checks (G25–G27), and the capability products that depend on all of that (G28–G30). The ginux-boot probe (G31) and docs (G32) come last.

### Fix tests

Each fix passes the three checks from the redesign skill:

- **Changes together, lives together:** each moves a fact to one owner.
- **Different reasons are no longer tied:** examples are the builder dropping its recqemu dependency, install-tests dropping distro-builder, and the TUIs dropping hand copies.
- **Dependency direction:** the new shared pieces (`distro-profile`, the registries and run records in `distro-contract`, the owners' exported constants in the rec* tools) are leaves or near-leaves that change less than their readers. No fix adds an import toward a more volatile crate. In particular, the on-target tools read the profile from `os-release` and the leaf crate, not from `distro-contract`.

### G01 Delete the pre-Recipe build engine and the builder's other dead code

Solves **101** problems (44 special, 23 hidden, 1 needless, 33 surplus).

- **Design:** Recipe is the only build engine. The leviso-era Rust engine and its store (`alpine/`, `artifact_store/`, `artifact/disk/`, `artifact/iso.rs`, `contracts/`, `component/`, `build/`, `cache`, `timing`, `executor/`, `preflight/`, uncalled recipe helpers) go, with the Cargo dependencies only they use, and the crate's public surface shrinks to what it does.
- **Done when:** No module in `distro/builder/src` without a live caller (`cargo check` plus a grep for each removed path); `fs2`, `tar`, `zstd` and dead `sha2` uses are gone from the builder's Cargo.toml.
- **Depends on:** none
- **Merges area designs:** BC-01 (builder-core), CL-12 (builder-cli) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [special] BC1008 `distro/builder/src/alpine/busybox.rs:51-55` both branches of the sbin/bin `if` pick the same target `/usr/bin/busybox`, so the `(dir, target)` tuple encodes only the directory.
- [special] BC1015 `distro/builder/src/alpine/modules.rs:85-94` `find_kernel_version` takes the first directory whose name contains `.`; with several kernel versions present, `read_dir` order decides.
- [special] BC1016 `distro/builder/src/alpine/ssh.rs:84` an unreadable `sshd_config` is swallowed into an empty string (`unwrap_or_else(|_| String::new())`) and the settings are written into a fresh file.
- [special] BC1031 `distro/builder/src/build/licenses.rs:128` and `:156` an exec failure of `rpm`/`apk` is swallowed with `.output().ok()?` and treated the same as "file not owned by any package".
- [special] BC1040 `distro/builder/src/artifact_store/mod.rs:683-690` `collect_referenced_blobs` silently skips index files it cannot read or parse, and `gc` (`:561-562`) deletes every blob not in that set, so a corrupt index entry gets its blob deleted.
- [special] BC1047 `distro/builder/src/artifact/disk/mod.rs:106` subtracts a magic `2 // 2MB for GPT overhead`, separate from `distro/builder/src/artifact/disk/assembly.rs:15` `FIRST_PARTITION_OFFSET_SECTORS = 2048` (1 MiB) that encodes the same overhead.
- [special] BC1048 `distro/builder/src/artifact/disk/assembly.rs:50-55` sends sfdisk's stdout and stderr to `/dev/null`, so a failure reports only "sfdisk failed" with sfdisk's reason discarded.
- [special] BC1051 `distro/builder/src/artifact/disk/helpers.rs:38` `result.is_err() || !result.unwrap().success()` folds a failure to run `which` itself into "tool missing".
- [special] BC2093 `distro/builder/src/artifact/filesystem.rs:53-62` `atomic_move` falls back to copy+delete on any `rename` error (permission denied, missing source), not just cross-device, and the fallback is not atomic despite the name.
- [special] BC2101 `distro/builder/src/alpine/modules.rs:105-109` silently copies nothing when the `kernel/` subdirectory is absent and prints "Copied 0 kernel modules" as a success.
- [special] BC2105 `distro/builder/src/alpine/firmware.rs:108,113` skips files and symlinks that already exist at the destination, so a stale firmware file is never replaced.
- [special] BC2108 `distro/builder/src/alpine/filesystem.rs:134` picks shared libraries with `name.contains(".so")`, which also matches names such as `.sock` or `ld.so.conf`.
- [special] BC2113 `distro/builder/src/artifact_store/mod.rs:889-900` `now_unix`/`tmp_name` use `unwrap_or_default()` on the clock, so with the clock before the epoch every temp name gets the same `-0` suffix.
- [special] BC2114 `distro/builder/src/artifact_store/mod.rs:877-879` `#[allow(dead_code)] _file: File` keeps the lock alive only by being held, and the `Drop` at `:883-886` deletes the lock file while another process may already be waiting on it, so a third process can lock a fresh file at the same path at the same time.
- [special] BC2116 `distro/builder/src/cache.rs:98-120` `is_newer` returns true (rebuild) on every metadata error, and `:22-41` `hash_files` returns None (rebuild) for a missing file but warns only for an unreadable one.
- [special] BC2117 `distro/builder/src/build/licenses.rs:121,151` `self.source.to_str().unwrap_or("")` passes `--root ""` for a non-UTF-8 rootfs path, so `rpm`/`apk` query the build host's package database instead of the rootfs's.
- [special] BC2118 `distro/builder/src/build/licenses.rs:135` detects "not owned" by a substring in rpm's stdout, `:165` parses `apk info -W` with `rsplit("is owned by ")`, and `:216-220` prints packages with no license dir as a note, not an error.
- [special] BC3011 `distro/builder/src/alpine/ssh.rs:100` comments out existing lines with `line.starts_with(setting)`, a prefix match (`PermitRoot` would also match `PermitRootLogin`). The already-set check at `:92` uses exact trimmed equality, so one function applies two strictness levels to the same setting.
- [special] BC3016 `distro/builder/src/alpine/filesystem.rs:127` `copy_libraries_recursive` tests `path.is_dir()`, which follows symlinks, before handling symlinks, so a directory symlink under a lib dir is copied as a real tree.
- [special] BC3023 `distro/builder/src/contracts/context.rs:34-46` doc comments tie each enum value to one product ("RPM (used by LevitateOS)", "APK (used by AcornOS / Alpine Linux)", "OpenRC (used by AcornOS)"), and so does `distro/builder/src/contracts/component.rs:36` ("systemd for LevitateOS, OpenRC for AcornOS"). They restate per-variant facts that the variant contracts own.
- [special] BC3033 `distro/builder/src/artifact/disk/assembly.rs:78,91` computes `dd seek=` in MiB by integer division of byte offsets (`bs=1M`), which is correct only while every offset stays MiB-aligned. The only guard is the comment on `FIRST_PARTITION_OFFSET_SECTORS` (`:15`).
- [special] BC3124 `distro/builder/.gitignore` ignores `Cargo.lock` for a crate that is a workspace member (root `Cargo.toml:3-6`), a per-crate standalone-repo convention left in the monorepo.
- [special] BC3131 `distro/builder/src/artifact_store/mod.rs:896-902` `tmp_name` uses only the nanosecond clock (no pid or counter), so concurrent writers can pick the same temp path. `:990` `materialize_file` uses `dest.with_extension("tmp")`, which replaces a real extension (`foo.erofs` becomes `foo.tmp`).
- [special] BP004 `distro/builder/src/alpine/context.rs:37-48` builds the source path as `<base_dir>/downloads/rootfs`. That is exactly the legacy `*/downloads/rootfs` layout `distro/builder/src/pipeline/plan.rs:8-11` forbids as a "policy violation".
- [special] BP005 `distro/builder/src/alpine/context.rs:85-87` `lib_path()` returns the constant `"usr/lib"`, an if-for-one-distro answer ("Alpine uses /usr/lib (musl), not /usr/lib64").
- [special] BP009 `distro/builder/src/alpine/busybox.rs:96-103` swallows both a failed and an unrunnable `busybox --list` and silently falls back to a caller-supplied applet list.
- [special] BP010 `distro/builder/src/alpine/firmware.rs:19-22,62-68` prints a warning and returns `Ok` when the firmware source is missing, so a firmware-less image builds silently.
- [special] BP012 `distro/builder/src/alpine/modules.rs:78` `depmod` failure hint says `sudo dnf install kmod`. The AcornOS-only module assumes a Fedora build host.
- [special] BP017 `distro/builder/src/artifact_store/mod.rs:755-773` `central_output_dir_for_distro` special-cases the directory names `"leviso"` → `levitate` and `"AcornOS"` → `acorn`, and assumes distro crates sit at `<repo>/<DistroDir>`. That layout no longer exists.
- [special] BP019 `distro/builder/src/artifact_store/mod.rs:371-385` kernel payload probes both `lib/modules` and `usr/lib/modules` (a per-distro layout guess), and `:267,327,393,410,421,475,483,1008` swallow `remove_dir_all`/`remove_file` errors with `let _ =`.
- [special] BP022 `distro/builder/src/contracts/disk.rs:22-38` The "distro-agnostic" `DiskImageConfig` contract is systemd-boot shaped (`loader/entries/*.conf`, `loader.conf`, a "systemd-boot EFI binary"), and `distro/builder/src/artifact/disk/partitions.rs:45,50-55` hard-codes `EFI/systemd/systemd-bootx64.efi`. An OpenRC sibling would have to pretend to be systemd-boot.
- [special] BP023 `distro/builder/src/artifact/disk/mtools.rs:64` names its temp file `mtools-<pid>`, so two concurrent writes in one process collide. `:68` drops the cleanup error.
- [special] BP024 `distro/builder/src/artifact/disk/mtools.rs:30-32` treats an `mmd` failure whose output contains "already exists" as success (string-matched error).
- [special] BP033 `distro/builder/src/build/licenses.rs:48-53,68-71` searches `usr/lib/systemd/` for every distro and branches lib paths on `PackageManager::Rpm` vs `Apk`. This is per-package-manager code inside a "shared" tracker.
- [special] BP036 `distro/builder/src/cache.rs:89-93` `needs_rebuild` writes a baseline hash as a side effect of a query and ignores the write error (`let _ =`), which trusts any existing output.
- [special] BR145 `distro/builder/src/executor/binaries.rs:88` resolves target library deps with the host's `ldd` (a glibc host inspecting musl binaries for AcornOS) and treats any `ldd` failure as "static binary, no deps" (`:94`), a swallowed error.
- [special] BR147 `distro/builder/src/executor/openrc.rs:39` error text tells the user to "Check that the corresponding package is installed in alpine.rhai", a file that no longer exists under that role (the list lives in `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:58`).
- [special] BR149 `distro/builder/src/preflight/mod.rs:8` has a runnable ```` ```rust ```` doctest (not `ignore`) for uncalled functions, so `cargo test --doc` compiles it and runs `which` on the host. `executor/mod.rs:8` and `:59` use `rust,ignore` doctests that point at a path (`distro_builder::component::executor`) that does not exist.
- [special] CL1053 `distro/builder/src/executor/mod.rs:106-115` the "generic" interpreter bails on `Bin`/`Sbin`/`Bins`/`Sbins` and `Custom` with "require distro-specific handling", so part of the `Op` vocabulary has no interpreter at all in this crate.
- [special] CL1060 `distro/builder/src/executor/binaries.rs:36-47` the "binary not found" error reports only `usr/bin` and `bin`, although `find_binary` (`:17-28`) also searches `usr/sbin` and `sbin`. The message has drifted from the search list.
- [special] CL1061 `distro/builder/src/executor/binaries.rs:107` (libraries) and `openrc.rs:22` (runlevel links) skip when the destination exists, while `files.rs:41-44` (symlinks) and `binaries.rs:60-70` (binaries) overwrite. Which component wins depends on which handler ran.
- [special] CL1062 `distro/builder/src/executor/users.rs:107,141` treats a user or group as present when the file merely `contains("name:")`, which also matches inside other fields. `:110,143` fall back to caller defaults via `unwrap_or` when the source rootfs lacks the account.
- [special] CL2101 `distro/builder/src/executor/openrc.rs:1-62` is an OpenRC-only module (runlevel symlinks, `conf.d`) with no systemd sibling, inside an executor whose doc calls it distro-agnostic "for LevitateOS, AcornOS" (`executor/mod.rs:3-4`).
- [special] CL2102 `distro/builder/src/executor/users.rs:111-115` (and the group writer at `:141-148`) appends `name:x:uid:gid:...` passwd/group lines without writing a matching `shadow`/`gshadow` entry, so an account created this way has `x` pointing at a missing shadow record.
- [hidden] BC1009 `distro/builder/src/alpine/busybox.rs:67` relies on "The FHS symlinks are created by FILESYSTEM component before this runs", a call order callers must know and nothing enforces.
- [hidden] BC1013 `distro/builder/src/alpine/modules.rs:29` reads modules from `<output>/staging/usr/lib/modules` (a layout written by some other kernel step) and `:44` writes them to `staging/lib/modules`, while the contract pins `module_install_path = "/usr/lib/modules"` (`distro/variants/*/build-host/build-host.toml`).
- [hidden] BC1032 `distro/builder/src/build/licenses.rs:274` is yet another private `copy_dir_recursive`, beside `artifact/filesystem.rs:35`, `executor/files.rs:79` and the alpine copiers, each with its own symlink/overwrite rules.
- [hidden] BC1038 `distro/builder/src/artifact_store/mod.rs:7` and `:356` document input keys as `output/.<artifact>-inputs.hash` / `output/.kernel-inputs.hash` files that nothing in the repo writes any more.
- [hidden] BC1041 `distro/builder/src/artifact_store/mod.rs:904` `atomic_rename`, `distro/builder/src/artifact/filesystem.rs:51` `atomic_move` and the inline rename-or-copy in `distro/builder/src/artifact/disk/mod.rs:136-142` are three copies of the same rename-then-copy+remove (EXDEV) fallback.

- [hidden] BR146 `distro/builder/src/executor/users.rs:86` `ensure_user` / `:123` `ensure_group` implement "add a system account to passwd/group", which `fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:185-192` re-implements in Rhai with `append_line_if_missing`.
- [hidden] CL1065 `distro/builder/src/executor/files.rs:11-29` `handle_writefile` and `handle_writefilemode` are copies that differ by one `set_permissions` call.
- [hidden] CL2104 `distro/builder/src/executor/files.rs:86-91` the private `copy_dir_recursive` checks `is_dir()` (which follows symlinks) before `is_symlink()`, so a symlink to a directory is copied as a full tree. This differs from the exported `crate::copy_dir_recursive` it duplicates.
- [needless] BP043 `distro/builder/src/lib.rs:47-64` exposes every module `pub` (including the dead ones) next to `pub(crate) mod pipeline`. External crates (xtask, install-tests) see a public surface that is mostly dead, so any rename there looks like a breaking change.
- [surplus] BC1018 `distro/builder/src/alpine/keys.rs:31-45` re-checks `key_path.exists()` right after a successful `fs::write`, then re-reads the file for a PEM header that `verify_keys` (`:58`) checks again.
- [surplus] BC1028 `distro/builder/src/build/mod.rs:6` module doc lists a `kernel` submodule ("Kernel building and installation") that does not exist.
- [surplus] BC1042 `distro/builder/src/artifact_store/mod.rs:1101`, `:1124`, `:1147` `#[cfg(not(unix))]` branches in a crate that uses `std::os::unix` unconditionally elsewhere (`tui_binary.rs:118`, `alpine/busybox.rs:61`).
- [surplus] BP001 `distro/builder/src/alpine/mod.rs:1-19` The whole `alpine` module (busybox, context, extract, filesystem, firmware, keys, modules, ssh, timing; ~1,000 lines) has no caller anywhere in the workspace. Its only links are inside the module and to the also-dead `artifact_store`.
- [surplus] BP006 `distro/builder/src/alpine/context.rs:72-80` `for_testing` is `#[allow(dead_code)]` and exists for unit tests the repo bans.
- [surplus] BP013 `distro/builder/src/alpine/timing.rs:1-29` is a byte-for-byte copy of `distro/builder/src/timing.rs:1-29`, and neither `Timer` has a caller.
- [surplus] BP014 `distro/builder/src/alpine/ssh.rs:16-74` generates live SSH host keys at build time. Nothing calls it, and live sshd setup now belongs to the live-overlay recipe.
- [surplus] BP015 `distro/builder/src/alpine/keys.rs:17-91` installs and verifies APK keys. Nothing calls it, and the Alpine rootfs recipe owns APK keys.
- [surplus] BP016 `distro/builder/src/artifact_store/mod.rs:1-1162` The whole content-addressed artifact store (blobs/index/locks/gc/kernel payload) has no caller except dead `alpine/context.rs:39,63` (`central_output_dir_for_distro`). It is the crate's largest file.
- [surplus] BP020 `distro/builder/src/artifact_store/mod.rs:80-83` `open_for_distro` takes the parent of a distro crate dir as the repo root, an extension point for the removed per-distro crates.
- [surplus] BP021 `distro/builder/src/artifact/disk/mod.rs:1-154` (with `helpers.rs`, `assembly.rs`, `partitions.rs`, `mtools.rs`) A sudo-free GPT disk-image builder. No `DiskImageConfig` implementor exists anywhere, and `build_disk_image*` has no caller beyond the `lib.rs:73-75` re-export.
- [surplus] BP025 `distro/builder/src/artifact/disk/helpers.rs:97-116` `calculate_dir_size` has no caller.
- [surplus] BP026 `distro/builder/src/artifact/iso.rs:1-93` is a "placeholder" whose `build_iso` is `unimplemented!()` ("actual implementation is in leviso"). leviso is gone, and ISOs come from reciso via the release hooks.
- [surplus] BP027 `distro/builder/src/contracts/context.rs:11-29` `DistroConfig` and `distro/builder/src/contracts/kernel.rs:7-13` `KernelInstallConfig` have no implementors. Their doc says "Implemented by leviso for LevitateOS and AcornOS".
- [surplus] BP028 `distro/builder/src/contracts/context.rs:61-76` the `BuildContext` trait has one generic consumer, `executor::execute_generic_op_ctx` (`distro/builder/src/executor/mod.rs:37`), which is itself uncalled, and no implementor. `SimpleBuildContext` (`distro/builder/src/build/context.rs:15-36`) does not even implement it.
- [surplus] BP029 `distro/builder/src/contracts/component.rs:12-185` `Installable`, `Phase` (9 phases) and `Op` with 13 variants plus 10 constructor helpers serve only the uncalled executor. `Op::Custom` (`:129`) is an extension point nothing uses.
- [surplus] BP030 `distro/builder/src/component/mod.rs:1-7` re-exports `contracts::component::*` "for backwards compatibility". There are no remaining callers to be compatible with.
- [surplus] BP031 `distro/builder/src/build/context.rs:9` and `distro/builder/src/contracts/mod.rs:8-11` and `distro/builder/src/lib.rs:66-70` re-export the same dead contract types at three paths.
- [surplus] BP032 `distro/builder/src/build/licenses.rs:22-295` `LicenseTracker` (RPM/APK ownership queries) has no caller. It is the only user of `PackageManager` (`distro/builder/src/contracts/context.rs:33-38`).
- [surplus] BP034 `distro/builder/src/build/filesystem.rs:28-139` `create_fhs_dirs`/`create_merged_usr_symlinks`/`create_var_symlinks`/`create_fhs_structure` have no caller. Recipe produces the filesystem.
- [surplus] BP035 `distro/builder/src/cache.rs:1-120` hash-based rebuild detection has no caller.
- [surplus] BP040 `distro/builder/src/artifact/filesystem.rs:51-64` `atomic_move` has no caller outside its `lib.rs:76` re-export.
- [surplus] BP041 `distro/builder/Cargo.toml` `fs2`, `tar`, `zstd` and `sha2` are pulled in only by dead modules (`artifact_store`, `cache`).
- [surplus] BP042 `distro/builder/src/lib.rs:1-45` The crate doc describes a "structural skeleton ... not all functionality is extracted from leviso yet" with an `Installable` example. None of that describes what the crate does now (product pipeline over Recipe). `distro/builder/src/artifact/mod.rs:1-15` and `Cargo.toml` (`repository = .../distro-builder`, "ISOs") are stale the same way.
- [surplus] BP079 `distro/builder/src/lib.rs:80-85` publicly re-exports `plan_product_build_chain`, `plan_release_prerequisite_products`, `is_release_buildable_product`, `ProductBuildPlan` and `ReleasePrerequisiteStep`, which only `planner.rs` itself uses.
- [surplus] BR085 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:388` `clear_cache` (removes `~/.cache/levitate`) has no callers.
- [surplus] BR143 `distro/builder/src/executor/` (`mod.rs`, `binaries.rs`, `directories.rs`, `files.rs`, `openrc.rs`, `users.rs`, 618 lines) is re-exported from `src/lib.rs:57` and `:70`, but no function in it is called anywhere in the workspace (`execute_generic_op`, `copy_binary`, `enable_service`, `ensure_user`, ... all 0 callers). It belongs to the old `Op`/`BuildContext` component pipeline.
- [surplus] BR150 `distro/builder/src/preflight/mod.rs:29` `command_exists` shells out to `which`, while the crate already depends on the `which` crate (`distro/builder/Cargo.toml:28`), and `recipes/linux-deps.rhai:20` shells out to `which` too.
- [surplus] BR152 `distro/builder/Cargo.toml:8` `repository = "https://github.com/LevitateOS/distro-builder"`, `keywords` and `categories` are publishing metadata for a crate that is a path-only workspace member.
- [surplus] CL1051 `distro/builder/src/executor/mod.rs:37-42` `execute_generic_op_ctx` only forwards `ctx.source()`/`ctx.staging()` to `execute_generic_op`.
- [surplus] CL1063 `distro/builder/src/executor/users.rs:153-168` `handle_user`/`handle_group` only forward to `ensure_user`/`ensure_group`.
- [surplus] CL1064 `distro/builder/src/executor/directories.rs:25-30` `handle_dirs` repeats `handle_dir` in a loop, and `executor/mod.rs:70-73` converts `Vec<String>` to `Vec<&str>` just to call it.
- [surplus] CL1127 `distro/builder/Cargo.toml:15` `dirs` is used only by the dead `recipe::clear_cache` (`recipe/mod.rs:389`), and `:28` `which` only by the last PATH fallback of `find_recipe` (`recipe/mod.rs:138`).

### G02 Delete the checkers and tests that nothing runs or that check one distro only

Solves **137** problems (11 parity, 42 special, 47 hidden, 4 needless, 33 surplus).

- **Design:** A check earns its place only if something runs it against both distros' current artifacts. `testing/hardware-compat`, fsdbg's unreached half and its LevitateOS-only `oracle/`, `testing/rootfs-tests` and `distro/builder/tests` are deleted; `distro/elf` folds into recinit as a private module.
- **Done when:** The workspace members, `.gitmodules`, CI and docs no longer name hardware-compat, rootfs-tests or leviso-elf; fsdbg keeps only what preflight calls (or leaves with G25); `distro/builder/tests` is gone.
- **Depends on:** none
- **Decisions:** Archive the `hardware-compat`, `rootfs-tests` and `leviso-elf` GitHub repos (default: archive). Builder tests: delete (default; CL-16 proposed wiring them into xtask instead).
- **Merges area designs:** TS-01 (testing-static), TS-03 (testing-static), TS-09 (testing-static), RG-14 (builder-recipes-guest), CV-16 (contract-variants), CL-16 (builder-cli) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] TF035 `testing/hardware-compat/README.md:26-28` and `src/main.rs:42` tell the user to pass `<rootfs>/usr/lib/firmware`, the Fedora merged-/usr location. Alpine keeps firmware at `lib/firmware`, which is the path the only AcornOS firmware code uses (the dead `distro/builder/src/alpine/firmware.rs:15-16,59-60`). Neither live rootfs recipe ships linux-firmware. The tool's documented input encodes only the LevitateOS layout, and that input exists on neither sibling.
- [parity] TF045 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/rootfs.rs:1-31,63-124` forbids `bin/busybox` and busybox-linked `usr/bin/{ls,cat,...}` as a "REWARD HACK", while AcornOS ships busybox by design (`testing/install-tests/src/distro/acorn.rs:1`, `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:105,254-257`, `distro/builder/src/alpine/busybox.rs`). The rule is a LevitateOS-only policy written as a universal one, and it would fail every AcornOS rootfs.
- [parity] TF046 `testing/fsdbg/src/oracle/etc.rs:56-70` requires `usr/lib64/libc.so.6`, `ld-linux-x86-64.so.2`, the glibc compat stubs `libpthread.so.0`/`libdl.so.2`/`librt.so.1`, `libsystemd.so.0` and `libselinux.so.1`. That is glibc/systemd/SELinux-only, so a musl AcornOS rootfs has no equivalent oracle.
- [parity] TF047 `testing/fsdbg/src/oracle/filesystem.rs:11,72,85` and `checklist/rootfs.rs:385`, `auth_audit.rs:198,212,340` hard-code `usr/lib64` and `usr/lib64/security` (Fedora multilib layout). Alpine uses `lib`/`usr/lib/security`.
- [parity] TF048 `testing/fsdbg/src/oracle/auth.rs:9,16,131-138`, `oracle/etc.rs:27-28` and `checklist/auth_audit.rs:44,71,98-99,311` require `sudo`, `sudoedit`, `sudoreplay`, `visudo`, `etc/sudoers`, `etc/sudo.conf`, `etc/pam.d/sudo` and `usr/libexec/sudo/*`. The privilege-tool difference (sudo vs doas) lives here as literals, and doas has no checklist at all.
- [parity] TF049 `testing/fsdbg/src/oracle/units.rs:6-190`, `oracle/systemd.rs:4-27` and `checklist/rootfs.rs:261-334,431-461` check the rootfs as a systemd system (units, `usr/lib/systemd/*`, `usr/sbin/init -> systemd`). `auth_audit.rs:379-430` repeats the init and logind checks. AcornOS (OpenRC) has no counterpart, so a whole rootfs contract exists for one sibling only.
- [parity] TF050 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/rootfs.rs:604-634` `CRITICAL_LICENSE_PACKAGES` uses RPM package names (`glibc`, `shadow-utils`, `iproute`, `openssh-clients`, `vim-minimal`, `NetworkManager`, `kernel`, `linux-firmware`), and `rootfs.rs:593` says "LevitateOS copies binaries from Rocky Linux". The license-compliance check is tied to one package base.
- [parity] TF051 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/qcow2.rs:15-25,64-68` expects `boot/EFI/systemd/systemd-bootx64.efi` and `etc/systemd/system/multi-user.target.wants/{NetworkManager,sshd,chronyd}.service`. That is LevitateOS-only, and AcornOS installed images have no checklist.
- [parity] TS1047 `testing/hardware-compat/src/profiles/*.rs` require `intel-ucode/*` and `amd-ucode/*` under the firmware dir; neither variant recipe or builder source mentions microcode packages, and the location of microcode differs per base distro, so the check assumes one distro's packaging.
- [parity] TS3051 `testing/fsdbg/src/oracle/filesystem.rs:81-86` `FHS_SYMLINKS` requires `bin -> usr/bin`, `sbin -> usr/sbin`, `lib -> usr/lib`, `lib64 -> usr/lib64` and `checklist/rootfs.rs:140-171` fails each as "merged-usr broken"; the built AcornOS rootfs (`.artifacts/out/acorn/releases/live-boot/*/rootfs-source`) has real `bin/`, `sbin/`, `lib/` directories and no `lib64`, so the rootfs checklist fails that sibling's layout by construction.
- [parity] Z026 `distro/builder/tests/preseed_contract.rs:13` copies only `distro/variants/acorn` into its fixture; the preseed contract is never checked for LevitateOS.
- [special] C127 `distro/elf/src/copy.rs:95-107` chooses the destination directory by substring tests on the source path (`contains("lib64/<dir>")`, `contains("lib64")`). Any path component containing "lib64" changes the result. `copy.rs:122,126-127` `unwrap()` on path conversions.
- [special] C128 `copy.rs:39-41` `copy_dir_recursive` returns `Ok(0)` when `src` is not a directory (it silently copies nothing), and `copy.rs:109-111` skips any library whose destination already exists, even a stale one.
- [special] CL3147 `distro/builder/tests/preseed_contract.rs` and `tests/recipe_outputs.rs` are not run by any xtask command, justfile recipe or CI workflow (no `cargo test` anywhere in `.github/workflows`, `xtask/src` or `justfile`). `recipe_outputs` also needs `GINUX_BIN` to be configured by hand (`distro/builder/README.md:113-116`), so these integration checks never run unattended.
- [special] CV3118 `distro/elf/src/copy.rs:50-52` `copy_dir_recursive_impl` tests `is_dir()` (which follows symlinks) before `is_symlink()`, so directory symlinks are copied as real trees; `:62-64` swallows a metadata error while counting bytes, and every caller (`distro/builder/src/artifact/filesystem.rs:38`, `tools/recinit/src/payload.rs:306,328`) discards the returned size.
- [special] RG1144 `distro/builder/recipes/custom-source-rootfs.rhai:63-64`: `cleanup` removes the output on `auto.build.failure`, which no other recipe here does. The other rootfs producers clean up only on `manual` (`fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:300`, `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:293`).
- [special] RG1147 `distro/builder/tests/preseed_contract.rs:107`: the test asserts that a legacy `AcornOS/downloads` directory is not created. It guards against an old layout that nothing in the current code produces.
- [special] RG1151 `distro/builder/tests/recipe_outputs.rs:151-154`: `managed_recfstab_input_install_reuse_and_removal` panics unless someone sets `RECFSTAB_PROGRAM` by hand, so a plain `cargo test` fails here.
- [special] RG1152 `distro/builder/tests/recipe_outputs.rs:50-52,124`: the tests plant a legacy `.recipe-output.json` to prove it is not reused, which keeps a former file name alive in the tests.
- [special] TF039 `testing/hardware-compat/src/profiles/dell_xps.rs:69,75`, `framework.rs:72,78`, `gaming_laptop.rs:222,228`, `linux_laptop.rs:194,200`, `nuc.rs:175,181`, `thinkpad.rs:195,201` and `workstation.rs:228,234` each list `CONFIG_USB4` twice ("USB4/Thunderbolt 4" and "Thunderbolt bus"). This is a leftover from the `CONFIG_THUNDERBOLT`→`CONFIG_USB4` rename, so the same check runs twice per profile.
- [special] TF042 `testing/hardware-compat/src/main.rs:49-56` selects `--profile` by case-insensitive substring and takes the first match, so `--profile desktop` returns "Desktop" or "Gaming Desktop" depending on `get_all_profiles` order (`profiles/mod.rs:45-67`).
- [special] TF043 `testing/hardware-compat/src/kernel.rs:31-33` downgrades "expected built-in, got module" to a Warning, which never fails (`report.rs:52-57`). So `ConfigValue::Y` vs `ConfigValue::M` (chosen by hand in all 786 entries) only changes the colour of the output.
- [special] TF044 `testing/hardware-compat/src/lib.rs:51-70` turns an invalid glob into `CompatResult::Fail` and counts unreadable glob entries as absent (`filter_map(Result::ok)`). I/O errors are swallowed into a "missing firmware" result.
- [special] TF060 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/rootfs.rs:185-193` special-cases `bash` and `openssl` into the binary list ("handled separately in leviso"; leviso no longer exists) instead of listing them in `oracle::BIN_UTILS`.
- [special] TF061 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/rootfs.rs:290-295` picks the `user/` vs `system/` unit directory by whether the name contains "pipewire" or "wireplumber". `oracle::PIPEWIRE_UNITS` (`oracle/units.rs:121`) already exists as a separate list but carries no directory.
- [special] TF062 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/rootfs.rs:305-309` changes the failure message when a unit name contains "halt"/"poweroff"/"reboot".
- [special] TF063 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/rootfs.rs:316-319` always adds a passing "Systemd units: N/M found" result, and `rootfs.rs:322-333` adds a second failure for the same missing units. The report's pass/fail counts include made-up summary rows.
- [special] TF064 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/rootfs.rs:477-482` reports broken symlinks only under `usr/bin`, `usr/sbin`, `usr/lib64` and `etc`, and ignores the rest without saying so.
- [special] TF065 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/rootfs.rs:432-447` adds no result when `usr/sbin/init` is a symlink with no target. `auth_audit.rs:381-397` adds none when it is targetless or is not a symlink at all. In those cases the init check silently passes.
- [special] TF066 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/qcow2.rs:602-633` requires root to have an empty password in the installed image, and a locked or set password fails. That is an installer and first-boot policy that exists only here and is not owned by recstrap, the builder or the variants.
- [special] TF086 `testing/fsdbg/src/lib.rs:69-79` checks the ISO 9660 magic only when the extension is `.iso`, and `lib.rs:90-96` maps any `.img` to CPIO. Format detection depends on the file name.
- [special] TF087 `testing/fsdbg/src/lib.rs:55-57` classifies any gzip file as `CpioGzip`.
- [special] TF088 `testing/fsdbg/src/main.rs:255,262` sleeps 500 ms and 300 ms, hoping qemu-nbd partitions have appeared.
- [special] TF089 `testing/fsdbg/src/main.rs:225-232,258-260,346-348` ignores failures of `sudo mkdir`, `partprobe` and `modprobe nbd` (`let _ =`), and `main.rs:303-309` turns a failed bind mount into a warning.
- [special] TF090 `testing/fsdbg/src/main.rs:207-211` only prints a note when not root and carries on. `qcow2.rs:651-657` then reports a Permission-denied as a check failure, so missing privileges show up as image defects.
- [special] TL096 `distro/elf/src/analyze.rs:41-48` readelf failures are classified by matching stderr text ("Not an ELF file", "is not readable", ...) and turned into "no dependencies"; an unreadable setuid binary silently copies without its libraries.
- [special] TL097 `distro/elf/src/analyze.rs:111-113` a NEEDED library that `find_library` cannot find is recorded but not followed, so its own dependencies are never collected; the failure surfaces later in `copy_library_to`.
- [special] TS1056 `testing/hardware-compat/src/lib.rs:40` skips `# CONFIG_X is not set` lines as comments, so an explicitly disabled option and an unknown symbol both report "Not found" (`kernel.rs:38`).
- [special] TS1058 `testing/hardware-compat/src/lib.rs:57` counts glob matches including directories (`intel/**/*` in two profiles) toward `min_files`.
- [special] TS1101 `testing/fsdbg/src/main.rs:288-291,318-321,382-397` swallow every cleanup `sudo umount` and `qemu-nbd -d` with `let _ =` (including in the `Qcow2Cleanup` drop); a failed cleanup leaves a mount or an NBD device attached without a report.
- [special] TS1103 `testing/fsdbg/src/main.rs:208` calls `unsafe { libc::getuid() }` only to print a note; `libc` (`Cargo.toml:20`) is a dependency for that one line.
- [special] TS1105 `testing/fsdbg/src/main.rs:344-366` probes `/dev/nbd0..15` by reading `/sys/block/nbdN/size == 0`, a race with other users of NBD; `max_part=16` and the 16-device bound are magic numbers.
- [special] TS1106 `testing/fsdbg/src/erofs.rs:43-57,81-104` falls back to `fsck.erofs` and returns an empty entry list on success or on a non-"error" failure; every later check sees an empty filesystem instead of an error.
- [special] TS1107 `testing/fsdbg/src/erofs.rs:116-160` parses `dump.erofs --ls` "format varies by version" by guessing ls-style vs bare-path lines; `unwrap_or(0)` for size.
- [special] TS1112 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/rootfs.rs:684-691` always adds a passing "N package licenses found" result, inflating the pass count reported by `main.rs` with a row that checks nothing.
- [special] TS1116 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/auth_audit.rs:328-353` silently skips missing "recommended" hardening files/modules (comments say "silently skipped") and `:357-375` accepts a regular-file `password-auth` without a result.
- [special] TS1118 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/qcow2.rs:319-381` accepts `vmlinuz`/`vmlinuz-*` and `initramfs.img`/`initramfs-*.img` "for compatibility with different builds"; two naming schemes are tolerated instead of one owner's name.
- [special] TS2039 `testing/fsdbg/src/erofs.rs:62-66` runs `dump.erofs --ls -r IMAGE`. The installed `dump.erofs` has no `-r` option ("invalid option -- 'r'" against `.artifacts/out/acorn/releases/base-rootfs/*/filesystem.erofs`), and `--ls` needs an inode (`--path`/`--nid`). So the primary path always fails, and `fsdbg inspect` on the product's EROFS rootfs reports an external-tool error, never the contents.
- [special] TS2040 `testing/fsdbg/src/erofs.rs:34-38` and `:44-48` check tool availability two different ways: `dump.erofs --help` must exit 0, while `fsck.erofs --help` only has to spawn (`.map(|_| true)`). `iso.rs:35-39` checks `isoinfo -version` the spawn-only way. A `dump.erofs` whose `--help` exits non-zero silently switches to the fsck path, which returns no entries.
- [special] TS2049 `testing/fsdbg/src/main.rs:454-523` `cmd_diff` always returns `Ok(true)` (exit 0) whatever the differences, so `fsdbg diff` cannot be used as a check, although every other subcommand's exit code reports success or failure (`main.rs:60-72`).
- [special] TS2081 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/qcow2.rs:236-266` treats "options missing root= parameter" and "no options line" as warnings and adds a PASS with them in parentheses, so a boot entry that cannot find its root still passes. The root= convention is owned by the installer's boot-entry writer.
- [special] TS2099 `testing/hardware-compat/src/lib.rs:58-64` returns a Warning for "Found only N files, expected M" whatever the requirement's severity, and Warnings never fail the run. So every `min_files` threshold only separates "warning" from "pass", and a Critical firmware set with one file left still exits 0.
- [special] TS3111 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/qcow2.rs:578-583` records a `locale.conf` without `LANG` as PASS "(exists, no LANG set)" and `:592-597` a missing `locale.conf` as PASS ("system will use defaults"); absence of the locale setting is reported as success.
- [hidden] CV2119 `distro/elf/src/copy.rs:52-61` non-overwrite mode keeps an existing destination symlink but always overwrites regular files (`fs::copy`), so "overwrite" governs symlinks only and callers of `copy_dir_recursive` vs `_overwrite` get the same behaviour for files.
- [hidden] CV2121 `distro/elf/.gitignore:3` ignores `Cargo.lock` but `distro/elf/Cargo.lock` is tracked; `distro/contract/.gitignore` has the identical rule and no lock: the two sibling crates follow opposite lockfile policies under the same ignore file.
- [hidden] CV2122 `distro/elf/README.md:82-84` says "License: MIT" while `distro/elf/Cargo.toml:7` says `MIT OR Apache-2.0` and both `LICENSE-MIT` and `LICENSE-APACHE` ship; the README's licence statement has drifted from the manifest.
- [hidden] RG1148 `distro/builder/tests/preseed_contract.rs:53-56`: the environment variable names `GINUX_BIN`, `RECIPE_WORKER`, `RECIPE_INVOCATION` and `RECIPE_BACKEND` belong to Recipe/ginux, and the test types them out by hand.
- [hidden] RG1149 `distro/builder/tests/preseed_contract.rs:46-48`: the test falls back to `target/debug/recipe`. That path convention for locating the Recipe binary is also built in `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:205` (`find_recipe`).

- [hidden] RG3079 `distro/builder/tests/preseed_contract.rs:65-69` and `:121-131` build their failure cases by text-editing the copied `distro/variants/acorn/ring3/sources.toml`: they drop lines that start with `preseed_` / `preseed_outputs` / `preseed_recipe_script` and replace the literal `iso = "file"`. The tests depend on that file's formatting (keys at column 0, the inline-table spelling at `:13`). If the formatting changes, the `replace` silently does nothing, and the `!success` assertion can still pass for an unrelated reason.

Alpine splits linux-firmware differently, and nothing links the thresholds to either distro's firmware package selection.

One firmware directory has three thresholds.

### G03 The contract is one typed file per variant, loaded and validated in one call

Solves **71** problems (1 parity, 14 special, 30 hidden, 3 needless, 23 surplus).

- **Design:** The Rust type is the TOML shape: one `distro.toml` per variant (plus a kernel fragment and signing keys beside it), no serde mirror or hand converter, no legacy flat layout, no build-host manifest or evidence script. One validation rule per value kind, run once at load and reporting the real field path. The loader owns the repo-root rule, the variant list (from `distro/variants/*`) and the distro-id rule. Values that cannot vary are code of their owner, not configuration.
- **Done when:** `variant.rs` has no `FlatRoot`/legacy filenames; every manifest field exists once; every declared field has a reader (grep); `build-host.toml`, the evidence scripts and the ring directories are gone from both variants.
- **Depends on:** none
- **Decisions:** Drop `extends` from variant TOML (default yes). Delete the declared-but-unbuilt `installed_uki` (default yes).
- **Merges area designs:** CV-02 (contract-variants), CV-03 (contract-variants), CV-04 (contract-variants), CL-04 (builder-cli), CL-14 (builder-cli) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] P084 `distro/variants/acorn/README.md:1-9` documents a shorter required-file list (no `ring0/hooks/build-release.sh`, no invariants) than `levitate/README.md`, which calls itself "the canonical build-host scaffold for all distro variants"; the AcornOS variant is described as derived from LevitateOS.
- [special] BR128 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/layout.rs:6` finds the repo through the compile-time `CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR`, so the binary only works in the checkout it was built from. The error message (`:14`) still says "for policy guard".
- [special] C020 `variant.rs:681-706`: the eight-arm match on `(flat_exists, owner_exists, owner_legacy_exists)` uses `unreachable!` and `.expect("legacy path present")`. It is a migration state machine that makes all seven manifests change together.
- [special] C022 `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:81-93` `resolved_build_host_support_root` swallows `PartialRingManifestSet { present: [] }` and falls back to the bare `variant_dir`. This back-compat path serves a variant dir with no manifests at all.
- [special] C035 `distro/contract/src/validate.rs:1226` calls `validate_build_runtime_non_kernel_inputs`, and `validate.rs:916` (inside `validate_build_contract`, called at `validate.rs:1227`) calls it again. One bad transform reports the same violations twice.
- [special] C036 `distro/contract/src/validate.rs:129-144` `first_transform_output` pushes a "must contain at least one output name" violation each time it is called. For the rootfs/initramfs/overlay transforms it runs three times through `expected_build_runtime_required_inputs` (twice via C035, once at `validate.rs:1129`) and again in `validate_artifact_identity_mirrors` (`:197-235`), so one empty `output_names` yields up to four duplicate violations.
- [special] C039 `validate.rs:29,1097-1157` `FORBIDDEN_BUILD_ROOTFS_TOKEN = "squashfs"` rejects squashfs naming in the rootfs name, the derived inputs and the build-tool names. It is a leftover migration guard. Both kconfigs still enable `CONFIG_SQUASHFS` (`levitate kconfig:181`, `acorn kconfig:195`), and `scenarios.toml:29` still matches `SQUASHFS error`.
- [special] CL1110 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/parse.rs:195-197` skips directories whose names start with `_` so that `_shared` is not taken for a distro. The shared tree is recognised by a naming prefix.
- [special] CL2046 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/artifacts.rs:340` (`sources.rootfs_source.preseed_recipe_script`), `:62` and `:91` (`ring2/products.toml`, `extends`, `boot_installed`) put the contract crate's file names and field paths into CLI error text, so renaming a contract field or file means editing the builder's messages too.
- [special] CV1055 `distro/variants/*/build-host/evidence/build-capability.sh:9,14` tests the kernel release file with `-s` (non-empty) but the kernel image and ISO with `-f` (exists). An empty `vmlinuz` or ISO passes the build-capability evidence.
- [special] CV1079 `distro/contract/src/variant.rs:787-792` `resolve_group_root` returns `OwnerDirectories` when none of the group's files exist. For the ring0 hooks group (`:461-471`) nothing else checks them at load time (the `validate_layout` calls at `:350-402` cover only build-host files), so a variant with no release hooks loads cleanly and fails later inside the builder (`distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/release_hook.rs:63-73`).
- [special] CV2046 `distro/contract/src/variant.rs:378` and `:398` return only `failures.first()` as `MissingRequiredFile`, discarding the rest of the `LayoutReport` the same `validate_layout` call computed, while the runtime path reports all failures.
- [special] CV2106 `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:27-32` reports violations under field names `scenarios.live_boot.rootfs_source_path`, `.envelope`, `.locale`, `.artifacts`, none of which exist in `BootScenario` (`schema.rs:305-318`) or any TOML; `ContractScope` (`error.rs:7-15`) likewise has no member for the live environment, installed tools or runtime policy, so reports point at fields and scopes nobody can edit.
- [special] CV3028 `distro/contract/src/validate.rs:569-578` `is_relative_contract_path("..")` returns true: it rejects only `../`, `/../` and a trailing `/..` by string tests, while ring2 fields reject any `ParentDir` component (`distro/contract/src/variant.rs:1244-1256`) and the builder has a third component-based copy (`distro/builder/src/pipeline/config.rs:147-173`); `kernel_*_path`, `recipe_script`, `signing_key` and evidence paths get the weaker rule.
- [special] CV3052 `distro/contract/src/variant.rs:366-375,447-456` the loader joins the TOML `evidence.script_path` onto the variant dir and requires the file to exist before `validate.rs:504` has checked the value is a safe relative path, so the existence of an unvalidated (possibly escaping) path is tested first.
- [hidden] BR125 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/release_hook.rs:18` requires exactly three live UKI output names and binds them by position to live, emergency and debug. The meaning of each slot in `ring1` TOML `output_names` is a positional convention.
- [hidden] BR127 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/build.rs:38`, `:52` and `artifacts.rs:161`, `:333`, `:360` take the repo from `std::env::current_dir()` (searched upward by `load_variant_contract_bundle_for_distro_from`), while `commands.rs:18`, `:92` and `:100` use `locate_repo_root()` (compile-time `CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR`, `layout.rs:6`). `release build iso` therefore discovers distros in the checkout the binary was built from and builds from the checkout containing the cwd, and `build_all` (`build.rs:38-39`) passes the cwd straight to `discover_distro_ids`, so `release build-all iso` works only from the repo root.
- [hidden] C010 `distro/variants/{levitate,acorn}/build-host/build-host.toml:4-18` `required_build_tools` is identical in both variants, and nothing outside the contract reads it (validated only at `distro/contract/src/validate.rs:686-691` and `:1144`). A second, different host-tool list sits in `distro/builder/src/preflight/mod.rs:40` (`REQUIRED_TOOLS`: adds `cpio`/`gzip`, lacks `cargo`/`make`/`curl`/`sha512sum`/`ukify`), and nothing calls that one either (BR148), so no build step checks the declared host tools.
- [hidden] C012 `distro/variants/levitate/build-host/evidence/build-capability.sh:5-7` hard-codes default paths `.artifacts/out/levitate/...levitateos-x86_64.iso`, and acorn's copy hard-codes the acorn ones. They repeat the builder's output layout and the `ring0/release.toml` ISO name. The builder always overrides them (`distro/builder/src/pipeline/kernel.rs:121-127`), so the defaults are dead copies that drift.
- [hidden] C013 `levitate/.../build-capability.sh` and `acorn/.../build-capability.sh` are the same script apart from the distro id in three defaults (and one blank line). It is a per-variant copy of a shared check.
- [hidden] C021 `variant.rs:92-100` `ResolvedManifestPaths` is a positional 7-tuple filled by `resolved_iter.next().expect(..)` (`variant.rs:733-745`). The order of `manifest_specs` at 602-652 must match the tuple order, and nothing enforces it.
- [hidden] C025 `variant.rs:572` takes `schema_version` from `build-host.toml` only. The `schema_version` in the other six manifests is parsed (`variant.rs:1532,1562,1619,1734,1762,1786`) and ignored, so bumping the schema means editing seven files of which only one is checked.
