### G04 Each variant declares its distro profile once, and every layer reads it

Solves **87** problems (7 parity, 23 special, 41 hidden, 2 needless, 14 surplus).

- **Design:** The facts that really differ underneath — package backend (rpm/apk), init (systemd/openrc), libc (glibc/musl), privilege tool (sudo/doas), desktop (Plasma/LXQt), kernel line (LTS/latest) — are declared once per variant. The vocabulary lives in one small leaf crate (`distro-profile`) that the contract, builder, install tools, tests and (generated) TUIs all use. The system recipe writes the profile into the image's `/etc/os-release` (`REC_*` fields), so recsetup, recab and the TUIs read it on the target instead of probing files. Every name built from the variant id (hostname, ISO label, artifact names, login prompt, localversion, Rust/bun target) is computed, not spelt. Nothing branches on a distro name.
- **Done when:** No `"levitate"`/`"acorn"` string comparisons and no `Distro::Levitate`/`Acorn` matches outside the variant files (grep); one `InitSystem` and one `Libc` type in the workspace; recsetup and recab do not probe for useradd/sudo/systemd files; install-tests has no `DistroContext`; the TUIs get a generated facts record.
- **Depends on:** G03
- **Merges area designs:** CV-01 (contract-variants), BC-06 (builder-core), RG-01 (builder-recipes-guest), TV-01 (testing-vm), PA-01 (parity-all), PA-08 (parity-all), TO-04 (tools), UD-01 (tui-docs), CL-11 (builder-cli) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] P009 [parity] the lock includes `glibc-langpack-en` only (`distro/builder/recipes/sources/levitate-rpm-lock.rhai:43`), so `recsetup` on LevitateOS offers English locales only (`tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:294` Precompiled), while AcornOS's `musl-locales` (`distro/builder/recipes/sources/alpine.rhai:20-21`) lists every musl locale. The locale choice in the install differs.
- [parity] P022 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:331` makes `/bin/bash` the default login shell when present, so users created on LevitateOS get bash and on AcornOS get busybox `ash`; `distro/variants/*/ring2/products.toml:8` makes the same split for the install screen (`/bin/bash` vs `/bin/sh`). bash is available in Alpine, so this is a base leak, not an intended difference.
- [parity] P044 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:309-316` warns on AcornOS when no keymap service exists; LevitateOS's `vconsole.conf` path has no check that something loads it (no `systemd-vconsole-setup` check), so a missing keymap loader is reported on one distro only.
- [parity] U015 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/01-getting-started.ts:15-16` requires x86-64-v3 and 16 GB RAM; `11-acorn-installation.ts:30` requires plain x86-64 and states no RAM; the two siblings declare different hardware baselines in prose only.
- [parity] U080 `docs/content/src/content/04-architecture/04-variants.ts:14-33` the variant matrix lists base, stack, admin tool and ISO, but neither it nor any install page states the intended kernel difference (LTS 6.12 vs latest 7.x) or the desktop (Plasma vs LXQt); the only per-distro kernel fact the user can find is the AcornOS "no initramfs" row.
- [parity] U093 `docs/website/src/lib/variants.ts:44-45,64-65` sets AcornOS's minimum at 4 GB RAM / 32 GB disk vs LevitateOS's 16 GB / 64 GB; 32 GB is below the default A/B layout both guides use (1 + 20 + 20 GiB + state), so AcornOS's advertised minimum cannot take the default install.
- [parity] UD1048 `docs/website/src/lib/variants.ts:6-24` the `Variant` model has no desktop or kernel field, so the website cannot state the intended Plasma/LXQt and LTS/latest differences; its strengths/trade-offs (`:42-43,62-63`: LevitateOS "Slower boot", "Higher memory use"; AcornOS "Faster boot", "Lower memory use") present the siblings as products of different capability.
- [special] BP046 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/live_tools.rs:13-17` maps distro id to init by name (`"acorn" => "openrc"`, `"levitate" => "systemd"`, else bail). The spec already carries the contract's `overlay: InitSystem` (`pipeline/products.rs:136`), so this copy ignores the contract, and a third variant fails here.
- [special] BR098 `distro/builder/src/recipe/tools.rs:297` derives the Rust target from the init system (`init == "openrc"` → musl, else gnu). libc is inferred from init by an `if` on one value instead of being declared by the variant.
- [special] C031 `variant.rs:937-953` parses `overlay_kind` from a free `String` with `trim().to_ascii_lowercase()` and two literal arms (`"systemd"`, `"openrc"`). Every other enum is a serde enum. This one is hand-matched.
- [special] CL1105 `distro/builder/src/recipe/tools.rs:147,165-168,183-251` builds a tool's source closure from `src/`, the build script, and files found by text-scanning for literal `include_str!`/`include_bytes!`/`include!` paths. Any other compile-time input (`#[path]`, a `build.rs` reading siblings, non-literal includes) is missed, and `:197-201` silently skips includes that do not resolve.
- [special] CL1106 `distro/builder/src/recipe/tools.rs:264-266` bails on any `workspace = true` dependency, so every live-tool crate must declare its dependencies explicitly. That rule is enforced only here, at product-build time; the tool crates' manifests say nothing about it.
- [special] CL2013 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/commands.rs:102` `prepare-tool-source` tests for an existing output with `symlink_metadata(output).is_ok()`, so any metadata error (e.g. EACCES on the parent) is treated as "does not exist" and swallowed.
- [special] CL2014 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/commands.rs:111-115` renames the `TempDir`'s path to the output and then lets the `TempDir` drop. Its cleanup of the now-missing path fails and the error is ignored, so the command depends on a swallowed drop error.
- [special] CL2097 `distro/builder/src/recipe/tools.rs:55-61` copies `[workspace.package]` from the root manifest into the generated workspace, so `package.x.workspace = true` fields resolve, but `:264-266` bails on `workspace = true` dependencies. Workspace inheritance is half supported in the tool source closure.
- [special] P092 `testing/install-tests/src/distro/mod.rs:38-47` and `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/docs.ts:18-19` keep their own lists of the two distros; `distro/variants/` is the list.
- [special] RG1079 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:70-76`: `validate_service` uses its own character allowlist for service names, separate from the naming rules recsetup and the variant contract apply to the same names.
- [special] RG2050 `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh:101` `id=$(cat /etc/machine-id 2>/dev/null || true)` swallows a read error under `set -eu` and treats it as "regenerate". Both producers ship an empty 0444 machine-id (`installed-system.rhai:33`, `live-overlay.rhai:68`), so the non-systemd path always takes the regenerate branch.
- [special] RG2159 `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh:27-30` `timezone` special-cases the value `UTC`: when the zoneinfo file is missing, it deletes `/etc/localtime` instead of failing. Both siblings ship `tzdata`, so this exists for a layout neither one has.
- [special] TI004 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:541` adds the "Initramfs on ESP" verify step only for initramfs distros; `install.rs:89` adds `--initrd` only for them; `ab_update.rs:269` the same for `recab stage`. Three separate `if uses_initramfs` branches for one fact.
- [special] TL011 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:42-56` `InitSystem::running()` detects the init by `/run/systemd/system` vs `/run/openrc`, and `installed_in` (`:59-77`) by `openrc-run` vs `usr/lib/systemd/systemd`: two detection rules for the same fact in one file.
- [special] TL015 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:674-678` service names get `.service` appended only on systemd; the request's service vocabulary means different things per init system.
- [special] TL016 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:564-569` keymap search directories are chosen by init system (`InitSystem::Openrc` adds `usr/share/bkeymaps`), though they depend on which keymap package the distro ships, not on the init system.
- [special] TL017 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:288-295` and `plan.rs:275-294` four locale methods (`LocaleGen`, `Localedef`, `Precompiled`, `Musl`), with `unreachable!` for two of them at `plan.rs:291-293`. Each distro reaches exactly one of them (LevitateOS `Precompiled`, AcornOS `Musl`), so `LocaleGen`/`Localedef` and their `system-setup.sh` verbs serve no shipped target, and the locale choice list comes from a different source on each distro (`usr/lib/locale/*/LC_CTYPE` vs `usr/share/i18n/locales/musl`).
- [special] TL019 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:165-169` and `:282-286` a `Variant { Levitate, Acorn, Other }` decided by matching os-release `ID` against `levitateos|levitate|acornos|acorn`; nothing in recsetup reads it (only serialized into `inspect` JSON), so it is a by-name special case with no consumer.
- [special] TL020 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/apply.rs:20` and `:228-230` a fixed `TARGET_PATH` with `/sbin` first-class "because Alpine keeps rc-update in /sbin"; a distro-specific reason encoded as the PATH for every chroot step.
- [special] TO1081 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:297-311` account and admin tools are picked by presence order (`useradd` before `adduser`, `sudo` before `doas`), so a target with both privilege tools is treated as the sudo distro.
- [special] TO1082 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:516-526` a branch for "Fedora ships no SUPPORTED list": when `usr/share/i18n/SUPPORTED` is missing, every locale source is offered in UTF-8; a base-distro special case in the shared installer.
- [special] Z014 `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh:55-69` keymap: systemd writes `vconsole.conf`; OpenRC probes for `kbd-bkeymaps` + `loadkmap`, else `keymaps`, else `loadkeys`, by looking at files at run time — three code paths, the variant doesn't declare which applies.
- [special] Z015 `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh:88-96` user creation probes `useradd` vs `adduser`; `:99-107` machine-id probes `systemd-machine-id-setup` — distro facts discovered by probing instead of declared by the variant.
- [hidden] BC2025 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/overlay.rs:27` turns `InitSystem` into the strings `"systemd"`/`"openrc"` for the live-overlay recipe, and `pipeline/live_tools.rs:13-14` spells the same two strings again for `build_tool_tree`. Both must match what `recipes/live-overlay.rhai` and `recipe/tools.rs` expect.
- [hidden] BP044 `distro/builder/src/artifact/live_overlay.rs:3-7` (`InitSystem{OpenRc,Systemd}`), `distro/builder/src/contracts/context.rs:42-47` (`InitSystem{Systemd,OpenRC}`, different casing) and `distro_contract::OverlayKind` (`distro/contract/src/schema.rs:158`) are three enums for one fact. `pipeline/config.rs:211-216` converts one to another one-to-one, and `pipeline/overlay.rs:27` converts again to the strings `"systemd"`/`"openrc"`.
- [hidden] BP047 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/live_tools.rs:32-39` passes that init string to `recipe::tools::build_tool_tree`, which uses it as a stand-in for libc (`src/recipe/tools.rs:297-301`: `openrc` → `x86_64-unknown-linux-musl`). Meanwhile AcornOS's `products.toml:63-103` also sets `target = "x86_64-unknown-linux-musl"` on every action but the first (`recpart`, `:58`). So the libc is decided in two places that must agree, and init and libc are wrongly treated as one fact.
- [hidden] BP048 `distro/builder/src/tui_binary.rs:29-58` decides the libc/CPU a third way, by probing the rootfs for a dynamic loader. The live tools' Rust target (BP047) and the TUI's bun target can disagree without anything noticing.
- [hidden] BR051 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:3` reads `/inputs/config` JSON whose keys (`os_name`, `issue`, `init`, `consoles`, `services`, `launcher`, `shell_ready_marker`, `role_file`, `role`) are built in `distro/builder/src/pipeline/overlay.rs:24`. The schema exists only as matching strings in Rust `json!` and Rhai field access.
- [hidden] BR106 `distro/builder/src/recipe/tools.rs:307` keys the work dir by `binary/target` only, not by init system, so a recab build for systemd and one for OpenRC with the same explicit target share a work dir and state.
- [hidden] BR154 `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:11` (`/inputs/system_setup`), `live-overlay.rhai:3,16,113,120` (`/inputs/config`, `/inputs/assets/live`, `/inputs/shadow`, `/inputs/serial_profile`) and `install-tools.rhai:23-24` (`/inputs/source`, `/inputs/program`) hard-code the worker's input mount point, while the rootfs, kernel and preseed recipes use `RUNTIME.paths.inputs` (`fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:10`, `linux.rhai:10`). Two conventions for the same engine contract, and a change to the engine's mount layout breaks only some recipes.
- [hidden] C044 `validate.rs:1207-1220` validates `identity.os_id` ("levitateos") while the loader, builder and tests key on the directory id ("levitate"). `ConformanceReport.distro_id` is filled with `os_id` (`validate.rs:1333`, `runtime.rs:401`), so the report labels a variant by a different id than the one it was loaded with. `testing/install-tests/src/distro/mod.rs:40-41` accepts both ids.
- [hidden] C084 `ring1/transforms.toml:31-34,41`, `ring0/release.toml:11,15`, `identity.toml:5-8` and `build-capability.sh:7`: UKI names, the ISO filename (declared twice in the same file and checked equal by C045), `iso_label` and `default_hostname` are all the `os_id` with fixed affixes. Each variant spells them out, and `testing/install-tests/src/distro/levitate.rs:16-18` hard-codes the hostname again.
- [hidden] C105 `RuntimeActionContract.target` (`schema.rs:233,238`) is the only per-action libc switch, while `distro/builder/src/tui_binary.rs:7-8` detects libc from the rootfs loader and `pipeline/live_tools.rs:13-16` derives the init from `distro_id` (not from `overlay_kind`). The rootfs's libc/init fact is expressed three different ways.
- [hidden] CL1007 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/commands.rs:97-118` `artifact prepare-tool-source` does its own existence check, temp dir, recursive copy and rename inline in the dispatcher, while every other command hands off to a `*_cmd` function in `artifacts.rs`. Its only callers are docs (`docs/recipe-local-validation.md:191`, `tools/recipe/README.md:93`), and both pass `recfstab`. That is a binary of the `recstrap` package, not a package in `recipe/tools.rs:10` `TOOLS`, so the documented call fails.
- [hidden] CL3153 `distro/builder/src/recipe/tools.rs:292` defaults a missing binary to the package's first listed binary, a convention stated only in the `TOOLS` doc comment (`:8-9`), while `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:9,12` defaults `BIN` to `recstrap`. The "default tool" is defined twice with different values.
- [hidden] P021 `guest/installed/system-setup.sh:55,110` branches on the rendered `@INIT@` for keymap and service, and `:88,99` on the presence of `useradd`/`systemd-machine-id-setup`; per-init behaviour is decided by two different kinds of test (template value vs probing) in the same script.
- [hidden] RG1049 `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:67,126`: `setup_program(root, "systemd", "glibc")` and `setup_program(root, "openrc", "musl")` hard-code the init system and libc for each branch. The variant contract and recsetup's own detection (`tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:247-264`) also know these facts.
- [hidden] RG1054 `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh:88-96`: the script re-decides the account tool with `command -v useradd`, though recsetup has already chosen it (`tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:297-303`, `account_tool`). Two detections of the same fact, and they can disagree.
- [hidden] TI006 `testing/install-tests/src/distro/{levitate,acorn}.rs` `uses_initramfs` and `init_system` restate facts the variant contract already owns (the contract has `transforms.initramfs_installed`, and the init system is a variant property). Change a variant and these hand-written structs must change too; nothing links them.
- [hidden] TI007 `testing/install-tests/src/distro/levitate.rs:16` and `acorn.rs:21` `default_hostname()` ("levitateos"/"acornos") repeats the variant identity (`identity.os_id`, read elsewhere from the contract at `scenarios/mod.rs:534`), and `ab_update.rs:164` compares the installed `/etc/hostname` against it.
- [hidden] TI008 `testing/install-tests/src/distro/{levitate,acorn}.rs:9-14` `name()`/`id()` duplicate the display names and ids that xtask's `Distro` (`distro.pretty_name()`, `distro.id()`) and the contracts already hold.
- [hidden] TL012 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:248-264` repeats recab's installed-system init detection (`init_system.rs:64-66`, same three `openrc-run` paths and `usr/lib/systemd/systemd`) with its own enum `target::InitSystem` (`target.rs:151`), while `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh` decides systemd vs OpenRC a third time for the same target.
- [hidden] TL072 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:15` and `:195-206` every configuration step calls `/usr/libexec/recipe-system-setup <verb> ...` (`timezone`, `locale <lang> precompiled|locale_gen|localedef ...`, `keymap`, `hostname`, `user <name> <shell> <groups>`, `machine-id`, `service`), a positional CLI defined only by `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh:25-111`; recsetup decides the locale method and service suffix, and the script re-decides init system behaviour.
- [hidden] TL075 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:356-372` checks the admin rule by reading `%wheel ... ALL` in sudoers or `permit ... :wheel` in doas.conf, and refuses users when it is absent (`plan.rs:369-374`); the rule text is written by the system recipe, so recsetup parses another component's config syntax per privilege tool.
- [hidden] TL076 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recchroot.rs:50-62` reads root's shell from the target passwd "because Acorn ships ash, not bash", and `recsetup/target.rs:331` decides the user default shell by `bin/bash` presence; two shell-selection rules.
- [hidden] TO1096 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recstrap/guards.rs:324` `ESSENTIAL_DIRS` (`bin`, `etc`, `lib`, `sbin`, `usr`, `var`) encodes what both distros' rootfs layouts contain (merged-usr symlinks on Fedora, real dirs on Alpine), a fact owned by the builder's rootfs recipes.
- [hidden] TO2130 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/apply.rs:20,208-210` `TARGET_PATH` is a hand-written PATH for the chroot ("Alpine keeps rc-update in /sbin"); recchroot itself (`tools/recstrap/src/bin/recchroot.rs:103-108`) passes the caller's environment through, so an interactive `recchroot` and a recsetup step see different PATHs in the same target.
- [hidden] TO3128 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:243` defines `ADMIN_GROUP = "wheel"`, but the admin-rule detectors hard-code `%wheel` (sudo, `:362`) and `:wheel` (doas, `:368`) instead of using it.
- [hidden] TO3145 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:623-660` offers systemd services only when the unit has an `[Install]` section and skips templates, but offers every file in `etc/init.d` on OpenRC. The inspect choice lists differ in kind between the siblings.
- [hidden] TV3074 `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:99,312,401,452` one preflight run loads and parses the whole variant contract bundle three times: once in `verify_conformance_contract` and twice through `canonical_runtime_artifact_names_for_distro`. The scenario runner's own contract loads come on top of these.
- [hidden] U011 The example disk `/dev/nvme0n1` and its partition numbers `p1..p4` are repeated in disk, boot and troubleshooting pages of both distros (`03-installation-disk.ts:114-117,167`, `06-installation-boot.ts:22,30`, `08-troubleshooting.ts:27-29,47`, and the acorn copies); the default layout's sizes (1 GiB / 20 GiB / 20 GiB / rest) are also restated in `01-getting-started.ts:17`, `11-acorn-installation.ts:31`, both disk pages' tables and fdisk/sfdisk scripts, while recpart owns them.
- [hidden] U037 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/08-troubleshooting.ts:36,40` and `17-acorn-troubleshooting.ts:62,66` hard-code the boot directory and entry names `levitateos-a`/`acornos-a` (also `06-installation-boot.ts:26`, `15-acorn-installation-boot.ts:26`, `04-architecture/01-faq.ts:192`), which derive from the variant's OS id that the variant declares and recab/recsetup compute.
- [hidden] U038 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/01-getting-started.ts:39-41` and `10-acorn-getting-started.ts:20-22` hard-code `releases.levitateos.org/latest/<id>-x86_64.iso` and the `.sha512` name; the ISO filename is declared by the variant (`build-host`/products: `levitateos-x86_64.iso`, `acornos-x86_64.iso`) and copied into `04-architecture/04-variants.ts:23,31` as well.
- [hidden] U039 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/04-installation-base.ts:21` and `13-acorn-installation-base.ts` (same line) hard-code `/run/live-rootfs.erofs`, the live-init path that recinit/builder define; the same path is also hard-coded in `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/app/handoff.ts:26`, `src/domain/steps.ts:72` and `docs/content/src/content/05-rec-tooling/12-recstrap.ts:117`.
- [hidden] U040 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/07-post-installation.ts:69` and `16-acorn-post-installation.ts:49` hard-code the ISO's internal layout (`/mnt/live/filesystem.erofs`, `/mnt/boot/vmlinuz`, `/mnt/boot/initramfs-installed.img`), which reciso/builder own; the same strings appear in `05-rec-tooling/03-recab.ts:30`.
- [hidden] U041 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/11-acorn-installation.ts:72-84` and `04-architecture/04-variants.ts:14-33` both tabulate per-distro facts (init, libc, privilege tool, network, boot, ISO name) by hand; `14-recsetup.ts:136-181` tabulates them a third time; the variants declare these facts in `distro/variants/*` and nothing ties the tables to them.
- [hidden] U081 `docs/content/src/content/04-architecture/01-faq.ts:134,192` (LevitateOS-only) and `02-how-we-compare.ts:23-24,81` restate Fedora-base, glibc and `/boot/levitateos-a/` facts; `01-faq.ts:20,36` describes AcornOS in one sentence, so a reader of the FAQ learns the AcornOS design only from that sentence.
- [hidden] U082 `docs/content/src/content/05-rec-tooling/14-recsetup.ts:136-181` tabulates every systemd vs OpenRC step of recsetup (locale, keymap, admin rule, boot, machine id, services) by hand; recsetup's implementation owns these and the install pages restate them again (U031-U033).
- [hidden] U092 `docs/website/src/lib/variants.ts:26-67` declares each variant's base ("Fedora 45 RPMs", "Alpine Linux 3.24 APKs"), stack, init, RAM and disk minimums by hand; the variants declare base and init in `distro/variants/*`, and the docs give other numbers (LevitateOS 16 GB RAM in `01-getting-started.ts:16`; AcornOS no RAM figure).
- [hidden] UD1032 `docs/content/src/content/04-architecture/01-faq.ts:90-100` the FAQ's system-requirements table (x86-64-v3, 16 GB, NVMe, UEFI) is another hand copy of the hardware baseline, also in `01-getting-started.ts:15-18`, `docs/website/src/pages/download.astro:49-51`, `docs/website/public/llms.txt:11`, `llms-full.txt:11,122-124` and the root `README.md:76`; no code defines it, and AcornOS's page states another (`11-acorn-installation.ts:30`, "x86-64").
- [hidden] UD1135 `docs/content/src/content/05-rec-tooling/14-recsetup.ts:114` states recsetup's kernel/initrd defaults `/run/live-media/boot/vmlinuz` and `/run/live-media/boot/initramfs-installed.img`, paths built from recinit's live mount point (`tools/recinit/src/live.rs:22`) and recsetup's defaults; the page is a hand copy.
- [hidden] Z016 `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh:5-6` `@INIT@`/`@LIBC@` are substituted by the system recipe, and `:48,52` the musl locale export is "installed by the recipe": one feature (locale) split between this script and a recipe.
- [hidden] Z018 the privilege tool is not a declared variant fact. It appears as a package in `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:75`, a required tool in both `scenarios.toml` (`:145`/`:132`), is detected by probing in `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:305-310`, configured by file lists in `target.rs:365-366` and `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:128-129`, special-cased in `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1375` (`"doas" => "doas -C /etc/doas.conf"`), named in `tui/apps/runtime/recab-tui/src/domain/recab.ts:128`, and spelt per distro in the docs pages. Changing it means finding all of them.
- [hidden] Z019 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:243` `ADMIN_GROUP = "wheel"` and `testing/fsdbg/src/oracle/users.rs:12` `"wheel"` are copies.
- [needless] TI011 `testing/install-tests/src/distro/mod.rs:49-76` `InstalledScenarioFacts` plus `load_installed_scenario_facts` copies four fields out of the `ScenarioContract` that `load_scenarios` returns whole. Both load and parse the whole variant contract from disk, and they are called again for every fact (`default_password` at `scenarios/mod.rs:1302`, `install_mode_for_distro` at :1328, and `load_scenarios` at 8 call sites across `scenarios/mod.rs` and `qemu/serial/mod.rs`), so one scenario re-reads the TOML a dozen times.
- [needless] TI012 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:534` and `ab_update.rs:92` call `distro_contract::load_variant_contract_for_distro_from` directly for `identity.os_id`, going around the crate's own `distro` module. That makes three ways to reach the contract.
- [surplus] BC1099 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/config.rs:211` `overlay_policy_from_contract` returns `Result` for an infallible 1:1 enum mapping.
- [surplus] BP045 `distro/builder/src/artifact/live_overlay.rs:1-7` is a module named `live_overlay` that holds only that enum. The live overlay logic moved to `recipes/live-overlay.rhai` (this file was churned 30 times and is now 7 lines), but `pipeline/*` still imports the enum from `artifact`.
- [surplus] BP049 `distro/builder/src/tui_binary.rs:37-57` lists aarch64 musl/glibc loaders while every other target in the pipeline (tools defaults, `products.toml` targets, kernels) is x86_64-only. This is speculative generality.
- [surplus] CL1101 `distro/builder/src/recipe/tools.rs:333-341` `build_tool_program` has no caller; the only live entry point is `build_tool_tree` (`pipeline/live_tools.rs:32`).
- [surplus] CL2098 `distro/builder/src/recipe/tools.rs:40-42` builds the tool source closure (with vendored crates) in a system tempdir, and `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/commands.rs:111-115` copies that whole tree into a second tempdir beside the output before renaming it. The vendored closure is copied twice.
- [surplus] CL3102 `distro/builder/src/recipe/tools.rs:325` passes only the `source` input. The recipe's alternative `--input program=` mode (`distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:8,24,44,51`) is exercised only by `distro/builder/tests/recipe_outputs.rs:167,194`.
- [surplus] RG1052 `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh:6`: `libc='@LIBC@'` is set but never read anywhere in the script. `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:13,15` still fills it in and checks that it was filled.
- [surplus] RG1056 `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh:48`: the `musl` locale method is accepted but never sent. recsetup's plan only ever passes `precompiled`, `locale_gen` or `localedef` (`tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:274-293`, where `Musl` is `unreachable!`).
- [surplus] RG2158 `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh:35-47` the `localedef` and `locale_gen` branches cannot run for either sibling. recsetup picks them only for glibc with `locale-gen` + `/etc/locale.gen`, or with `localedef` + locale sources (`tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:288-295`). LevitateOS locks precompiled `glibc-langpack-en` with no `glibc-locale-source` and no `locale-gen`, and AcornOS is musl.
- [surplus] RG2172 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:104` checks again that `init` is `systemd` or `openrc`, though `distro/builder/src/pipeline/overlay.rs:27` produces that string from the two-variant `InitSystem` enum. This is a second validator for a value that cannot be anything else.
- [surplus] TI009 `testing/install-tests/src/distro/mod.rs:40` `context_for_distro` accepts the aliases `"levitateos"`/`"acornos"`. The only caller (xtask) always passes the canonical `Distro::id()`.
- [surplus] TI010 `testing/install-tests/src/distro/mod.rs:47` `AVAILABLE_DISTROS` ("for CLI help") has no users.
- [surplus] TV1046 `testing/install-tests/src/distro/mod.rs:50`, `:70` `InstalledScenarioFacts.installed_boot` is copied from the contract and never read; installed-boot patterns are loaded separately through `load_scenarios` (`src/qemu/serial/mod.rs:88`).
- [surplus] TV3055 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/install.rs:19-24` `TestSystem.uses_initramfs` is a copy of `ctx.uses_initramfs()` (`scenarios/mod.rs:520`), and `install()` (`install.rs:33-37`) already takes `ctx`. The field only passes along a value the callee can read itself.

### G05 One registry of products and scenarios, and scenario expectations derived from it

Solves **106** problems (6 parity, 22 special, 46 hidden, 3 needless, 29 surplus).

- **Design:** `distro-contract` names the four products and the scenarios once: their ids, parents, what each scenario consumes and which product feeds it. The builder holds one table of each product's build properties; ring0/1/2 product data leaves the variants; output roles are named, not positional. xtask's CLI, help and `all` selection, the builder CLI, the planner and install-tests all read the registry. Scenario expectations (services, patterns, prompts, evidence) are derived from the capability set (G07), the init backend and the scenario id; `scenarios.toml` goes.
- **Done when:** One `ProductId` and one `ScenarioId` in the workspace; no scenario→product mapping outside the registry (grep `release_product`, `scenario_for`, `scenarios_for`); `test all` covers every registered scenario; `scenarios.toml` deleted.
- **Depends on:** G03, G04
- **Decisions:** Uniform `<stem>-base-rootfs.iso` naming (default yes). Distro argument required everywhere instead of defaulting to levitate (default yes). `test all` runs every scenario and doesn't stop at the first failure (default yes).
- **Merges area designs:** XT-01 (xtask), CL-01 (builder-cli), BC-04 (builder-core), CV-06 (contract-variants), CV-09 (contract-variants), TV-08 (testing-vm) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] C100 `scenarios.toml:58` (levitate `live_environment = ["sshd","auditd"]`) vs `:47` (acorn `["networking","dhcpcd","sshd"]`): AcornOS's live system runs no audit daemon although both require `audit=1`. LevitateOS's live environment requires no network service (NetworkManager is not required or checked, see C050).
- [parity] C102 `scenarios.toml:38-39` (levitate live fatal patterns include `Failed to start sshd.service` and `sshd.service: Failed with result`) vs acorn `:29-30` (`ERROR: cannot start`, and a bare `ERROR: ` that matches any line containing it). The two distros have different strictness for the same live-boot check. LevitateOS also lists `SQUASHFS error`, `emergency shell`, `emergency.target`, `rescue.target` and `Timed out waiting for device`, which AcornOS lacks.
- [parity] C103 `scenarios.toml:126` (levitate `service_failure_patterns = ["Failed to start","[FAILED]","Dependency failed"]`) vs acorn `:113`, and only `installed_boot` has the field. `live_boot.service_failure_patterns` is defaulted empty in both, although `testing/install-tests/src/qemu/serial/mod.rs:56,77` reads it.
- [parity] TI124 `distro/variants/acorn/scenarios/scenarios.toml:87` the installed-boot success patterns include a bare `"login:"` and `"Welcome to AcornOS"`. LevitateOS's are `"levitateos login:"` and `"multi-user.target"` (`levitate/.../scenarios.toml:94`). AcornOS's bare `login:` passes on any getty, and LevitateOS's `multi-user.target` can pass before login works.
- [parity] X003 `xtask/src/cli/types.rs:33-42` the top-level `AFTER_HELP` "Typical loop" covers LevitateOS only; nothing in `--help` shows the AcornOS loop (README.md:27-34 and architecture.md:21-44 are the same, apart from one `build live-tools acorn` line).
- [parity] XT2161 `xtask/src/cli/types.rs:4` documents `Distro` as "A LevitateOS variant" and `:44` the whole CLI as "The LevitateOS workspace front door". The shared front door's own help describes AcornOS as a sub-variant of LevitateOS.
- [special] BC1105 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/planner.rs:225-230` the cycle error says "product 'X' was visited twice", naming the same product twice and not the cycle.
- [special] C038 `validate.rs:648-658` requires exactly 3 build-runtime inputs (`required_build_runtime.len() != 3`). That is a hard-coded count for a list derived from three fixed transforms. It also names a field `transforms.build_runtime.required_inputs` (`validate.rs:47`) that exists in no manifest.
- [special] C042 `validate.rs:1305-1311` validates `installed_tools` evidence under `ContractScope::Runtime` with prefix `"installed-"`, the same prefix as installed-boot. It is the only scenario mapped to a generic scope.
- [special] C054 `runtime.rs:95-126` `build_runtime_artifacts_from_contract` falls back to `contract.artifacts.*`, and for the overlay to the literal `"overlayfs.erofs"` (`runtime.rs:119`). These fallbacks cover an empty `output_names` that the validator already rejects, and they copy `ring1/transforms.toml:12`.
- [special] C078 `ring2/products.toml:39-40`: `boot_installed` reuses `payload_profile = "boot_baseline"`, so the installed-system payload also gets the live init's `.live-payload-role = rootfs` marker.
- [special] CL1018 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/parse.rs:97-110` `parse_release_product` rejects exactly `installed-boot` by name after `parse_product` has accepted it. "Is a release product" is an `if` on one value here and a second rule in `pipeline/planner.rs:169` (`is_release_buildable_product`).
- [special] CL1046 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/artifacts.rs:95` and `:307` end in `unreachable!` on product name. `runtime_product_decl` relies on callers never passing `base-rootfs`, a rule that is not stated where it is called.
- [special] CL2024 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/build.rs:207-211` `derive_product_iso_filename` quietly accepts a contract ISO name without `.iso` and appends `-<suffix>.iso`. It is a fallback for a name shape the contract never declares or validates (`distro/contract/src/validate.rs:237-247` checks only that `artifacts.iso_filename` mirrors `transforms.iso.output_names[0]`).
- [special] CL3012 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/parse.rs:168-169` only installed-boot sets `live_overlay_dir_name = "boot-overlay"` and `.rootfs-source.path`. So the field named `live_overlay_dir_name` holds a non-live overlay for this one product (used at `artifacts.rs:48,77`, exported as `LIVE_OVERLAY_DIRNAME` at `release_hook.rs:111`), while the shell defaults (`_shared/ring0/hooks/release-artifacts.sh:59-60`) only know the live spelling.
- [special] CL3030 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/build.rs:197-205` `iso_filename_for_product` names live-boot and live-tools ISOs `<base>-<product>.iso`, but gives base-rootfs the bare contract name (`:199`). Base-rootfs is exempt from its own `iso_suffix`. As a result, the contract's `ring0/release.toml` `primary_outputs` (`distro/variants/levitate/ring0/release.toml:15`) describes only the base-rootfs ISO correctly.
- [special] CL3149 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/parse.rs:166` installed-boot alone has `release_hook_script_name: None`. A product-kind distinction becomes an `Option` that `release_hook.rs:56-61` checks at runtime, with an error that `parse_release_product` (`parse.rs:97-110`) already prevents.
- [special] CV1087 `distro/contract/src/variant.rs:1359-1374` (`artifact_identity_from_transforms`) indexes `output_names[0]` on five transforms while loading. `VariantRingTransform.output_names` (`:1754`) is a plain `Vec`, so an empty list panics the loader before `validate.rs:129-144` (`first_transform_output`) can report "must contain at least one output name".
- [special] CV2071 `distro/variants/acorn/scenarios/scenarios.toml:30` makes the bare `"ERROR: "` a fatal live-boot pattern (matching any OpenRC service error), while installed boot lists only `"* ERROR:"` as a recorded, non-fatal `service_failure_patterns` entry (`:113`): the same console line fails one scenario and is tolerated in the other.
- [special] TI021 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:205-211` `BuildPreflight` "runs" by returning a constant `Ok`. Its real work is the `require_preflight` gate at `:159-168`, which runs before *every* ISO-backed scenario. The scenario exists only so the gate has a cached name.
- [special] TI022 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:216,223,230,240` four `.expect("... requires ISO")` calls encode the invariant that `release_product()` returns `Some` for those arms. Changing the mapping turns into a panic, not a type error.
- [special] TI027 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:813-820` `scenario_input_fingerprint` special-cases `ScenarioId::AbUpdate` (ISO plus install run) by name. Every other scenario is "ISO or install run", and nothing declares which inputs a scenario has.
- [special] TI059 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:41-46` ab-update `bail!`s at runtime when the variant is mutable, instead of the scenario being unavailable for that variant. `print_failure` (`mod.rs:1447`) lists "the variant installs mutable" as a "common cause".
- [special] TV1021 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:289-294` `scenario_statuses` turns any error resolving the current ISO or computing its fingerprint into `Stale` with `.unwrap_or(false)`, so a missing or corrupt artifact shows as "stale".
- [special] TV3021 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:257-262` a failed scenario returns `Ok(false)` after `print_failure` prints the error and `state.record` flattens it to a string. xtask (`xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:210-211`) then makes a generic `scenario X failed for Y` error, so the cause never reaches the caller's error chain or exit-code mapping.
- [special] X002 `xtask/src/cli/types.rs:87,96,174,219,229,301,361` every distro argument defaults to `Distro::Levitate`; AcornOS must always be named. `ProductArgs` repeats the default by hand (`types.rs:247` doc, `tasks/build.rs:14` `unwrap_or(Distro::Levitate)`), and `KernelArgs` (`types.rs:257`) has no default at all: three conventions for one argument.
- [special] X004 `xtask/src/cli/types.rs:23-30` `pretty_name()` loads and validates the whole variant contract on every call (twice per `run`: the boot message and `start_vm`'s title, `tasks/scenarios.rs:252,327,524,589`) and swallows any load error, silently falling back to the id.
- [special] XT1036 `xtask/src/tasks/build.rs:9-30` product builds are peeled off with `BuildCmd::product()` before the `match`, which then ends in `unreachable!("product builds are handled above")`. The command enum's shape forces a call-order convention inside `run`.
- [hidden] BC1080 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:67-69` `ParentRootfsInput.release_dir_name` and `.rootfs_filename` are filled by `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/artifacts.rs:71-73` but never read; the planner resolves the parent image itself from `planner.rs:182` `release_dir_name_for_product` and `contract.artifacts.rootfs_name` (`planner.rs:187`), while the bin derives the filename from `contract.transforms.rootfs_image` (`bin/workflows/prepared_products.rs:34`), two sources for the rootfs file name.
- [hidden] BP074 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/planner.rs:9-12` The product names `base-rootfs|live-boot|live-tools|installed-boot` are redefined in `src/bin/distro-builder.rs:7-10` (`PRODUCT_*`, used by the `product_*()` tables in `src/bin/workflows/parse.rs:81-170`), `xtask/src/cli/types.rs:185-198` and `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:45-47`, and the scenario names in `distro/contract/src/error.rs:21-24` reuse three of them. Nothing links these copies.
- [hidden] BP077 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/planner.rs:169-174` `is_release_buildable_product` (no installed-boot) is copied by hand into the usage text at `src/bin/distro-builder.rs:47` ("release products: base-rootfs | live-boot | live-tools").
- [hidden] BP080 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/io.rs:56-58,66-68` and `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:524` put `distro-builder` CLI syntax (`release build iso <distro> <product>`, `product prepare <product> <distro> <output_dir>`) into library error text. A CLI change in `src/bin` breaks these messages silently.
- [hidden] BR118 product names are spelled in `distro/builder/src/bin/distro-builder.rs:7-10` (constants), `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/parse.rs:113-118` (contract logical names `product.rootfs.base`, `product.payload.boot.live`, ...), `parse.rs:130,142,154` (hook script names that must match files in `distro/variants/*/ring0/hooks/`), xtask's `Product` enum (`xtask/src/cli/types.rs:184`) and the hooks' `PRODUCT_NAME` default (`distro/variants/levitate/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:8`).
- [hidden] C032 `variant.rs:1357-1376` `artifact_identity_from_transforms` derives `ArtifactIdentity` from the transforms. `validate.rs:193-312` (`validate_artifact_identity_mirrors`) then checks that `artifacts.*` mirror those same transforms, a check that cannot fail for any loaded contract.
- [hidden] C040 `validate.rs:1117-1125` requires `rootfs_name` to end with `.erofs`, while `ring1/transforms.toml:7` also declares `format = "erofs"`. The format is stated twice and only the filename suffix is checked. `format` is otherwise unread (C064).
- [hidden] C041 `validate.rs:1228-1311` maps each scenario's evidence to a hard-coded filename prefix (`"live-"`, `"install"`, `"installed-"`, `"automated-"`) and a fixed marker (C011). Scenario names, script filenames under `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/` and markers are tied by convention in three places: the variant TOML, this validator and the test-scripts directory.
- [hidden] C075 `distro/variants/{levitate,acorn}/ring2/products.toml:15-53`: `rootfs_base`, `live_overlay`, `boot_live`, `live_tools`, `boot_installed` and `boot_baseline` (logical names, descriptions, `extends`, producers) are byte-identical in both variants. Only `overlay_kind` differs.
- [hidden] C076 `ring2/products.toml:16,20,25,31,37,99` logical names (`product.rootfs.base`, `product.payload.boot.live`, …) are declared per variant but hard-coded in the builder (`distro/builder/src/pipeline/config.rs:36-40`, `bin/workflows/parse.rs:115-120`, `pipeline/planner.rs:277-280`), which rejects any other value. They are config for a vocabulary that cannot vary.
- [hidden] C077 `ring2/products.toml:50-53` `write_text .live-payload-role = "rootfs\n"` spells out recinit's init protocol (`tools/recinit/src/live.rs:72-73` `PAYLOAD_ROLE_FILE`/`ROLE_ROOTFS`) in both variant TOMLs, while the overlay's role comes from the recinit constant via `distro/builder/src/pipeline/overlay.rs:32`.
- [hidden] C080 `ring2/products.toml:6-13` `[install_ux]` (consoles, apps, hub, comments) is identical in both variants apart from `shell` and `docs`. `ring1/transforms.toml:36`/`26` `extra_cmdline = "video=1920x1080"` and the full `rootfs_image`/`overlay_image`/`initramfs_live` blocks are identical too.
- [hidden] C081 `scenarios.toml` live-boot `fatal_patterns` (levitate `:8-40`, acorn `:8-34`) are repeated nearly verbatim as `installed_boot.fatal_patterns` in the same file (levitate `:95-125`, acorn `:88-112`) and across the two variants. The ~20 firmware/kernel lines (UEFI, `Kernel panic`, `VFS:`) are distro-neutral and still copied four times.
- [hidden] C082 `scenarios.toml:53-55,81-83,89-91,130-132,140-142,147-149` (levitate) and the same blocks in acorn: all six `evidence` blocks are identical across the variants and fully determined by C041. They name scripts that live in `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/` (a shared, distro-agnostic folder), not in the variant.
- [hidden] C083 `scenarios.toml:3-6` comments reference `live_boot::ROOTFS_MISSING_MESSAGE` and the protocol's readiness marker as implicit success/fatal patterns that the manifest does not list. Correct `success_patterns = []` depends on test-harness code (`testing/install-tests/src/qemu/serial/mod.rs:39,68`) adding them.
- [hidden] C111 `sshd` as a mandatory live service is stated in `schema.rs:12`, merged in `variant.rs:1477-1480`, re-checked in `validate.rs:1313`, `runtime.rs:587` and `distro/builder/src/pipeline/config.rs:82-87`, and also declared by both variants (`scenarios.toml:51,58`/`:40,47`). That is six statements of one rule.
- [hidden] C112 `audit=1` is in `BOOT_REQUIRED_KERNEL_CMDLINE_BASE` (`schema.rs:9`) and also hand-declared by both variants (`scenarios.toml:45`/`:38`).
- [hidden] CL1019 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/build.rs:203` `iso_filename_for_product` has `unreachable!("validated in parse_product")` for `installed-boot`, but `parse_product` accepts `installed-boot`. The arm stays unreachable only because `parse_release_product` (`parse.rs:99`) and the planner's `is_release_buildable_product` filter (`planner.rs:103`) run first. `ensure_release_prerequisites` (`build.rs:26`) passes prerequisites through plain `parse_product`, so its safety rests on that planner filter alone.
- [hidden] CL3009 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/artifacts.rs:82-97` `runtime_product_decl` maps CLI product names to `contract.products.{boot_live,live_tools,boot_installed}` by hand. It is one more product-name table beside `parse.rs:80-95` (`parse_product`) and `parse.rs:113-124` (`product_for_logical_name`), and all of them must change together when a product is added.
- [hidden] CV1023 `distro/variants/levitate/scenarios/scenarios.toml:94,138` and `distro/variants/acorn/scenarios/scenarios.toml:87,125` each write `"<hostname> login:"` twice, once in `installed_boot.success_patterns` and once as `automated_login.login_prompt_pattern`. The hostname is a copy of `identity.default_hostname` (`identity/identity.toml:8`), so renaming the default hostname means editing four scenario strings that nothing derives.
- [hidden] CV1033 `distro/variants/levitate/scenarios/scenarios.toml:152-153` and `distro/variants/acorn/scenarios/scenarios.toml:139-140` both describe in comments what an `immutable` install does (A/B slots, shared /var). The two copies already disagree ("remounted read-write" vs "mounted read-write"), and the behaviour belongs to recstrap/recab and install-tests, not the variant.
- [hidden] CV2034 `distro/contract/src/validate.rs:48-50`/`:865-869` (`LIVE_UKI_PRIMARY/EMERGENCY/DEBUG_OUTPUT_FIELD` = `output_names[0]/[1]/[2]`) encodes the live/emergency/debug role of each `live_uki` output by position, a second copy of the positional convention in `release_hook.rs:18`; `build-release.sh:298-300` relies on the same order. Nothing in `transforms.toml` names the roles.
- [hidden] CV2077 `distro/variants/{levitate,acorn}/scenarios/scenarios.toml:5` cite `live_boot::ROOTFS_MISSING_MESSAGE`; the constant is `recinit::live::ROOTFS_MISSING_MESSAGE` (`tools/recinit/src/live.rs:84`) and no `live_boot` module exists, so both copies of the comment name a stale owner.
- [hidden] CV3034 `distro/variants/*/scenarios/scenarios.toml` the same service set is stated three times per variant with no shared source: `live_boot.required_live_services`, `live_environment.required_services` and `install.required_services` (Levitate `:51,58,87` = `[sshd]`, `[sshd, auditd]`, `[sshd, auditd]`; Acorn `:40,47,80` = `[networking, dhcpcd, sshd]` three times).
- [hidden] CV3054 `distro/variants/*/ring0/release.toml:11,15` `output_names`/`primary_outputs = ["levitateos-x86_64.iso"]` name only the base-rootfs ISO; the builder renames per product (`distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/build.rs:197-212`, `iso_filename_for_product`), and the built live ISOs are `levitateos-x86_64-live-tools.iso`/`-live-boot.iso` (verified in `.artifacts/out/levitate/releases/live-tools/*/`), so the declared release outputs match one of three products.
- [hidden] P031 `acorn/scenarios/scenarios.toml:47` lists `networking`/`dhcpcd` as live services that `installed-system.rhai:110-113` already enables in the rootfs; the same enablement is declared twice for AcornOS and zero times in TOML for LevitateOS (NetworkManager).
- [hidden] P050 `distro/variants/levitate/ring0/release.toml:16-20` lists `initramfs-installed.img` as a supporting artifact, while `ring0_release.iso.dependencies` (`:5-10`) omits `artifact.initramfs.installed`; the hook adds it to the ISO by `if [ -f ]` (`distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:304-306`), so the contract does not say the ISO carries it.
- [hidden] TI013 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:50-113` `ScenarioId` spells every scenario three times in parallel matches (`key`, `display_name`, `parse_key` with `_` aliases), plus `ALL`.
- [hidden] TI016 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:45-47` `PRODUCT_BASE_ROOTFS`/`PRODUCT_LIVE_BOOT`/`PRODUCT_LIVE_TOOLS` repeat the product names that the builder and xtask `Product::id()` own. `preflight.rs:245` repeats `"base-rootfs"` and `preflight.rs:302` repeats `"live-boot" | "live-tools"` as literals.
- [hidden] TI017 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:348` `parse_scenario_name`'s error message lists the scenarios by hand and leaves out `ab-update`. The module doc at `:6-14` also leaves out ab-update.
- [hidden] TI023 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1398-1452` `print_failure` hand-writes failure hints per scenario. They are stale and systemd-centric ("UKI not found by systemd-boot": installs use vmlinuz+initramfs entries, not UKIs; "No serial getty enabled"), and the `_ => {}` arm silently covers BuildPreflight.
- [hidden] TI024 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1172` installed-boot, automated-login, runtime and ab-update all take their input from "the latest successful install run" on disk. The order (install first) is a convention the callers have to know: `require_inputs` in xtask duplicates it, and only the error text says it.
- [hidden] TI028 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:807-812` the fingerprint includes the harness binary's mtime and size (`current_exe`, which is xtask). Any rebuild of xtask, whatever changed in it, makes every cached scenario stale for both distros. This coupling is hidden behind "links recqemu, recshuttle and recpart".
- [hidden] TV1019 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:790-834` the cache fingerprint covers only the ISO path+mtime (or install run id) and the harness binary. `test-scripts/*.sh` are read from the repo at run time (`:932`) and `scenarios.toml` is loaded at run time, so editing an evidence script or the contract's patterns, required tools or evidence leaves a cached PASS "current".
- [hidden] TV1091 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:43` the error text names "rootfs_mutability in its scenarios.toml", spelling the contract's field and file layout in the harness.
- [hidden] X001 `xtask/src/cli/types.rs:5-21` hard-codes `Distro { Levitate, Acorn }` and its id strings; the same list lives in `testing/install-tests/src/distro/mod.rs:38-47` (`context_for_distro`, `AVAILABLE_DISTROS`) and `distro/builder/src/pipeline/paths.rs:13-18` (`normalize_distro_id`), while `distro-builder release build-all` discovers ids from `distro/variants/*` (`distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/build.rs:39`). Adding a variant needs all of them edited; nothing links them.
- [hidden] X010 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/check.rs:100-110` `install_screen_docs` loops over xtask's own `Distro::ALL` to find each variant's install-screen docs; a variant missing from the enum is never rendered at its docs-pane size.

A new scenario needs both enums and `scenarios_for` changed.

### G06 One artifact layout, one artifact index and one run record for builds and scenarios

Solves **55** problems (1 parity, 23 special, 22 hidden, 4 needless, 5 surplus).

- **Design:** One layout module keyed by a validated variant id owns every `.artifacts` path (retired names and guards go). The producer's artifact index answers "where is the latest output" for every caller. Release runs and scenario runs are records in one schema with an atomic allocator and a prune that keeps the last success and anything a kept run references; scenario inputs (install disks) are never modified. The record type lives in `distro-contract`, not in the builder. Files and selections are published by one atomic helper.
- **Done when:** One run-manifest schema (grep writers/readers); no hard-coded `.artifacts/...` paths outside the layout module; installed scenarios boot an overlay of the install disk; `status` reads run records.
- **Depends on:** G03, G05
- **Merges area designs:** BC-02 (builder-core), BC-03 (builder-core), XT-02 (xtask), CL-06 (builder-cli), CL-09 (builder-cli), TV-09 (testing-vm) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] TI038 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:647-714` automated-login and runtime write no `ScenarioRun` manifest or evidence file, while install, installed-boot and ab-update do. Two runtime-record conventions coexist among scenarios.
- [special] BC1096 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/source.rs:159` falls back to a work dir named `recipe` when the source recipe script has no file stem.
- [special] BC1147 `distro/builder/src/run_history.rs:151-153` shifts the pid left by 16 bits under `nanos << 32`, so pids above 65535 (Linux `pid_max` is up to 4194304) overlap the timestamp bits; uniqueness then rests on the 32-try retry in `allocate_run_dir` (`:124`).
- [special] BC1149 `distro/builder/src/run_history.rs:106-115` `prune_old_runs` keeps the newest `keep` runs regardless of status, so a series of failed or building runs can delete the last successful run that `latest_successful_run_id` (`:99`) needs; run dirs without a manifest (`:92`) are never pruned.
- [special] BC2066 `distro/builder/src/run_history.rs:92` one run directory with an unparsable manifest makes `load_run_metadata` fail, which fails `latest_successful_run_id` for every product in that directory. A run directory with no manifest at all is silently skipped (`:59-61`).
- [special] BC3139 `distro/builder/src/run_history.rs:127-131` `allocate_run_dir` checks `run_root.exists()` and then calls `create_dir_all`, which succeeds on an existing directory, so two processes can be handed the same run dir. The 32-try loop (`:124`) cannot detect that race.
- [special] BP050 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/paths.rs:13-19` `normalize_distro_id` hard-codes the two distros plus legacy aliases `"leviso"` and `"acornos"`. Every work-dir path goes through it (`paths.rs:22`, `source.rs:135`), so a third variant needs this edited, while `bin/workflows/parse.rs` `discover_distro_ids` reads the variant list from `distro/variants/`.
- [special] BP051 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/plan.rs:8-11,241-276` keeps a guard against the legacy `leviso/downloads/rootfs` and `acornos/downloads/rootfs` paths, which no producer can create any more. It runs twice per source, at `source.rs:105` and again at `plan.rs:70`.
- [special] BP052 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/source.rs:109-133` `cleanup_legacy_provider_dir` deletes any `*-rootfs-provider` dir in the product output on every live-boot/installed-boot prepare (`products.rs:260,464`). This is migration code for an old layout, run forever.
- [special] BP125 `distro/builder/src/run_history.rs:124-133` retries run-id allocation up to 32 times on collision.
- [special] BP126 `distro/builder/src/run_history.rs:140-144` orders runs by comparing timestamp strings lexicographically, which depends on the writer always emitting the same ISO format.
- [special] BR131 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/build.rs:176-184` turns a run-metadata write failure into a warning when the build already failed (the error is swallowed).
- [special] CL1027 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/build.rs:186-191` prunes old release runs only after a successful build, keeping `RELEASE_RUN_RETENTION_COUNT = 5` (`bin/distro-builder.rs:12`). install-tests has its own literal `5` for its runs (`testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1100`), and the variant contract declares neither.
- [special] CL1041 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/prepared_products.rs:129-142` writes `.prepared-product.json` with a plain `fs::write`, while `distro/builder/src/bin/run_manifest.rs:12-29` writes run metadata through temp+rename. The same binary uses two write policies for its "manifest beside artifacts" files.
- [special] CL1087 `distro/builder/src/recipe/linux.rs:95` and `outputs.rs:83` mark `select_kernel_bundle`/`select_directory` `#[cfg(unix)]`, while their callers (`recipe/qemu.rs:30`, `recipe/linux.rs:79`, `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:226`) have no cfg. The gate cannot do anything without breaking the build.
- [special] CL1092 `distro/builder/src/recipe/outputs.rs:103-112` `select_directory` moves any real (non-symlink) destination into a `previous-*` dir created with `.keep()`. Nothing ever removes these dirs beside `.artifacts/tools/` or `.artifacts/kernel/<distro>/`.
- [special] CL2084 `distro/builder/src/recipe/outputs.rs:97-101` creates a named tempfile only to delete it and reuse its name for a symlink, and `:105-110` does the same with a kept tempdir before renaming into it. Both open the name race the tempfile API exists to prevent.
- [special] P090 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/paths.rs:15-16` accepts legacy aliases `leviso`/`acornos`, `distro/builder/src/artifact_store/mod.rs:764-765` maps directory names `leviso`/`AcornOS`, `distro/builder/src/pipeline/plan.rs:8-11` and `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:18` each list forbidden `leviso|acornos/downloads/rootfs` sequences; each distro's old repo name survives in shared code, in two copies for the legacy check.
- [special] TI032 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/state.rs:113` `unix_timestamp_string` writes `"<n>s_since_epoch"` with `unwrap_or_default()`, a third timestamp format in the same crate, alongside `now_unix_nanos` (`mod.rs:1268`) and `now_utc_sortable`.
- [special] TI035 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:481-483` `run_installation` deletes an existing `disk.qcow2` in a run directory that `allocate_run_dir` has just created fresh, so it can never exist.
- [special] TI036 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:595,637` and `ab_update.rs:71` `let _ = scenario_run.finish_failed(...)` discards the error from writing the failure evidence and manifest. A failed run can be left marked `building` forever.
- [special] TI037 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:642` `run_installed_boot` re-wraps the error as `anyhow!("{:#}", e)`, flattening its context chain, unlike every other scenario.
- [special] TV2088 `testing/install-tests/src/lib.rs:15-18` `repo_root()` is the compile-time `CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR/../..` with `canonicalize().unwrap_or(root)`; test scripts, contracts and run outputs are located relative to where the crate was compiled, not the checkout the binary runs in (the same pattern as xtask's own `repo_root`).
- [special] XT1090 `xtask/src/tasks/build.rs:43-49` `latest_iso` swallows resolution errors (`.ok().flatten()`), so a corrupt run index reads as "not built" in doctor (`setup.rs:189`) and in `require_iso`.
- [hidden] BC1090 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/plan.rs:249-250` remediation text points at `.artifacts/prepared/<distro>/<product>/`, a location owned by `xtask/src/tasks/build.rs:92-113` (`build erofs`).
- [hidden] BC1109 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/paths.rs:15-16` accepts `leviso`/`acornos` aliases for work dirs, while `distro/builder/src/run_history.rs:22-24` `distro_output_dir` takes the distro id raw, so a retired alias resolves for `.artifacts/work` but not for `.artifacts/out`.
- [hidden] BC1146 `distro/builder/src/bin/run_manifest.rs:12` and `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1236` each define their own `write_json_atomic` for the run manifest that `distro/builder/src/run_history.rs:68` names; the module that owns the manifest exposes no writer.
- [hidden] BC2034 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:260,464` live-boot and installed-boot call `cleanup_legacy_provider_dir`, but live-tools (`:383-444`) does not. The legacy cleanup is a per-product convention nobody states.
- [hidden] BP081 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/io.rs:44-73` and `:75-88` are two copies of "find the latest successful run's rootfs". One errors and the other returns bool, and the planner calls both (`planner.rs:117`, `:183`).
- [hidden] BP122 `distro/builder/src/run_history.rs:22-38`, `distro/builder/src/pipeline/paths.rs:22-27`, `distro/builder/src/pipeline/source.rs:135-147`, `src/recipe/tools.rs:307-308` and `src/recipe/qemu.rs:12` each build their own piece of `.artifacts/{out,kernel,work}/...` from string literals. `testing/rootfs-tests/src/main.rs:107`, `testing/hardware-compat/src/main.rs:15` and `xtask/src/tasks/tui/inspect.rs:49` hard-code more of it. No single module owns the layout.
- [hidden] BP123 `distro/builder/src/run_history.rs:42-54` `RunMetadata` is read-only here. The writer is a separate serde struct in `src/bin/workflows/build.rs:83-161` via `src/bin/run_manifest.rs`, and `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1039-1052` writes a third, differently shaped `ScenarioRunManifest` to the same file name. The schema is split three ways.
- [hidden] BP124 `distro/builder/src/run_history.rs:110` `prune_old_runs` deletes `run_root_dir.join(run.run_id)`, which assumes the manifest's `run_id` always equals its directory name. `latest_successful_run_id` (`:99-104`, used at `io.rs:54,63`) relies on the same convention.
- [hidden] BP128 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/paths.rs:22-27` puts every distro recipe's work under `.artifacts/work/<distro>/downloads`, with the live overlay nested under it by product dir name (`overlay.rs:41`). Live-boot and live-tools both use `live-overlay` (`bin/workflows/parse.rs`), so they share one work dir.
- [hidden] BR090 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/paths.rs:22` `recipe_work_dir` (`.artifacts/work/<distro>/downloads`) is the shared `--build-dir`, and so the shared `recipe-state` dir (`src/recipe/mod.rs:321`), for the kernel recipes (`src/recipe/linux.rs:35`, `src/pipeline/kernel.rs:45`, `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:131,190`) and for the rootfs preseed (`src/recipe/rootfs_source.rs:105`). The live overlay uses a subdirectory of it (`src/pipeline/overlay.rs:41`). Unrelated recipes share one build/state directory, and nothing shows it.
- [hidden] BR094 `distro/builder/src/recipe/outputs.rs:84` `select_directory` (symlink swap with a `previous-*` save) and `distro/builder/src/recipe/rootfs_source.rs:151` `save_preseed_outputs` and `distro/builder/src/bin/run_manifest.rs:12` `write_json_atomic` are three separate atomic-write helpers with different durability (fsync in two, none in `run_manifest`).
- [hidden] CL1026 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/build.rs:239-250` `now_utc_compact` writes run timestamps as `YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ`. The shared hook writes its own start time for the same run in another format (`distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:57`, `date -u '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ'`) into `build-log.json`.
- [hidden] CL3085 `distro/builder/src/recipe/outputs.rs:97-114` after `rename(next.path(), destination)` the `NamedTempFile` guard `next` still owns the old name. When it drops, it deletes a path that no longer exists and the error is swallowed. If anything has recreated that name in the meantime, the guard removes it.
- [hidden] TI025 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:612,649,674` installed-boot, automated-login and runtime boot the install run's `disk.qcow2` and OVMF vars read-write, so each run changes the shared install result (first-boot state, logs, machine-id generation, EFI vars). ab-update (`ab_update.rs:50-62`) uses a copy-on-write overlay precisely to avoid this. The cache fingerprint (install `run_id`, `:827-831`) does not change when an earlier scenario has already booted the disk.
- [hidden] TI026 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1100` `prune_old_runs(.., 5)` deletes old install runs after each successful install. ab-update's overlay (`ab_update.rs:55`, `-b <install disk>`) and the scenario state point at a specific install run, and pruning it silently breaks that backing file.
- [hidden] TI030 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1038-1052` `ScenarioRunManifest` is a second hand-written schema for the run manifest that `distro_builder::run_history::RunMetadata` (`distro/builder/src/run_history.rs:43`) reads back (`run_id`, `status`, `created_at_utc`, `finished_at_utc`). Renaming a field on either side breaks `latest_successful_run_id` for scenario runs, and nothing shares the type.
- [hidden] TI031 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1259-1266` `now_utc_sortable` (`{secs:020}{nanos:09}`) must produce the same sortable format the builder writes, because `run_history::run_sort_key` orders both. `:1236` `write_json_atomic` duplicates `distro/builder/src/bin/run_manifest.rs:12`.
- [hidden] TI033 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/state.rs:106-111` `state_path` reads `LEVITATE_SCENARIO_STATE_DIR`, a process-global env override (set only by `xtask/tests/cli.rs:9`), and defaults to `<repo>/.scenarios` while every run artifact lives under `run_history::distro_output_dir`. Results and run directories are in two unrelated places.
