### G07 One shared capability set that both images provide

Solves **24** problems (11 parity, 5 special, 7 hidden, 1 surplus).

- **Design:** One list of capabilities both siblings must provide — userland and shell, editor and diagnostics, base services, locales/keymaps/time zones, network, audit, D-Bus — that each variant maps to its packages, that the build checks (G13) and that the scenarios test. The tool list in the contract (`required_tools`) is the only one.
- **Done when:** Both variants' package sets satisfy the same capability list (checked at build); `live-tools.sh`, the Rust tool table and `required_tools` are one list; the capability matrix in Problems has no differing row except the three intended ones.
- **Depends on:** G04
- **Decisions:** **User-visible.** Defaults: bash on both (AcornOS adds bash; new users get bash); the union tool set on both (LevitateOS gains vim, htop, smartctl, hdparm, tar, gzip); GNU userland on AcornOS where scripts need it; one locale list on both; kbd keymaps on both; chrony on both; sshd installed but disabled on both installed systems; serial console only when asked for on both installed systems. **auditd: designers disagree** — CV/RG/BC: auditd on both; PA: on neither. Default here: on both (AcornOS already boots with `audit=1`).
- **Merges area designs:** CV-08 (contract-variants), PA-02 (parity-all), PA-06 (parity-all), PA-07 (parity-all), TV-03 (testing-vm) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] BR007 `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:114` LevitateOS ships `audit`, and `fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:57` requires `auditd` and its unit. AcornOS has no audit daemon.
- [parity] BR008 `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:86` LevitateOS ships D-Bus (dbus-broker, socket-activated at `fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:168`). The AcornOS package list (`alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:58`) has no dbus. Both planned desktops need it.
- [parity] BR009 `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:25` LevitateOS gets bash plus bash-completion, GNU sed, gawk, diffutils, procps-ng, kbd and usbutils. AcornOS (`alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:58`) has only some GNU tools (coreutils, findutils, grep); for sh, sed, awk, diff, ps, keymaps and lsusb it falls back to BusyBox applets (`installed-system.rhai:72` `busybox_applets`). The shell and userland users meet differ.
- [parity] C091 `ring3/sources.toml:151-163` (levitate) vs `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:58-60` (acorn): AcornOS ships WiFi (`wpa_supplicant`, `iw`), `htop`, `hdparm`, `smartmontools` and `vim`. LevitateOS ships `NetworkManager` but no `wpa_supplicant`/`iw`, `htop`, `smartmontools`, `hdparm` or `vim`.
- [parity] C092 `scenarios.toml:62-79` (levitate) vs `:51-72` (acorn): AcornOS's `live_tools.required_tools` includes `smartctl`, `hdparm`, `htop`, `vim` and `mount`. LevitateOS's has `vi` and none of those. Meanwhile `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/live-tools.sh:78-95` tests `smartctl`, `hdparm`, `vim` and `htop` unconditionally, so the shared evidence script assumes AcornOS's tool set.
- [parity] C094 `ring2/products.toml:8`: the install screen's shell is `/bin/bash` on LevitateOS and `/bin/sh` (busybox ash) on AcornOS. `scenarios.toml:145`/`132` `installed_tools` correspondingly require `bash` vs `ash` (`test-scripts/installed-tools.sh:48-49`). The install and installed shell experience differ.
- [parity] P068 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/live-tools.sh:78-95` is shared by both distros but requires `smartctl`, `hdparm`, `vim`, `htop`, which only AcornOS ships; LevitateOS's contract asks for `vi` (`levitate/scenarios/scenarios.toml:73`), which it also lacks. `.scenarios/levitate.json` records live-tools failing on "Missing tools (not in PATH): vi".
- [parity] PA1138 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/03-installation-disk.ts:41` tells LevitateOS users to run `timedatectl set-ntp true`, but the locked RPM set has no NTP client (no `systemd-timesyncd` in the `systemd` package, no `chrony` in `levitate/ring3/sources.toml`), so the step cannot work; AcornOS's `ntpd -q -n -p pool.ntp.org` (`12-acorn-installation-disk.ts:41`) uses the busybox applet it ships. Neither installed policy (`installed-system.rhai:36-127`) enables time sync.
- [parity] PA2021 `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:109-112` AcornOS's installed runlevels link `networking` (boot) and `dhcpcd` (default) but not `sshd`, and LevitateOS's `systemd()` links only `NetworkManager.service` (`:62-65`). Yet `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/15-acorn-installation-boot.ts:39` says "The system recipe adds networking, dhcpcd and sshd to OpenRC's runlevels", and both variants list `sshd` under `scenarios.install.required_services` (`distro/variants/acorn/scenarios/scenarios.toml:80`, `levitate/scenarios/scenarios.toml:87`). `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:110` confirms "Installed systems leave sshd off". The guide promises installed SSH on one sibling, and neither sibling ships it.
- [parity] PA2112 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:58` AcornOS installs `alpine-base`/`alpine-baselayout`, which ship Alpine's own identity: `/etc/alpine-release` and an `/etc/motd` that greets every login with "Welcome to Alpine! ... See <https://wiki.alpinelinux.org/>" (present in the built rootfs). `distro/builder/src/pipeline/plan.rs:45-63` overwrites only `/etc/os-release`. LevitateOS's motd is empty and it carries no `fedora-release`, so one sibling's users see the base distro's branding and the other's do not.
- [parity] PA3082 `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:106-114` AcornOS's installed base services (sysinit `devfs dmesg mdev hwdrivers`, boot `modules sysctl hostname bootmisc syslog hwclock seedrng networking`, shutdown `mount-ro killprocs savecache`) are a hand-written copy of "what setup-alpine enables". LevitateOS gets the same capabilities (journald, random seed, module loading, and so on) from the static `.wants` the systemd packages ship under `usr/lib/systemd/system/*.target.wants`. Logging, clock, entropy and device handling on the installed systems come from two unrelated sources that nothing compares.
- [special] PA3077 `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh:29` the `timezone` verb removes `/etc/localtime` when `UTC` is asked for and `usr/share/zoneinfo/UTC` is missing. It matches `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:432`, which always offers a synthetic `UTC` choice even with no zoneinfo. Both siblings ship tzdata (`distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:156`, `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:61`, and both built rootfs trees have `usr/share/zoneinfo/UTC`), so the branch is for a target that no variant produces, and it is stated in two places.
- [special] PA3092 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/live-tools.sh:77-80,88` tests `smartctl` and `hdparm` only `if command -v` finds them ("ship on some variants only"), and tests `vim` if present and otherwise `vi`. The shared evidence script accepts the gap between siblings by probing for tools at run time, and AcornOS's `scenarios.toml:65-66` separately requires `smartctl`/`hdparm`, so the script and the contract each carry part of which sibling has which tool.
- [special] TI109 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/installed-tools.sh:27-50` switches on `/etc/os-release` `ID` (`levitateos` / `acornos`, with an error for anything else) to pick sudo+visudo vs doas, and bash vs ash. A per-distro name branch inside a shared script repeats the contract's `installed_tools.required_tools`.
- [special] TI110 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/lib/common.sh:69-101` `test_tool` treats exit 127 as "not found" and anything else as "broken", while `check_tool` in Rust (`mod.rs:1346-1357`) uses `command -v`. These are two definitions of "missing" that disagree for a binary that exits 127 on `--version`.
- [special] TV2031 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1347` `check_tool` interpolates the contract's tool name unquoted into `command -v {tool} >/dev/null`, while the same module shell-quotes every other guest argument with `install::shell_quote`; it relies on contract tool names being shell-safe words.
- [hidden] PA1071 `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:52-53` lists the four glibc C.UTF-8 locale paths (`lib`, `usr/lib`, `lib64`, `usr/lib64` + `/locale/C.utf8/LC_CTYPE`), the same list the contract keeps twice in `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:919-924` and `1018-1023`; the recipe's installed check and the contract's live-boot checks must agree on where the locale payload lives, but nothing ties the three copies together.
- [hidden] PA2110 `docs/content/src/content/05-rec-tooling/14-recsetup.ts:147` the shared recsetup page's per-init table says the OpenRC keymap goes into "/etc/conf.d/keymaps (or loadkeys)". `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh:57-62` first uses busybox's `loadkmap` with a `.bmap.gz` from `/usr/share/bkeymaps`, and AcornOS ships `kbd-bkeymaps` (`distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:61`), so on AcornOS the keymap lands in `/etc/conf.d/loadkmap`. The page restates a fact the script owns, and it is already wrong for the one sibling it describes.
- [hidden] TI107 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1362-1384` `tool_run_command` keeps a per-tool table of "harmless invocations" (`ip link show`, `ping -V || ping -h || ping --help`, `doas -C /etc/doas.conf`, `vi => None` for busybox, ...). `test-scripts/live-tools.sh:49-99` and `installed-tools.sh:28-49` run their own invocations for the same tools (`ip -V`, `ping -V`, `dmesg --version || dmesg -h`, `ash -c 'echo test'`). Two copies with different commands for one tool, and a tool can pass one and fail the other.
- [hidden] TI108 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/live-tools.sh:49-99` hard-codes its own tool list (vim, htop, smartctl, hdparm, lsusb, wipefs, fdisk, awk, sed, ...), separate from the contract's `live_tools.required_tools`, which the harness checks over SSH right before running this script. LevitateOS's contract does not list vim/htop/smartctl/hdparm (`distro/variants/levitate/scenarios/scenarios.toml:62-79`), yet the script requires them on both distros.
- [hidden] TV1040 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:403-436` (live-tools, over SSH) and `:681-701` (runtime, over serial) each re-implement the same `missing`/`found`/`broken` tool loop around `check_tool`, with different error texts ("Missing tools (not in PATH)" vs "Missing daily driver tools").
- [hidden] TV1043 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1364` `tool_run_command` names the installer tools `recpart|recstrap|recfstab|recchroot` for a `--help` probe; `recsetup` (in both contracts' `live_tools.required_tools`, `levitate/scenarios/scenarios.toml:67`) and `recab` (installed tools) fall to the generic `--version` arm, so the per-tool table must track which rec* tools take which flag.
- [hidden] TV1073 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/live-tools.sh:99` "shell can execute toolbox binaries" (`command -v recstrap recfstab recchroot`) repeats the presence of the tools already run at `:48-50`.
- [surplus] PA2057 `distro/builder/recipes/sources/alpine.rhai:22` pins and installs `openrc-init` into AcornOS (the built rootfs has `sbin/openrc-init`), and `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:190-195` has an `openrc-init` -> `openrc-shutdown -r now` reboot branch keyed on `/proc/1/comm`. But AcornOS's `sbin/init` is the busybox link, both inittabs (`installed-system.rhai:96-101`, `live-overlay.rhai:24`) are busybox-init inittabs, and nothing passes `init=/sbin/openrc-init`. The package and the branch serve a PID 1 that AcornOS never runs.

### G08 Both package sets are declared the same way and locked by one tool

Solves **52** problems (10 parity, 14 special, 22 hidden, 1 needless, 5 surplus).

- **Design:** Both variants declare backend, release, signing keys and packages per capability in the same shape; `cargo xtask base lock <distro>` resolves dependencies and records the lock with its trust check for both; doctor reports staleness for both. One recipe library for locked downloads and RPM unpacking.
- **Done when:** AcornOS's package list is variant data, not a Rhai string; both distros have a lock file, a refresh and a doctor row; no hand-pinned APK list.
- **Depends on:** G03, G07
- **Decisions:** AcornOS gets a lock and refresh, i.e. security updates through the lock (default yes).
- **Merges area designs:** CV-07 (contract-variants), RG-05 (builder-recipes-guest), PA-10 (parity-all), XT-07 (xtask), RG-11 (builder-recipes-guest) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] BR002 `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:21` keeps LevitateOS's package list in variant TOML. `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:58` hard-codes AcornOS's list inside the recipe's `_profile_packages`, and `distro/builder/recipes/sources/alpine.rhai:15` holds its pinned extras. The same fact ("what the base system contains") lives in TOML for one distro and in Rhai for the other.
- [parity] BR004 `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:80` and `:121`: the Fedora path does no dependency solving, so every shared library has to be listed by hand (the lock equals the TOML list one for one). The Alpine path lets `apk add` resolve dependencies from the ISO (`alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:244`). On LevitateOS a missing library shows up only at runtime ("error while loading shared libraries"); on AcornOS it does not.
- [parity] BR005 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:58` AcornOS gets `wpa_supplicant`, `wpa_supplicant-openrc` and `iw` (WiFi). The LevitateOS lock (`sources.toml:155` NetworkManager) has no `wpa_supplicant`, so LevitateOS has no WiFi backend.
- [parity] BR006 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:60` and `recipes/sources/alpine.rhai:16-27`: AcornOS ships `htop`, `vim`, `hdparm` and `smartmontools`. None of them is in the LevitateOS package list (`distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:21`).
- [parity] BR037 `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:6` gives the LevitateOS rootfs build 8192 MiB and `acorn/ring3/sources.toml:6` gives AcornOS 4096 MiB, and the kinds differ (`recipe_rpm_set` vs `recipe_custom`). The contract validates the two kinds by different rules (`distro/contract/src/validate.rs:985-1051`).
- [parity] C109 `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/keys/RPM-GPG-KEY-fedora-45-primary` is a file only one variant has (`map/variant-diff.txt:2`). AcornOS's package-signing trust (Alpine keys) is not declared in its variant at all.
- [parity] RG1005 `distro/builder/recipes/sources/levitate-rpm-lock.rhai:9-138`: the LevitateOS lock has no `tar` or `gzip` package (no row; only `bzip2-libs`/`xz-libs` libraries). AcornOS gets `tar` and `gzip` as BusyBox applets through `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:72-92`. So only one of the two installed systems can unpack a tarball.
- [parity] RG2001 `distro/builder/guest/live/shell-ux.sh:8-9` sets `EDITOR=vi` (and `VISUAL`) as the default on both live ISOs. The LevitateOS lock (`distro/builder/recipes/sources/levitate-rpm-lock.rhai`) ships no `vi`, `vim` or `nano`, so on LevitateOS the default editor is a program that does not exist. AcornOS has both vim and BusyBox vi.
- [parity] RG2162 `distro/builder/recipes/sources/alpine.rhai:25` AcornOS pins `systemd-boot-260.2-r0`, while LevitateOS's lock has `systemd-boot-unsigned-262.1-1.fc45` (`levitate-rpm-lock.rhai:127`). The bootloader both siblings share runs at two different versions, and the Acorn pin is edited by hand.
- [parity] X006 `xtask/src/tasks/base.rs:50-107` LevitateOS's base packages are re-resolved to the newest signed builds by a command (`base lock`, with `--check`) and checked for staleness by `doctor` (`setup.rs:203-242`); AcornOS's base pins (`distro/builder/recipes/sources/alpine.rhai:2-37`, ISO version, apk-tools and `extra_apks` sha256s) are hand-edited, with no lock command, no update check and no doctor check.
- [special] BR017 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:47` `_profile_packages(profile)` takes a profile parameter whose only accepted value is `"acorn"`, and `ctx.profile` is hard-coded to `"acorn"` (`:20`). That is an extension point for a value that never varies, and distro identity sits inside a recipe named after the base distro.
- [special] BR019 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:200` the ISO is extracted with `xorriso` if present, otherwise `7z`. That fallback exists for one host situation; `build-host.toml` declares only `xorriso`.
- [special] BR034 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-preseed.rhai:26` catches the download error and rethrows it with a hint to run `cargo xtask base lock levitate`. The recipe names a specific xtask command and distro id, and the Alpine preseed gives no equivalent hint.
- [special] C090 `distro/contract/src/schema.rs:96-104` `RpmBaseContract.fedora_release` and `validate.rs:985-1052` put Fedora-specific vocabulary in the general schema. `kind = "recipe_rpm_set"` is a LevitateOS-only rule set (preseed required, defines forbidden) at `validate.rs:985-989,1008-1012`.
- [special] RG1125 `distro/builder/recipes/linux-deps.rhai:10-13,49`: kernel build dependencies come from `dnf_download` of whatever the build host's repositories hold, with no versions or hashes. Every other downloading recipe in this folder pins URL + sha256 (`qemu-tools.rhai:12-27`, `sources/*`).
- [special] RG1126 `distro/builder/recipes/linux-deps.rhai:16-34`: `is_installed` first accepts any matching tool on the host (`which`), so whether the extracted deps are used at all depends on the build host. `ld` is in the checked list, while `install` deletes the extracted `ld` (`:69-72`), so `ld` must always come from the host.
- [special] RG1142 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:244-245`: `apk add` runs with `--allow-untrusted --force-non-repository --no-scripts`, so apk's signature checking is off. The comment at `distro/builder/recipes/sources/alpine.rhai:12-13` makes up for it with a hand review recorded in prose.
- [special] RG2163 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:196-215` counts the ISO as fully extracted as soon as `iso-contents/apks` exists. There is no completion marker, so an extraction cut off after `apks/` was created is reused on every later build.
- [special] X005 `xtask/src/cli/types.rs:76` and `xtask/src/tasks/base.rs:1-7` `cargo xtask base lock` is described as "the packages a variant takes from its Fedora base", but it takes any distro with default `levitate`; for `acorn` it fails with a usage error (exit 2, `base.rs:54-59`). It is a LevitateOS-only command presented as a per-distro one.
- [special] X079 `xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:236-238` a 30-day staleness threshold for the Fedora lock is a magic number restated in `architecture.md:25` ("a month old").
- [special] XT1060 `xtask/src/tasks/base.rs:115` `curl --retry 3`, `base.rs:394` `let _ = fs::remove_file(&partial)` before the bail, and `base.rs:165` `child.stdout.take().unwrap()`.
- [special] XT1061 `xtask/src/tasks/base.rs:35-36` `nevra()` leaves out the epoch while `evr_cmp` (`base.rs:273-279`) compares it. `report_changes` compares `nevra()` strings (`base.rs:366`), so it cannot see an epoch-only change.
- [special] XT1062 `xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:205` `base_lock_check` drops a contract load error with `.ok()?`, so a broken contract silently removes the base-lock row from doctor. `setup.rs:232` `unwrap_or(then)` turns a clock error into a lock age of 0 days.
- [special] XT2079 `xtask/src/tasks/base.rs:349-352` `read_locked` treats an unreadable lock file as an empty lock (`let Ok(text) = .. else { return BTreeMap::new() }`), so `base lock --check` reports every package as new instead of reporting the read error.
- [hidden] BR032 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-preseed.rhai:20,45` / `fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:10` and `alpine-preseed-source-assets.rhai:20` / `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:11-15`: the named preseed outputs (`rpms`, `iso`, `apk_tools`, `extra_apks`) have to agree in three places: the preseed recipe's `ctx.outputs`, the materializer's `RUNTIME.paths.inputs/<name>`, and `preseed_outputs` in `distro/variants/*/ring3/sources.toml:13`. Nothing ties them together except matching strings.
- [hidden] BR033 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-preseed.rhai:26-44` and `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-preseed-source-assets.rhai:32-58` are two preseed recipes with the same shape (for each pinned file: download if missing or forced, otherwise verify sha256; publish named outputs). Each has its own copy of the fetch/verify loop.
- [hidden] BR035 `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:9` / `acorn/ring3/sources.toml:9` declare the preseed network host (`dl.fedoraproject.org:443`, `dl-cdn.alpinelinux.org:443`), while the URLs live in `recipes/sources/levitate-rpm-lock.rhai` and `recipes/sources/alpine.rhai:8`. Changing a mirror needs both edits.
- [hidden] BR072 `distro/builder/recipes/linux-deps.rhai:10` lists Fedora/RHEL RPM names and uses `dnf_download` (`:49`), so the kernel build's dependency recipe assumes an RPM-based build host. Its `is_acquired` (`:37`) treats any `*.rpm` in the build dir as "acquired".
- [hidden] C085 `ring3/sources.toml:4-5` `storage_max_objects = 131072` and `inputs.system_setup = distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh` are identical in both variants. `memory_mib` differs (8192 vs 4096, `:6-7`) with no stated reason.
- [hidden] CV1034 `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:15-17` comment explains how `cargo xtask base lock levitate` resolves builds and where it writes (`distro/builder/recipes/sources/levitate-rpm-lock.rhai`). That behaviour belongs to xtask and drifts from it without warning.
- [hidden] CV3109 `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:43-44,80-81,121-124` package-list comments state facts owned by tools and the lock generator (recpart formats ext4/FAT, recab stage formats a slot, `bootctl install` copies systemd-boot, "RPM dependency solving is not active in this extraction path"); they drift when those tools change.
- [hidden] PA3124 `docs/content/src/content/04-architecture/04-variants.ts:47` says "Recipes own the package facts". LevitateOS's package list is owned by `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml` (`rpm_base.packages`, locked by `cargo xtask base lock`). Only AcornOS's list is owned by a recipe (`distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:58-61`). The architecture doc states one ownership rule, and only one sibling follows it.
- [hidden] RG1012 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:50-52`: a comment lists which packages come from `extra_apks` ("findutils, grep, less, musl-locales, openrc-init, systemd-boot, hdparm, smartmontools, vim"). That list belongs to `distro/builder/recipes/sources/alpine.rhai:15-30`, and the comment is already out of date: it leaves out `pixman`, `musl-locales-lang`, `vim-common` and `xxd`.
- [hidden] RG1015 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:234,236`: the mirror URL `https://dl-cdn.alpinelinux.org/alpine/` + `release_branch` is written out again for `/etc/apk/repositories`, though `distro/builder/recipes/sources/alpine.rhai:8` already builds the same `mirror` from it. Changing the mirror means editing both files.
- [hidden] RG1137 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-preseed.rhai:12` and `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:24,121`: the rule "the file name is the basename of the URL" for the `rpms` directory is written separately in the producer and the consumer. `alpine-preseed-source-assets.rhai:57` and `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:34,43` do the same with `apk[0]`.
- [hidden] RG1138 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:22-28,35,40,113`: `_verify_rpms` re-hashes every locked RPM in `is_acquired`, `acquire` and `build` (three passes per build), repeating the preseed's own verification. `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:27-38,169,178` does the same for the ISO, apk-tools and extra APKs.
- [hidden] RG2144 `distro/builder/recipes/sources/alpine.rhai:13-14` a comment in the facts file defines the extra-APK URL scheme (`extra_apks_url + repository + "/x86_64/" + file name`), and `alpine-preseed-source-assets.rhai:57` implements it. The URL layout is split between a comment and the code.
- [hidden] RG2176 `distro/builder/recipes/sources/levitate-rpm-lock.rhai:5-6` the lines `fn release() { 45 }` and `fn locked_on() { "2026-11-07" }` are really a data format. `xtask/src/tasks/base.rs:438-447` writes them, and `xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:213-217` reads them back by matching the text `fn <name>() { ` ... ` }`. Reformatting the Rhai breaks `doctor`, and no recipe reads `locked_on`.
- [hidden] RG2177 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:5,12` and `fedora-rpm-preseed.rhai:4,9` take the package set only from the generated lock. Nothing compares the lock with `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml` `packages`; doctor (`xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:218-224`) compares only the Fedora release number. If someone edits the package list and does not relock, the build silently uses the old set.
- [hidden] RG2179 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:53-57` and `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:43-44` record the install tools' runtime needs only in comments. On the AcornOS side these are GNU `cp` xattr preservation for recstrap, `tzdata` for recsetup and `kbd-bkeymaps` for loadkmap; on the LevitateOS side, e2fsprogs/dosfstools/systemd-boot for recpart, recab and bootctl. The two package lists have different shapes, and nothing checks a tool's needs against either one.
- [hidden] RG3074 RPM extraction is written three ways: `distro/builder/recipes/linux-deps.rhai:58-64` (`shell` with redirect, then `shell_in` cpio), `distro/builder/recipes/qemu-tools.rhai:77-81` (`exec_checked sh -c` with positional args and a `--nonmatching` filter), and the Fedora producer's engine call `target_extract_rpm` (`fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:30-32`). Each has its own comment about not swallowing `rpm2cpio` pipe errors, and a fix to one does not reach the others.
- [hidden] RG3077 `distro/builder/recipes/qemu-tools.rhai:40-50` is a third copy of the "download unless present or forced, else re-verify sha256" loop, beside the two preseed copies (`alpine-preseed-source-assets.rhai:32-36` `_fetch`, `fedora-rpm-preseed.rhai:35-45`).
- [hidden] X070 `xtask/src/tasks/base.rs:45-48` writes the lock as `<distro>-rpm-lock.rhai` into the builder's recipe tree, while the recipes import the literal `sources/levitate-rpm-lock.rhai` (`fedora-rpm-preseed.rhai:4`, `fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:5`); a second RPM variant would get a lock no recipe reads.
- [hidden] X071 `xtask/src/tasks/base.rs:438-464` renders the lock's Rhai text by hand; `base.rs:349-360` (`read_locked`) and `setup.rs:213-217` (doctor's `field`) each parse it back by line prefix. Three places know the format, and the recipe is a fourth.
- [hidden] X116 `architecture.md:25` documents the LevitateOS-only lock file path and flow; no Acorn equivalent is described (X006).
- [hidden] XT2081 `xtask/src/tasks/base.rs:426-427` decides that signatures are valid by checking that each `rpmkeys --checksig` stdout line ends with `signatures OK` and that the line count equals the file count. That output format is owned by rpm, and a wording or line-layout change in rpm breaks the check.
- [needless] X069 `xtask/src/tasks/base.rs:20,64-67,98-101` xtask carries the Fedora mirror URL, the repo layout (`releases/<r>/Everything/x86_64/os/`, `updates/<r>/Everything/x86_64/`), arch `x86_64`, RPM download/cache (`.artifacts/rpm-cache/fedora-<r>`) and signature checking: source acquisition, which AGENTS.md assigns to recipes. `fedora-rpm-preseed.rhai:18-42` then downloads the same RPMs again into its own place.
- [surplus] C069 `ring3/sources.toml:8` `network_hosts = []` in both variants restates the serde default (`variant.rs:1579-1580`).
- [surplus] CV1069 `distro/contract/src/schema.rs:126` `RootfsSourceContract::defines` is validated (`validate.rs:1008,1072-1093`) and passed through the builder (`distro/builder/src/pipeline/source.rs:50`, `recipe/rootfs_source.rs:46`), but neither variant's `ring3/sources.toml` declares it. It is forbidden for `recipe_rpm_set`, so it can only ever matter for `recipe_custom`, and no variant uses it there.
- [surplus] RG1013 `distro/builder/recipes/sources/alpine.rhai:23`: `pixman-0.46.4-r0.apk` is pinned, fetched and installed. No package in `_profile_packages` (`alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:58-61`) and no comment says why it is needed.
- [surplus] RG1136 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-preseed.rhai:49-52` and `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-preseed-source-assets.rhai:63-66`: the `is_built`, `build`, `is_installed` and `install` stubs exist only because the lifecycle requires every hook. `is_built` and `is_installed` re-run `is_acquired`, which re-hashes every file.
- [surplus] X072 `xtask/src/tasks/base.rs:272-346` a full `rpmvercmp` re-implementation, repomd.xml parsing by string search (`:140-146`, `attr_in`/`between` `:171-181`) and a hand-walked quick-xml event loop for the primary index (`:183-268`) live in the CLI crate.

### G09 One host-command layer: one Recipe runner, one spawn helper, host prerequisites declared once

Solves **53** problems (1 parity, 19 special, 24 hidden, 1 needless, 8 surplus).

- **Design:** Every subprocess runs under one policy (one binary resolution, explicit environment, signal-aware exit reporting). One `recipe::Invocation` resolves Recipe once. Host prerequisites are declared once (with per-host package names) and checked once at the start of a build; doctor, setup and CI read the same list. Each install tool declares the external commands it runs, and the build checks them.
- **Done when:** One spawn helper in the builder (grep `Command::new`); no `REQUIRED_TOOLS` copies; doctor and the build check the same list; spawn failures say what failed instead of "Is it installed?".
- **Depends on:** G01
- **Merges area designs:** CL-08 (builder-cli), BC-13 (builder-core), CL-13 (builder-cli), XT-09 (xtask), TO-14 (tools) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] X076 `xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:185` doctor checks only `live-boot` and `live-tools` ISOs, not `base-rootfs` (which `test build-preflight` needs), for both distros.
- [special] BC2073 `distro/builder/src/process.rs:287` `.run().ok()?` turns a failure to spawn the host `which` binary (absent on minimal hosts) into "program not found", so `artifact/rootfs.rs:87` then reports mkfs.erofs missing even when it is installed.
- [special] BC2076 `distro/builder/src/artifact/rootfs.rs:94` the "erofs-utils 1.8+ for zstd" requirement exists only as hint text, and `:120-121` needs `--tar=f -T0` support that nothing checks before the multi-GB tar is written; an old mkfs.erofs fails only at the end.
- [special] BC2144 `distro/builder/src/process.rs:38,209` gates the signal-name lookup on `#[cfg(unix)]`, while the same crate uses `std::os::unix` unconditionally (`pipeline/io.rs:117`, `tui_binary.rs:118`, `artifact/filesystem.rs:68`). The non-unix branch can never be compiled.
- [special] BC3042 `distro/builder/src/process.rs:28-30` `CommandResult::code()` returns `-1` for a signal death (`unwrap_or(-1)`), losing which signal killed the process. Nothing calls it.
- [special] BC3045 `distro/builder/src/process.rs:148` and `:190` report every spawn error as "Failed to execute '...'. Is it installed?", including permission-denied and exec-format errors.
- [special] BP131 `distro/builder/src/artifact/rootfs.rs:87-96` prints install hints for Fedora/Ubuntu/Arch hosts and erofs-utils version notes inside the image builder, a host-distro special case in library code.
- [special] BR087 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:72` `find_recipe` has five fallbacks (`RECIPE_BIN`, `RECIPE_SRC`, `target/{release,debug}`, build the submodule, `PATH`), and the debug/release choice reads a third env var `RECIPE_BUILD_RELEASE` (`:226`). Which `recipe` binary runs depends on ambient env and leftover build products, and `xtask/src/util/distro_builder.rs:8` picks its profile a different way (`cfg!(debug_assertions)`).
- [special] CL1071 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:72-158` `find_recipe` is called again at every call site (`pipeline/overlay.rs:42`, `pipeline/kernel.rs:52`, `recipe/linux.rs:66`, `recipe/qemu.rs:7`, `recipe/tools.rs:306`, `recipe/rootfs_source.rs:38,113`, `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:118`). Resolution is never cached, so any one of them can start a `cargo build` of Recipe partway through a product build.
- [special] CL1073 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:106-107` orders workspace binaries first "to avoid stale global binaries missing helpers required by this repo", an ordering kept for one past failure. `:96` guesses the workspace root for `RECIPE_SRC` as `src.parent()`, and `:207-218` falls back to a crate-local `target/` for a non-workspace case that does not occur in this repo.
- [special] CL1080 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:51-67` `RecipeBinary::is_valid` maps a metadata error to `false` (`Err(_) => false`), and `:78-89` tests `exists()` and `is_valid()` separately only to print two different messages.
- [special] CL2016 `distro/builder/src/bin/distro-builder.rs:74-77` `arm_parent_death_signal` has a no-op `#[cfg(not(unix))]` twin beside the Linux-only `prctl(PR_SET_PDEATHSIG)` arm (`:62-72`). The builder only runs on Linux, so the non-unix arm is speculative.
- [special] CL2064 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:226-230` reads `RECIPE_BUILD_RELEASE`, while CI sets `REC_BUILD_RELEASE: "1"` (`.github/workflows/ci.yml:12`). Nothing in the repo reads the knob CI sets, and nothing sets the knob the builder reads.
- [special] CL2068 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:322-324` expands one `force_acquire` flag into both `--force-acquire` and `--force-install`, so `artifact preseed-rootfs-source --refresh` (`commands.rs:124-130`) also forces a reinstall. xtask maps its own refresh flags to Recipe flags separately (`xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:166`, `:209`).
- [special] CL2069 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:197` and `:373` report `status.code().unwrap_or(-1)`. When a Recipe or cargo child is killed by a signal (including the PDEATHSIG the builder arms), the error says "exit code -1" and the signal is lost.
- [special] TO1059 `tools/recpart/src/exec.rs:55,267-270` e2fsck alone gets an "exit 1 is success" list (`E2FSCK_OK`) patched into the generic `Run` action after construction; NTFS resize gets a stdin `y\n` patched in the same way (`:289-292`).
- [special] TO1130 `tools/reciso/src/main.rs:37-42` the help text gives Fedora host instructions (`dnf install systemd-boot xorriso mtools systemd-ukify`) for a tool that builds both distros' ISOs (copied into `docs/content/src/tool-help.ts:6` by `xtask generate`).
- [special] TO2089 `tools/recpart/src/inspect.rs:534-570` used-space probes parse English human output of dumpe2fs (`"Free blocks"`), `fsck.fat -v` (`"bytes per cluster"`, `" clusters"`) and ntfsresize (`"resize at "`); any tool version or locale change returns `None`, which then reads as "needs root".
- [special] XT1068 `xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:104,111,132` doctor's fix hints name Fedora package names (`libvirt-client`, `libvirt-daemon-kvm`, `edk2-ovmf`), while CI installs Debian's `ovmf`/`qemu-system-x86` (`ci.yml:47`) and `util/repo.rs:51` says "edk2-ovmf / ovmf". The host package vocabulary is chosen per message.
- [special] XT3022 `xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:84-165` doctor checks `virsh`, the libvirt connection, `passt`, `qemu-img` and an ISO tool, but never `qemu-system-x86_64`, the emulator libvirt starts for every VM. The libvirt connection row (`setup.rs:110`) falls back to "connected" when `virsh version` prints no hypervisor line (`unwrap_or("connected")`), so a host without the emulator passes doctor.
- [hidden] BC1063 `distro/builder/src/process.rs:34-58` `CommandResult::exit_description` and `:205-229` `exit_description_from_status` are two copies of the same exit/signal-name table.
- [hidden] BC1108 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/io.rs:107` extracts parent images with the host `fsck.erofs`, a tool not listed in either variant's `required_build_tools` (`distro/variants/*/build-host/build-host.toml:4-18`, which lists only `mkfs.erofs`).
- [hidden] BC2075 `distro/builder/src/process.rs:322-329` the `find_first_existing` doc example falls back from the rootfs systemd-boot EFI to the build host's `/usr/lib/systemd/boot/efi/systemd-bootx64.efi`, documenting a build-host leak as the intended use.
- [hidden] BC2081 `distro/builder/src/tui_binary.rs:186-198` its own `run()` helper re-implements `process::Cmd::run` (capture, status check, stdout+stderr error), next to a call to `process::which` (`:74`).
- [hidden] BC3149 `mkfs.erofs` is checked three times with different wording and strictness: `distro/builder/src/artifact/rootfs.rs:87-96` (spawns `which`, prints Fedora/Ubuntu/Arch hints), `distro/builder/src/preflight/mod.rs:41` (`REQUIRED_TOOLS`) and `distro/variants/*/build-host/build-host.toml:11` (`required_build_tools`).
- [hidden] BC3150 `distro/variants/levitate/build-host/build-host.toml:4-17` (and acorn's) `required_build_tools` leaves out `bun`, which every live-tools build needs on the host (`distro/builder/src/tui_binary.rs:74`). The declared host requirements do not cover what the product build runs.
- [hidden] BP039 `distro/builder/src/process.rs:286-297` `which()` shells out to the `which` program, while the crate also depends on the `which` crate (`distro/builder/Cargo.toml` `which = "5.0"`, used in `src/recipe/mod.rs`). That is two PATH-lookup implementations, and `install-split-pane/src/main.rs:136-142` has a third.
- [hidden] BR091 `distro/builder/src/recipe/rootfs_source.rs:39`, `:120` and `src/recipe/qemu.rs:17` pass the recipe file's parent directory as `--recipes-path`, so every import (`sources/alpine.rhai`, `installed-system.rhai`, `kernel/metadata.rhai`) only works if the recipe sits directly in `distro/builder/recipes`. `recipe_script` in TOML looks movable but is not.
- [hidden] BR141 `distro/builder/src/bin/distro-builder.rs:63-71` and `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:351-357` both arm `PR_SET_PDEATHSIG` with their own `unsafe` block, and `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:121`, which spawns `recipe` directly, arms neither, so orphan protection depends on which path started the process.
- [hidden] CL1067 `distro/builder/src/preflight/mod.rs:40-48` `REQUIRED_TOOLS` and `distro/builder/src/artifact/disk/helpers.rs:20-28` `BASE_REQUIRED_TOOLS` (with its own `check_host_tools`, `:33`) are two host-tool tables in one crate. Both list `mcopy`/`mmd` and a dosfstools tool, with different messages, beside the variants' `required_build_tools`. A tool requirement has three owners.
- [hidden] CL1072 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:113-126,205,209` hard-codes `<repo>/target/<profile>/recipe` and ignores `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`, which xtask honours for the same workspace (`xtask/src/util/repo.rs:12-17` `cargo_target_dir`). With a custom target dir the builder misses the built binary and rebuilds or falls through to a stale PATH binary.
- [hidden] CL1078 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:314-365` the Recipe child inherits the builder's whole environment, so ambient `GINUX_BIN`, `RECIPE_WORKER`, `RECIPE_INVOCATION` and `RECIPE_BACKEND` change how Recipe runs with no sign of it at the call. The contract test has to strip them by hand (`tests/preseed_contract.rs:53-56`).
- [hidden] CL1091 `distro/builder/src/recipe/outputs.rs:63-67` `optional_directory` repeats `declared_path`'s lookup of `ctx.outputs` (`:8-11`) with the same error message.
- [hidden] CL2072 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:173-174`, `:184-186`, `:211`, `:221` print Recipe self-build progress to stdout, while `:299-301` print run progress to stderr. Commands that print their result path on stdout (`commands.rs:94`, `:116`) can therefore have progress text mixed into it whenever `find_recipe` builds Recipe mid-run.
- [hidden] CL3074 `distro/builder/tests/preseed_contract.rs:79,100,142` decide whether Recipe was launched by matching the progress line `"Running recipe:"`, which `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:299` prints to stderr. Rewording a progress message breaks the contract tests.
- [hidden] CL3083 `distro/builder/src/recipe/outputs.rs:14-19` checks for emptiness after `trim()` but builds the path from the untrimmed string. `" /x"` is therefore rejected as non-absolute, while `"/x "` is accepted as a path with a trailing space. `metadata_string` (`:74-79`) applies the same trim-only-for-the-test rule and returns the untrimmed value.
- [hidden] TO1099 `tools/recstrap/src/fstab.rs:407-420` recfstab requires util-linux `findmnt`, `swapon --show` and `lsblk` on the live system (`tool_missing ... "util-linux"`); AcornOS's live ISO must ship util-linux for install to work, a requirement recorded only in the Alpine recipe's package string (`distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:59`).
- [hidden] TO1135 `tools/recpart/src/exec.rs:278-295` and `src/inspect.rs:424-430` resizing FAT and NTFS partitions (dual boot) runs `fatresize` and `ntfsresize`, which neither variant's package list provides (`distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml`, `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:58-61`; only `testing/fsdbg/src/oracle/bins.rs:293` expects `ntfsresize`); the used-space probe (`inspect.rs` `probe_free_bytes`) returns `None` silently when the tool is missing, so a resize is refused as "used space unreadable" rather than "tool missing".
- [hidden] TO2148 `tools/recinit/src/modules.rs:290-310` module decompression shells out to host `xz`/`gzip`/`zstd`; none is in `required_build_tools` (`distro/variants/levitate/build-host/build-host.toml:4-16`), and `tools/recinit/README.md:7` says "No external dependencies".
- [hidden] TO3033 `tools/reciso/README.md:86-91` and `tools/reciso/src/main.rs:37-41` list the host requirements as systemd-boot, xorriso, mtools and ukify, and omit `mkfs.fat` (dosfstools) and `sha512sum`, which `tools/reciso/src/iso.rs:42,61` run.
- [hidden] X073 `xtask/src/tasks/base.rs:113-170,405-436` `base lock` needs `curl`, `zstd/gzip/xz` and `rpmkeys`; doctor checks none of them (`setup.rs:144-165`).
- [hidden] X074 `xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:97-165` doctor keeps its own host-tool list (virsh, passt, qemu-img, ssh, ssh-keygen, bun, xorriso|genisoimage|mkisofs), while the variant contract declares `required_build_tools` (`build-host.toml:4-17`: cargo, make, curl, mkfs.erofs, xorriso, mkfs.fat, mmd, mcopy, sha512sum, ukify). Doctor never checks most of the build requirements, and accepts genisoimage/mkisofs although the contract requires xorriso.
- [hidden] XT1006 `.github/workflows/ci.yml:46-47` the apt package list (erofs-utils, xorriso, mtools, dosfstools, cpio, zstd, qemu, ovmf, systemd-container, systemd-ukify) is a third, separate copy of the host prerequisites, next to `build-host/build-host.toml` `required_build_tools` and doctor's list (`xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:97-165`). None is derived from another, and CI installs neither libvirt nor passt, both of which doctor marks required for scenario VMs.
- [hidden] XT2057 `xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:157-165` doctor's "ISO tool" check (`xorriso`, `genisoimage`, `mkisofs`, "boot injection media") copies the fallback chain in `testing/install-tests/src/boot_injection.rs:115-119`. Which tools make injection media is decided in install-tests and restated in doctor.
- [needless] BR153 `distro/builder/Cargo.toml:21` `levitate-recipe = { path = "../../tools/recipe" }` makes the builder a library dependent of the Recipe engine (`levitate_recipe::target::{load,save,merge_tree,write_tar}` in `pipeline/products.rs:584`, `pipeline/overlay.rs:62`, `artifact/rootfs.rs:115`), while every recipe run goes through a separately resolved `recipe` binary (`src/recipe/mod.rs:72`). The in-process `target` metadata reader and the subprocess that wrote the metadata can come from different builds (`RECIPE_BIN`, `PATH`, stale `target/`).
- [surplus] BP037 `distro/builder/src/process.rs:243-282,313-333` `run`, `run_in`, `shell`, `shell_in`, `ensure_exists` and `find_first_existing` have no callers. Only `Cmd`, `which` and `exists` are used.
- [surplus] BP038 `distro/builder/src/process.rs:334-337` ends in a "Tests" section banner with nothing under it, left over from removed unit tests.
- [surplus] BR083 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:296-297` `run_recipe_phase_json_impl` takes `force_acquire` and `storage_max_objects`, which both public wrappers (`:242`, `:271`) hard-code to `false`/`None`. Only `rootfs_source.rs:56`, `:114` reach into the private impl to set them, so the wrappers are pass-throughs and callers that need the full knob set bypass them.
- [surplus] BR084 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:234` `run_recipe_phase_json_with_defines` is used only by `distro/builder/tests/recipe_outputs.rs:131`.
- [surplus] BR086 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:30` `RecipeSource` exists only to pick a word for a `println!` (`:168`).
- [surplus] BR088 `distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:3` module doc says "Recipe is the general-purpose package manager used by Alpine-based distros to manage build dependencies", which is stale; it runs every producer for both distros.
- [surplus] BR148 `distro/builder/src/preflight/mod.rs:40` `REQUIRED_TOOLS`, `check_host_tools` and `check_required_tools` have no callers. The declared host-tool list is `required_build_tools` in `distro/variants/*/build-host/build-host.toml:4`, which nothing checks against the host either (`distro/contract/src/validate.rs:1143` only rejects squashfs tokens), and the two lists differ (the TOML adds `cargo`, `make`, `curl`, `sha512sum` and `ukify`, and lacks `cpio` and `gzip`).
- [surplus] CL3088 `distro/builder/src/recipe/rootfs_source.rs:46-55` copies the spec's `BTreeMap`s into `Vec<(&str,&str)>` / `Vec<(&str,&Path)>` only to fit the runner's slice signature.

### G10 The builder has one typed command interface and a thin CLI

Solves **16** problems (6 special, 5 hidden, 2 needless, 3 surplus).

- **Design:** distro-builder exposes one typed command list; its CLI has one exact grammar (no aliases, no unused commands, usage errors exit 2); xtask builds commands from the typed interface instead of argv strings matched by slice patterns, and links nothing else of the builder.
- **Done when:** No alias forms in the CLI or hooks; xtask has no `&["..."]` argv literals for the builder.
- **Depends on:** G05, G09
- **Merges area designs:** CL-02 (builder-cli), XT-05 (xtask) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [special] BR117 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/parse.rs:37-51` accepts `<distro> <product>` or `<product> <distro>` in either order, and a single argument as either. That leniency exists so every calling order works, and forces the parse to try both.
- [special] CL1010 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/parse.rs:29-34` `parse_release_one_arg` swallows the distro-parse error (`if let Ok`) and retries the argument as a product, so a misspelt distro (e.g. `acron`) is reported as "unsupported product 'acron'". `parse_release_two_args` (`:42-51`) drops the first error the same way.
- [special] CL3021 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/commands.rs:34` and `:136` report bad argv with `bail!(crate::usage())`, so a usage error comes out as an ordinary anyhow `Error:` with exit 1, and nothing tells it apart from a build failure. Errors from non-release commands are wrapped in "dispatching workflow for ..." (`commands.rs:138`). Release commands skip that wrapping because `main` sends them down a separate path (`distro-builder.rs:55-57`).
- [special] CL3096 `distro/builder/src/recipe/qemu.rs:31` returns, and `bin/workflows/commands.rs:94` prints, the immutable published tools dir rather than the `.artifacts/tools/current` selection that consumers read (`qemu.rs:30`). The only caller ignores stdout anyway (`xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:12` via `run_bare`, `xtask/src/util/distro_builder.rs:40-52`).
- [special] CL3148 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/parse.rs:81` `parse_product(None)` silently means base-rootfs. `usage()` restates this default ("product defaults to base-rootfs", `distro-builder.rs:47`), and `release build iso <distro>` relies on it (`parse.rs:30`).
- [special] X065 `xtask/src/util/distro_builder.rs:8-27` builds distro-builder with `cargo build` on every call, choosing the profile from xtask's own `debug_assertions`; distro-builder picks recipe's profile by different rules (`distro/builder/src/recipe/mod.rs:109-110,202`), and `generate.rs:136` and `tui/mod.rs:58,313` hard-code `target/debug`. Three profile conventions.
- [hidden] BR115 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/build.rs:37` `build_all` and `xtask/src/tasks/build.rs:11` (`--all`) both loop over distros to build a product. The CLI discovers distros from `distro/variants/*/identity/identity.toml` (`parse.rs:174`), while xtask uses its `Distro::ALL` enum.
- [hidden] CL3125 `distro/builder/Cargo.toml:5,33-35` `autobins = false` plus a hand-declared `[[bin]]` for `src/bin/distro-builder.rs`, while `src/bin/run_manifest.rs` and `src/bin/workflows/` sit in Cargo's auto-bin directory as modules. The layout works only because autobins is off, and nothing near those files says so.
- [hidden] X062 `xtask/src/tasks/build.rs:22-26,76-79,103-110` and `setup.rs:12` call distro-builder with literal argv (`artifact preseed-rootfs-source <d> [--refresh]`, `release build iso <p> <d>`, `product prepare ...`, `transform build product-erofs`, `artifact prepare-qemu-tools`) that must match slice patterns in `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/commands.rs:17-136` exactly; `--refresh` only works as the 4th positional. Nothing type-checks the pair.
- [hidden] X064 `xtask/src/util/distro_builder.rs:30-50` distro-builder resolves its inputs from the current directory (`bin/workflows/build.rs:38,52` use `current_dir()`), so xtask must `current_dir(root)` on every call; distro-builder in turn finds the repo by looking for an `xtask/` directory (`bin/workflows/layout.rs:9`), so each depends on the other's existence by layout.
- [hidden] XT2072 `xtask/src/util/distro_builder.rs:1-2` the module comment says "the release hooks call that same binary back", a fact owned by `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/release_hook.rs:87,121` (`current_exe` passed as `DISTRO_BUILDER_BIN`). The comment goes stale if the hook changes how it finds the builder.
- [needless] BR140 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/mod.rs:1-28` re-exports every sibling's functions, and the siblings call each other only as `crate::workflows::X` (`map/mod-fanin.txt`: `builder::workflows` fan-in 38). Every module depends on the hub, not on the module that owns the function.
- [needless] X063 `xtask/src/util/distro_builder.rs:1-53` xtask both spawns the distro-builder binary and links distro-builder as a library (`run_history::kernel_output_dir`, `recipe::{find_recipe, recipe_work_dir, linux::select_kernel_bundle}`, `install_ux::SERIAL_KEY`); half of distro-builder's interface is CLI and half is internals.
- [surplus] BR111 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/commands.rs:13` and `:55-60` accept `iso build ...` and `iso build-all ...` as undocumented aliases of `release build iso` / `release build-all iso`. `usage()` (`src/bin/distro-builder.rs:46`) does not list them, and nothing in the repo calls them.
- [surplus] BR113 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/commands.rs:4` `is_release_build_invocation` repeats the slice patterns that `run_release_build_command` (`:17`) matches again. Splitting release and non-release dispatch into two matchers adds nothing.
- [surplus] BR114 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/commands.rs:44-60` `release build-all iso [product]`, `:97` `artifact prepare-tool-source` and `:131` `artifact materialize-rootfs-source` have no caller in xtask, the justfile, hooks or tests. xtask uses only `release build iso`, `product prepare`, `transform build product-erofs`, `artifact preseed-rootfs-source` and `artifact prepare-qemu-tools`.

### G11 The kernel has one owner, one pin and one policy

Solves **79** problems (5 parity, 19 special, 36 hidden, 19 surplus).

- **Design:** The variant declares only the kernel line. The exact pin lives once with its source in the kernel recipe (`kernel/metadata.rhai`); localversion is derived; one exact release check; atomic swaps. The kernel is a builder product: the builder's kernel module owns the recipe invocation, verification and a status query; xtask and install-tests stop re-implementing it. The kconfig is one shared policy plus per-variant fragments, and every symbol it sets is enforced on the kernel that ships (prebuilt payloads carry their final `.config`). Recipe's e2e interface for the kernel recipes is kept (file names, `KERNEL_TARGET`, `kernel_archive`, `build_dir`).
- **Done when:** The version/sha appear once per distro (grep); one `kernel.release` check; xtask has no kernel recipe call; both kconfigs differ only in the version-line fragment; a dropped symbol fails the build.
- **Depends on:** G03, G04, G09
- **Decisions:** **Kernel policy convergence** (about 40 differing options: PREEMPT vs PREEMPT_VOLUNTARY, HZ, NO_HZ_FULL, ZRAM, LIVEPATCH, CHECKPOINT_RESTORE, debug). Default: one shared policy (PREEMPT_DYNAMIC, HZ=1000, ZRAM on both). Where prebuilt kernels are published (default: unchanged, two repos at tag `0.0.0-pre-alpha`).
- **Merges area designs:** CV-05 (contract-variants), RG-10 (builder-recipes-guest), BC-15 (builder-core), CL-07 (builder-cli), XT-06 (xtask), TS-02 (testing-static), PA-11 (parity-all) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] C106 `distro/variants/levitate/build-host/kernel/kconfig` vs `acorn/.../kconfig` (~720 lines each, ~40 differing): LevitateOS has `CONFIG_LIVEPATCH` (`:91`), `CHECKPOINT_RESTORE` (`:82`), `MODULE_FORCE_UNLOAD` (`:88`), `SCHED_DEBUG` (`:649`) and `IWLWIFI_DEBUG` (`:306`). AcornOS has `ZRAM` (`:53-54`), full `PREEMPT` (`:25`), `HZ=1000` (`:648`) and `NO_HZ_FULL` (`:646`). The kernel feature sets diverge beyond the intended LTS-vs-stable version difference.
- [parity] TF029 `testing/hardware-compat/src/main.rs:12-17` defaults `--kernel-config` to `.artifacts/kernel/levitate/current/kernel-build/.config`. Nothing names AcornOS, and README `Requirements` (`README.md:24-27`) says "Built LevitateOS ISO". The tool only covers one distro.
- [parity] TF030 `testing/hardware-compat/src/profiles/*.rs` (all 789 `KernelRequirement`s): each symbol has one expectation, with no notion of kernel line. LevitateOS builds 6.12.116 (`distro/variants/levitate/build-host/build-host.toml:25`) and AcornOS builds 7.3.3 (`distro/variants/acorn/build-host/build-host.toml:25`). No entry says which line it was written against, or why the same `=y/=m` applies to both.
- [parity] TF033 `testing/hardware-compat/src/profiles/homelab.rs:379` expects `CONFIG_EDAC_DECODE_MCE=m`. Only LevitateOS's kconfig sets it (`distro/variants/levitate/build-host/kernel/kconfig:686`, with the long-gone `CONFIG_EDAC_MM_EDAC=m` at `:685`). AcornOS's EDAC block (`distro/variants/acorn/build-host/kernel/kconfig:695-701`) leaves both out, with no reason given. The one profile check whose result differs per distro is unexplained.
- [parity] TF034 `testing/hardware-compat/src/profiles/*.rs` checks nothing for the documented kernel differences between the siblings (`CONFIG_ZRAM`, `CONFIG_LIVEPATCH`, `CONFIG_CHECKPOINT_RESTORE`, `CONFIG_PREEMPT*`, `CONFIG_HZ*`, `CONFIG_NO_HZ_*`, `CONFIG_SCHED_DEBUG`, `CONFIG_IWLWIFI_DEBUG`, `CONFIG_MODULE_FORCE_UNLOAD`, `CONFIG_BLK_DEV_INITRD`; see diff of the two kconfig files). A capability lost from one sibling's kernel would go unnoticed.
- [special] BP118 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/kernel.rs:57-58` falls back to the uncanonicalized path when `canonicalize` of the `current` kernel symlink fails, which swallows a dangling-symlink error that Recipe then misreports.
- [special] BR060 `distro/builder/recipes/kernel/metadata.rhai:3` `target()` accepts only the literal strings `"levitate"` and `"acorn"`, and `source_spec` (`:11`, `:19`) and `linux-prebuilt.rhai:28`, `:41` branch on each name. Adding a variant means editing shared recipes, and the variant's own `build-host.toml` pins are ignored by the recipe.
- [special] BR069 `distro/builder/recipes/linux.rhai:161` runs `mv lib/modules/* usr/lib/modules/ 2>/dev/null || true` and swallows a failed module relocation, right under the file's own "Do NOT add `|| true`" rule at `:27`.
- [special] CL1130 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/build.rs:59-66` creates `kernel_output_dir` (`.artifacts/kernel/<distro>/current`) with `create_dir_all` before any kernel check. `select_directory` (`recipe/outputs.rs:103-112`) later swaps that path for a symlink, so a directory created here is moved aside as a `previous-*` dir.
- [special] CV1097 `distro/contract/src/validate.rs:584-596` `is_kernel_version_token` accepts any digits-and-dots string with at least one dot (`"."`, `"6..12"`) and rejects `-rc` versions. `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:326` then only checks `kernel.release.starts_with(version)`, so a declared `6.12.1` matches a built `6.12.116` release.
- [special] CV3045 `distro/contract/src/build_host.rs:20` `REQUIRED_KERNEL_RELEASE_PATH = "kernel-build/include/config/kernel.release"` is a kernel source-tree build-dir layout kept for prebuilt kernels: `.artifacts/kernel/levitate/current/kernel-build/` contains only `include/config/kernel.release`, a directory that exists to satisfy this path.
- [special] P061 `distro/builder/recipes/linux-prebuilt.rhai:31,44` fetches LevitateOS's prebuilt kernel from GitHub repo `LevitateOS/leviso` and AcornOS's from `LevitateOS/AcornOS`; the two siblings publish kernels through different legacy repos.
- [special] RG1129 `distro/builder/recipes/linux.rhai:372-377`: `install` re-reads `kernel.release` and falls back to `ctx.kernel_version` when the file is missing, though `is_built` and `is_installed` already treat a missing file as not ready.
- [special] RG2114 `distro/builder/recipes/linux-prebuilt.rhai:174-198` validates the payload in `prebuilt.tmp` and then validates it again after copying it to the destination "for explicit diagnostics": a second validation of a copy that has not changed.
- [special] RG2123 `distro/builder/recipes/linux.rhai:120-127` applies kconfig lines through an unquoted shell loop (`while read line; ... --set-val $(echo $line | sed 's/=/ /')`), which splits string values that contain spaces or quotes. Then `:134-136` overrides `CONFIG_LOCALVERSION` from the spec ("this wins"), so the recipe holds two owners of localversion.
- [special] RG2125 `distro/builder/recipes/kernel/metadata.rhai:67,70,83,86` the source recipe accepts any `kernel.release` that `starts_with` the pinned version and `ends_with` the localversion. A release such as `6.12.1160-levitate` or `6.12.116-rc1-levitate` passes for pin `6.12.116`. The prebuilt recipe, by contrast, compares exactly (`linux-prebuilt.rhai:84`).
- [special] RG2129 `distro/builder/recipes/linux.rhai:287-289` and `:388-390` "Swap into place atomically-ish" first runs `rm -rf` on the old tree and then `mv`s the tmp tree. If the process dies between the two, there is no build or staging tree at all.
- [special] RG2173 `distro/builder/recipes/linux.rhai:178-186,201-211` counts the source as acquired when the extracted tree has a `Makefile`. In that case both download and hash check are skipped, so a partly extracted or modified tree is reused and never checked against `spec.sha256` again.
- [special] X050 `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:104-106` accepts modules under either `lib/modules` or `usr/lib/modules`, though the contract names one (`kernel_modules_path`, `module_install_path`); `:86` skips the version check when the contract version is empty.
- [special] X078 `xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:171-183` a kernel that fails verification for any reason (wrong localversion, missing modules) is reported as "not built", and the real error is dropped.
- [special] XT1049 `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:172` silently clamps `--autofix-attempts 0` to 1 (`.max(1)`).
- [special] XT2042 `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:86` checks the built kernel with `rel.starts_with(&t.version)`, so a declared `6.12.11` accepts a built `6.12.116-levitate`. `distro/builder/recipes/linux-prebuilt.rhai:84` compares the exact `expected_release`, so xtask's "built+verified" is looser than the recipe's check of the same bundle.
- [special] XT2043 `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:66-68` `kernel_is_built` is `verify_one(..).is_ok()`. `xtask/src/tasks/build.rs:63-72` therefore reports a wrong-version or wrong-localversion kernel as "the kernel is not built" and drops `verify_one`'s reason, the same as doctor does (X078).
- [special] XT2049 `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:161` and `:206` `if let Ok(target) = fs::canonicalize(&kernel_artifact_root)` silently leaves out the `target=` input when the path cannot be resolved (for example a dangling `current` symlink), which changes what the recipe is given without any message.
- [hidden] BC3148 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/kernel.rs:9-21` `KernelSpec`/`EvidenceSpec` are plain `String` copies of contract fields. Both `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/build.rs:72,126` and `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:193,251` fill them field by field from the contract, so adding a field means editing both callers.
- [hidden] BP117 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/kernel.rs:97` embeds xtask's CLI (`cargo xtask build kernel <d>`, `--rebuild`, `--prebuilt`) in library text, which is coupled to `xtask/src/cli/types.rs:262-267`.
- [hidden] BP120 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/kernel.rs:105-150` Build evidence is a variant shell script, run with `sh`, given three env vars, and passing only if a marker string appears in its combined stdout and stderr. Script, env names and marker are agreed only by convention.
- [hidden] BP121 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/kernel.rs:54` passes `KERNEL_TARGET=<distro_id>` to the kernel recipe, so the recipe branches on the distro name (`distro/builder/recipes/kernel/metadata.rhai:4,11,19`: `if target == "levitate"` / `"acorn"`), not on a declared kernel fact (LTS vs stable).
- [hidden] BR056 `distro/builder/recipes/kernel/metadata.rhai:13-24` (version, sha256, localversion per distro), `distro/builder/recipes/linux-prebuilt.rhai:28-52` (version, localversion, expected_release, asset name containing the version) and `distro/variants/*/build-host/build-host.toml:25-27` (`kernel_version`, `kernel_sha256`, `kernel_localversion`) hold the same kernel pins three times. A kernel bump has to edit all three.
- [hidden] BR058 kernel.release checks are written five times: `kernel/metadata.rhai:65` `release_mismatch` and `:76` `release` (same checks, one returns and one throws), `linux-prebuilt.rhai:77` `_validate_payload_or_throw` and `:113` `_release_mismatch`, `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:86` `verify_one`, and `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:334`.
- [hidden] BR059 the kernel bundle layout (`kernel-build/include/config/kernel.release`, `staging/boot/vmlinuz`, `staging/{usr/,}lib/modules/<rel>`) is spelled out in `kernel/metadata.rhai:66,103`, `linux.rhai:228-320`, `linux-prebuilt.rhai:78,94,99,132,210,245`, `distro/variants/*/build-host/build-host.toml:22-24` (`kernel_release_path`, `kernel_image_path`, `kernel_modules_path`), `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:72-105` and the shared hook `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:208-210`.
- [hidden] BR062 `distro/builder/recipes/linux.rhai` (source build) and `distro/builder/recipes/linux-prebuilt.rhai` (download) both install into the same artifact root (`RUNTIME.paths.target`, i.e. `.artifacts/out/<distro>/current`). The release path's readiness check runs only the one named in `build-host.toml:20` (`linux-prebuilt.rhai`), so a source-built kernel is accepted only if its `kernel.release` happens to equal the prebuilt pin.

Editing `build-host/kernel/kconfig` therefore changes nothing on the release path, and nothing flags that the published prebuilt was built from a different config.

A convention callers just have to know.
- [hidden] BR077 `distro/builder/recipes/linux-prebuilt.rhai:31`, `:44` the prebuilt assets come from repos `LevitateOS/leviso` (old project name) and `LevitateOS/AcornOS`. The release-host network grants live separately in `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:203` (`github.com:443`, `release-assets.githubusercontent.com:443`), so changing where kernels are published needs both edits.
- [hidden] BR079 `distro/builder/src/recipe/linux.rs:28` `linux_for_distro` (uncalled, BR080) and `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:121` `build_kernel_via_recipe` both build the `recipe install linux.rhai` command (KERNEL_TARGET define, kconfig/kconfig_fragments/target inputs, recipes path, json output, bundle selection). xtask builds its own `Command` instead of using `run_recipe_phase_json_*`, so it passes no `--state-dir` (the builder uses `<build_dir>/recipe-state`, `src/recipe/mod.rs:321`) and arms no PR_SET_PDEATHSIG, so subprocess policy differs between the two.
- [hidden] BR082 `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:183` `install_prebuilt_kernel_via_recipe` and `distro/builder/src/pipeline/kernel.rs:28` `check_kernel_installed_with_recipe` invoke the prebuilt kernel recipe through two different code paths (raw `Command` vs `run_recipe_phase_json_with_inputs`), with different inputs (the check passes `kconfig`, the install does not).
- [hidden] C001 `distro/variants/levitate/build-host/build-host.toml:25-27` (`kernel_version`, `kernel_sha256`, `kernel_localversion`) restate `distro/builder/recipes/kernel/metadata.rhai:11-25` (`source_spec`) and `distro/builder/recipes/linux-prebuilt.rhai:28-52` (`_prebuilt_spec_or_throw`). Same for acorn `build-host.toml:25-27`. A kernel bump has to edit all three, and nothing ties them together.

- [hidden] C004 `build-host.toml:28` `module_install_path` repeats `metadata.rhai:16`/`linux-prebuilt.rhai:37` and `ctx.module_install_path` at `linux-prebuilt.rhai:17`. The contract forces it to the constant `/usr/lib/modules` (`build_host.rs:29`, `validate.rs:789`).

- [hidden] C107 `distro/variants/levitate/build-host/kernel/kconfig` and `distro/variants/acorn/build-host/kernel/kconfig` (718 and 728 lines, about 60 differing lines) are full copies of one file, with the shared hardware, filesystem and driver sections duplicated. `distro/builder/src/pipeline/kernel.rs:46-62` supports a `kconfig.d` fragments directory, but neither variant uses one.

- [hidden] CL2126 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/build.rs:126-132` passes the contract's kernel paths to the evidence runner unjoined, and `distro/builder/src/pipeline/kernel.rs:117-118` joins them with `kernel_output_dir`. `release_hook.rs:75-76` joins the same two contract paths itself before exporting them. - [hidden] RG1117 `distro/builder/recipes/linux.rhai:235-245` and `:303-313`: `is_built` and `is_installed` repeat the same block (release-metadata file present, kconfig identity hash, `release_mismatch`, `release`). A change to one has to be copied to the other.
- [hidden] RG2119 `distro/builder/recipes/linux.rhai:147-150` a doc comment in the shared kernel recipe says "LevitateOS is UsrMerge". It names one sibling for a rule that holds for both, since AcornOS also installs to `/usr/lib/modules`.
- [hidden] RG3063 `distro/builder/recipes/linux-prebuilt.rhai:182-188`: the comment says the previous publication stays intact if the copy fails, but the code deletes `kernel-build` and `staging` from the artifact root before `copy_tree_contents`, so a failed copy leaves no kernel at all. Also, `acquire` (`:159-167`) logs "Downloading prebuilt kernel artifact..." and the URL before it checks for a supplied `kernel_archive`, and it downloads every time with no check for an existing file or `force_acquire`.
- [hidden] RG3067 `distro/builder/recipes/linux.rhai:203` (URL built in `kernel/metadata.rhai:40`) downloads the source tarball from `cdn.kernel.org`, but neither caller grants that host. `xtask/src/tasks/kernels/common.rs:137-178` passes no `--network-host` for the source build, while its prebuilt path grants hosts with a comment saying "recipe networking is off unless granted" (`:202-205`). `distro/builder/src/recipe/linux.rs:67-77` passes `&[]` network hosts. No caller supplies the `kernel_archive` input either, so the source-kernel acquisition path only works with a tree that has already been extracted.

Product kernels now come from prebuilt release payloads (`distro/builder/recipes/linux-prebuilt.rhai:77-108`), which validate only `kernel-build/include/config/kernel.release` plus vmlinuz/modules. In the current checkout `.artifacts/kernel/{levitate,acorn}/current/kernel-build/` has no `.config`, so the default input does not exist. Nothing ties the checker's input to what the payload actually carries.
