### G12 One rootfs producer lifecycle with RPM and APK backends

Solves **52** problems (11 special, 31 hidden, 2 needless, 8 surplus).

- **Design:** Both rootfs producers run one lifecycle (verify, unpack, reproduce package-script effects from the packages' own data, policy, finalize, stamp, check) with one readiness rule. The RPM and APK backends supply only unpacking and script effects. One rootfs-tree module owns Recipe's target metadata, copying and EROFS pack/unpack. The rootfs source is one spec with one option set for preseed and materialize. Every product recipe stamps a digest of its inputs and recipe files beside (not inside) its output.
- **Done when:** `fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai` and `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai` share the lifecycle code; one completeness rule (from G07); the Alpine freshness marker covers package and pin changes.
- **Depends on:** G04, G07, G08
- **Merges area designs:** RG-04 (builder-recipes-guest), BC-14 (builder-core), CL-10 (builder-cli), RG-07 (builder-recipes-guest) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [special] BC3151 `distro/builder/src/artifact/rootfs.rs:114-115` writes the whole rootfs as an uncompressed tar temp file in the output directory (`output.parent().unwrap_or(Path::new("."))`) before `mkfs.erofs --tar=f` reads it, roughly doubling peak disk use there. `:121` `-T0` pins every timestamp, and no comment says who owns that reproducibility choice.
- [special] BC3154 `distro/builder/src/artifact/rootfs.rs:104-107` and `:130` print progress to stdout from a library function, and `:135-153` `format_size_human` is a private size formatter beside the other ad-hoc byte formatters in the crate (`alpine/firmware.rs:44,75`, `artifact/disk/mod.rs:99,112,150`).
- [special] BR029 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:115-116` runs `chmod -R u+rwX ` + rootfs and `rm -rf ` + rootfs with the path unquoted, while every other shell call in the file quotes paths.
- [special] CL3087 `distro/builder/src/recipe/rootfs_source.rs:114-126` the preseed passes no defines, no inputs and `storage_max_objects = None`, while materialisation of the same source passes `spec.defines`, `spec.inputs` and `spec.storage_max_objects` (`:46-67`). The two rootfs-source phases can express different sets of Recipe options.
- [special] RG1031 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:148-165`: six `if !exists(<a>/X) && exists(<b>/X)` links (agetty, modprobe, login, sfdisk, lspci, lsusb) can never fire. `:129-131` makes `usr/sbin` a link to `bin`, so both sides name the same file, and the locked payloads already ship all six binaries in `/usr/bin` (checked in the cached RPMs: util-linux, util-linux-core, kmod, pciutils, usbutils).
- [special] RG1033 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:145-147`: `usr/bin/sh -> bash` is created only if it is missing. The locked `bash` RPM's payload already contains `/usr/bin/sh`, so the branch never runs.
- [special] RG1044 `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:72-92`: `busybox_applets` is an Alpine-only step in the shared installed-system module. `installed::openrc` does not call it, so the Alpine producer calls it separately (`alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:254`) before its own completeness step and before `installed::openrc` (`:265`). The producer has to know to call both, and in that order.
- [special] RG2018 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:76-88` `_resolve_apk_static` tries three places for `apk.static` (`sbin/`, `usr/sbin/`, the root) inside one sha256-pinned `apk-tools-static` package whose layout is fixed. At least two of the three branches cannot match the pinned payload.
- [special] RG2030 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:202-221` copies PAM `system-auth` only if `usr/share/factory/etc/pam.d/system-auth` exists, and writes `password-auth`/`postlogin` only if they are missing. That is degrade-and-continue: `is_built` checks none of the three, so the shipped PAM stack is whichever branch happened to run.
- [special] RG2165 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:253,266` calls `target_finalize(rootfs)` twice, once before policy ("readable before applying policy", `:252`) and once after. The Fedora producer calls it once, after policy (`fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:278`). The two producers follow different protocols for the same Recipe target helper.
- [special] RG2169 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:127-131` links `usr/sbin -> bin` only `if !exists(usr/sbin)`, which relies on the `filesystem` RPM listing `/usr/sbin` as a ghost (per the comment). If a lock ships it as a real directory, the merged-sbin layout that later lines assume (`:148-165`) is silently not made.
- [hidden] BC1092 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/plan.rs:93-115` `CopyTree` goes through `levitate_recipe::target::merge_tree`, while `CopySymlink` and `CopyFile` (`:116-200`) are hand-written with `fs::read_link`/`symlink`/`fs::copy`, so the three copy producers take different write paths although `products.rs:584` and `artifact/rootfs.rs:115` read Recipe's target metadata afterwards.
- [hidden] BC3072 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/io.rs:107-114` runs `fsck.erofs` with a raw `std::process::Command` (its own error context, no exit description) rather than the crate's `process::Cmd`, which `artifact/rootfs.rs:117` uses for the paired `mkfs.erofs`. Packing and unpacking one image format go through two spawn helpers.
- [hidden] BP073 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/io.rs:106-129` extracts EROFS with `--no-preserve-owner/perms` and then grants `u+rw(x)` on everything, relying on the comment "the embedded manifest is authoritative". So it depends on Recipe's target metadata surviving inside the image, and `artifact/rootfs.rs:112-125` must keep writing images through `levitate_recipe::target::write_tar`.
- [hidden] BR001 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:1` and `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:1` are two separate rootfs producers with the same lifecycle (verify inputs → unpack without scripts → patch what scriptlets would have done → finalize target metadata → write marker → validate). They share almost no code, so a policy fix lands in one and has to be copied by hand. Co-change history confirms it: `alpine-live-source-rootfs` changed together with its Fedora twin 6 times.
- [hidden] BR013 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:54` (`required_paths`, 45 entries) and `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:100` (`_rootfs_valid`, a chain of ifs) are two independent "is the rootfs complete" checks with different coverage. Fedora checks sshd, curl, ping, lspci and lsusb but not the systemd-boot EFI binary; Alpine checks the systemd-boot EFI binary and `mkfs.fat` but not sshd, curl or lspci. What "complete" means differs per distro, and nothing says so.
- [hidden] BR014 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:24` the freshness marker is `"alpine-source-v12-runlevels-" + iso_version`. It covers neither the package list (`:58`), the `extra_apks` pins (`sources/alpine.rhai:15`) nor the shared `installed-system.rhai`, so changing any of them does not rebuild the rootfs unless someone also bumps `v12`. Fedora's marker (`fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:16`) does include every NEVRA, so the two producers invalidate differently.
- [hidden] BR015 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:17` is versioned by hand as `"fedora-rpm-set-v3-target-metadata"`, and neither marker covers `installed-system.rhai` or the `system_setup` input. An edit to shared installed policy only reaches a cached rootfs if someone remembers to bump a string in each producer.
- [hidden] BR016 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:221` `build()` returns early when the rootfs is already valid. `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:115` `build()` always wipes and rebuilds. The two producers place the readiness decision differently.
- [hidden] BR021 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:129-165` re-creates by hand the merged-/usr and sbin→bin links (`agetty`, `modprobe`, `login`, `sfdisk`, `lspci`, `lsusb`) that the Fedora packages' scriptlets would make. Each entry is tied to a specific package's file layout, so a package moving a binary breaks it silently (the `if exists` guards skip it).
- [hidden] BR022 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:185-192` hard-codes the `sshd` (74) and `dbus` (81) passwd/group/shadow entries from Fedora's scriptlets, a copy of upstream package data. The AcornOS producer relies on `alpine-baselayout`'s files instead.
- [hidden] BR023 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:196-221` writes the PAM stack (`password-auth`, `postlogin`, `system-auth` copied from `usr/share/factory`) that `authselect` would generate. It is a hand copy of Fedora defaults, with no AcornOS counterpart (Alpine uses no PAM stack).
- [hidden] BR030 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:274` and `:275` delete and re-create `var/log`, then add `var/log/audit` 0700 because `auditd` needs it, and `:94` requires that path. The live overlay separately mounts a tmpfs on `/var/log` for AcornOS only (`live-overlay.rhai:35`).
- [hidden] BR031 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:279` and `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:267` write `.source-rootfs.recipe-version` into the root of the rootfs tree, which becomes the shipped product. Nothing in this area strips it, so a build bookkeeping file rides along into the image.
- [hidden] BR053 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:113` builds the overlay's `/etc/shadow` from a snapshot of the rootfs's shadow (`/inputs/shadow`). Any account the rootfs adds later is only in the live session if the overlay is rebuilt from the new rootfs.
- [hidden] BR054 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:92-97` `is_built` is always ready and `is_installed` checks only the `.recipe-live-overlay` marker, never the config, assets or shadow. Whether a config change re-runs `install` is left to the engine's input handling, which this recipe does not show.
- [hidden] BR093 `distro/builder/src/recipe/rootfs_source.rs:163` the preseed writes `rootfs-source-inputs.json` into the shared `recipe_work_dir`, and `:174` `preseed_inputs` reads it back during materialization. The preseed → materialize handoff is a file in a shared directory, keyed only by the recipe path.
- [hidden] BR105 `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:55-60` `is_built` only checks that a binary exists and that `--version` starts with the tool name. A source change that keeps the version does not count as stale inside the recipe, so freshness is left to the engine.
- [hidden] CL1093 `distro/builder/src/recipe/rootfs_source.rs:17-23` `resolve_recipe_path` is a copy of `distro/builder/src/pipeline/paths.rs:4-11` `resolve_repo_path`.
- [hidden] CL1095 `distro/builder/src/recipe/rootfs_source.rs:128-140` checks preseed output kinds through `outputs::file`/`outputs::directory`, while `preseed_inputs` (`:185-199`) repeats the File/Directory check with `is_file`/`is_dir` and different messages.
- [hidden] CL1096 `distro/builder/src/recipe/rootfs_source.rs:105` runs the preseed in `recipe_work_dir` (`.artifacts/work/<distro>/downloads`). Materialization of the same source runs in `.artifacts/work/<distro>/rootfs-source-provider/<recipe stem or "recipe">`, chosen by `pipeline/source.rs:135-161`. One source's two halves get their work dirs from two modules.
- [hidden] P013 `fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:166-221` and `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:154-166` each re-create what package scripts would have done (sshd/dbus accounts and PAM stack vs doas.conf, empty.sshd, locale.conf, interfaces, dhcpcd conf), in two recipes with no shared list of "scriptlet effects the system needs".
- [hidden] RG1021 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:537-539`: whether a parent product was "built with the current recipes" is decided by whether one file, `usr/libexec/recipe-system-setup` (written at `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:16`), exists. Any other recipe change leaves an old rootfs passing this check.
- [hidden] RG1023 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:61-89`: the required-path list names individual shared libraries by soname (`libbpf.so.1`, `libkrb5.so.3`, `libbrotlidec.so.1`, `libevent-2.1.so.7`, `libsasl2.so.3`, `libexpat.so.1`, ...). These copy the lock's library rows. A soname bump after `cargo xtask base lock` makes `is_built` fail here, and nothing links the two.
- [hidden] RG1025 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:118-121` and `:260-263`: the same `[ -L sh ] || [ -f sh ]` shell check appears twice in one file, and so does the `bin/busybox` check (`:105` and `:257`).
- [hidden] RG1036 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:178-179`: the comment is two half-sentences joined together ("Ensure OpenSSH / Privilege-separation account entries must exist") and says the accounts are for "live boot". The same rootfs is also the installed system (`installed-system.rhai:1-2`).
- [hidden] RG1051 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:1,7-8,154,241`: the recipe name, its `description` ("Alpine live rootfs source producer"), `_ensure_openrc_live_source_completeness` and the "live source package baseline" log all say "live". Its output is the installed rootfs, which it finishes with `installed::openrc` (`:265`) and which recstrap deploys (`installed-system.rhai:1-2`).
- [hidden] RG1078 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:26,31,43-44,50` and `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:554-557`: the leak check looks for the recipe's getty argument shapes (`-n -l`, `--autologin`, `serial-autologin`) in an installed inittab. If the recipe's getty arguments change, the check silently stops detecting a leak.
- [hidden] RG2012 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:115,129` `_rootfs_valid` checks the exact literals `permit persist :wheel` and `LANG=C.UTF-8`, which `_ensure_openrc_live_source_completeness` (`:157,161`) writes in the same file. Each policy text is typed twice, and an edit to one makes every cached rootfs look invalid, or a stale one look valid.
- [hidden] RG2032 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:56,65` the producer's required paths include `usr/sbin/sshd` and `usr/lib/systemd/system/sshd.service`. `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:622-645` requires the same files (and `var/empty/sshd`) in the same rootfs. The producer and the contract each keep their own list of required SSH files.
- [hidden] RG2081 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:124` writes the readiness marker `.recipe-live-overlay` into the overlay tree, and the tree becomes the live ISO's top layer. A built `overlayfs.erofs` (`.artifacts/out/acorn/releases/live-tools/*/overlayfs.erofs`) has `/.recipe-live-overlay` at its root, so a recipe-internal state file ships in the product.
- [hidden] RG3027 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:59`, `:92-93` and `:99` make `etc/sudoers.d/10-wheel`, `etc/systemd/system/dbus.service`, `etc/systemd/system/sockets.target.wants/dbus.socket` and `usr/local/bin/gnu-smoke` required paths, and this same file's `build()` writes every one of them (`:169-176`, `:270`). The readiness list retypes its own writer's paths, so a rename in `build()` makes every cached rootfs look incomplete (the same pattern as RG2012 on the Alpine side).
- [needless] BR095 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:584`, `src/pipeline/live_tools.rs:41`, `src/pipeline/plan.rs:114`, `src/pipeline/overlay.rs:62` and `src/artifact/rootfs.rs:115` use `levitate_recipe::target::*` in-process to read and merge the ownership/mode metadata that recipes record with `target_mode`/`target_finalize`/`target_capture_apks`. Every consumer of a recipe-made tree depends on the Recipe engine's internal metadata format, not on a plain filesystem tree.
- [needless] BR135 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/artifacts.rs:332-357` `preseed_rootfs_source_cmd` (a ring3 source acquisition) gets its recipe and outputs by loading the live-boot product spec (`canonical_live_boot_product_spec`, `:374`), which requires the ring2 `extends` edge and parent layout (`:381`). Preseeding the base rootfs depends on the live-boot product's configuration.
- [surplus] BC1067 `distro/builder/src/artifact/rootfs.rs:71` `create_erofs` takes compression, level and chunk-size parameters, but its only callers (`build_erofs_default` at `:174` and `overlayfs.rs:19`) always pass the same `EROFS_*` constants (`:156-158`).
- [surplus] BP129 `distro/builder/src/artifact/overlayfs.rs:12-26` `create_overlayfs_erofs` forwards all arguments to `create_erofs`, and `build_overlayfs_default` (`:29-39`) repeats `build_erofs_default` (`artifact/rootfs.rs:173-181`) with the same constants.
- [surplus] BP130 `distro/builder/src/artifact/filesystem.rs:35-40` `copy_dir_recursive` wraps `leviso_elf::copy_dir_recursive_overwrite`, dropping the count. `leviso-elf` is a dependency kept for this one call.
- [surplus] BR018 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:9-19` ctx fields `alpine_version`, `apk_tools_version`, `iso_name` and `apk_tools_name` are never read (`ctx.<field>` occurs 0 times).
- [surplus] BR027 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:30` `_extract_rpm_into_rootfs` only forwards to `target_extract_rpm`.
- [surplus] BR028 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:134-144` runs eleven `mkdir -p` shell calls for directories the RPMs already create (and `usr/sbin` right after it was made a symlink at `:130`). `:224` repeats four of them.
- [surplus] BR092 `distro/builder/src/recipe/rootfs_source.rs:11` `RootfsSourceRecipeSpec.defines` (and `RootfsSourcePolicy.defines` in `src/pipeline/source.rs:50`) is plumbed through for a TOML field that no variant sets and that the contract forbids for `recipe_rpm_set` (`distro/contract/src/validate.rs:1008`).
- [surplus] RG1026 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:283-297` and `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:272-290`: `is_installed` and `install` only re-run the readiness check (`is_built` / `_rootfs_valid`) and set `outputs`. They pass the readiness check straight through.

### G13 One installed-system policy and one live-system policy, each over a small init backend

Solves **112** problems (16 parity, 37 special, 46 hidden, 6 needless, 7 surplus).

- **Design:** Each system feature (consoles and autologin, network bring-up, shutdown cleanup, sysrq, machine-id, volatile logs, services, locale, keymap, user creation, hostname) is stated once; a small systemd/OpenRC backend supplies only the on-disk mechanism. The live session is one typed config built from product configuration (not from scenario expectations); the overlay recipe owns everything it writes, including the live-only check. All guest templates go through one strict renderer; `guest/` is delivered one way; test-harness hooks (serial test profile, probe, smoke) leave the shipped product and are switched on by boot injection. The builder checks the trees it produces by backend at build time. System identity (`os-release`) is written once from the contract by the system recipe.
- **Done when:** `live-overlay.rhai` and `installed-system.rhai` have no per-init feature lists, only backend calls; no OpenRC service whitelist; `system-setup.sh` probes nothing; one `@NAME@` renderer; both live sessions have the same features (Z001–Z011 gone).
- **Depends on:** G04, G07, G12
- **Decisions:** **User-visible.** Live: autologin on tty1–6 on both, sysrq on both, quiet boot on both (drop `inst.sshd=0` and `selinux=0`). Shared live services: ssh + network (+ audit per G07).
- **Merges area designs:** RG-02 (builder-recipes-guest), RG-03 (builder-recipes-guest), BC-07 (builder-core), BC-08 (builder-core), BC-09 (builder-core), RG-08 (builder-recipes-guest), RG-09 (builder-recipes-guest), PA-05 (parity-all), CV-13 (contract-variants) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] BP068 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:544-562` That guard checks the systemd live paths (getty drop-ins) and the OpenRC `inittab` autologin, but not the other OpenRC live-only files the recipe writes (`etc/local.d/01-efivarfs.start`, `etc/network/interfaces`, `etc/sysctl.d/50-live-sysrq.conf`, the tmpfs `/var/log` `etc/fstab`), nor the systemd `live-net-setup`/`live-shutdown-cleanup` units. Each init system is protected differently.
- [parity] BR045 `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:104` gives AcornOS `etc/profile.d/locale.sh` to export `LANG`. LevitateOS relies on systemd reading `locale.conf`. The locale works through different mechanisms, and only the OpenRC branch has a shell hook.
- [parity] BR047 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:23` (OpenRC live) vs `:41` (systemd live): the systemd live session gets `live-net-setup` (`:57`) and `live-shutdown-cleanup` (`:62`). The OpenRC live session gets neither; instead it gets `efivarfs` mounting (`:36`), `sysrq` (`:37`), a `/var/log` tmpfs (`:35`) and a QEMU-only `eth0` DHCP stanza (`:39`). The live sessions differ in networking, shutdown cleanup, sysrq and log storage.
- [parity] C048 `runtime.rs:608-616` vs `runtime.rs:741-748`: only the systemd (LevitateOS) branch checks the merged-usr symlinks (`validate_live_boot_usrmerge_symlinks`), the `/run/sshd` tmpfiles policy (`runtime.rs:665-679`) and the sshd keygen unit. The OpenRC (AcornOS) branch has no merged-usr check.
- [parity] P004 `fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:54` checks `usr/bin/sudo` and `etc/sudoers.d/10-wheel` exist only, while `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:115` checks the doas rule's exact text; the privilege rule is validated more strictly on AcornOS.
- [parity] P018 `installed-system.rhai:62-65` enables NetworkManager on LevitateOS, `:110-113` enables `networking` + `dhcpcd` on AcornOS: different network stacks and user-facing tools (`nmcli` vs `/etc/network/interfaces`, `dhcpcd`) on the installed systems.
- [parity] P019 `installed-system.rhai:97-101` always spawns a getty on ttyS0 on installed AcornOS; installed LevitateOS gets a serial getty only when the user passes `console=ttyS0` to `recsetup --options` (systemd's generator), so serial access after install differs by default.
- [parity] P025 `live-overlay.rhai:45` puts `zz-autologin.conf` on the `getty@.service` template, so every LevitateOS VT autologins root; `:26-27` gives AcornOS's tty2-6 a plain login prompt (root with empty password). Switching VTs on the live ISO behaves differently.
- [parity] P027 `live-overlay.rhai:62-66` installs `live-shutdown-cleanup` (unmount live loops before umount.target) for LevitateOS only; AcornOS's live shutdown has no equivalent.
- [parity] PA1106 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/installed-boot.sh:36` the "consoles authenticate" check fails on any autologin in AcornOS's `/etc/inittab`, but on LevitateOS only checks the template drop-in `getty@.service.d/zz-autologin.conf`, not the per-console drop-ins `live-overlay.rhai:51` writes (`getty@tty1.service.d/`, `serial-getty@ttyS0.service.d/`). `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:545-546` has the same gap (template drop-ins only). The leak check is stronger on AcornOS.
- [parity] PA3065 `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:36` and `distro/builder/src/pipeline/overlay.rs:26` write one live `/etc/issue` hint, "Login as 'root' (no password)", for both siblings. On LevitateOS every VT autologins root (`distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:43-45`, template `getty@.service.d/zz-autologin.conf`), so the hint describes a login that never happens. On AcornOS the non-install VTs (`live-overlay.rhai:25-27`) really show a login prompt. The shared text matches one sibling's console behaviour only, and `testing/install-tests/src/qemu/serial/mod.rs:71` also uses it as a readiness marker.
- [parity] Z001 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:57-61` LevitateOS's live system brings up every NIC through NetworkManager (`live-net-setup.sh`, 45 s retry loop); AcornOS's `:39` writes a static `/etc/network/interfaces` with only `eth0` dhcp ("QEMU-specific"), so an AcornOS live boot on hardware whose NIC isn't named `eth0` gets no network.
- [parity] Z007 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:36-37` sysrq is enabled and efivarfs mounted by a `local.d` script only on AcornOS live; `:67-68` an empty `machine-id` and `/run/sshd` tmpfiles only on LevitateOS; `:35` a tmpfs `/var/log` only on AcornOS. Each is a live-system property decided per init system rather than once.
- [parity] Z008 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:105-108` install screen consoles: systemd accepts only `tty1`/`ttyS0`, OpenRC accepts `tty1`–`tty6`/`ttyS0`; `:25-27` OpenRC spawns six gettys, `:53` systemd links only `getty@tty1`.
- [parity] Z009 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:29-31` vs `:44-51` serial login: AcornOS's ttyS0 runs `serial-autologin` (prints the ready marker, `exec /bin/sh -l`), LevitateOS's runs `agetty --autologin root` and relies on the `99-serial-test.sh` profile; two mechanisms for one test protocol.
- [parity] Z011 `distro/builder/guest/live/shell-ux.sh:26-45` history, completion and the coloured prompt exist only under bash; AcornOS's live shell is ash (`distro/variants/acorn/scenarios/scenarios.toml:132` requires `ash`, levitate's `:145` `bash`), so it gets `[live \u@\h \W]` and no history settings.
- [special] BC1161 `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:101-104` without a hub the left pane is the literal `"$SHELL" -l`, substituted unquoted into `--left @LEFT@` and relying on shell word splitting, while the docs argv goes through `shell_words` (`:130`).
- [special] BC3103 `distro/builder/guest/install-ux/install-ux.sh:5,9,18,28` interpolates `@SHELL@` and `@SPLIT@` unquoted, while `@DOCS_ARGV@` is shell-quoted in Rust (`distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:111`, `shell_words`). Which placeholders arrive quoted is a convention that each value must know.
- [special] BC3157 `distro/builder/src/guest.rs:25-41` `render` recognises only `@[A-Z0-9_]+@`, so a lowercase or mixed-case placeholder (for example `@Shell@`) passes through to the guest as literal text. The module doc (`:19-21`) promises that "a misspelt name never reaches the guest as literal text".
- [special] BP067 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:536-565` `validate_installed_rootfs` hard-codes a list of live-only paths that mirrors what `recipes/live-overlay.rhai` writes (`serial-autologin`, `live-help`, `20-live-shell.sh`, `99-serial-test.sh`, the systemd getty drop-ins). It also lists two paths no producer writes any more (`usr/local/lib/scenario-tests`, `usr/local/bin/live-boot.sh`). A new live file in the recipe is not guarded unless someone also edits this list.
- [special] BP069 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:554` reads `etc/inittab` with `unwrap_or_default()`, so a read error passes the guard as if the file were empty.
- [special] BP070 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:537-538` requires `usr/libexec/recipe-system-setup`, a path also hard-coded in `recipes/installed-system.rhai:16`, `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:15` and `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/installed-boot.sh:32`, with no shared constant.
- [special] BP085 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/config.rs:82-87` The builder hard-requires `sshd` in the service list ("OpenSSH is first-class"). This is a policy for one service, inside generic config loading.
- [special] BP086 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/config.rs:218-227` lowercases service names. systemd unit names are case-sensitive, and the OpenRC branch allows only three names, so this rule fits neither init system.
- [special] BP100 `distro/builder/guest/live/efivarfs.start:6-7` swallows a failed efivarfs mkdir and mount (`2>/dev/null || true`), so an AcornOS live session whose efivarfs did not mount continues silently. Tools that need EFI variables find out later.
- [special] BP105 `distro/builder/guest/install-ux/install-ux.sh:17-18` Smoke mode always runs `"$SHELL" -l` on the left, not `@LEFT@`, and `install-split-pane/src/main.rs:351-360` replaces the right pane with a `command -v` probe plus `sleep 0.2`. The install-UX smoke test therefore exercises neither the hub nor the docs app it is meant to vouch for.
- [special] BR012 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:230` writes a `/usr/local/bin/gnu-smoke` script into the LevitateOS rootfs, and `:99` makes it a required path. Nothing in the repo runs it (the only reference is this recipe), and AcornOS has no counterpart. The rootfs ships a test helper that exists for one distro and nothing uses.
- [special] BR038 `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:36` `systemd(root)` and `:94` `openrc(root)` are two whole policy functions picked by name by each producer. Only `lock_root` and `setup_program` are shared. Every installed-system feature has to be written twice.
- [special] BR042 `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:47` falls back to `etc/ssh/sshd_config.anaconda` and then to a hand-written two-line config. Both branches are for the Fedora package layout inside the generically named `systemd()`.
- [special] BR043 `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:52` probes four locale locations (`lib`, `usr/lib`, `lib64`, `usr/lib64`) and `:57` adds a `/lib/locale` symlink for one layout. These are guards for package layouts the pinned Fedora set does not have.
- [special] C023 `runtime.rs:16-19` `LEGACY_ROOTFS_COMPONENT_SEQUENCES` names the old `leviso/downloads/rootfs` and `acornos/downloads/rootfs` paths. They are per-distro legacy repo names baked into the runtime checker (`runtime.rs:508-520`).
- [special] C050 `runtime.rs:784-864` runs the OpenRC networking/dhcpcd file checks only `if required_live_services contains "networking"` / `"dhcpcd"`. These are `if`s for one service name each, with hard-coded `/etc/init.d/<name>` and runlevel paths (`boot/networking`, `default/dhcpcd`). The systemd branch has no counterpart, so LevitateOS's live networking (NetworkManager) is never checked (see also C100).
- [special] CV1080 `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:199-213` records a violation when owner-path resolution fails with any error other than the empty-manifest case, then continues with `variant_dir` as the build-host root. The layout checks that follow run against the wrong root and add follow-on violations to the real one.
- [special] CV1105 `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:928` accepts only `usr/share/i18n/locales/musl/en_US.UTF-8` as the musl locale payload, while the same check (`:867-916`) requires `locale.conf` to say `LANG=C.UTF-8`. The payload file it looks for is not the locale the system is configured to use.
- [special] CV1112 `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:74-79` `read_trimmed` turns every read error into `None` (`.ok()`), so an unreadable `kernel.release` or rootfs pointer is reported as "missing or empty" and the real I/O error is lost.
- [special] P029 `live-overlay.rhai:85-86` rejects any OpenRC live service except `sshd`, `networking`, `dhcpcd` and hard-codes `networking` into the boot runlevel; systemd live services are unrestricted. A new live service works on LevitateOS and throws on AcornOS (`docs/desktop.md:92` already hits it).
- [special] P033 `guest/live/live-net-setup.sh:20-55` polls 45 times with `sleep 1` and needs three stable readings, because "virtio NIC names and NetworkManager startup can race"; AcornOS has no such wait, so the two live ISOs have different readiness semantics before sshd.
- [special] RG1065 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:38-39`: the AcornOS live overlay brings up `eth0` with ifupdown `inet dhcp`, and the same overlay also enables the `dhcpcd` daemon (from `required_services` in `distro/variants/acorn/scenarios/scenarios.toml:46-47`). So two DHCP mechanisms run for the same interface. Installed AcornOS (`alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:67-70`) instead lets dhcpcd discover interfaces on its own.
- [special] RG1072 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:54-56`: `serial-getty@ttyS0` is linked into both `basic.target.wants` and `getty.target.wants`.
- [special] RG1073 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:58-60`: `live-net-setup.service` has `DefaultDependencies=no` and is ordered only after `basic.target local-fs.target`. Its script drives `nmcli` (`distro/builder/guest/live/live-net-setup.sh:18,34`), but the unit has no `After=NetworkManager.service`, so the script's retry loop is what absorbs that missing ordering.
- [special] RG1080 `distro/builder/guest/live/serial-autologin.rootfs.sh:2-5`: the shell-ready marker is printed three times (stdout, `/dev/console`, `/dev/kmsg`) before `exec /bin/sh -l` even starts. The `99-serial-test.sh` profile (`tools/recqemu/guest/serial-test.sh:10-15`) prints it again only after turning echo off and clearing the prompt. On the AcornOS serial console, the host therefore sees "ready" before the shell is set up for the protocol.
- [special] RG1087 `distro/builder/guest/live/live-net-setup.sh:27-31`: a hand-written denylist of interface-name prefixes (`sit`, `tun`, `tap`, `docker`, `virbr`, `br`, `wg`, ...) decides which NICs get connected. NetworkManager has its own device management for the same decision.
- [special] RG1088 `distro/builder/guest/live/live-net-setup.sh:34`: `nmcli --wait 5 device connect ... >/dev/null 2>&1 || true` swallows every connection error.
- [special] RG1089 `distro/builder/guest/live/live-net-setup.sh:18`: `NMCLI_BIN="$(command -v nmcli)"` under `set -eu` exits the script at once if nmcli is missing. The `ip` lookup just above (`:4-13`) has three fallbacks, so the two required tools are handled in two different ways.
- [special] RG1094 `distro/builder/guest/live/live-shutdown-cleanup.sh:4,8,13,18,23,26`: `set +e`, `|| umount -l`, `|| true` and a final `exit 0` swallow every failure. The cleanup always reports success.
- [special] RG1097 `distro/builder/guest/install-ux/install-ux.sh:8-15`: a `--probe` mode prints `@PROBE@<path>` and exits 3. It exists only so tests can check that the install screen is wired up, and it ships in every live ISO.
- [special] RG1098 `distro/builder/guest/install-ux/install-ux.sh:17-19`: an `@SMOKE@` environment switch runs the split pane with `--smoke` for tests. A test-only path sits in the shipped launcher.
- [special] RG2053 `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh:110-111` `service` on OpenRC always enables into the `default` runlevel, while the recipe puts `networking` in `boot` (`installed-system.rhai:111`). A service enabled later through recsetup lands in a different runlevel from the same service enabled at build time.
- [special] RG2059 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:120` puts recqemu's serial test profile (`etc/profile.d/99-serial-test.sh`, `tools/recqemu/guest/serial-test.sh:11-15`: `stty -echo`, `PS1=''` on `/dev/ttyS0`) into every product live ISO. A real user's interactive shell on the serial console therefore has no echo and no prompt: the test-harness mode ships in the product.
- [special] RG2070 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:63-65` `live-shutdown-cleanup.service` is ordered `Before=run-live-media.mount`, a unit name typed by hand from recinit's `LIVE_MEDIA_DIR` (`/run/live-media`, `tools/recinit/src/live.rs:22,56`). The unit also does its real work in `ExecStop` behind `ExecStart=/bin/true` + `RemainAfterExit`. If recinit's mount path changes, the ordering silently stops applying.
- [special] RG2080 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:119-120` installs `20-live-shell.sh` as 0755 and `99-serial-test.sh` as 0644 into the same `profile.d`. The mode of each sourced profile fragment is chosen by hand, file by file.
- [special] Z003 `distro/builder/guest/live/live-net-setup.sh:4-13` probes `command -v ip`, then `/usr/sbin/ip`, then `/usr/bin/ip`, although it only ever runs on LevitateOS where the path is known.
- [special] Z006 `distro/builder/guest/live/live-shutdown-cleanup.sh:22-24` unmounts `/media/cdrom` (an Alpine convention) in a script only LevitateOS installs.
- [hidden] BC1082 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:555-557` detects leaked live autologin in `etc/inittab` by matching `serial-autologin`, `-n -l` and `--autologin`, the exact strings `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:26-30` emits for OpenRC; changing the recipe's getty line silently disables the guard.
- [hidden] BC1087 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/plan.rs:46-49` writes `etc/os-release` with only `NAME`, `ID`, `PRETTY_NAME`, while the contract also carries `identity.os_version` (`distro/variants/*/identity/identity.toml:7`), which reaches only the release hook as `IDENTITY_OS_VERSION` (`bin/workflows/release_hook.rs:95`, `_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:55`) and never os-release.
- [hidden] BC2041 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:570-576` `apply_system_payload` requires the parent product to already contain a regular `etc/os-release`. That holds only because `pipeline/plan.rs:60-64` wrote it in base-rootfs and nothing in between replaced it with a symlink, an ordering contract between products that nothing states.
- [hidden] BP065 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:567-582` Identity comes from base-rootfs's three-line `os-release` written by Rust (`pipeline/plan.rs:46-49`: `NAME`, `ID`, `PRETTY_NAME` only, built with `format!` and no quoting or escaping). It overwrites whatever fuller `os-release` the vendor tree had. Both distros ship this minimal identity, and the format is a Rust string.
- [hidden] BP066 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/plan.rs:50-53` hand-formats `usr/lib/product-manifest.json` with `format!` (no JSON escaping) and a `"product": "base-rootfs"` literal that copies `planner.rs:9`.
- [hidden] BP090 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/overlay.rs:22-35` The JSON config handed to `recipes/live-overlay.rhai` (`os_name`, `issue`, `init`, `consoles`, `services`, `launcher`, `shell_ready_marker`, `role_file`, `role`) has no schema. The recipe reads the keys by name (`live-overlay.rhai:3,103-122`), so a rename on either side breaks only at run time.
- [hidden] BP091 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/overlay.rs:38-40` hard-codes the repo paths `distro/builder/guest` and `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai`, and the recipe expects `/inputs/assets/live/<name>` (`live-overlay.rhai:16`). The guest asset layout is a convention three places share.
- [hidden] BP092 `distro/builder/src/guest.rs:9-45` renders `@NAME@` placeholders strictly for one script (`INSTALL_UX`). `recipes/live-overlay.rhai:15-22` has its own two-placeholder renderer for `guest/live/*`, and `recipes/installed-system.rhai:10-16` a third for `guest/installed/system-setup.sh`. That is three renderers with different strictness for the same `guest/` templates.
- [hidden] BP093 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/overlay.rs:26` builds `/etc/issue` text in Rust (`"\n{os_name} {label} - \\l\n\n{hint}\n\n"`), the label coming from the binary's product table (`bin/workflows/parse.rs` `issue_banner_label`), while `recipes/installed-system.rhai:37` writes the installed `etc/issue` in the recipe. The same file is produced by two layers.
- [hidden] BP098 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:113-117` (live) and `recipes/installed-system.rhai:19-34` (installed) each rewrite the `root:` line of `etc/shadow` with their own copy of `20000:0:99999:7:::`. `pipeline/overlay.rs:39` passes the rootfs shadow in for this.
- [hidden] BR024 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:172` writes the privilege policy (`sudoers.d/10-wheel`), and `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:155` writes `doas.conf`. That is the one intended user-facing difference, yet each lives inside its base-specific producer instead of in shared installed policy (`installed-system.rhai`), and the two are written and validated (`:115`) differently.
- [hidden] BR025 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:159` creates `usr/share/empty.sshd` and `:161` `etc/locale.conf` for AcornOS, while for LevitateOS the same two things are done in `installed-system.rhai:41` and `:60`. The same installed-policy fact lives in different files per distro.
- [hidden] BR026 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:162-165` writes the installed network config (`etc/network/interfaces`, `etc/conf.d/dhcpcd`), while the services that use it are enabled in `installed-system.rhai:111,113` (`networking`, `dhcpcd`). AcornOS's network setup is split across two recipes. LevitateOS's NetworkManager enablement sits entirely in `installed-system.rhai:62`.
- [hidden] BR039 `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:96-101` (installed inittab) and `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:24-32` (live inittab) build the same BusyBox/OpenRC inittab string twice: the same sysinit/boot/default/ctrlaltdel/shutdown lines and tty1-6 + ttyS0 getty loop. Only the getty flags differ (the live copy adds the `linux` term and `-n -l <launcher>`). A change to one is not mirrored in the other.
- [hidden] BR040 `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:3` `put()` sets modes through `target_mode` (recorded in Recipe target metadata), while `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:7` `put()` uses plain `chmod`. Two helpers with the same name use two different file-mode models, and which one is right depends on whether the consumer reads the tree through `levitate_recipe::target`.
- [hidden] BR041 `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:11` reads `/inputs/system_setup`. That input is declared only in `distro/variants/*/ring3/sources.toml:5` (`inputs = { system_setup = ... }`) for whichever producer imports this module. A shared library function depends on an input that its caller's TOML has to provide.
- [hidden] BR044 `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:37` and `:103` write the same `etc/issue` in both init branches.
- [hidden] BR052 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:16` reads `/inputs/assets/live/<name>` and replaces `@OS_NAME@` and `@SHELL_READY_MARKER@`. The asset directory layout (`distro/builder/guest/live/`) and placeholder names are a convention shared with `src/pipeline/overlay.rs:38`, which passes the whole `guest` dir.
- [hidden] BR055 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:122` and `:80` branch on `c.init` for every feature, and `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:113` branches on `INIT_SYSTEM` for the recab service. Every recipe that touches services carries its own systemd/openrc switch.
- [hidden] C047 `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:1104-1132` picks systemd vs OpenRC checks by sniffing `usr/lib/systemd/system/sshd.service` / `etc/init.d/sshd` in the rootfs. The contract already declares this as `product_config.live_overlay.kind` (`ring2/products.toml:22`), and the runtime check ignores it.
- [hidden] C049 `runtime.rs:867-942` (`validate_openrc_live_boot_locale_completeness`) and `runtime.rs:966-1040` (`validate_systemd_live_boot_locale_completeness`) are two near-identical 75-line functions. They differ only in that the OpenRC one also accepts a musl locale path.
- [hidden] CV1092 `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:460-472` `has_file`/`has_directory`/`has_symlink` re-implement the three arms of `distro/contract/src/fs_layout.rs:156-164` `entry_matches_kind` in the same crate.
- [hidden] CV2057 `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:650-651` hard-codes `etc/systemd/system/multi-user.target.wants/sshd.service` and `:665-666` the tmpfiles names `usr/lib/tmpfiles.d/sshd.conf`/`etc/tmpfiles.d/sshd-local.conf`, file names the overlay recipe owns (`distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:67`, `:83`); `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/live-boot.sh:44` repeats the tmpfiles pair a third time.
- [hidden] P024 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:23` `openrc()` and `:41` `systemd()` are two parallel live-session implementations (getty/autologin, network, shutdown, sysctl, fstab, tmpfiles); each feature is present in one and absent in the other (P025-P029).
- [hidden] P030 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/config.rs:49-55` takes the live overlay's enabled services from the scenario contract `scenarios.live_environment.required_services`; what the live ISO enables is defined by a test expectation, so the scenario can never catch a missing service, and the lists differ per distro (`sshd auditd` vs `networking dhcpcd sshd`) while LevitateOS's network service comes from `installed-system.rhai:64` instead.

Each place knows each init's on-disk layout.

- [hidden] RG1069 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:112-117`: the live `shadow` copies `/inputs/shadow` and rewrites the `root:` line. If no `root:` line exists, the live system silently gets no root entry, while `installed-system.rhai:lock_root` throws for the same condition (`:31`).

- [hidden] RG1082 `distro/builder/guest/live/serial-autologin.rootfs.sh`: the `.rootfs.` in the file name does not match where the file goes. Only the live overlay installs it, and only on OpenRC (`distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:34`).

The init template parses the same file with `sed` (`tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:351-354`). Only the path is shared (`recinit::live::BOOT_INJECTION_ENV`), not the format.
- [hidden] RG2054 `distro/builder/guest/installed/system-setup.sh:2-3` the header says that recsetup validates choices and that no compiler image or build runtime is needed on the live ISO. Both are facts owned by recsetup and by the live product, restated in a comment.
- [hidden] RG2085 `distro/builder/guest/live/shell-ux.sh:41,44` the plain prompt literal `'[live \u@\h \W]\$ '` is written twice in one file.

Next to it, `/etc/issue` carries `LIVE_LOGIN_HINT` (`distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:36`, written by `pipeline/overlay.rs:26`) and the install screen shows its own key line. That is three user-facing hints, owned by three layers.
- [hidden] RG2089 `distro/builder/guest/live/live-help.sh:9-10` says "Leaving the login shell (exit or Ctrl-D) brings the install screen back". This restates the getty respawn behaviour that `live-overlay.rhai` and `guest/install-ux/install-ux.sh:2-4` own, and that comment says the same again. On a serial console that is not in `consoles`, exiting returns to serial-autologin or agetty autologin instead, so the help text is wrong there.
- [hidden] RG2168 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:172-176` writes `etc/sudoers.d/10-wheel` (`%wheel ALL=(ALL:ALL) ALL`), a second copy of the wheel rule that Fedora's `sudo` package already enables in `/etc/sudoers`. The readiness list (`:59`) requires the drop-in, so the recipe owns a duplicate of package policy.
- [hidden] RG3007 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:271` (`shell("chmod 0755 ...gnu-smoke")`) and `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:160` (`shell("chmod 755 ...usr/share/empty.sshd")`) set modes with a shell `chmod`, while the same functions set other modes through `target_mode` a few lines away (`fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:175`, `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:158`). Only the `target_mode` ones are recorded in Recipe target metadata, so which files keep their intended mode depends on which helper each line happened to use.
- [hidden] RG3010 `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:188` and `:192` spell the shadow aging fields `20000:0:99999:7:::` again for the `sshd` and `dbus` accounts, beside the root-line copies in `installed-system.rhai:27` and `live-overlay.rhai:114`. The `lastchg` value 20000 is a magic number in four places with no name.
- [hidden] RG3045 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:57-61` and `:62-66` give `live-net-setup.service` and `live-shutdown-cleanup.service` an `[Install] WantedBy=multi-user.target` section and also hand-link `multi-user.target.wants/<unit>`. The enablement is stated twice for each unit, and only the hand-made link has any effect.
- [hidden] U120 `distro/builder/guest/live/live-help.sh:5-7` lists `install-docs` and `recpart / recstrap / recfstab / recchroot` as the live ISO's commands, but not `install-hub`, `disk-plan` or `recsetup`, which both install guides and the hub now use; it is one text for both distros, copied from an older install flow.
- [hidden] Z005 `distro/builder/guest/live/live-shutdown-cleanup.sh:6,16` hard-codes `/live-overlay /rootfs /run/live-media` and `/dev/loop1 /dev/loop0`, which recinit's initramfs owns (`tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:144` creates `/rootfs` and `/live-overlay`, `:202` sets up `/dev/loop0`, `tools/recinit/src/live.rs:22` names `/run/live-media`). If recinit's mount layout changes, this script silently stops cleaning up.
- [hidden] Z013 `distro/builder/guest/live/efivarfs.start:3` says efivarfs is "needed for efibootmgr, bootctl"; neither tool is used on AcornOS (recab writes EFI variables itself).
- [needless] BP059 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:297-308,409-420` call `create_live_overlay` with ten positional arguments (`pipeline/overlay.rs:9-20`), two of them `&str` labels that are easy to swap. Live-boot passes `&[]` for consoles, so it differs from live-tools only by an empty slice nothing names.
- [needless] BP064 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:569-587` `apply_system_payload` reaches into `levitate_recipe::target` metadata (`load`, `entries.remove("etc/os-release")`, `save`) to undo Recipe's content binding. When Recipe's metadata format changes, this caller breaks.
- [needless] BP094 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/overlay.rs:12,41` takes `distro_id` only to choose a work dir, and `:30` always passes `launcher` even for live-boot (consoles empty). The recipe then branches on whether a console is listed.
- [needless] C056 `runtime.rs:42-48` `LiveBootRuntimeArtifacts.{live_overlay_dir,rootfs_source_pointer}` make callers rebuild builder-internal names. `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:321-325` hard-codes `"live-overlay"`, `".live-rootfs-source.path"` and `"initramfs-installed.img"`, copies of `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/parse.rs:132-133` and `_shared/.../build-release.sh:44`.
- [needless] CV1113 `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs` (the build-runtime and live-boot rootfs checks: SSH, locale, services) is called only from `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:121,134`. The builder that produces those artifacts never calls `validate_build_runtime_with_artifacts` or `validate_live_boot_runtime`, so a broken payload is caught only at test time, in another crate.
- [needless] Z025 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/overlay.rs:31` imports `recqemu::serial::protocol::SHELL_READY_MARKER`: the image builder depends on the VM test harness crate to know a string it bakes into the ISO; `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:269` reads the same constant.
- [surplus] BC1088 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/plan.rs:50-53` writes `usr/lib/product-manifest.json` into every base rootfs, and nothing in the repo reads it.
- [surplus] C055 `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:161-187,407-458,1141-1151` and `lib.rs:19-25`: `validate_build_runtime`, `validate_build_runtime_with_split_artifact_roots`, `require_valid_build_runtime`, `require_valid_build_runtime_with_artifacts`, `require_valid_build_runtime_with_split_artifact_roots` and `require_valid_live_boot_runtime` have no callers. Only `validate_build_runtime_with_artifacts` and `validate_live_boot_runtime` are used, by `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:121,134`. The other six are pass-through wrappers.
- [surplus] CV1075 `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:128-130` `live_boot_scenario` only returns `&contract.scenarios.live_boot` and has one caller (`:1048`).
- [surplus] RG1083 `distro/builder/guest/live/efivarfs.start` / `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:36`: the script is installed in `/etc/local.d`, but nothing enables OpenRC's `local` service. It is not in `installed-system.rhai:109-114` or in the overlay's allowed services (`live-overlay.rhai:85`), and a repo-wide search finds no `local` runlevel link. APK also runs with `--no-scripts` (`alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:245`), so no package creates the link either. The script most likely never runs.
- [surplus] RG1090 `distro/builder/guest/live/live-shutdown-cleanup.sh:6-10`: `/live-overlay` and `/rootfs` are mount points inside the initramfs (`tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:144,237,243`). The init moves only `/dev`, `/proc`, `/sys` and the boot medium into the live root (`:371-379`), so the unmount loop never finds those two paths.
- [surplus] RG2074 `distro/builder/guest/live/efivarfs.start:5-8` (installed as `etc/local.d/01-efivarfs.start`, `live-overlay.rhai:36`) mounts efivarfs. OpenRC's own `sysfs` service already mounts it under the same condition (`/etc/init.d/sysfs:109-115` in the built AcornOS rootfs).
- [surplus] RG3037 `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:29-30,34` always writes `/usr/local/bin/serial-autologin` on OpenRC and uses it only when `ttyS0` is not an install console. Both shipped variants list `consoles = ["tty1", "ttyS0"]` (`distro/variants/levitate/ring2/products.toml:7`, `distro/variants/acorn/ring2/products.toml:7`), so the branch never runs and the script is shipped unused. `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:541,555` and `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/installed-boot.sh:36-37` still guard against it.

### G14 Every product is the installable system rootfs plus layers

Solves **39** problems (9 special, 20 hidden, 4 needless, 6 surplus).

- **Design:** There is one installable system rootfs; base-rootfs, live-boot, live-tools and installed-boot are it plus layers, prepared by one library routine per product and recorded in one typed manifest. What recstrap installs is what installed-boot boots.
- **Done when:** base-rootfs is no longer a 4 KB empty image; live-boot and installed-boot prepare share one routine; installed-boot boots exactly the install result.
- **Depends on:** G05, G06, G12, G13
- **Merges area designs:** BC-05 (builder-core), CL-05 (builder-cli) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [special] BC1079 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:97` only `LiveBootProductSpec.overlay` is `pub` while `LiveToolsProductSpec.overlay` (`:136`) is private, and `:349` calls `.clone()` on the `Copy` enum `InitSystem`.
- [special] BC1107 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/io.rs:6-23` `create_unique_output_dir` is not unique: it deletes and recreates `<output>/<file_name>`, and falls back to the name `rootfs-source` when the logical name has none.
- [special] BC2059 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/source.rs:44,48` trims `recipe_script` and `preseed_recipe_script`, but `:53-56` does not trim the `inputs` paths, so the contract strings get two normalisation rules.
- [special] BC3077 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/plan.rs:185-189` a `CopyFile { optional: true }` producer skips a missing source with a bare `continue` and leaves no record, while a missing `CopyTree` source always fails (`:101`).
- [special] BP088 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/config.rs:57,91-99` The shared loader always loads the *live* producers, and the installed loader then overwrites them. Installed-boot works only because of that order.
- [special] CL1045 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/artifacts.rs:212-219` `prepare_product_inputs` checks that the planner step it was just handed names the requested product ("planner mismatch"). It guards against a plan its only caller (`:183-189`) built a few lines earlier.
- [special] CL2047 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/artifacts.rs:104-125` `build_prepared_product_erofs_cmd` checks the prepared overlay and rootfs dirs with `is_dir()` only. An empty prepared dir passes and produces an empty EROFS image.
- [special] CL3062 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/prepared_products.rs:121-123` `resolve_prepared_product_path` joins relative strings from the manifest onto the prepared dir without checking that the result stays inside it, while the writer requires each path to be under the output dir (`:144-153`). Reader and writer apply different path-safety rules to the same fields (`artifacts.rs:101-103,127-128`).
- [special] CL3152 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/commands.rs:102-104` `prepare-tool-source` refuses an existing output, while `product prepare` writes into whatever `output_dir` it is given (`artifacts.rs:189-193`; only the overlay subdir is wiped, `pipeline/io.rs:25-34`), and xtask deletes the prepared dir itself before calling it (`xtask/src/tasks/build.rs:98-101`). The overwrite policy differs per command, and for one command the caller enforces it.
- [hidden] BC1157 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:230`, `:347` and the installed-boot loader create every derived spec with `resolved_parent_rootfs_image: None`, and `prepare_*_product` fails (`:516-531`) unless the caller first chains `with_resolved_parent_rootfs_image` with the image the planner resolved; the order is a convention the type does not enforce.
- [hidden] BC1160 `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:11` justifies putting the install screen in the overlay with "the rootfs is what `recstrap` installs", a fact owned by `tools/recstrap` and the live-tools product layout.
- [hidden] BC2051 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/config.rs:147-173` `normalized_relative_path` re-implements the empty/absolute/`..` checks that `distro/contract/src/variant.rs:1227-1257` (`normalize_relative_string`, applied to the same producer fields at `:1022-1046`) already makes, with its own "Ring 2 payload producer field" wording.
- [hidden] BC2058 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/source.rs:15-25` `RootfsSourcePolicy` copies `RootfsSourceContract` field for field, and `source.rs:95-102` copies it again into `RootfsSourceRecipeSpec`, so a new source field needs three structs changed. `RootfsSourcePolicy.kind` (`:17`, filled at `:43`) is never read.
- [hidden] BC2061 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/io.rs:6-23`, `:25-42` and `:90-104` are three copies of "remove the dir if it exists, then create it".
- [hidden] BC2138 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:106-117` `rootfs_source_preseed` exposes the preseed recipe and outputs through the live-boot product spec, while `bin/workflows/artifacts.rs:347-348` reads `preseed_network_hosts`/`preseed_memory_mib` straight from the contract. `RootfsSourcePolicy` (`pipeline/source.rs:15-25`) copies the preseed recipe and outputs but not those two fields, so one preseed request is assembled from two sources.
- [hidden] BP053 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:25-47` defines four structs (`BaseRootfsProduct`, `LiveBootProduct`, `LiveToolsProduct`, `InstalledBootProduct`) with identical fields `{rootfs_source_dir, live_overlay_dir}`. The binary maps each into a fifth copy, `PreparedProductInputs` (`src/bin/workflows/artifacts.rs:236-302`).
- [hidden] BP054 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:119-122,141-144,160-163` `with_resolved_parent_rootfs_image` is written three times, once per derived spec.
- [hidden] BP055 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:242-314` (`prepare_live_boot_product`) and `:446-514` (`prepare_installed_boot_product`) are the same sequence line for line: resolve parent, create dir, clean legacy provider, extract EROFS, detect copy producers, materialize source, apply payload, validate. They differ only in the overlay at the end. A fix to one must be copied to the other.
- [hidden] BP056 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:274-281,478-485` and `distro/builder/src/pipeline/plan.rs:76-83` repeat the same `matches!(CopyTree|CopySymlink|CopyFile)` test three times to decide whether a source rootfs is needed. Adding a producer kind means finding all three.
- [hidden] BP060 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:409-420` live-tools does not inherit its parent's live overlay. It rebuilds the overlay from scratch, with the install consoles added, so "live-tools extends live-boot" holds for the rootfs only. An overlay change in live-boot reaches live-tools only because both call `recipes/live-overlay.rhai` with the same config apart from `consoles`.
- [hidden] BP061 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:408,422-429` live-tools runs `validate_installed_rootfs` before `add_required_tools`, which merges `rootfs_workspace_binary` actions (recab, recstrap) into the rootfs (`pipeline/live_tools.rs:25-30,41`). The "installable rootfs" check therefore never sees what is written afterwards, despite `pipeline/overlay.rs:1` "no live policy writes the rootfs".
- [hidden] BP062 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:446-514` installed-boot derives from base-rootfs, but the rootfs recstrap actually installs is live-tools' rootfs, which carries recab and recstrap added at BP061. The installed-boot product is a different system from what an install produces, and nothing states this.
- [hidden] BP072 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/products.rs:282-287,486-491` Each product prepare re-runs `materialize_source_rootfs` (a Recipe run), and the binary calls it again through `materialize_live_boot_source_rootfs` (`products.rs:316-318`, `bin/workflows/artifacts.rs:366`). Only Recipe's own caching keeps these cheap.
- [hidden] BP082 `distro/builder/src/pipeline/config.rs:151-167` The producer validation errors say "Ring 2 payload producer". The ring model lives in `distro/builder/docs/04_MIGRATION_RING_MODEL.md` and `ring2/` folder names, so that vocabulary is fixed in error strings too.
- [hidden] BR134 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/artifacts.rs:221-306` `prepare_product_inputs` has three near-identical match arms (live-boot, live-tools, installed-boot), each with its own `load_*_product_spec` → `with_resolved_parent_rootfs_image` → `prepare_*_product` chain copied with the names swapped.
- [hidden] BR137 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/artifacts.rs:47` and `:69` hard-code `"rootfs-source"` as the prepared rootfs dir name twice.

Each has its own wording.
