### G15 Both installed systems carry a complete boot payload and boot through one initramfs

Solves **18** problems (6 parity, 2 special, 8 hidden, 2 surplus).

- **Design:** The image carries its own kernel, its modules and firmware in the rootfs (today no rootfs has modules, so modular drivers such as WiFi can't load after boot on either distro), and both installed systems boot through an initramfs. One busybox-based recinit initramfs, independent of init and libc, serves both installed systems (and by default is the same image as the live one), with root fsck, LUKS/LVM and the trial's emergency reboot.
- **Done when:** Both rootfs trees contain `usr/lib/modules/<release>` and the curated firmware; AcornOS's installed boot has an initramfs; the ~8 places that decide "is there an installed initramfs" are one fact; the ab-update scenario stages with the installed initramfs.
- **Depends on:** G11, G12
- **Decisions:** **User-visible.** AcornOS gains an installed initramfs (default yes). One shared busybox initramfs replacing LevitateOS's systemd initramfs (default yes; alternative: a busybox backend for AcornOS only). Firmware: a curated set on both (default) vs full linux-firmware.
- **Merges area designs:** CV-12 (contract-variants), PA-04 (parity-all), BC-16 (builder-core), TO-07 (tools) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] C096 `ring1/transforms.toml:21-25` (levitate) defines `initramfs_installed`, which acorn's `transforms.toml` lacks. `ring0/release.toml:16-20` (levitate) ships `initramfs-installed.img` as a supporting artifact, and acorn's `:16` does not. The installed boot chains differ (systemd initramfs vs OpenRC with no initramfs).
- [parity] P096 `distro/variants/levitate/build-host/kernel/kconfig:304,309` and `distro/variants/acorn/build-host/kernel/kconfig:318,322` build `iwlwifi`/`ath9k` as modules, but neither distro's sources ship firmware (no `linux-firmware` in either `ring3/sources.toml` or `recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai`). The builder's only firmware-copy code, `distro/builder/src/alpine/firmware.rs:15`, is dead, and `tools/recinit/src/payload.rs:325-331` copies a nonexistent `usr/lib/firmware` into the installed initramfs. AcornOS ships `wpa_supplicant`/`iw` (`alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:61`) with no firmware for most WiFi chips.
- [parity] TL001 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:436` defaults the installed initramfs to `boot/initramfs-installed.img` only when the target is systemd; OpenRC targets (AcornOS) boot the installed kernel with no initramfs at all, so AcornOS installs cannot use anything an initramfs provides (fsck of root before mount, LUKS/LVM/dm, modular storage drivers, emergency handling).
- [parity] TL002 `tools/recinit/src/install.rs:1` and `tools/recinit/src/payload.rs:13-56` the only installed-system initramfs recinit can build is systemd-based (systemd, udevd, journald, systemctl as PID 1 of the initramfs); there is no OpenRC/busybox installed initramfs, which is the root cause of TL001.
- [parity] TL003 `tools/recinit/src/payload.rs:217` and `:224` the install initramfs payload hard-codes glibc x86_64 (`LIB_DIR = usr/lib64`, `LOADERS = usr/lib64/ld-linux-x86-64.so.2`), so it cannot be built from a musl (AcornOS) rootfs or for aarch64 even if an OpenRC payload existed.
- [parity] TL005 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:109-114` trial boots get `rd.emergency=reboot systemd.crash_action=reboot` on systemd and nothing on OpenRC; on AcornOS a trial whose root cannot mount or whose init dies relies only on a kernel panic plus `panic=10`, while LevitateOS's initramfs turns the same failures into a clean reboot (and anything that fails without a panic before `recab trial` runs is handled on one distro and not the other).
- [special] TL056 `tools/recinit/src/payload.rs:111-113` `initrd-udevadm-cleanup-db.service` deliberately not enabled because it breaks boot ("TEAM_144"), and `:165-170` `ConditionPathIsReadWrite=/sys` lines stripped from four udev units; patches against a specific systemd version's units.
- [special] TO2154 `tools/recinit/src/payload.rs:325-330` firmware is copied only when `usr/lib/firmware` exists, silently skipped otherwise, via `leviso_elf::copy_dir_recursive` (another recursive copier next to `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:363-401`).
- [hidden] C097 `_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:266-277` decides whether to build the installed initramfs by testing `${ROOTFS_SOURCE_DIR}/usr/lib/systemd/systemd`, not by whether the variant declares `initramfs_installed` (C096). Its comment names AcornOS inside the shared hook (`:267-268`).
- [hidden] P047 Whether the installed system boots through an initramfs is decided separately in `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:268` (rootfs has `usr/lib/systemd/systemd`), `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:436` (target init is systemd), `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:109-114` (trial options), `testing/install-tests/src/distro/{levitate,acorn}.rs` `uses_initramfs()`, `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:383` (`distro_id == "levitate"`), `distro/variants/levitate/ring1/transforms.toml:21` and `ring0/release.toml:19` (declared for Levitate only) and the two post-installation doc pages; none reads the others.
- [hidden] TL055 `tools/recinit/src/payload.rs:193-207` hand-written `etc/passwd`, `etc/group` (with `wheel:x:10:`) and `etc/shadow` for the initramfs, independent of the rootfs's accounts and of recsetup's `ADMIN_GROUP = "wheel"` (`tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:243`).
- [hidden] TL058 `tools/recinit/src/payload.rs:221` `PRIVATE_LIB_SUBDIRS = ["systemd"]` while `distro/elf/src/paths.rs:21-22` hard-codes `usr/lib64/systemd` and `usr/lib/systemd` in the generic library search; the systemd private-library rule is in both crates.
- [hidden] TL059 `tools/recinit/src/payload.rs:1-5` the payload tables are also the checklist of `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/install_initramfs.rs:10-31`, which adds `usr/lib/modules` to `MOUNT_DIRS` itself; the module directory placement (`install.rs:62` `"usr/lib/modules"` vs `tiny.rs:118` `"lib/modules"`) is a third convention passed as a string argument to `KernelModules::install`.
- [hidden] TO1103 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:72-135` `recab stage` accepts any initrd, while `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:439-455` refuses the live medium's `boot/initramfs.img` because it "ignores root="; the rule about which initramfs can boot an installed slot lives in only one of the two tools that install a slot.
- [hidden] TO3159 `tools/recstrap/src/fstab.rs:486-490` `boot_fsck_supported` gives fsck pass 2 to ext2/3/4, xfs and f2fs, while the systemd install initramfs ships only `fsck.ext4`/`e2fsck` (`tools/recinit/src/payload.rs:36-39`) and AcornOS boots without an initramfs. Which filesystems really get a boot fsck is decided separately in recfstab, recinit and the init system.
- [hidden] TO3188 `tools/recab/src/systemd_boot.rs:39` `INITRD_FILE = "initramfs.img"` names each slot's installed-system initramfs with the same file name as the live medium's own initramfs (`tools/recinit/src/live.rs:45` `INITRAMFS_LIVE_ISO_PATH`), which recsetup refuses to install (`tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:429-455`). Two different kinds of initramfs share one name.
- [surplus] TL057 `tools/recinit/src/modules.rs:74-89` `INSTALL_MODULES` includes `hid`/`usbhid` "for LUKS prompts" and `dm-mod`/`dm-crypt`, and `xfs`/`btrfs`, but the payload has no `cryptsetup`, no `systemd-cryptsetup`, no xfs/btrfs fsck (`payload.rs:17-56`) and recpart only creates ext4/vfat/swap; modules for features nothing else supports.
- [surplus] TO3062 `tools/recinit/src/payload.rs:141` `EMERGENCY_REBOOT_OPTION` is defined but has no user anywhere in the workspace. The unit text beside it spells `rd.emergency=reboot` three more times (`:149,:151,:159`), and recab spells it itself.

### G16 Release assembly is a Rust builder step, and the boot command line is a build fact

Solves **56** problems (3 parity, 15 special, 28 hidden, 2 needless, 8 surplus).

- **Design:** The release ISO is assembled by the builder calling the recinit and reciso libraries with a typed spec; the ring0 shell hooks, `_shared/ring0/hooks`, the per-variant wrappers and the ~30 env vars between Rust and shell are deleted; busybox becomes a recipe. The boot command line for every ISO product and every UKI role is built from shared tokens + init-backend tokens + role tokens, with no foreign-distro tokens. The live medium's boot chain comes from the target rootfs.
- **Done when:** No `.sh` hook under `distro/variants`; no env-var handshake between builder and shell; `inst.sshd=0` not on AcornOS; release metadata comes from typed values.
- **Depends on:** G03, G05, G14, G15
- **Merges area designs:** CL-03 (builder-cli), CV-11 (contract-variants), CV-10 (contract-variants), TO-10 (tools) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] C099 `scenarios.toml:41-50` (levitate) vs `:35-39` (acorn): LevitateOS's live cmdline carries `selinux=0`, `audit_backlog_limit=8192`, `loglevel=3` and the `systemd.show_status=0`/`rd.systemd.show_status=0` quiet flags. AcornOS carries only the consoles and `audit=1`, so its live boot is noisier and has no audit backlog setting.
- [parity] TL022 `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:32` and `:43` the ISO always boots `EFI/BOOT/BOOTX64.EFI` from a default host path `/usr/lib/systemd/boot/efi/systemd-bootx64.efi`, while recsetup (`target.rs:318-321`, `plan.rs:509`) and recab init (`init.rs:24-29`) handle aa64 names; architecture support differs between the live medium and the installer.
- [parity] TO3006 `tools/recuki/src/lib.rs:128-133` never passes `--stub`, and `tools/recuki/README.md:61-62` gives the host requirement only as Fedora `dnf install systemd-ukify`. Both siblings' UKIs therefore get the build host's ukify and EFI stub, so AcornOS's live UKI stub comes from a Fedora host's systemd, not from AcornOS's own boot tooling.
- [special] C051 `runtime.rs:681-699` checks for `anaconda-sshd.service` (a Fedora installer unit) and demands `inst.sshd=0`. This rule exists for one upstream package that no variant installs.
- [special] C052 `distro/contract/src/schema.rs:9` `BOOT_REQUIRED_KERNEL_CMDLINE_BASE = ["audit=1", "inst.sshd=0"]` is merged into *both* variants' live cmdline (`variant.rs:1469-1483`), which reaches the UKI via `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/release_hook.rs:152-164`. So AcornOS boots with the Fedora/anaconda-only `inst.sshd=0`, and neither variant's `scenarios.toml` shows it.
- [special] C114 `_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:48-50` pins the busybox URL and sha256 (1.35.0 musl) in the shell hook, overridable by env, and downloads it with `curl --retry 3 --retry-delay 2` (`:247`). It is a retry, and it is an artifact source that sits outside Recipe, which per `AGENTS.md` owns acquisition.
- [special] C117 `release-artifacts.sh:26-39` `run_distro_builder` tries three ways to find the builder in turn: the `DISTRO_BUILDER_BIN` env var, `distro-builder` on PATH, then `cargo run`. The builder always passes `DISTRO_BUILDER_BIN` (`release_hook.rs:121`), so the PATH and cargo fallbacks never run.
- [special] C121 `build-release.sh:78-81,86-105,217-229`: `stat`→`wc`, `numfmt`→awk and `sha256sum`→`shasum` are portability fallbacks for host tools, and `file_size_bytes` returns `0` for a missing file. Its errors are swallowed into the build log.
- [special] CL2032 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/release_hook.rs:27` `extra_cmdline.clone().unwrap_or_default()` turns an absent live cmdline into `""`, and `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:285` defaults it again with `${LIVE_UKI_CMDLINE-}` even though the builder always sets it. Absent and empty are conflated, and the default is written in two layers.
- [special] CL2034 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/release_hook.rs:89-127` the hook subprocess inherits the caller's whole environment (no `env_clear`), so stray `ISO_SHA`, `BUSYBOX_URL`, `BUSYBOX_SHA256`, `BUSYBOX_PATH`, `INITRAMFS_INSTALLED_FILENAME` or `FORCE_COLOR` in the user's shell silently change the release build (`build-release.sh:25,33,44,48-50`).
- [special] CL2035 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/release_hook.rs:137-139` reports a failed hook as the generic "builder command failed for '<distro>' with status ...". Unlike the spawn error at `:128-135`, it names neither the hook script nor the product.
- [special] CV3063 `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:110` honours `NO_COLOR` only when it is exactly `"1"` (and `FORCE_COLOR` likewise at `:114`), an `if` for one value where the variable's convention is "any non-empty value".
- [special] TO2167 `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:203-205` the size report is skipped when `metadata` fails (`if let Ok`).
- [special] TO2171 `tools/recuki/src/lib.rs:151` `status.code().unwrap_or(-1)` reports a signal-killed ukify as exit -1.
- [special] TO3004 `tools/recuki/src/lib.rs:129-133` passes the kernel, initrd and output paths to ukify through `to_string_lossy()`, so a non-UTF-8 path is silently rewritten (U+FFFD) and ukify is pointed at a different file.
- [special] TO3009 `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:165-172` `--extra-file src:dst` joins `dst` onto the ISO root with `Path::join`, so an absolute `dst` (`/etc/x`) replaces the root and `../` climbs out of it. The copy then writes onto the build host outside the private work dir, and there is no path-safety check.
- [special] TO3022 `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:428-430` `entry_stem` uses `trim_end_matches(".efi")`. That strips repeated suffixes (`x.efi.efi` becomes `x`) and is case-sensitive (`X.EFI` becomes `X.EFI.conf`), while UKI file names come from callers unchecked.
- [special] TO3028 `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:191-200` with `--no-checksum`, an older `<iso>.sha512` from a previous build is left next to the new ISO. The checksum is also written with plain `fs::write` (`src/iso.rs:52`) after the atomic ISO rename, while `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:311` says "reciso writes the ISO atomically and its checksum next to it".
- [hidden] BR112 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/commands.rs:82` and `:87` accept `artifact build-rootfs-erofs` / `artifact build-overlayfs-erofs` as undocumented aliases of `transform build rootfs-erofs|overlayfs-erofs` (`:67`). The shared hook calls only the undocumented spelling (`distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/release-artifacts.sh:47`, `:100`), so removing the "alias" breaks releases while the documented form is unused.
- [hidden] BR120 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/parse.rs:132` `".live-rootfs-source.path"` and `"live-overlay"` are passed to the hook as env vars, and the hook repeats them as defaults (`distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/release-artifacts.sh:59-60`).
- [hidden] BR122 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/release_hook.rs:89-126` hands about 30 values to the shell hook through env vars, and `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:9-38` asserts each with `: "${X:?}"`. The Rust–shell interface is a list of names kept in sync by hand. `INITRAMFS_INSTALLED_FILENAME` is not passed at all, and the hook falls back to its own default (`build-release.sh:44`).
- [hidden] BR138 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/prepared_products.rs:73-99` records the rootfs source twice: in the pointer file `.live-rootfs-source.path` (read by the shell hook) and in `.prepared-product.json` (read by `product-erofs`). `PreparedOutputNames` (`:25`) and `PreparedProductManifest` (`:14`) copy the same three fields.
- [hidden] C087 `variant.rs:50-52` (`BUILD_RELEASE_HOOK_FILENAME` …) and `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/parse.rs:130,142,154` (`release_hook_script_name`) each hard-code the three hook filenames. The `PRODUCT_NAME` defaults inside the wrappers (`base-rootfs`, `live-boot`, `live-tools`) repeat the builder's product table as a third copy.
- [hidden] C088 `ring0/hooks/*.sh:5` and `_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:5` compute `REPO_ROOT` as `$SCRIPT_DIR/../../../../..`, a fixed directory depth. Moving the hooks breaks it silently, and the builder passes `current_dir(repo_root)` anyway (`release_hook.rs:91`).
- [hidden] C098 `_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:44` defaults `INITRAMFS_INSTALLED_FILENAME=initramfs-installed.img`, which the builder never passes (`release_hook.rs:89-126`). It is a copy of `levitate/ring1/transforms.toml:24` that `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:321` copies again.
- [hidden] CL1032 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/commands.rs:39` runs `ensure_release_prerequisites` and then `build_one`. The release hook then calls `distro-builder product prepare` (`_shared/ring0/hooks/release-artifacts.sh:62`), and `prepare_product_cmd` runs `ensure_release_prerequisites` again (`artifacts.rs:164`). Prerequisites are planned and checked twice in every release build, once in each process.
- [hidden] CL2052 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/prepared_products.rs:73-75` writes the rootfs-source pointer file as an absolute host path plus a trailing newline, and `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/release-artifacts.sh:65` reads it with `tr -d '\n'`. The pointer file's content format is a convention between Rust and shell that nothing defines in one place.
- [hidden] CV1045 `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:33` derives `ISO_SHA` as `${ISO_PATH%.iso}.sha512` and checks it with `need_file` at `:313`. That copies reciso's naming rule (`tools/reciso/src/iso.rs:8,51`, `ISO_CHECKSUM_SUFFIX` with `with_extension`), and the hook's own comment (`:311`) says reciso owns it.
- [hidden] CV2011 `distro/variants/levitate/ring1/transforms.toml:36` and `distro/variants/acorn/ring1/transforms.toml:26` declare `extra_cmdline = "video=1920x1080"` on the whole `live_uki` transform, but `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/release_hook.rs:27`/`:100` passes it only as `LIVE_UKI_CMDLINE`, and `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:286-287` builds the emergency and debug UKIs from `emergency`/`debug` alone. The emergency and debug UKIs silently lack it, though the field reads as applying to all three outputs.
- [hidden] CV2093 `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:257`, `:271` and `:309` always run recinit and reciso via `cargo run`, while the same hook resolves distro-builder through `release-artifacts.sh:26-39` (`DISTRO_BUILDER_BIN`, then host `PATH`, then `cargo run`): two binary-resolution policies in one release script.
- [hidden] CV2095 `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:262-263` sets recinit template keys `BOOT_BANNER` and `PRODUCT_TAG` by name; the placeholder vocabulary is owned by `tools/recinit/src/tiny.rs:138-139`, and the hook must know it.
- [hidden] CV3053 `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:286` passes the bare cmdline token `emergency` to the emergency UKI, a literal that must match the `emergency)` case in `tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:62`; the debug UKI's `debug` token (`build-release.sh:287`) is matched by nothing in the init, so the two role tokens follow different contracts with nothing naming either.
- [hidden] CV3069 `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:263` passes `--set PRODUCT_TAG=${PRODUCT_ARTIFACT_TAG}` to recinit, and the value ends up only in a comment of the generated `/init` (`tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:395`, default `"untagged"` at `tools/recinit/src/tiny.rs:139`); the hook must know the placeholder key for a value nothing reads.
- [hidden] TL103 `tools/recuki/src/lib.rs:87-95` builds a synthetic os-release (`NAME`, `ID`, `VERSION`, `PRETTY_NAME`) from three CLI strings for UKI branding, separate from the rootfs's real os-release that recinit copies into the initrd (`payload.rs:314-319`) and recab reads; `build-release.sh:294-296` passes the same values again.
- [hidden] TL106 `tools/reciso/src/main.rs:69-72` and `:123-142` the `name:cmdline:filename` spec string protocol and `main.rs:144-157` `src:dst` extra-file protocol are produced by shell (`build-release.sh:297-305`); the installed initramfs reaches the ISO only through such an `--extra-file` naming `boot/initramfs-installed.img`, which recsetup must then find (TL041).
- [hidden] TL107 `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:401-402` ESP image size = payload x1.2 + 8 MB, min 16 MB; an ad-hoc sizing rule separate from recpart's ESP sizing (`policy.rs:14-18`).
- [hidden] TO1125 `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:41-43` and `src/main.rs:99-101` the ISO's systemd-boot defaults to the build host's `/usr/lib/systemd/boot/efi/systemd-bootx64.efi` ("a variant should pass its own rootfs's copy"), and `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:289-309` does not pass `--systemd-boot`; the live ISO boots the build host's systemd-boot while the installed system gets the target rootfs's (`recsetup/target.rs:322`), two versions per release.
- [hidden] TO2155 `tools/recinit/src/tiny.rs:225-228` `BOOT_BANNER` default text "initramfs starting..." is repeated by the caller `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:262` (`"${OS_NAME} ${PRODUCT_BOOT_LABEL} initramfs starting..."`).

The same artifact check is repeated at two layers.
- [hidden] TO3020 `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:387,408,433` spell the `entries` subdirectory of systemd-boot's loader dir three times as a literal, next to the `LOADER_ENTRIES_DIR` constant (`:38`).
- [hidden] TO3021 `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:409` the list of directories to `mmd` (`"EFI", ISO_EFI_DIR, UKI_EFI_DIR, LOADER_ENTRIES_DIR, entries_path`) is hand-ordered parent before child. It works only while `ISO_EFI_DIR`/`UKI_EFI_DIR` (`:30`, `:36`) are exactly one level under a literal `"EFI"`.
- [hidden] TO3024 `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:299-302` gives prebuilt UKIs their file stem as the boot-menu title, while built UKIs get the spec name (`:328`). One menu has two title policies.
- [hidden] TO3025 `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:210-246` `validate_inputs` checks kernel, initrd, rootfs and prebuilt UKIs with `.exists()` but the overlay and systemd-boot with `.is_file()`, and never checks `--extra-file` sources, which fail mid-build at `fs::copy` (`:170-171`). Inputs of the same kind get different strictness.
- [hidden] TO3029 `tools/reciso/src/iso.rs:57-59` `create_fat16_image(size_mb)` multiplies by 1024*1024 (MiB), and `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:204` prints `Size: N MB` from `/1024/1024`. The same MB/MiB mixing appears in `tools/recinit/src/install.rs:84` (MB) and `tiny.rs:91` (KB from `/1024`).
- [hidden] TO3080 `tools/recinit/src/tiny.rs:87-92` and `src/install.rs:80-85` print progress to stdout unless `-q`. `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:257,271` runs `cargo run -q -p recinit -- build-…`, where `-q` quiets cargo, not recinit, so tool chatter is mixed into the release hook's output.
- [needless] BR124 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/release_hook.rs:86` passes its own `current_exe()` as `DISTRO_BUILDER_BIN` so the shell hook can call back into `distro-builder product prepare` and `artifact build-*-erofs` (`distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/release-artifacts.sh:26-38`, `:47`, `:62`, `:100`). The release build runs Rust → shell → the same Rust binary. The hook's `PATH` and `cargo run` fallbacks are reached only when the hook is run outside distro-builder, where they may pick up a different build of the binary.
- [needless] C113 `release_hook.rs:152-164` builds the UKI's kernel cmdline from `scenarios.live_boot.required_kernel_cmdline`, a test-scenario field. It does so only for `live-boot`/`live-tools`, so the `base-rootfs` ISO gets no console or audit tokens. Build output depends on the scenario owner, and one product is special-cased out.
- [surplus] C086 `distro/variants/{levitate,acorn}/ring0/hooks/{build,boot,live-tools}-release.sh` are six 8-line wrappers that `export DISTRO_ID` and default `PRODUCT_NAME`, then `exec` the shared hook. The builder already sets both env vars (`release_hook.rs:92,105`), and `variant.rs:460-470` requires all three files in every variant.
- [surplus] C115 `build-release.sh:21-22` requires both `PRODUCT_DIRNAME` and `PRODUCT_ARTIFACT_TAG`, which `release_hook.rs:106-107` sets to the same `release_dir_name`. `ISO_FILENAME` (`:31`) is required but never used. `RELEASE_ROOT_DIR`, `RELEASE_RUN_DIR` and `BUILD_RUN_ID` are passed (`release_hook.rs:117-120`) and never read.
- [surplus] C116 `_shared/ring0/hooks/release-artifacts.sh:76-92` `prepare_release_inputs` only checks for an empty product name and forwards to `prepare_product_inputs` (`:50-74`), a pass-through wrapper. `build_rootfs_erofs`/`build_overlayfs_erofs` (`:41-48,94-101`) just forward to `distro-builder artifact …`.
- [surplus] C120 `build-release.sh:61-187,316-330` contains a hand-rolled JSON writer (`json_escape`, sizes), a `numfmt`/awk human-size fallback and a colored artifact table. `build-log.json` (`:56`) has no reader in the repo outside this hook.
- [surplus] CL2049 `distro/builder/src/bin/workflows/prepared_products.rs:19` and `:95` `PreparedProductManifest.rootfs_source_pointer_filename` is written into `.prepared-product.json`, but nothing reads it: `build_prepared_product_erofs_cmd` (`artifacts.rs:99-157`) ignores it, and the hook finds the pointer file through the `ROOTFS_SOURCE_POINTER_FILENAME` env var (`release-artifacts.sh:59`).
- [surplus] CV1047 `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:151` assigns `artifact_name` inside `collect_release_artifacts` and never uses it.
- [surplus] CV2099 `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:26` takes `ARTIFACT_NAME_WIDTH` from the environment, but `set_artifact_name_width` (`:143`, called at `:318`) always overwrites it before use; the "Color policy" comment at `:28` sits above the unrelated `KERNEL_RELEASE_PATH` check, not the color variables at `:25`.
- [surplus] CV3057 `distro/variants/_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:50,233-236` `BUSYBOX_SHA256="${BUSYBOX_SHA256:-<hash>}"` uses `:-`, so an empty value also gets the default; the `-z "$BUSYBOX_SHA256"` error branch in `verify_busybox_sha256` is unreachable.

### G17 On-disk conventions have one owner each, and everyone else reads the owner

Solves **87** problems (2 parity, 24 special, 54 hidden, 3 needless, 4 surplus).

- **Design:** The installed disk layout is one typed table in recpart, and recpart's handoff is the layout record every tool reads. recab owns the installed ESP (boot entries, `loader.conf`, systemd-boot copied from the target) for A/B and mutable installs. `recinit::live` owns the live protocol (media label and paths, overlay chain, ready marker, serial console) without builder dependencies; the medium is found by label with a deadline. Builder, install-tests and the TUIs take these from the owners' exported names and queries; the builder stops depending on recqemu.
- **Done when:** `ROOT_A`/`ROOT_B`/`STATE`, `loader/entries`, `initramfs-installed.img`, `/live-overlay`, the ready marker each have one definition (grep); install-tests depends only on stable crates.
- **Depends on:** G04
- **Decisions:** recab gains `stage --from-media` and `status --root` (default add). Mutable installs get the same boot entry options as A/B (default yes).
- **Merges area designs:** TO-02 (tools), TO-03 (tools), TO-08 (tools), TV-06 (testing-vm), TV-12 (testing-vm) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] TL021 `tools/recab/src/systemd_boot.rs:757-767` `RootSelector::device` resolves every root selector through `/dev/disk/by-partuuid|by-uuid|by-label` links, so `slot_device` (`:312-327`, used by `stage`) needs a udev that creates them. AcornOS's package list (`distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:58-61`) names no udev provider, and installed AcornOS runs busybox `mdev` (`installed-system.rhai:110`). recpart already treats udev as optional (`tools/recpart/src/exec.rs:329-333`), but recab assumes it.
- [parity] TO1055 `tools/recpart/src/exec.rs:330-335` runs `udevadm settle --timeout=10` only when `udevadm` is on PATH ("without it (mdev on AcornOS)"); on AcornOS the only wait is `VerifyPartitions`' 10 s poll, so the two distros take different paths through apply and only LevitateOS waits for udev rules (by-partuuid links) before formatting.
- [special] TI054 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:246` `wait_committed` is a guest shell retry loop (`for i in $(seq 90); do ... grep -q '"committed": true' ...; sleep 2`). It string-matches recab's JSON formatting and sits inside an outer `COMMIT_WAIT` of 200s, although the loop itself runs 180s.
- [special] TI058 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:131-134` finds slot B's device by `sed`-parsing `root=PARTUUID=` out of the loader entry, which ties the test to recab's entry format.
- [special] TI100 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:294-297` `status()` finds the first `{` in console output to skip anything printed before the JSON.
- [special] TL013 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:323` chooses `bootctl install` only when the target is systemd and else copies the EFI binary (`plan.rs:506-520`): two ways of installing the same boot loader, keyed on init system rather than on what the target offers, so the two distros get different ESP contents (bootctl adds `loader/`, random seed, entry-token files; the copy path writes only the binaries).
- [special] TL046 `tools/recinit/src/live.rs:100-110` the QEMU test channel (`opt/levitate/boot-injection` fw_cfg name, `LEVITATE_INJECT` ISO label, `/dev/sr1 /dev/sr2 /dev/sdb /dev/vdb`) lives in the production live init; it is named for LevitateOS but used by both distros, and `/dev/sdb` is both a boot device candidate (`live.rs:90`) and an injection device.
- [special] TL048 `tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:88-98` fixed `sleep 1` and, if neither `/dev/sr0` nor `/dev/sda` exists, `sleep 2`, hoping slow devices appear by then; no wait-for-device loop.
- [special] TL049 `tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:200-213` losetup failure falls back to a direct mount, and a failed loop mount falls back again; retries that hide which path works.
- [special] TL050 `tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:276-285` a "two-layer overlay (shouldn't happen from ISO, but handle gracefully)" branch for a case the build never produces, which still creates the live marker.
- [special] TL051 `tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:288-289` `/run` and `/tmp` tmpfs mounts with `|| true` "expected by OpenRC/system services in live mode", errors swallowed.
- [special] TL085 `tools/recab/src/init.rs:101-112` when efivarfs is missing, init only warns that stale variables may select another entry; `stage` later resets them (`lib.rs:221-228`). The cleanup belongs to whichever command runs first with efivars.
- [special] TL088 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:211-214` reading the slot's current label swallows blkid errors and falls back to `ROOT_<slot>`.
- [special] TL092 `tools/recab/src/init.rs:21-32` and `:117-132` init requires a systemd-boot EFI binary and an os-release `ID` (lowercased into the entry prefix), while `systemd_boot.rs:548-592` infers the prefix by four fallbacks (env, default entry, os-release ID, the one paired prefix); the prefix rule is written once to create and once, differently, to read.
- [special] TO1064 `tools/recpart/src/handoff.rs:8-15` a plan with no `/` partition mounted yields an empty `install_target` (`unwrap_or_default`) in the handoff rather than an error.
- [special] TO1065 `tools/recpart/src/manual.rs:578,588-593` sizes over `u32::MAX` MiB saturate (`unwrap_or(u32::MAX)`) and a missing EFI role reports `efi_size_mb: 0` in the resolved layout, values the frontend shows as real.
- [special] TO1095 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recstrap/guards.rs:303-321` the EROFS-support check reads `/proc/filesystems` (`unwrap_or(false)`) and falls back to `modprobe -n -q erofs`; the answer depends on which `modprobe` the live system has (kmod or busybox), and every failure, a missing `modprobe` included, reads as "no EROFS".
- [special] TO1114 `tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:46-50,162-166,202,319-360` failures are swallowed with `2>/dev/null` and `|| true`: a failed module `insmod` prints "already loaded or failed" and continues, payload probe mounts, `mknod`, and the injection `chmod`s/umounts ignore errors, so a missing module only shows up later as a different failure.
- [special] TO2047 `tools/recab/src/systemd_boot.rs:383` `starts_with("panic=")` prefix match keeps any user `panic=` value, but `ALL_TRIAL_OPTIONS` uses exact matches: two matching rules for kernel options in one function.
- [special] TO3035 `tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:78-82` `emergency_shell` prints "Type 'exit' to retry boot" and then runs `exec busybox sh` as PID 1, so `exit` kills init and panics the kernel. The UI text contradicts the behaviour. The `emergency` cmdline path (`:137-142`) uses the same wording, and there the shell is not exec'd and boot does continue.
- [special] TO3036 `tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:34-38` moves all init output and the emergency shell's stdin/stdout to `/dev/ttyS0` whenever that character device exists. On real PCs the 8250 driver creates `ttyS0` with or without a cable, so the user's screen goes silent and an emergency shell is unreachable from the keyboard. A test-harness console convention is applied to every product boot.
- [special] TO3059 `tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:381` accepts `/sbin/init` if it is `-x` or `-L`, so a dangling `/sbin/init` symlink passes the check and `switch_root` (`:390`) fails afterward with the less specific "switch_root failed".
- [special] TO3184 `tools/recpart/src/policy.rs:130,172` and `src/types.rs:103-104` set the legacy-BIOS bootable flag on the ESP in both templates, although nothing in the product boots by BIOS (`tools/reciso/README.md:84` "Support legacy BIOS boot" under what reciso does not do).
- [special] TV1092 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:108` asserts `/var` is its own partition by `findmnt -n -o SOURCE /var` output containing `"/dev/"`.
- [special] TV2038 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1016` `.unwrap_or("")` turns a probe output with no `PROBE_PREFIX` line into an empty helper string, reported as an "<empty>" helper mismatch rather than as the probe failing to report.
- [special] TV2052 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:283` `umount {MEDIA_MOUNT}; losetup -D` joins with `;`, so a failed unmount is ignored, and `losetup -D` detaches every loop device in the guest rather than the one `stage_from_media` created (`:272`).
- [hidden] TI039 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/install.rs:83` builds the kernel path as `format!("{LIVE_MEDIA_DIR}/boot/vmlinuz")` by hand, while `recinit::live::KERNEL_ISO_PATH` (`tools/recinit/src/live.rs:44`) holds `boot/vmlinuz` and `ab_update.rs:266` uses that constant.
- [hidden] TI040 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/install.rs:92` spells `boot/initramfs-installed.img` as a literal. The same name is spelled in `preflight.rs:321` and `:404`, `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:18`, `tools/recinit/src/main.rs:85`, `distro/variants/levitate/ring0/release.toml:19`, `ring1/transforms.toml:24` and `_shared/ring0/hooks/build-release.sh:44`. recinit's live protocol has a constant for the live initramfs but none for this one.
- [hidden] TI042 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:529-569` the post-install verify list hard-codes recsetup/recab's ESP layout: `boot/loader/loader.conf`, `boot/vmlinuz` vs `boot/<os_id>-a/vmlinuz`, `initramfs.img`, `loader/entries/<os_id>.conf` vs `<os_id>-a.conf`/`-b.conf`, and `var/lib/recab/state.json`. `ab_update.rs:96-101` builds `/boot/loader/entries/{os_id}-{slot}.conf` again on its own. Neither recab nor recsetup exports these names.
- [hidden] TI043 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:560` checks A/B install state with `grep -q '"known_good_slot": "A"'` on recab's `state.json`, which depends on recab's serde field name *and* its pretty-print spacing. `ab_update.rs:292-299` meanwhile parses `recab status --json` into `recab::Status`.
- [hidden] TI044 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:484-489` hard-codes the disk size `"48G"` for A/B ("two 20 GiB root slots and at least 4 GiB of state (recpart's defaults)") and `"20G"` for mutable. If recpart's defaults change, this must change too.
- [hidden] TI052 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:261-263` finds the rootfs on the update medium with a shell heuristic. It treats `live/filesystem.erofs` as real if it is larger than 65536 bytes, else builds a `losetup --partscan` device and polls for `p{ROOTFS_PAYLOAD_PARTITION}` (`seq 50; sleep 0.1`). This encodes reciso's two layouts (a file, or an appended partition 2 with a stub file) in the test, and `recinit::live::ROOTFS_SEARCH_PATHS` is not used.
- [hidden] TI063 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1278` checks the literal `/live-boot-marker`, while `recinit::live::BOOT_MARKER` (`tools/recinit/src/live.rs:81`) holds it.
- [hidden] TI064 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1284-1288` checks `lowerdir=/live-overlay:/rootfs` in `mount` output, a mount layout private to `tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:269` with no constant in `recinit::live`.
- [hidden] TI070 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1007-1036` the install-UX split probe uses `distro_builder::install_ux::{LAUNCHER, PROBE_PREFIX, SMOKE_ENV, SPLIT, SPLIT_SMOKE_OK}` and composes `/{LAUNCHER}` paths with a leading `/` by hand. The builder stores them root-relative, so every consumer has to know to prepend `/`.
- [hidden] TI094 `tools/recqemu/src/serial/protocol.rs:16-21` and `guest/serial-test.sh` are the two halves of the serial protocol. The guest half is installed by `distro/builder/src/pipeline/overlay.rs:31-37`, so recqemu (a VM library) is a build-time dependency of the distro builder (`distro/builder/Cargo.toml:13`).
- [hidden] TI095 `tools/recqemu/guest/serial-test.sh:11` the profile only arms on `/dev/ttyS0`, matching libvirt's `<target port='0'/>` (`libvirt.rs:171`) and the harness's `console=ttyS0,115200n8` (`install.rs:16`). One fact is spelled in three places.
- [hidden] TL023 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:318-321` picks the systemd-boot EFI file name from the build host's `std::env::consts::ARCH`, not the target's architecture.
- [hidden] TL025 `tools/recab/src/init.rs:50-75` writes the slot entries, kernel dir and file names by hand (`format!("{id}-{}", ...)`, `"vmlinuz"`, `"initramfs.img"`, `{id}-{}.conf`, `{id}-a.conf`) instead of using `entry_name`, `slot_boot_dir`, `KERNEL_FILE`, `INITRD_FILE` in `systemd_boot.rs:38-39,595-602`; the layout recab later parses is defined twice in one crate.
- [hidden] TL028 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:618-659` the mutable layout writes its own systemd-boot entry and loader.conf: entry `<os_id>.conf`, kernel at `/vmlinuz`, options `rw rootwait` without `panic=`, `set_loader_key` (`:106-124`) adding `timeout 5`; recab writes A/B entries as `<id>-a.conf` with `/id-a/vmlinuz`, `ro rootwait panic=10`, and `loader_with_default` (`systemd_boot.rs:641-654`) adding `timeout 5` and `editor 0`. Same file formats, two writers with different defaults (editor locked on A/B only, `ro` vs `rw`, panic reboot on A/B only).
- [hidden] TL029 `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:363-369` writes a third loader.conf (`default ...`, `timeout 5`, `console-mode max`) and `:432-446` a third entry writer; `distro/builder/src/artifact/disk/partitions.rs:57-61` writes a fourth for disk images. The `timeout 5` default is repeated in three of them.
- [hidden] TL030 `tools/recab/src/init.rs:24-29` the list of systemd-boot EFI paths recab accepts as "installed" (`EFI/systemd/systemd-boot{x64,aa64}.efi`, `EFI/BOOT/BOOT{X64,AA64}.EFI`) must match where `recsetup/plan.rs:509-518` copies the binary and where `bootctl install` puts it; nothing ties them.
- [hidden] TL031 `tools/recab/src/init.rs:74` A/B entries are written `ro`, and the root becomes writable only because the installed fstab/init remounts it; `recsetup/plan.rs:628` mutable entries are `rw`; `recfstab` drops `rw` from options (`tools/recstrap/src/fstab.rs:296`). Whether the root ends up read-write depends on three tools agreeing implicitly.
- [hidden] TL032 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:462-488` `write_fstab` rewrites the root line to `PARTUUID=` and `ext4`, while the first install's fstab comes from `recfstab` with `UUID=` by default (`tools/recstrap/src/bin/recfstab.rs:37-45` defaults to `IdType::Uuid`, and install-tests / the TUI run it without flags: `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/install.rs:74`, `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:105`); after the first update the root line switches identifier type, and the two fstab writers share no code.
- [hidden] TL033 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:43` `SLOT_FILESYSTEM = "ext4"` and `:214` fallback label `ROOT_{slot}` repeat recpart's template (`tools/recpart/src/policy.rs:137-150` ext4, `ROOT_A`/`ROOT_B`) and recpart's manual defaults (`tools/recpart/src/manual.rs:93-110`); recinit's install initramfs carries only `fsck.ext4`/`e2fsck` (`payload.rs:37-39`) for the same reason. A slot filesystem change needs all four.
- [hidden] TL034 `tools/recab/src/store.rs:40-42` and `:49-51` the state, audit and lock paths are written twice (`from_env` and `under`); the state's location under `/var` is a hidden dependency on recpart putting the STATE partition at `/var` (`tools/recpart/src/policy.rs:162`, `manual.rs:87`), stated only in `state.rs:11-13` and `lib.rs:129-130` comments.
- [hidden] TL035 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:220` mounts the slot at `/run/recab/stage-<a|b>` and calls `recstrap` there; recstrap's `MIN_FREE_BYTES` 2 GiB guard (`tools/recstrap/src/bin/recstrap/guards.rs:27`) and recab's own fit check (`stage.rs:174-182`, compressed image bytes vs partition bytes) are two unrelated size rules for the same write, and recpart's `MIN_AB_ROOT_MB` 8 GiB (`policy.rs:12`) a third.
- [hidden] TL036 `tools/recpart/src/policy.rs:14-16` `SLOT_BOOT_FILES_MB = 128` and `BOOTLOADER_MB = 8` size the ESP for recab's per-slot kernel+initramfs dirs and systemd-boot; the actual sizes come from the kernel and recinit outputs, and nothing checks them against these numbers.
- [hidden] TL037 `tools/recpart/src/policy.rs:121-187` the template layout (names, labels, filesystems, mountpoints) and `tools/recpart/src/manual.rs:82-111` (`role_mountpoint`, `default_filesystem`, `default_label`) are two tables of the same role facts; `Role::as_str`/`from_template_name` (`tools/recpart/src/types.rs:405-432`) bridge them by string name.
- [hidden] TL038 `tools/recpart/src/handoff.rs:19-34` derives slot devices by the template names `"root_a"`/`"root_b"` and emits slots as strings `"A"`/`"B"`, defaulting to `("B","A")` whenever root_a is not mounted at `/`; recab's `Slot` enum and recsetup's `--slot-a/--slot-b` take the same facts in other forms.
- [hidden] TL039 `tools/recpart/src/plan.rs:360-373` `partition_device` (`p` suffix for names ending in a digit) is reimplemented as `part_name` in the live init (`tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:146-153`).
- [hidden] TL040 `tools/recpart/src/types.rs:206-225` handoff and `recsetup` boot request carry slot devices, and `recab init` re-checks them (`init.rs:40-45`), recsetup checks them against the root mount (`plan.rs:548-559`); three tools validate the same slot pair.
- [hidden] TL052 `tools/recinit/src/live.rs:87-93` `BOOT_DEVICE_PROBE_ORDER` (`sr0 sda sdb vda nvme0n1`) is the only way the live init finds its medium; USB sticks that enumerate as `sdc`, or a second NVMe, are not probed, and `LIVE_MODULES` (`tools/recinit/src/modules.rs:20-42`) has no usb-storage/xhci/nvme, so real-hardware USB and NVMe boot depend on those being built into each distro's kernel config, which nothing here checks.
- [hidden] TL053 `tools/recinit/src/modules.rs:19` leaves EROFS out of `LIVE_MODULES` because it assumes `CONFIG_EROFS_FS=y`, and the live init's mount fallbacks say so in their error text (`templates/init_tiny.template:206,212`). The template's overlay errors (`:271,:281`) still say `CONFIG_OVERLAY_FS=y`, although `overlay` is loaded as a module (`modules.rs:41`). Both distros' kernels (6.12 LTS vs 7.x) must keep EROFS built in, and only comments say so. `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recstrap/guards.rs:280-300` checks erofs again at install time with a different rule (built in or loadable).
- [hidden] TL054 `tools/recinit/src/tiny.rs:58-70` `INITRAMFS_DIRS` lists `rootfs`, `overlay`, `newroot`, `live-overlay`, and the template creates them again with its own `mkdir -p` list (`templates/init_tiny.template:144`, which also adds `/probe` and `/overlay/upper|work`). The directory set is defined in two places.
- [hidden] TL071 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:560-581` the A/B boot step runs `recab init` as a host command found on PATH, so the live system's recab (not the target's) writes the installed layout; the live ISO must ship a recab compatible with the installed system's, and the argv is assembled by hand to match `tools/recab/src/main.rs:26-46`.
- [hidden] TL077 `tools/recpart/src/cli.rs:86-87` `--mount-root` default `/mnt`, `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/main.rs:45,51,59` `--root` default `/mnt`, and the guides' `recstrap /mnt`: the shared install root is three independent defaults.
- [hidden] TL104 `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:179` and `:186` the appended partition type `0x83` is written twice.
- [hidden] TL112 `tools/recpart/src/policy.rs:129-181` template partitions use sfdisk shorthand types `"U"`/`"L"`, while `tools/recpart/src/manual.rs:20-23` spells the same types as GUIDs (`EFI_TYPE`, `LINUX_TYPE`, ...); two spellings of one fact in one crate.
- [hidden] TO1049 `tools/recpart/src/plan.rs:117-130` `partition_device` derives the planned device names by "last char is a digit → `p<N>`", while the real names come from the kernel (`preflight.rs:347` `kernel_partition_names`); apply then waits for the guessed names to match the kernel's (`exec.rs:533-579`), so the naming rule is a guess about the kernel's convention that also feeds the handoff and mounts.
- [hidden] TO1143 `tools/recpart/src/types.rs:16-24` `InstallMode { Ab, Mutable }` (default `Ab`) and `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/request.rs:63-70` `BootMode { Ab, Mutable }` (no default) are two enums for the same install choice with the same JSON spelling; the partition layout and the boot setup must agree on the mode, and the install TUI and install tests pass it to each tool separately.

- [hidden] TO2113 `tools/recstrap/src/fstab.rs:178-185` recfstab accepts the ESP at `/boot`, `/efi` or `/boot/efi`, while recsetup (`tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:486-498`), recab init (`tools/recab/src/init.rs:21`) and recpart's role table (`/boot`) require `/boot`; the ESP location rule differs per tool.

The template spells `/mnt`, `/probe`, `/rootfs`, `/live-overlay`, `/newroot`, `/boot-injection-media`, `/sys/firmware/qemu_fw_cfg/by_name/`, `/root/.ssh/authorized_keys`, `SSH_AUTHORIZED_KEY(S)`, `/sbin/init` and the `iso9660`/`erofs` fs types directly (`init_tiny.template:144,162,314,329,333,352-359,381,390`).

These are two mechanisms for one echo problem, and the guest one is the reason the protocol profile has to be in the guest at all.

The stable tools import a volatile build tool to get the live protocol.

- [surplus] TO3044 `tools/recinit/src/live.rs:103-104` `BOOT_INJECTION_ISO_LABEL` is not in `init_template_vars` (`:113-133`), and the init accepts any iso9660 on the injection devices that holds `boot-injection.env` (`init_tiny.template:331-334`). Only the test harness uses the label (`testing/install-tests/src/boot_injection.rs:123`), so recinit owns a protocol value its init never checks.

### G18 The install tools are one family with shared target-side primitives, built one way

Solves **72** problems (1 parity, 33 special, 24 hidden, 3 needless, 11 surplus).

- **Design:** recpart and recab join recstrap's crate family with one shared layer for target-side primitives (mount table, guards, blkid, os-release, copy); one static musl build per tool for both siblings; recab installs its own trial service; recab reads one `Config` and takes one lock per command. Failed probes stop with an error everywhere. Unused surfaces in recinit, reciso and recuki go.
- **Done when:** No duplicate guard/mount-table/blkid code across tools (grep); the install tools on both ISOs are the same static binaries; `install-tools.rhai` doesn't know recab's service paths.
- **Depends on:** G04, G17
- **Decisions:** Merge the recpart and recab repos into one install-tools repo (default yes; alternative: shared crate, separate repos). LevitateOS install tools become static musl (default yes). Ship the dual-boot resize tools on both (default yes).
- **Merges area designs:** TO-01 (tools), RG-13 (builder-recipes-guest), TO-09 (tools), TO-11 (tools), TO-12 (tools) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] P067 Levitate's install tools are built for `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu` (dynamic glibc, `install-tools.rhai:22`), Acorn's statically for musl; the same `recpart`/`recstrap`/`recab` binaries differ in linkage, and only LevitateOS's break if the rootfs's glibc changes.
- [special] BR099 `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:21-22` defaults `INIT_SYSTEM` to `"systemd"` and `RUST_TARGET` to gnu (LevitateOS values), and `distro/builder/src/recipe/tools.rs:339` `build_tool_program` hard-codes `"systemd"`. AcornOS correctness depends on every caller overriding the defaults.
- [special] BR100 `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:108` `if ctx.name == "recab"` adds a service-file install step for one tool inside the generic tool recipe, and branches again on init system (`:113`) to choose a unit or an OpenRC script.
- [special] BR155 `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:122`, `linux.rhai:194` and `linux-prebuilt.rhai:161` each accept an optional "supplied" input (`trial_service`, `kernel_archive`) that replaces the normal acquisition when present. Each is an override hook for one caller, with its own name and no shared pattern.
- [special] RG1109 `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:29-34,71-78`: GNU builds must match the compiler image's native host triple (checked by parsing `rustc -vV`) and land in `target/release/`. musl builds cross-compile with `--target` and land in `target/<triple>/release/`. The two supported targets are built and located in two different ways.
- [special] TL080 `tools/recab/src/lib.rs:274-289` when the ESP or /var cannot be read during `trial`, a second path reads the firmware alone (`SystemdBoot::from_env().ok()...firmware_trial()`) and reboots; `:293-304` a third rule turns `Ignore` into `Fail` when state is missing and the firmware says one-shot. The trial verdict has three sources of truth.
- [special] TL086 `tools/recab/src/init.rs:92` `last_operation: "init-A"` is a literal outside `Operation::label` (`tools/recab/src/state.rs:127-135`), so the state file's operation vocabulary has an entry the state machine does not know.
- [special] TL089 `tools/recab/src/systemd_boot.rs:327` `/proc/cmdline` read errors become an empty string in `slot_device` (the running-root guard silently does nothing), while `active_slot` (`:478-487`) treats the same read failure as an error.
- [special] TL090 `tools/recab/src/systemd_boot.rs:442-445` `set_loader_efivar` writes a plain file when the directory is not efivarfs ("a scratch tree"): a production branch for the test tree.
- [special] TL113 `tools/recstrap/src/session.rs:233-243` when `unshare(CLONE_NEWNS)` fails with EPERM the session proceeds without a private namespace, warning only when root; mount cleanup then depends on the process not being killed, the guarantee the module header promises.
- [special] TL114 `tools/recstrap/src/session.rs:130-135` `mount_image` always mounts with `-o ro,loop`, also when the rootfs is the payload partition or a slot-staging block device handed in by recab, so a block device is wrapped in a loop device because the file case needs it.
- [special] TO1035 `tools/recab/src/lib.rs:176` the run lock is taken only when running as root (`geteuid() == 0`); a non-root `stage` gets a lock-free `System` and relies on the later `require_root` (`:217`) to stop it.
- [special] TO1061 `tools/recpart/src/preflight.rs:271-311,347-351` disk-in-use checks swallow errors: an unreadable `holders/` dir or `/proc/swaps` counts as "not in use", `canonicalize` failures fall back to the raw path, a missing disk name becomes `""`.
- [special] TO1088 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/apply.rs:228-231` a failed password write to the child's stdin is ignored (`let _ = pipe.write_all`), relying on the child's exit status to notice.
- [special] TO1101 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recchroot.rs:81-97` DNS inside the chroot is a bind of the host's resolv.conf, skipped silently when the target's resolv.conf is a symlink or `/etc/resolv.conf` cannot be canonicalized, and a bind failure is only a warning.
- [special] TO1136 `tools/recpart/src/inspect.rs:196,211-247,483-490` `wipefs --json` and `sfdisk --json` failures are swallowed (`.ok()?`), falling back to lsblk's single signature or no table data.
- [special] TO2038 `tools/recab/src/lib.rs:338-341` the failed-trial path drops its locked `System` and calls the public `rollback()`, which re-opens, re-reads and re-locks; between the two another recab run can interleave, and the plan is recomputed from a state the first half already wrote.
- [special] TO2043 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:155,164` `fs::metadata(..).ok()` on the slot device and on `/` turns stat failures into "not the same device", weakening the "rootfs is the slot partition / is mounted at /" guards.
- [special] TO2044 `tools/recab/src/store.rs:180,191,208` `let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp)` swallowed three times in the durable-write helpers.
- [special] TO2045 `tools/recab/src/store.rs:125` audit timestamp `unwrap_or_default()` writes epoch 0 when no transition time was stamped.
- [special] TO2046 `tools/recab/src/systemd_boot.rs:150,766` `read_optional(..).ok().flatten()` and `canonicalize(link).ok()` swallow IO errors in firmware-trial detection and root-device resolution.
- [special] TO2050 `tools/recab/src/systemd_boot.rs:80-86` `at_boot_root` builds the running-system backend from env and then patches three fields; `os_release`, `proc_cmdline` and `efivars` still point at the host, so `recab init` for a target mixes target and host paths.
- [special] TO2078 `tools/recpart/src/plan.rs:62`, `tools/recpart/src/inspect.rs:131` and `tools/recpart/src/manual.rs:340,904` `logical_sector_bytes.max(512)` silently clamps a 0 sector size from lsblk/inventory in four places.
- [special] TO2094 `tools/recpart/tests/loop_disk.rs:52-53` `let _ = umount -R` / `let _ = losetup -d` in Drop swallow cleanup failures.
- [special] TO2107 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recstrap/guards.rs:56-58` the rootfs candidate list picks the first that canonicalizes, `.ok()` dropping why the others failed; `:246-248` `unwrap_or(false)` turns a stat error into "not an image".
- [special] TO2109 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recstrap/main.rs:130` `rootfs_bytes: metadata(..).unwrap_or(0)` and `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recstrap/rootfs.rs:57,65,77` `let _ = pipe.read_to_string`, `line.is_err() => break`, `join().ok().unwrap_or_default()` swallow errors in progress reporting and cp's error text.
- [special] TO2132 `tools/recstrap/src/session.rs:58` private dirs go under `std::env::temp_dir()`, so the caller's `TMPDIR` steers where the rootfs image is mounted during extraction.
- [special] TO2149 `tools/recinit/src/main.rs:209-217` an unrecognised `--modules` preset name is parsed as a one-element custom module list (lenient parsing), so a mistyped preset fails later in `resolve` (`tools/recinit/src/modules.rs:236-240`) as a kernel missing a module of that name, not as an unknown preset.
- [special] TO3072 `tools/recinit/src/cpio.rs:89-104` archives every entry that is not a directory or symlink as a regular file (`0o100000`), with size 0 when it is not a file. Device nodes, FIFOs and sockets in the tree become empty regular files without an error.
- [special] TO3073 `tools/recinit/src/cpio.rs:63` builds archive names with `rel_path.display()` (lossy for non-UTF-8 names), and `:99,:101` truncate sizes with `as u32`, so a file over 4 GiB wraps silently instead of failing.
- [special] TO3107 `tools/recab/src/systemd_boot.rs:462-475` `loader_efivar` turns any read error (EACCES, EIO) into "variable not set" (`.ok()?`), and `read()` then infers default and one-shot from loader.conf as if the firmware had nothing set.
- [special] TO3136 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:440-443` the "live initramfs refused" guard's `same()` falls back to comparing raw paths when either `canonicalize` fails. `:750-752` `block_device("mount source", ..).ok()` drops the btrfs mount-source lookup error, so `source_dev` silently becomes `None`.
- [special] TO3142 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:333-336` `boot_is_mountpoint` is `false` on any metadata error, which only produces a warning. `Root::read`/`list` (`:110-129`) turn read errors into "missing" or empty, so an unreadable `/etc/group` reads as "group does not exist".
- [special] TO3163 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recchroot.rs:103-110` runs `session.close()?` before the chrooted command's status is inspected, so an unmount failure replaces the command's exit code. `:120` `u8::try_from(code).unwrap_or(1)` maps an out-of-range code to 1.
- [hidden] BR097 `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:13` keeps an allowlist of tool binaries (`recpart`, `recstrap`, `recchroot`, `recfstab`, `recsetup`, `recab`, `install-split`), and `distro/builder/src/recipe/tools.rs:10` `TOOLS` keeps the same set with crate paths. Co-change history shows the pair changed together 5 times.
- [hidden] BR101 `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:109` reads recab's service files from `tools/recab/src/services` inside the vendored source closure. That only works because `src/recipe/tools.rs:147` copies each crate's `src/` tree; moving the service files out of `src/` breaks the install silently (it falls through to the `trial_service` input error).
- [hidden] BR102 `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:23` hard-codes `/inputs/source`, while `distro/builder/src/recipe/tools.rs:95` writes `/inputs/source/vendor` into the closure's `.cargo/config.toml`, and `:325` names the input `source`. The input name and in-worker path are a three-way convention, and other recipes use `RUNTIME.paths.inputs` instead of the literal (`fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:10`).
- [hidden] BR103 `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:20` defaults `INSTALL_PATH` to `"usr/bin/" + bin`, and `distro/builder/src/recipe/tools.rs:313` computes the same default before always passing it. The default is written twice, and the recipe's copy never applies.
- [hidden] P054 The trial-service file location per init is written in `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:113-120` (copies `recab-trial.service`/`.openrc` to `usr/lib/systemd/system`/`etc/init.d`) and again in `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:97-101` and `:143-153` (`include_str!` of the same files); the recipe and the binary must agree on both paths for each distro.
- [hidden] RG2103 `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:36-41,58,66,92,102` `_verify` (the output of `<bin> --version` must start with `<name> `) runs in `is_built`, `build`, `is_installed` and `install`, so each phase runs the binary again. It also ties the recipe to the `--version` output format of every tool.
- [hidden] RG2104 `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:44,51,56,65,101` the choice between "source tree (`Cargo.toml`) or supplied program" is recomputed with the same `is_file` test in five hooks.
- [hidden] TL026 `tools/recab/src/init.rs:117-132` `os_identity` parses os-release, and `tools/recab/src/systemd_boot.rs:616-625` `os_release_id` parses it again; `recsetup/target.rs:272-281` and `recinit/src/payload.rs:213-214,314-319` read the same files with their own rules (four os-release readers).
- [hidden] TL027 `tools/recab/src/init.rs:134-153`, `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:183-186,240-243` and `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:706-722` each shell out to `blkid -s PARTUUID -o value` with their own error handling; `tools/recstrap/src/fstab.rs:276-287` is a fourth blkid reader.
- [hidden] TL062 `tools/recpart/src/preflight.rs:433-502` is a verbatim copy of `tools/recstrap/src/guard.rs:9-74` (`PROTECTED_PATHS`, `is_protected_path`, `resolve_for_protection`, `is_root`), down to the doc comment that says "Tools like recstrap and recchroot use this list".
- [hidden] TL063 three mount-table readers: `tools/recstrap/src/guard.rs:91-136` parses `/proc/self/mountinfo`, `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:303-324` parses `/proc/self/mounts`, `tools/recstrap/src/fstab.rs:181-195` runs `findmnt`; each has its own unescaping.
- [hidden] TL064 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:46-49` EROFS superblock offset and magic, and `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recstrap/guards.rs:21-22` the same values; recab checks the image itself and then hands it to recstrap, which checks again.
- [hidden] TL065 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recstrap/rootfs.rs:18-22` extracts with GNU `cp -aT --preserve=xattr`, which is why AcornOS must install `coreutils` (`alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:53-54`), while `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:358-401` `copy_tree` reimplements copying "rather than with cp, whose options differ between GNU coreutils and busybox"; the two tools take opposite positions on the same portability problem.
- [hidden] TL066 `tools/recab/src/store.rs:167-216` `write_durable`/`copy_durable` and `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/apply.rs:266-287` `write` are two temp-file-and-rename writers with different durability (recsetup does not fsync the directory).
- [hidden] TO1028 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:224-233` stage shells out to `recstrap --quiet --rootfs <img> <dir>` (binary overridable by `RECAB_RECSTRAP_BIN`); recstrap's CLI flags are a contract recab depends on with no shared type, the mirror of recsetup calling `recab init` by argv (TL071).
- [hidden] TO2006 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:521-553` private `path_str`/`run`/`output` command helpers, and `tools/recab/src/init.rs:134-139` / `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:200-206` each spawn commands their own way with different error text and exit-code handling (`unwrap_or(1)` for signal deaths).
- [hidden] TO2081 `tools/recpart/src/inspect.rs:577-585` `block_device_size` (sysfs `size` * 512) re-implements `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:286-300` `block_device_bytes`; same rule, separate crates.
- [hidden] TO2082 `tools/recpart/src/inspect.rs:145,459,470` recpart upper-cases table ids, type GUIDs and PARTUUIDs; recab lower-cases PARTUUIDs (`tools/recab/src/init.rs:140-142`, `tools/recab/src/systemd_boot.rs:746`); consumers comparing a recpart inventory with recab's entries must know each tool's case rule.
- [hidden] TO2101 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recstrap/guards.rs:271-279` refuses an image file whose name does not end in `.erofs`; `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:92-98` accepts any file with the EROFS magic and then hands it to recstrap, so recab's own input check passes inputs its callee refuses (same validation, two strictnesses).
- [hidden] TO3113 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:183-186,240-243` reads the slot's PARTUUID from blkid twice (once to check, once to write fstab) and uses it raw, while `tools/recab/src/init.rs:134-142` lowercases the same value for boot entries. PARTUUID normalisation differs inside one crate, and the fstab root line and the entry can spell the partition in different cases.
- [hidden] TO3118 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:220` mounts the slot at the fixed `/run/recab/stage-<a|b>`, while the run lock (`/run/lock/recab.lock`) and the state paths are defined in `tools/recab/src/store.rs:40-42`. recab's runtime paths have two owners, and only the store's are overridable.
- [hidden] TO3155 `tools/recstrap/src/fstab.rs:299-319` `unescape_octal` (fstab) and `tools/recstrap/src/guard.rs:115-136` `unescape_mountinfo` decode the same `\NNN` octal escapes twice in one crate. The fstab copy pushes each byte as a `char`, which mangles escaped non-ASCII bytes, while the mountinfo copy keeps bytes. `fstab.rs:423-442` `unescape_raw` is a third decoder (`\xNN`).
- [hidden] TO3160 `tools/recstrap/src/fstab.rs:294-296` `--write` replaces fstab via `fs::write(tmp)` + `rename` with a fixed `fstab.recfstab.tmp` name and no fsync. It is a third "replace a target file" helper beside recab's `write_durable` and recsetup's `write`, each with different durability.
- [hidden] TO3164 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recchroot.rs:50-62` (root's shell), `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:613-621` (`account_names`), `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:419-450` (`merge_accounts`) and `tools/recinit/src/payload.rs:193-207` (hand-written passwd/group/shadow) each parse or write the same colon-separated account files with their own code.
- [needless] TL060 `tools/recinit/src/modules.rs:110-118` `ModulePreset::Custom` and `main.rs:209-217` let `--modules` take any list, but the live build always uses `ModulePreset::Live` (`main.rs:135`, no flag) and the release passes no `--modules` for the install build; a parameter for no caller.
- [needless] TL070 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/apply.rs:207-213` and `:226-233` recsetup runs chroot steps by spawning the `recchroot` binary (found next to its own exe, else on PATH) although `install_tools::session::Session` in the same crate is what recchroot itself uses (`tools/recstrap/src/bin/recchroot.rs:76-109`).
- [needless] TL091 `tools/recab/src/systemd_boot.rs:88-113`, `store.rs:35-44`, `init_system.rs:41-86,186-188,211-213,274,326-327`, `stage.rs:224` thirteen `RECAB_*` environment overrides read deep inside the modules (paths, init system, systemctl/rc-status/reboot/recstrap binaries) exist for `tests/lifecycle.rs:126-137`; every module reads process globals set by the test harness.
- [surplus] RG1104 `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:122-123`: no caller ever supplies the `trial_service` input override. A repo-wide search finds the name only in this recipe.
- [surplus] TL045 `tools/recinit/src/main.rs:52-58` and `tiny.rs:30-34` `--rootfs-path`/`--live-overlay-image-path` parameters exist for paths that `recinit::live` fixes (`ROOTFS_ISO_PATH`, `LIVE_OVERLAYFS_ISO_PATH`) and that reciso hard-wires where it copies the payloads (`tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:261,266`). A value other than the default makes the live init look for a file reciso never writes, and nothing passes it.
- [surplus] TL061 `tools/recinit/src/main.rs:101-110` and `:169-203` a `modules` subcommand that only prints the preset lists.
- [surplus] TL105 `tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:336-355` a fallback UKI built when no UKI is requested, and `UkiSource::Prebuilt` (`:84`, `main.rs:66-67`), have no caller; the release always passes three `--build-uki` specs (`build-release.sh:297-299`).
- [surplus] TO1036 `tools/recab/src/lib.rs:215-216` `plan` returns `Option<Transition>` and `stage` `.expect`s it is always `Some`; the `Option` exists for the other requests only.
- [surplus] TO1129 `tools/recuki/src/main.rs:1-79` the `recuki` binary has no caller (the release hook and fsdbg use the library through reciso); its help examples brand it `LevitateOS`.
- [surplus] TO2138 `tools/recinit/src/tiny.rs:37` `TinyConfig::boot_devices` is a config parameter whose only producer is `default_boot_devices()` (`tools/recinit/src/tiny.rs:51-56`), a wrapper that copies `BOOT_DEVICE_PROBE_ORDER` (called once at `tools/recinit/src/main.rs:134`); nothing passes another list.

Nothing reads `product-artifact-tag:` back, yet the release hook must pass it (`build-release.sh:263`).

No caller passes a template: not the release hook (`build-release.sh:257-263`) and not the crate's tests.
