### G19 An A/B trial is one contract on both init systems, and the carried config is declared

Solves **36** problems (6 parity, 12 special, 18 hidden).

- **Design:** The image enables the trial service on both; recab's init adapter has three operations; the trial verdict is one function (`recab health --json`), shared by the A/B trial, the ab-update scenario and ginux-boot. What survives an A/B update is a list the image declares (it includes `sudoers.d`, WiFi and NetworkManager config), and recab carries exactly that. The scenario checks trial outcomes on both distros, including a trial that fails before init.
- **Done when:** `init_system.rs` has no per-init carry lists; both distros pass a failed-trial and a healthy-trial scenario; LevitateOS keeps `sudoers.d` and AcornOS keeps its WiFi config across an update.
- **Depends on:** G15, G17, G18
- **Decisions:** Which config files A/B updates carry (default: the image's declared list, including privilege-tool drop-ins and network config).
- **Merges area designs:** TO-05 (tools), TO-06 (tools), TV-07 (testing-vm) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] P057 `tools/recab/src/services/recab-trial.service` runs as a supervised systemd service (`Type=exec`, sysinit) on LevitateOS, while `recab-trial.openrc` backgrounds `recab trial &` and returns `eend 0` immediately on AcornOS, logging to `/dev/kmsg`; on AcornOS a crashed supervisor is invisible to the init system.
- [parity] TI002 `testing/install-tests/src/distro/acorn.rs:25` `uses_initramfs() == false` for AcornOS: the installed AcornOS boots without an initramfs, LevitateOS with one. Every downstream check branches on it (TI003, TI004, TI005, TI006), so AcornOS installs are checked for less.
- [parity] TI003 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:122` checks that slot B's staged entry has `rd.emergency=reboot` only when `uses_initramfs()`. On AcornOS nothing reboots a trial that fails before init (no initramfs emergency path), and nothing tests one.
- [parity] TL006 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:118-131` the configuration `stage` carries into a new slot differs by init system in ways that are really distro/package choices: systemd carries `etc/systemd/system` and NetworkManager connections; OpenRC carries `etc/network/interfaces`, `etc/inittab`, `etc/doas.conf`, `etc/runlevels`, `etc/conf.d`, `etc/doas.d`. AcornOS joins WiFi with wpa_supplicant/dhcpcd (`distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:55-61`) whose configs (`etc/wpa_supplicant/`, `etc/dhcpcd.conf`) are not carried, so AcornOS loses saved WiFi on an A/B update while LevitateOS keeps NetworkManager connections.
- [parity] TL007 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:128-129` AcornOS carries `etc/doas.conf`/`etc/doas.d` (the admin rule) from the old slot over the new image's, while LevitateOS does not carry `etc/sudoers`/`etc/sudoers.d`; the privilege policy follows the old machine on one distro and the new image on the other.
- [parity] TO1008 `tools/recab/src/services/recab-trial.openrc:5-7` vs `recab-trial.service:4-6,14` the trial service is ordered differently per init: systemd starts it under `sysinit.target` with `DefaultDependencies=no`, `Conflicts=shutdown.target`/`Before=shutdown.target`; OpenRC only says `after sysfs` and has no stop handling, so a shutdown during the trial wait is handled on one distro and not the other.
- [special] TL008 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:128` OpenRC carries `etc/inittab` over the image's, so a new image cannot change its inittab (consoles, getty, init style) through an A/B update on AcornOS; systemd has no equivalent override.
- [special] TL010 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:189-197` OpenRC reboot picks `openrc-shutdown -r now` or busybox `reboot` by reading `/proc/1/comm`; a second branch inside the OpenRC branch for which PID 1 the image ships.
- [special] TL079 `tools/recab/src/lib.rs:409` `InitSystem::running()` failure is swallowed into "no trial options" (`unwrap_or(&[])`), so a set-next on a host whose init cannot be told schedules a trial without the systemd reboot options.
- [special] TL082 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:217-236` and `:281-303` startup waits poll with fixed sleeps (250 ms for systemd's child, 500 ms for OpenRC's state dir); `tools/recab/src/main.rs:77` a 900 s default trial timeout shared by both init systems.
- [special] TL083 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:287` and `:343-357` OpenRC "finished" means softlevel `default`, empty `starting/`, and no process named `openrc` in `/proc`; a scan of every process's `comm`, a heuristic with no systemd counterpart.
- [special] TL084 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:317-341` OpenRC failures merge `failed/` entries with `rc-status --crashed` output (errors ignored), suffixing `(crashed)`; systemd uses `list-units --failed` (`:252-269`, errors become an empty list). Two failure formats in the same `TrialRecord.detail`.
- [special] TL087 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:236-239` the new slot's trial service is enabled only if it is enabled on the running root, a conditional that hides a slot without the service.
- [special] TO1016 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:285,312-326` OpenRC startup probing swallows read errors: an unreadable `softlevel` reads as empty (polls until timeout), `dir_is_empty` treats an unreadable `starting/` as empty (`map_or(true, ..)`), and `service_names` turns any unreadable state or runlevel directory into "no services" (`unwrap_or_default`), so a broken probe reads as a healthy, finished startup and commits the trial.
- [special] TO2026 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:231-232` `let _ = child.kill(); let _ = child.wait();` swallowed on timeout.
- [special] TO2040 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:420-425` `merge_accounts` returns `Ok(())` when the running account file cannot be read for any reason (not only NotFound), so a permission/IO error silently drops the machine's users from the new slot.
- [special] TV1087 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:110-112` runs `ssh-keygen -A` because "installed systems leave sshd off", so the host-key carry check depends on the scenario creating the keys itself.
- [special] TV2054 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:244` `identity()` runs `cat /etc/machine-id 2>/dev/null`, so a missing machine-id drops out of the identity string on both slots and the identity-carry comparison still passes.
- [hidden] TI060 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:203` evidence says "across 7 boots", a count hand-kept in sync with the sequence of `boot`/`run_unattended` calls above it.
- [hidden] TI061 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:301-302` the doc says "the VM runs with -no-reboot, so QEMU exits". VMs are libvirt domains with `on_reboot=destroy` now (`tools/recqemu/src/libvirt.rs:116-119`), so the comment describes a mechanism that is gone.
- [hidden] TL009 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:23-33` the carried list for every system includes the systemd-only `etc/vconsole.conf`, beside the init-specific lists in `init_system.rs:118-131`, while the OpenRC keymap (`etc/conf.d/keymaps`) is carried only through the OpenRC `etc/conf.d` dir. Whether a file is systemd-only, OpenRC-only or shared is split across two files and decided by where someone put it.
- [hidden] TL024 `tools/recab/src/systemd_boot.rs:29` `ALL_TRIAL_OPTIONS` repeats the values of `InitSystem::trial_options` (`init_system.rs:111`), and `rd.emergency=reboot` is defined a third time as `EMERGENCY_REBOOT_OPTION` in `tools/recinit/src/payload.rs:141` (with `ConditionKernelCommandLine=rd.emergency=reboot` spelled again at `:151` and `:159`); adding a trial option needs all of them changed.
- [hidden] TL073 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:37-38`, `:96-102` and `:143-153` recab embeds its trial service files and their install paths, and `distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:108-124` maps init system to the same service file and destination path again; `install_trial_service` (write) and `enable_trial_service` (require packaged) are two ways to get the same file, and the service file header says "Installed by `recab install-unit`" (`tools/recab/src/services/recab-trial.service:1`) though Recipe packages it.
- [hidden] TL074 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:89-94` `trial_service_link` is `pub` and consumed by install-tests (`testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:109`, `scenarios/mod.rs:564`), and `InitSystem` itself is the install-tests' distro init enum (`testing/install-tests/src/distro/acorn.rs:30-31`, `levitate.rs:24-25`); the test harness depends on recab's internals to describe a distro.
- [hidden] TO1010 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:92` enables the OpenRC trial service in the `boot` runlevel (`etc/runlevels/boot/recab-trial`), while the AcornOS guide (`docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/15-acorn-installation-boot.ts:26`) says recab's trial service "is added to the default runlevel"; the doc restates a fact this file owns and has drifted.
- [hidden] TO1141 `tools/recab/README.md:72-80` restates by hand the list of what an update carries over (hostname, machine-id, accounts merge, `/etc/systemd/system` + NetworkManager on systemd, runlevels/conf.d/interfaces/inittab/doas on OpenRC), which the code owns in `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:23-40` and `init_system.rs:118-131`; a fourth copy beside the two user guides (P039).
- [hidden] TO2030 `tools/recab/src/main.rs:137` comment "The journal's record of the decision" and `tools/recab/REQUIREMENTS.md:226-227` name the systemd journal for output that on OpenRC goes to `/dev/kmsg` via the openrc script.
- [hidden] TO2031 `tools/recab/src/state.rs:254-265` the shared `Startup` enum is documented in systemd vocabulary ("failed units", "emergency/rescue target", "systemd unreachable") although `openrc_startup` produces the same variants.
- [hidden] TO2032 `tools/recab/REQUIREMENTS.md:170-185` REQ-SM-021/022 are written in systemd terms (`recab-trial.service`, "degraded or in maintenance", trial entries carry `rd.emergency=reboot`/`systemd.crash_action=reboot`), which `init_system.rs:112` deliberately does not do on OpenRC; the requirement and the AcornOS behaviour disagree.
- [hidden] TO2033 `tools/recab/README.md:151-152` "To keep committing by hand, disable `recab-trial.service`" gives only the systemd unit name; AcornOS's `rc-update del recab-trial boot` is not mentioned.
- [hidden] TO2066 `tools/recab/tests/lifecycle.rs:620-628,846-853` the install-unit tests assert the service paths and link targets (`etc/systemd/system/sysinit.target.wants/...`, `etc/runlevels/boot/recab-trial`) by literal, a third copy next to `init_system.rs` and `install-tools.rhai`.
- [hidden] TO3084 `tools/recab/src/services/recab-trial.openrc:11` and `recab-trial.service:10` hard-code `/usr/bin/recab`, while the recipe that installs recab takes an overridable `INSTALL_PATH` (`distro/builder/recipes/install-tools.rhai:20`). Neither service passes `--timeout`, so the 900 s default in `tools/recab/src/main.rs` is the only trial deadline.
- [hidden] TO3089 `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:328-352` the OpenRC health rule counts as failed every service listed in `sysinit`/`boot`/`default` that is not in OpenRC's `started/` or `inactive/` state directory. The verdict depends on OpenRC's state-directory names and on which services the AcornOS recipes enable (both owned elsewhere), and a service left stopped on purpose fails the trial.
- [hidden] TO3094 `tools/recab/src/stage.rs:23-37` `CARRIED_FILES`/`ACCOUNT_FILES` restate which files recsetup's install steps write (hostname, locale, keymap, accounts, machine-id: `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:274-330,668`, through `recipe-system-setup`). If recsetup gains a step that writes another file, that file is silently lost on the next A/B update.
- [hidden] TV1080 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:122`, `:127`, `:177` assert recab's boot-entry option strings (`panic=`, `rd.emergency=reboot`) by literal; the options are owned by `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs` / `systemd_boot.rs`.
- [hidden] TV1088 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:5` and `:247` say `recab-trial.service` commits the trial; on AcornOS the commit is done by the OpenRC `recab-trial` script, so the docs name one init system's unit.

### G20 Tool contracts are generated from one typed definition

Solves **63** problems (11 special, 48 hidden, 3 needless, 1 surplus).

- **Design:** Every machine-read output of a tool (JSON, error codes, refusals, status fields, argv) comes from one typed definition; each tool exports its own contract and `cargo xtask gen` iterates one export list; the TUIs get tool rules, sizes, codes and messages generated, and their checks run the real tool on a fake root or from recordings.
- **Done when:** `cargo xtask check` passes "generated files current" (it fails at baseline); no TS copy of a tool constant (e.g. EROFS magic) or a shell re-implementation as a stand-in.
- **Depends on:** G17, G18
- **Merges area designs:** TO-13 (tools), XT-11 (xtask), UD-09 (tui-docs) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [special] TO1090 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/main.rs:209-218` an `erased::Name` blanket trait serializes any value to JSON to print its enum name, swallowing failures to `""`.
- [special] TO3079 `tools/recinit/tests/build_tiny.rs:95-107` the negative test pins the English error text "neither a module nor a built-in" from `tools/recinit/src/modules.rs:238`.
- [special] TO3137 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/plan.rs:457-465` builds the fix text's CLI flag from the human `what` string (`--{first word}`). "kernel" gives `--kernel` and "initrd" gives `--initrd` only while each description starts with its flag name.
- [special] TO3165 `tools/recstrap/tests/recstrap.rs:20-46` skips the not-root assertion when run as root. The tests pin `E0NN:` prefixes, English text ("must run as root", "root") and literal exit codes (`Some(8)`) rather than the crate's `Code` values.
- [special] U074 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/domain/setup.ts:28-30` `efiFilesMb` assumes 128 MiB per kernel+initramfs for every distro; AcornOS boots kernel-only (`15-acorn-installation-boot.ts:26`), so the EFI room estimate is LevitateOS's.
- [special] UD1107 `tui/apps/runtime/recab-tui/src/domain/recab.ts:12-19` and `src/app/recab-runner.ts:19-28` invalid `recab status --json` output is swallowed into null and reported as a version mismatch; the only shape check is `active_slot`.
- [special] UD2063 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/presentation/pages.tsx:397` says "`A/B` is what this live system was started with". `defaultMode` is `--mode` or recpart's `DEFAULT_INSTALL_MODE` (`src/main.tsx:48`), and nothing on either ISO passes `--mode` (install-hub runs bare `["disk-plan"]`, `install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:94`). The sentence states a boot-time fact that no code provides.
- [special] UD2064 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/presentation/disk-plan-screen.tsx:282-283` picks `INSTALL_MODES[first ? 0 : 1]` and `METHODS[first ? 0 : 1]`. The two-choice page assumes recpart has exactly two modes, so a third generated mode could never be picked.
- [special] UD2079 `tui/apps/install/base-system/fixtures/recstrap:40` fakes a copy failure on `$target/usr/lib64/libLLVM.so.19`, a Fedora-layout path, in a stand-in that serves both distros' checks.
- [special] UD3158 `tui/apps/runtime/recab-tui/fixtures/recab:15-22` keeps the stand-in's slot state in `${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/recab-fixture-$PPID` and loads it with `. "$file"`, shell-sourcing a file in a shared temp dir keyed by a reusable parent pid. It also deletes other runs' state files whenever `kill -0` fails for their pid.
- [special] XT1125 `xtask/src/tasks/generate.rs:27` `fs::read_to_string(path).ok()` treats an unreadable generated file the same as a missing one and rewrites it, or reports it stale under `--check`.
- [hidden] TL067 three error systems for one install flow: `tools/recab/src/error.rs:3-38` (classes, `RECAB_ERR_*` tags, exit codes 13/20-23), `tools/recstrap/src/error.rs:12-56` (`Code`, exit statuses, `recsetup` `ErrorPayload` in `apply.rs:46-64`), and recpart's `RecpartError`/`ErrorCode` E0xx with its own `ERROR_SCHEMA_VERSION` (`tools/recpart/src/types.rs:8`); front ends parse each differently.
- [hidden] TL068 `tools/recab/src/error.rs:33-37` the `[RECAB_ERR_*]` tag text and exit codes are parsed by regex in `tui/apps/runtime/recab-tui/src/domain/recab.ts:144-154`, which also hard-codes `/var/lib/recab/state.json` (`:130`); the tag format is a string contract with no shared definition.
- [hidden] TL069 schema version constants are scattered per tool and per payload (`recsetup/plan.rs:14`, `apply.rs:17-18`, `recsetup/main.rs:116` inline `schema_version: 1`, `recpart/src/types.rs:5-10`, `recab/src/state.rs:14`); the inspect payload's version is a literal at the call site.
- [hidden] TO1053 `tools/recpart/src/types.rs:390-402` `LayoutAction::fields` hand-lists each action's JSON fields, a copy of the enum's variants (`:463-516`) kept because serde cannot deny unknown fields on an internally tagged enum; adding a field to a variant without updating the list makes the layout reader (`cli.rs:262`) reject it.

- [hidden] UD2080 `tui/apps/install/base-system/src/app/runner.ts:34-52` parses `recfstab --json` by hand (`JSON.parse`, schema check, "printed no JSON"), while disk-plan uses the generated `readRecpartOutput` (`tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/generated/recpart.ts`).
