- [hidden] UD3070 `tui/apps/install/base-system/src/presentation/use-base.ts:97` checks `schema_version === STRAP_SCHEMA_VERSION` only on recstrap's `start` event. The `checked` event (`:55`) and the `done` event (`:107`) are accepted at any schema, although all three carry `schema_version` (`src/generated/recstrap.ts:6,24,38`).
- [hidden] UD3075 `tui/apps/install/base-system/src/presentation/pages.tsx:161,197,299` restate recstrap's policy in UI prose: "the target must be an empty, mounted filesystem with room", "keeping ownership, permissions, capabilities and extended attributes", "recstrap won't copy onto it again until it's empty". recstrap owns these rules.
- [hidden] UD3085 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/machine.ts:14` `DISK_CHANGED = "E023"` retypes recpart's error code (also in the comments at `src/app/plan-runner.ts:27` and `fixtures/recpart:17`, and `exit 23` at `fixtures/recpart:92`). The generated recpart module exports no error-code constants.
- [hidden] UD3087 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/fixtures/record.sh:16-19` re-records each fixture disk with `recpart inspect --inventory inspect-<disk>.json`, reading the very file it then overwrites. The fixture disks have no source outside their own recordings, so a change to recpart's inventory format can only be followed by editing them by hand.
- [hidden] UD3088 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/fixtures/recpart:4-7` lists the fixture disks as vdz, vdy, vdx, vdw, vdv. `record.sh:15-16` also records `vdu` (a bare LUKS container), so the stand-in's header is out of date with its recorder.
- [hidden] UD3098 `tui/apps/runtime/recab-tui/src/domain/recab.ts:129-134` `ERROR_HELP` keys remediation text by recab's exit codes 13/20/22/23 and restates recab's runtime needs ("blkid, mkfs.ext4, mount and recstrap", "/sys/firmware/efi/efivars"). The codes and dependencies are owned by `tools/recab`, and nothing generated carries them.
- [hidden] UD3100 `tui/apps/runtime/recab-tui/src/app/recab-runner.ts:31-41` copies recab's EROFS superblock magic and offset ("as recab checks") to pre-check image paths. This is a third copy beside recab's and recstrap's own (TL064).
- [hidden] X081 `xtask/src/tasks/generate.rs:65,72` writes into other crates' trees by path (`tools/recpart/docs/frontend-handoff/schemas`, `tools/recab/docs`), `:148` into `docs/content/src/tool-help.ts`, and `:101` into `<app>/src/generated/<tool>.ts`.
- [hidden] X083 `xtask/src/tasks/generate.rs:97-104` the `modules` table ties manifest `levitate.tools` names to the two declaration sets xtask builds; an app that lists any other tool silently gets no generated types.
- [hidden] X084 `xtask/src/tasks/generate.rs:111-118` `DOCUMENTED_TOOLS` restates which binaries each package builds (e.g. recstrap → recstrap, recchroot, recfstab, recsetup), which the packages' Cargo manifests own; `:136` runs them from `target/debug` regardless of profile.
- [hidden] XT1122 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/mod.rs:307-308` passes each manifest `levitate.tools` name to `cargo build -p`, puts `target/debug` on PATH (`:313`) to run it as a binary, and `generate.rs:110` uses the same names as module keys. Manifest tool name, cargo package name and binary name are assumed identical, so a tool that is a second binary of a package (e.g. `recfstab` from recstrap, `generate.rs:129`) cannot be listed.
- [hidden] XT2083 `xtask/src/tasks/generate.rs:66-76` and `:79-107` list each recpart/recab/recstrap output type twice, once for `write_schema` and once for `types_from`. Adding an output type takes two edits in xtask, and a type added to only one list gets a schema without TS types or the reverse.
- [hidden] XT2085 `xtask/src/tasks/generate.rs:198-224` `RECSTRAP_OUTPUT` is a hand-written TypeScript reader for recstrap/recfstab output, embedded as a Rust string. It encodes their stream convention (one JSON event per line; on failure the last `\n{` object on stderr after mount/cp noise), which recstrap's Rust code owns, and nothing checks that the two agree (X082 covers only the recpart reader).
- [hidden] XT3069 `xtask/Cargo.toml:27` depends on the package `recstrap`, but its lib is named `install_tools` (`tools/recstrap/Cargo.toml:10-11`), so `xtask/src/tasks/generate.rs:74-106` imports it under that name. One crate goes by two names, and nothing at the dependency line shows the second one.
- [needless] X080 `xtask/src/tasks/generate.rs:65-95` xtask names every recpart/recab output type, schema-version constant, value enum and the schema file names, so each change to recpart's or recab's public JSON needs an xtask edit; xtask depends on both crates with their `contracts` feature (`xtask/Cargo.toml:25-26`).
- [needless] X082 `xtask/src/tasks/generate.rs:156-183` the TypeScript reader for recpart's output protocol (result on stdout, error document on stderr, schema-version check) is a Rust string constant in xtask: recpart's I/O contract and the TUI kit's parsing both live in the front door.
- [needless] X085 `xtask/src/tasks/generate.rs:48` `gen` (and `gen --check`) runs `bun install` as a side effect because the TS formatter is a workspace dependency (`:255` `node_modules/.bin/oxfmt`); a "check" command mutates `node_modules`.
- [surplus] UD2088 `tui/apps/runtime/recab-tui/src/generated/recab.ts:49` `SLOT_HELP = { A: "", B: "" }` (emitted by `xtask/src/tasks/generate.rs:99`) is empty generated help that nothing reads.

### G21 The install screen is declared once and its parts are built one way

Solves **61** problems (3 parity, 19 special, 31 hidden, 5 needless, 3 surplus).

- **Design:** The install screen is one fixed composition (launcher, split pane, hub, docs, extra apps) declared once for both distros, with its interface constants in the contract and a smoke test that exercises the real layout and left program. The split pane owns geometry and terminal capabilities and always lands in its restartable state. The install TUI binary is a declared, reproducible build. TUI app discovery leaves the contract for the builder; one TUI launch definition serves `tui`, `inspect` and `check tui`.
- **Done when:** Both `products.toml` install-screen blocks are one declaration; the smoke runs the hub and docs; one launch environment for TUIs.
- **Depends on:** G04, G05
- **Merges area designs:** BC-10 (builder-core), BC-11 (builder-core), BC-12 (builder-core), CV-14 (contract-variants), CV-15 (contract-variants), UD-12 (tui-docs), XT-13 (xtask) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] P038 `01-getting-started/11-acorn-installation.ts:44` tells AcornOS users to partition with `disk-plan` or `recpart`; `02-installation.ts:31` mentions only `recpart`, though both ISOs ship `disk-plan` (`ring2/products.toml:11`).
- [parity] P039 `16-acorn-post-installation.ts:59` says A/B carries "hostname, accounts, enabled services and SSH host keys", `07-post-installation.ts:79` adds "network connections"; matches `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:123-129` where AcornOS's WiFi/dhcpcd config is not carried (P056).
- [parity] UD2024 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/presentation/ink/screens/install-viewer.tsx:93-97` copies by writing an OSC 52 escape. On the live ISO the docs app runs inside install-split-pane, which renders through `vt100` 0.15 (`distro/builder/install-split-pane/Cargo.toml:19`). That crate handles only OSC 0/1/2, and the pane has no clipboard handling in `src/`, so the escape is dropped. The advertised "ready to copy into the shell beside it" (`package.json:108`) cannot work on either distro's install screen.
- [special] BC1127 `distro/builder/install-split-pane/src/main.rs:363` smoke types `printf '<marker>'\r` into the left pane, so the smoke check only passes when the left program is a POSIX shell, which ties it to `install-ux.sh:18` running `"$SHELL" -l` there.
- [special] BC1134 `distro/builder/guest/install-ux/install-ux.sh:21-24` recognises the serial console by the pattern `/dev/ttyS*`, separate from the variant's `install_ux.consoles` list, and swallows a missing boot-injection file with `2>/dev/null`, falling back to a shell.
- [special] BC2009 `distro/builder/install-split-pane/src/main.rs:298-301` `try_wait().ok().flatten()` turns a wait error into "no status", which the match at `:302-307` reports as a plain "exited".
- [special] BC2010 `distro/builder/install-split-pane/src/main.rs:249-250` `Process::stop` discards `kill()` and `wait()` errors (`let _ =`), `:233` discards the failed `Closed` send, and `:674-675` discards terminal-restore errors.
- [special] BC2011 `distro/builder/install-split-pane/src/main.rs:316-331` a write or resize error sets `ended` but leaves `process` as `Some`. The title then says "Enter restarts" (`:506`), but `:455` restarts only when `process.is_none()`, so Enter is written to the broken PTY instead.
- [special] BC2080 `distro/builder/src/tui_binary.rs:95-105` the cross-target `bun build --compile --target=…` downloads the target runtime from the network at build time, unpinned and unhashed, with the host's bun version (`:74`), so the TUI binary on AcornOS depends on whatever bun fetched that day.
- [special] BC3098 `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:122-127` `ships()` matches a TUI app's required tool by Cargo package name only. An action that ships just one binary of a multi-binary package (for example `recstrap`, which also builds `recsetup`, `recfstab` and `recchroot`) satisfies the check for every binary in that package.
- [special] BC3110 `distro/builder/src/tui_binary.rs:86-88` writes the bun entry file to a host `tempfile::tempdir()` (location set by `TMPDIR`), and `entry_source` (`:162-172`) imports each app by its absolute checkout path (`app.dir.join(&app.entry)`). The compiled bundle's inputs therefore include the checkout and temp-dir locations.
- [special] BP111 `distro/builder/src/tui_binary.rs:19` names the shared binary `usr/local/libexec/levitate-tui` on both distros. AcornOS ships a LevitateOS-branded internal path.
- [special] BP114 `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:85` `.expect("the docs app is among the apps")` relies on the insertion at `:58-61` having run first, a call-order invariant enforced by panic.
- [special] C095 `acorn/ring2/products.toml:9`: AcornOS's docs entry needs `page = "acorn-installation"` and `args = ["--product", "acorn"]`. LevitateOS's uses `page = "installation"` with no args. `install-docs` already detects the product from os-release (`tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/docs.ts:30`), and docs pages are duplicated per distro (`docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/10-17-acorn-*.ts`).
- [special] UD2065 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/presentation/disk-plan-screen.tsx:106-109` narrows the rail to 28 columns below 110 terminal columns ("the install screen's half"), and `:94` sets `DISK_CARD_COLUMNS = 40`. base-system (`base-screen.tsx:46`) and recab-tui (`recab-screen.tsx:42`) repeat the same `< 110` threshold. These thresholds encode install-split-pane's 50% split (`distro/builder/install-split-pane/src/lib.rs:9`) without referring to it.
- [special] UD2135 `tui/kit/core/src/app/app-provider.tsx:49-54` falls back to 80x24 "when stdout is not a terminal", but `run-app.tsx:135-137` exits before rendering whenever stdin or stdout is not a TTY, and `AppProvider` is rendered only from `runApp`. The documented case cannot occur, and the fallback only hides a terminal that reports 0 columns.
- [special] UD3107 `tui/kit/core/src/surfaces/shell.tsx:93-94` `fitFooter` drops every footer line past half the terminal (`FOOTER_MAX_SHARE`, `:23`). Apps append the notice segment last (e.g. `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/presentation/hub-screen.tsx:95`), so at small sizes the notice is the first thing cut, with no sign that it was.
- [special] XT1127 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/mod.rs:18-39` parses `--stand-ins` and `--story` by hand because `TuiCmd::Run` is an `external_subcommand` (`xtask/src/cli/types.rs:166`). Clap's help and validation never see these flags.
- [special] XT1136 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/inspect.rs:42,70-75` uses the story name as an output file name (`"{name}.txt"`, `.ansi`, `.transcript`). Story names contain spaces and colons ("edit: nothing can shrink"), and a `/` in one would write outside the run dir.
- [special] XT1137 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/inspect.rs:27-32` silently clamps `--columns`, `--rows`, `--settle-ms` and `--timeout-seconds` of 0 to 1.
- [special] XT2108 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/inspect.rs:50-51` the run dir is `<app>-<unix seconds>` (`:270-274`), and `create_dir_all` accepts an existing dir, so two inspects of one app in the same second write into the same dir and overwrite each other's screens.
- [special] XT2114 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/check.rs:201-206` every file in a `snapshots/<size>/` dir counts as a page named by its file stem, so a stray file (an editor backup, a `.orig`) becomes a page argument that is rendered and compared.
- [hidden] BC2004 `distro/builder/install-split-pane/src/main.rs:107` (`program_exists`, a Rust PATH/is_file walk at `:136-142`) and `:352` (`command -v` in sh) check the same "right program exists" fact twice, by two different rules, in one smoke run.
- [hidden] BC2006 `distro/builder/install-split-pane/src/main.rs:38-39,352` the smoke markers `__LEVITATE_SPLIT_LEFT_OK__`/`__LEVITATE_SPLIT_RIGHT_OK__`/`__LEVITATE_SPLIT_RIGHT_MISSING__` carry LevitateOS branding in a binary both variants ship (`levitate/ring2/products.toml:95-96`, `acorn/ring2/products.toml:101-102`), and the MISSING marker is an inline literal next to the two constants.
- [hidden] BC2012 `distro/builder/install-split-pane/src/main.rs:345-350` the smoke runs both panes at a fixed 24x80 `PtySize` instead of the sizes from its own lib's `screen_layout`/`pane_terminal_size` (`install-split-pane/src/lib.rs:17,36`), which `cargo xtask check tui` uses to size the docs pane. The smoke tests a pane size the real screen never gives.
- [hidden] BC2128 `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:49-54` `write_payload` requires the split pane to already be in the overlay. That holds only because `pipeline/products.rs:422` runs `add_required_tools` before `write_payload` at `:430`; the call order is the contract.
- [hidden] BC3097 `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:86-90` passes `--title "<os> install guide"` to the docs app as a hard-coded flag, while the page flag is read from the app's manifest (`:92-97`, "The flag that opens a page is the one the app's manifest declares"). The same app's CLI is reached through two policies, so renaming `--title` in the app breaks the builder silently.
- [hidden] BC3158 `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:22` `LAUNCHER = "usr/local/bin/install-ux"` spells out the directory `usr/local/bin` itself, while `distro/builder/src/tui_binary.rs:17` `BIN_DIR = "usr/local/bin"` is a separate constant for the same directory that `install_ux.rs:87,102` uses for the app paths.
- [hidden] BP102 `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:25` `SPLIT = "usr/bin/install-split"` must equal the install path `recipe/tools.rs:311-313` defaults to (`usr/bin/{binary}`) for the `install-split-pane` action, which declares no `destination`. The check at `install_ux.rs:49-54` catches a mismatch only at build time.
- [hidden] BP103 `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:32` `SPLIT_SMOKE_OK = "split-smoke:ok"` is the string `install-split-pane/src/main.rs:386` prints, typed again rather than shared, though xtask already depends on the `install_split_pane` lib.
- [hidden] BP104 `distro/builder/install-split-pane/src/main.rs:172-177,123` and `install-split-pane/src/lib.rs:12` label the left pane "Shell" and say "Runs the left command (the shell)", but `install_ux.rs:101-104` now puts the variant's hub in the left pane (commit 84dc5eb). The labels and help text describe a layout that has changed.
- [hidden] BP110 `distro/builder/src/tui_binary.rs:144-156` The musl TUI build needs `libstdc++.so.6` in the *rootfs*, so the AcornOS installed system must carry libstdc++ because of a live-only tool. `distro/variants/levitate/ring3/sources.toml:99` lists `libstdc++`, while AcornOS's `ring3/sources.toml` gets it only transitively, with nothing naming the dependency.
- [hidden] BP112 `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:56-62` silently moves the docs app and the hub to the front of `apps`, and the docs path at `:87` and hub path at `:102` are rebuilt from `tui_binary::BIN_DIR` in `install_ux.rs`. Two modules must agree on where app commands live.
- [hidden] BP113 `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:67-78` checks that each TUI app's `tools` are shipped by comparing Cargo package names against the variant's runtime actions. `ships()` (`:122-127`) lists both `RuntimeActionContract` variants by hand, so every new action kind in `distro-contract` has to be edited here too (the build breaks until it is), and the app manifest's `tools` must use Cargo package names, not binary names.
- [hidden] C074 `tui_apps.rs:15,144`: `APPS_PREFIX = "tui/apps/"` and the `"tui/"` prefix restate the root `package.json` workspace layout. The variants' `install_ux.apps`/`hub`/`docs.tui_app` names (`ring2/products.toml:9-13`) must match `bin` keys in `tui/apps/**/package.json`, and nothing checks that at contract load.
- [hidden] CV1031 `distro/variants/*/ring2/products.toml:3-5` comment says the serial console runs the install screen only on "the INSTALL_UX_SERIAL boot-injection key". That key is owned by `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:27` (`SERIAL_KEY`), and the comment goes stale when the constant changes.
- [hidden] CV1107 `distro/contract/src/variant.rs:1063-1079` accepts any `ttyN`/`ttySN` in `install_ux.consoles`, while `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:105-108` accepts only `tty1`/`ttyS0` on systemd and `tty1`-`tty6`/`ttyS0` on OpenRC. Two rules for "which consoles are allowed" live in two places, so a manifest that passes contract load (for example `tty2` on LevitateOS) fails only during the overlay build.
- [hidden] U046 `distro/variants/acorn/ring2/products.toml:9` opens `page = "acorn-installation"` while `distro/variants/levitate/ring2/products.toml:9` opens `page = "installation"`; with the counterpart rule (U045) both could name `installation`, so the per-distro page name is a second copy of the prefix convention.
- [hidden] U121 `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:86-90` builds the docs title as `"{os_name} install guide"` while the hub builds `"{NAME} install"` from os-release (`tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/presentation/hub-screen.tsx:103`); the two panes' titles are formed by different owners from different sources.
- [hidden] UD2023 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/presentation/ink/theme/index.ts:63-64` sets `navWidth: 32, minColumns: 88`. Whether the install screen's docs pane goes single-pane (`src/presentation/ink/screens/install-viewer.tsx:40-41`, "When only one pane fits (the install screen's docs pane)") depends on install-split-pane's 50% split (`distro/builder/install-split-pane/src/lib.rs:9`), and neither side states that dependency.
- [hidden] UD3141 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/snapshots/38x21/` and `120x36/` are pane sizes worked out by hand from `xtask/src/tasks/tui/check.rs:21` `CONSOLES` through install-split-pane's split. `compare_snapshots` (`:178-179`) renders at whatever size a directory is named, separately from the computed `docs_pane_size` (`:74`), so after a console size or split change the snapshots stay at sizes the screen no longer uses.
- [hidden] X091 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/check.rs:20-22` `CONSOLES` restates the live kernel's `video=1920x1080` (`distro/variants/*/ring1/transforms.toml` `extra_cmdline`) and an 8x16 font as 240x67; a cmdline change leaves the docs-pane check testing the wrong size.
- [hidden] X092 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/check.rs:27` `BROKEN` matches the literal UI strings "Terminal too small" (`tui/kit/core/src/surfaces/shell.tsx:190`) and React's "Invalid hook call"; rewording the kit's message silently disables the check.
- [hidden] X095 `xtask/src/cli/types.rs:113-142` the `tui` long help hard-codes app names (`disk-plan`, `install-docs`, `recab-tui`), story names ("edit: nothing can shrink", "running a trial"), a page slug and a fixture env var (`RECPART_FIXTURE_DISKS`) from the tui manifests; super co-change shows `tui` + `cli/types.rs` changing together 16 times.
- [hidden] X096 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/mod.rs:61-72` resolves `--story` by number or name; `xtask/src/tasks/tui/inspect.rs:163-168` (`Launch::story`) resolves by name only. Two lookups for the same manifest field, with different rules.
- [hidden] X097 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/mod.rs:235-277` `readable_keys` is a second parser for the printf-escape key grammar that `util/pty_capture.rs:196-246` `parse_keys` defines; they disagree: `\e[A` renders as "Esc [A" instead of "↑", and `\033` (accepted by `parse_keys`) is not recognised and is printed as typed.
- [hidden] X098 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/mod.rs:58` the env var `LEVITATE_REAL_TOOLS` (pointing at `target/debug`) is a private protocol with one stand-in script (`tui/apps/install/disk-plan/fixtures/recpart:12,47`); the comment at `:54-56` special-cases disk-plan.
- [hidden] X100 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/mod.rs:290-316` and `inspect.rs:130-160` two PATH builders (real tools from `target/debug` vs stand-ins from `inspect.path`) plus `tools_env` (X030) all prepend to PATH by hand.
- [hidden] XT1126 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/mod.rs:57-59` sets `LEVITATE_REAL_TOOLS=<target>/debug` for `tui --stand-ins`, which only `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/fixtures/recpart:47` reads. `inspect` and `check tui` set neither it nor `RECPART_FIXTURE_LIVE`, so the same stand-in behaves differently when driven interactively and when captured headlessly.
- [hidden] XT1134 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/mod.rs:157` and `xtask/src/tasks/tui/inspect.rs:179` restate the manifest key names `levitate.inspect.stories` / `levitate.inspect.pages` in messages. The keys are owned by `distro_contract::tui_apps`.
- [hidden] XT1138 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/inspect.rs:49` puts the default capture output in `.artifacts/out/tui/inspect`, under the tree that `justfile:38-40` `clean-out` deletes with a comment saying it holds "release products and scenario runtimes".
- [hidden] XT2104 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/inspect.rs:128-140` `stand_in_path` checks for a stand-in only for each manifest `tools` entry, but base-system also runs `recfstab` (`tui/apps/install/base-system/src/app/runner.ts:17`) and recab-tui runs `reboot` (stand-ins `fixtures/recfstab`, `fixtures/reboot`). The guard behind "a run on this PATH never reaches a real tool" does not cover them, so removing either stand-in would let a capture run the real binary without any error.
- [hidden] XT2118 `xtask/src/util/pty_capture.rs:264-269` `apply_terminal_env` forces `TERM=xterm-256color` and strips `COLORTERM`/`FORCE_COLOR`/`NO_COLOR` for every capture, while the install screen's panes get their `TERM` separately from `distro/builder/install-split-pane/src/main.rs:206` (BC1132). Captures match what the ISO shows only because the two copies agree, and the colour-variable stripping has no counterpart on the ISO.
- [needless] BP109 `distro/builder/src/tui_binary.rs:78-84` Preparing the live-tools product runs `bun install --frozen-lockfile` at the repo root ("the one way xtask does"). A product build mutates the checkout's `node_modules` and silently depends on xtask's convention.
- [needless] C071 `distro/contract/src/tui_apps.rs:1-195` (JS workspace/`package.json` discovery, `inspect` stories and masks for `cargo xtask tui`) lives in the crate whose `lib.rs:1-7` calls itself "intentionally conformance-only". Changes to the TUI tooling (for example `a7b1048`, "a story's about") churn the variant contract crate that builder and install-tests depend on.
- [needless] C072 `distro/contract/Cargo.toml:13` `serde_json` is a dependency only for `tui_apps.rs`.
- [needless] UD2134 `tui/kit/core/src/surfaces/shell.tsx:62-71` `useShellLayout` takes the nav width from the theme (`layout.navWidth`, `theme/tokens.ts:94`) and from a per-call `options.navWidth`. install-docs sets it in its theme (`theme/index.ts:63`); disk-plan (`disk-plan-screen.tsx:109`), install-hub (`hub-screen.tsx:90`, `22`), base-system (`base-screen.tsx:46`) and recab-tui (`recab-screen.tsx:42`) pass it per call. The frame's geometry has two owners.
- [needless] X101 `xtask/src/cli/types.rs:399-408` CLI defaults are read from `crate::tasks::tui::inspect::DEFAULT_SIZE/DEFAULT_WAIT`, so the CLI type module imports a task module (`modules-levitate-xtask.txt`: `cli/types -> tasks`).
- [surplus] C073 `tui_apps.rs:139-157,160-169`: `tui_packages` and `discover` each re-read and re-parse the root `package.json`. `find` re-discovers and re-parses every app manifest to look up one name, and `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:65` calls it once per app.
- [surplus] X099 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/mod.rs:51,60` with `--stand-ins`, `app_command` first builds the real tools and puts them on PATH, then the stand-in PATH replaces it; the build is used only via X098.
- [surplus] XT2119 `xtask/src/util/pty_capture.rs:248-259` has `#[cfg(unix)]` and `#[cfg(not(unix))]` variants of `kill_process_group`, while xtask depends on `libc` unconditionally (`xtask/Cargo.toml:21`, also used by `util/exit.rs:59`) and only runs on Linux; the non-unix branch is never built.

### G22 One install procedure, valid on both userlands, rendered by every consumer

Solves **41** problems (6 parity, 7 special, 27 hidden, 1 needless).

- **Design:** The install steps (order, titles, commands, guide sections) are declared once as a function of the distro facts and are valid on both live userlands; the hub, install-state, disk-plan, website, guide pages and the install scenario all render or run them. Install state is a typed, validated handoff. The hub completes an install without typing, as the default user.
- **Done when:** No install command is written in more than one place (grep `recstrap`, `recsetup` command lines in TS, docs and Rust); the install scenario runs the same steps without `--force` and logs in as the created user using the privilege tool.
- **Depends on:** G04, G20, G21
- **Decisions:** Keymap command on the live ISO: `loadkeys` on both (default).
- **Merges area designs:** UD-03 (tui-docs), UD-07 (tui-docs), TV-14 (testing-vm), PA-15 (parity-all) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] P045 Neither install scenario creates a non-root user or exercises the privilege tool: `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/install.rs:81` calls recsetup with `--root-password` only, and `scenarios.toml` `automated_login.default_username` (`levitate`/`acorn`) is never used (`scenarios/mod.rs:654` logs in as root). The sudo/doas wheel rule — the one intended difference — is untested on both.
- [parity] P080 The install TUIs (`install-hub`, `disk-plan`) are not driven by any VM scenario on either distro: `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/install.rs:50-131` installs over SSH with the CLI tools; only the split pane's launch is smoke-tested (`scenarios/mod.rs:447`).
- [parity] U021 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/03-installation-disk.ts:32-41` vs `12-acorn-installation-disk.ts:31-41`: keyboard layout (`loadkeys us` vs `zcat ... | loadkmap`) and clock (`timedatectl set-ntp true`, no network caveat, vs `ntpd -q -n -p pool.ntp.org`, "needs a connection") differ in step 1.
- [parity] U022 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/03-installation-disk.ts:191-228` manual partitioning on LevitateOS is an interactive fdisk walk-through; `12-acorn-installation-disk.ts:205-217` is an `sfdisk` heredoc because busybox fdisk cannot write GPT; the same alternative is two different procedures.
- [parity] U023 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/03-installation-disk.ts:246-247` uses `mount --mkdir`, `12-acorn-installation-disk.ts:236-238` uses `mkdir -p` + `mount` (busybox mount lacks `--mkdir`); userland differences leak into the guide.
- [parity] U059 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:112-126` the Configuration step's "about" promises "root password and a user", but its commands set only timezone/locale/hostname and the root password; the user-account step (docs step 14, `05-installation-config.ts:70-80`) is missing from the hub on both distros, and the AcornOS-only WiFi carry-over (`15-acorn-installation-boot.ts:49-58`) and the per-distro admin tool are not reflected in any hub step.
- [special] TI099 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:662` automated-login judges the shell by `output.contains("SCENARIO_LOGIN_OK")` after `login()` has already confirmed the shell with its own LOGIN_OK marker (`auth.rs:66`), so the shell probe runs twice.
- [special] U054 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:84` the Preflight step's docs slug is `installation-disk` (there is no preflight page; the UEFI check lives in disk page step 1), and `:155` Finish reuses `installation-boot`.
- [special] U058 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:46-47` substitutes the literal placeholders `SLOT_A`, `SLOT_B`, `ROOT` when the disk result lacks a device, producing a runnable-looking but invalid recsetup line instead of refusing.
- [special] UD1073 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:58` with no disk result the target defaults to the literal `/mnt`, one more copy of the install root that recpart (`--mount-root`), recsetup (`--root`) and the guides each default separately (TL077).
- [special] UD1078 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/app/handoff.ts:29-38` an unreadable or wrong-schema state file (which `readState` refuses, `install-state/src/index.ts:77-79`) is swallowed into an empty state plus a notice, so the hub shows every step as not done over an install in progress.
- [special] UD3061 `tui/kit/install-state/src/index.ts:77-83` `readState` checks only `schema` and that `steps` is an object, then casts to `InstallState`. A state file with a malformed step result (a `disk` without `target` or slots) is accepted, and the failure surfaces later inside the hub or a step app.
- [special] UD3078 `tui/apps/install/base-system/package.json:45,97,123,145,175,213` every story points `LEVITATE_INSTALL_STATE` at the fixed `/tmp/levitate-base-system-story/state.json`. The file is shared by every run on the host and never cleared, so one story's recorded steps become the next story's starting state, and concurrent `check tui` runs share it.
- [hidden] PA3061 `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:56-78` the builder adds the docs app and the hub to the shipped apps and checks each app's declared `tools` against the runtime actions. Nothing reads the hub's own app launches (`argv: ["disk-plan"]` and `["base-system"]` in `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:94,107`; the hub's `package.json` `levitate` block declares no apps), so the hub-to-app dependency is a string in TS that each variant's `install_ux.apps` must mirror by hand.
- [hidden] TI045 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/install.rs:51` picks the install disk as the first `!read_only && !/dev/loop*` disk. That relies on recqemu attaching the boot ISO a second time as a read-only SCSI disk (`tools/recqemu/src/libvirt.rs:138-142`), and on recpart reporting `read_only` for it.
- [hidden] TI046 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/install.rs:15-16` and `:84` choose the installed kernel console args (`console=tty0 console=ttyS0,115200n8`) in the test. The installed system's serial console, which installed-boot and the login scenarios depend on, exists only because the harness asks for it, so a real user install has whatever recsetup defaults to.
- [hidden] TI047 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/install.rs:1-3` the doc says the steps follow "the order the live install docs give them". The order recpart, recstrap, recfstab, recsetup and the `recfstab ... >> etc/fstab` redirection are hand-copied from the docs (the docs content, the install UX), with nothing linking them.
- [hidden] TV2080 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/automated-login.sh:26` asserts "User is root" (`id -u` = 0), binding the guest script to the Rust harness's hard-coded `"root"` login (`scenarios/mod.rs:654`) rather than to the contract's `automated_login.default_username`.
- [hidden] TV3053 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/install.rs:70` runs `recstrap --force {t}`, while the user docs (`docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/04-installation-base.ts:47` `recstrap /mnt`) and the TUI disk-plan page (`tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/presentation/pages.tsx:858` `recstrap ${target}`) run it without `--force`. The install test skips recstrap's safety check that users hit.
- [hidden] TV3054 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/install.rs:123-128` writes `root:{password}` lines into `recsetup apply --passwords-stdin`. The `user:password` stdin format belongs to recsetup (`tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/apply.rs:59,73`), the harness retypes it, and the user is the literal `root`.
- [hidden] U010 Step numbers ("steps 1-5", "6-9", "10-14", "15-18") are hand-written in `02-installation.ts:46-49`, `11-acorn-installation.ts:59-62`, every page intro and every "Continue to" line (e.g. `03-installation-disk.ts:186`, `12-acorn-installation-disk.ts` same place); inserting a step means editing ~12 strings across both copies.
- [hidden] U053 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:39-40` gives every step a `docs` slug "(AcornOS opens its counterpart)", but no code reads `Step.docs` (the hub never tells the docs pane which page to show; `hub-screen.tsx:195` just says "The guide beside this has the step in full"): dead field whose comment depends on install-docs' prefix rule.
- [hidden] U055 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:105`, `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/presentation/pages.tsx:855-861` (`nextInstallSteps`), `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/04-installation-base.ts:36,61` (+ AcornOS copy), `docs/website/src/pages/index.astro:54-59` and `docs/content/src/content/05-rec-tooling/02-installation-tools-overview.ts` all spell `recstrap <t>` / `recfstab <t> >> <t>/etc/fstab` / `recsetup inspect --root <t>`; previous untangle C011/C137 left two copies, there are now at least five.
- [hidden] U056 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:121` hard-codes `--timezone Europe/Brussels --locale en_US.UTF-8 --hostname myhost`, a copy of `05-installation-config.ts:28,34` (which says `myhostname`, `:49`) and of `docs/website/src/pages/index.astro:58`; the copies already disagree on the hostname.
- [hidden] U057 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:44-48` `bootArgs` rebuilds recsetup's `--boot ab --slot-a X --slot-b Y` / `--boot mutable --root-device X` syntax, also written in `06-installation-boot.ts:22,30`, both AcornOS copies and `08-troubleshooting.ts:47`; recsetup owns the flags and nothing generates them (unlike recpart's, which reach disk-plan through `cargo xtask gen`).
- [hidden] U061 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:131` "Install systemd-boot and the boot entries" hard-codes the boot loader; the docs say LevitateOS runs `bootctl install` and AcornOS copies the EFI binary by hand (`15-acorn-installation-boot.ts:26`), and which happens is recsetup's decision.
- [hidden] U062 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:153` and both boot pages (`06-installation-boot.ts:61`) run `umount -R`; the AcornOS pages already had to replace `mount --mkdir` for busybox (`12-acorn-installation-disk.ts:236-238`), and nothing checks the hub's command lines against each distro's userland.
- [hidden] U063 `tui/kit/install-state/src/index.ts:15` `STEP_NAMES` (preflight, disk, base, setup, boot) and `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:55-157` (the same five plus `finish`, with titles) are two lists of the install steps in order; `recordStep`'s "drop the later steps" (`index.ts:85`) uses the first, the rail uses the second.
- [hidden] U064 `tui/kit/install-state/src/index.ts:32-38` types `preflight`, `base`, `setup`, `boot` results as `unknown`, while `install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:81,106,124,140` writes `{uefi, media}`, `{target}`, `{manual: true}`, `{mode}`; the shapes are known only to the writer and the `as never` cast at `install-hub/src/app/handoff.ts:42` hides the mismatch.
- [hidden] U065 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/presentation/pages.tsx:903-909` tells the user to run the next commands "from this shell", but on the install screen disk-plan is started by the hub (`steps.ts:93`), which runs those commands itself; disk-plan does not know it was launched from the hub and the two give different next-step instructions.
- [hidden] U066 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:7,72` and `src/app/handoff.ts:26` hard-code `/run/live-rootfs.erofs` (owned by `recinit::live`) and `/sys/firmware/efi`, also restated in `docs/install-tui-todo.md:179` and both base pages (U039); the hub declares no `levitate.tools` (`install-hub/package.json:28-102`), so the builder's tool check (`distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:67-78`) never verifies that recstrap, recfstab, recsetup and recchroot ship.
- [hidden] U098 `docs/website/src/pages/index.astro:53-59` copies the install command sequence "from the repository README" (another copy of U055/U056).
- [hidden] U105 `docs/install-tui-todo.md:30-33` plans separate step apps "(disk-plan, base, setup, boot)"; `install-hub/README.md:10-11` and `steps.ts` treat base/setup/boot as "the guide's commands" until those apps exist, so the hub's step definitions are a placeholder copy of the docs that the plan expects to replace.
- [hidden] U125 `tui/kit/install-state/src/index.ts:18-30` `DiskResult` restates recpart's handoff (target, mounts, slot devices) by hand, while disk-plan has the generated `ApplyResult` (`disk-plan/src/generated/recpart.ts`) and converts it in `disk-plan/src/app/install-state.ts:10-22`; a recpart handoff change reaches the hub only through this hand copy.
- [hidden] UD1077 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/app/handoff.ts:34` builds the fallback state with a literal `schema: 1 as const` instead of `STATE_SCHEMA` from `@levitate/install-state` (`tui/kit/install-state/src/index.ts:9`).
- [hidden] UD2123 `docs/content/src/content/05-rec-tooling/14-recsetup.ts:21` calls recsetup "the backend of the install's setup and boot apps". No setup or boot app exists (`tui/apps/install/` holds base-system, disk-plan and install-hub); the hub opens a shell for setup and runs commands for boot.
- [hidden] UD3053 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/app/handoff.ts:26` (and the `Facts` doc at `src/domain/steps.ts:5`) decides "booted in UEFI mode" by whether `/sys/firmware/efi` exists. The guides beside it check `ls /sys/firmware/efi/efivars` (`docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/03-installation-disk.ts:25,29`, `12-acorn-installation-disk.ts:25,29`), so there are two definitions of the same preflight fact.
- [hidden] UD3071 `tui/apps/install/base-system/src/presentation/use-base.ts:139-148` records the base step with `rootfs: ""` and `entries: 0` when the copy's start event or check result is missing, and `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:102` special-cases `entries > 0` to choose its summary. The two apps agree that "0 means unknown" without stating it anywhere.
- [hidden] UD3076 `tui/apps/install/base-system/src/presentation/pages.tsx:102-103` says "disk-plan mounts the new system at /mnt". The target is recpart's `handoff.install_target` (`tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/app/install-state.ts:15`), not a disk-plan constant, so this is one more hand copy of the install root default.
- [hidden] UD3077 `tui/apps/install/base-system/src/presentation/pages.tsx:463-464` restates the configuration step's contents ("time zone, locale, keymap, hostname, the root password and a user"), the same list as install-hub's `about` (`tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:114`).
- [needless] U126 `tui/kit/install-state` sits under `tui/kit/`, whose rule is generic, vocabulary-free code (`tui/kit/core/README.md:7-11`, `tui/AGENTS.md`), but holds install-domain types (disk, slots, steps) used by two install apps.

### G23 One install guide and every user-visible string written once from the facts

Solves **116** problems (22 parity, 17 special, 59 hidden, 2 needless, 16 surplus).

- **Design:** Each guide page is written once as a function of the distro facts; the docs build renders it for every distro and is strict (one generated index, strict loading, duplicate slug fails); one content model that every renderer handles exhaustively; the website renders the same docs for both distros. Apps get their distro identity only from the launcher, and nothing says "levitate" on AcornOS (`/run/levitate-install`, `LEVITATE_INSTALL_*`, `recstrap --help`, disk-plan's title). Tool docs link generated facts.
- **Done when:** The 8 `acorn-*` pages are gone; install-docs does not read `/etc/os-release` (F13 restored); no "LevitateOS" string on an AcornOS ISO except where it names the project; the website has an AcornOS entry point.
- **Depends on:** G04, G22
- **Decisions:** **User-visible.** AcornOS page slugs lose `acorn-` (old links break). Hardware baseline: the same CPU level and RAM for both (default). State path renamed to `/run/install/` (default).
- **Merges area designs:** UD-02 (tui-docs), UD-04 (tui-docs), UD-05 (tui-docs), UD-06 (tui-docs), UD-13 (tui-docs), PA-14 (parity-all), TO-15 (tools) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] PA1131 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recstrap/main.rs:34-38` recstrap's own `--help` says "Extract LevitateOS rootfs ... the pacstrap equivalent for LevitateOS", and `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/request.rs:115,256` gives "such as levitate-pc" as the hostname example and in the validation error; the same binaries ship on AcornOS's installer, which shows LevitateOS branding.
- [parity] TO1140 `tools/recpart/README.md:3`, `tools/recpart/REQUIREMENTS.md:8,35`, `tools/recab/REQUIREMENTS.md:8`, `tools/recstrap/README.md:3-5,94`, `tools/recstrap/src/lib.rs:1` and `tools/recstrap/Cargo.toml:8` describe the shared install tools as LevitateOS tools ("Partition planning and apply backend for LevitateOS install flows", "LevitateOS install tools", "LevitateOS live ISO"), while AcornOS's installer runs the same binaries.
- [parity] TO2090 `tools/recpart/src/preflight.rs:175` the not-root remediation says "Re-run with root privileges (sudo)." in the shared tool; AcornOS's privilege tool is doas.
- [parity] TO2174 `tools/recstrap/docs/recchroot.md:23-24` the shared tool's guide shows `recchroot /mnt bootctl install`, a command AcornOS targets cannot run (no bootctl; recsetup copies the EFI binary instead, `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recsetup/target.rs:323-329`).
- [parity] U013 `docs/content/src/content/04-architecture/01-faq.ts` and `04-architecture/02-how-we-compare.ts` take the inferred `product: "levitate"` (generated `metaBySlug` "faq", "how-we-compare"), so AcornOS's install-docs has no FAQ and no comparison page at all.
- [parity] U014 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/10-acorn-getting-started.ts:8` AcornOS's Getting Started has no "Requirements" section (CPU, RAM, storage, UEFI/Secure Boot, A/B note) that `01-getting-started.ts:9-27` has; AcornOS puts a shorter Requirements list on its installation page instead (`11-acorn-installation.ts:24-36`) while LevitateOS's installation page has none.
- [parity] U016 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/01-getting-started.ts:86-95` QEMU example uses `-m 16G -smp 4` and an NVMe disk; `10-acorn-getting-started.ts:67-75` uses `-m 4G -smp 2` and `if=virtio`; the same VM instructions differ per distro for no stated reason.
- [parity] U018 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/02-installation.ts:5` titles are "Installation", "Disk Preparation", "Base System", "Configuration", "Bootloader & Finish", "Post-Installation", "Troubleshooting", "Getting Started"; AcornOS's are "Installing AcornOS", "AcornOS Disk Preparation", ..., "AcornOS Boot & Finish" (`15-acorn-installation-boot.ts:5`): the boot step is even named differently ("Bootloader" vs "Boot"), and the sidebar of each install screen reads differently.
- [parity] U019 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/11-acorn-installation.ts:67-87` only AcornOS's guide has a "How It Differs From LevitateOS" table; it lists differences beyond the intended three (network stack, initramfs, install-screen shell), i.e. the docs advertise the parity gaps as features.
- [parity] U025 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/03-installation-disk.ts:99` names only NVMe and SATA devices; `12-acorn-installation-disk.ts:109` also explains virtio `/dev/vda`; and only LevitateOS's step 1 links back to Getting Started (`03-installation-disk.ts:14`).
- [parity] U028 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/08-troubleshooting.ts:150-188` "Package Issues" exists only for LevitateOS; `17-acorn-troubleshooting.ts:10-33` "Install Issues" (recpart refuses the disk) exists only for AcornOS.
- [parity] U033 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/05-installation-config.ts:84-85` LevitateOS's admin sentence never names `sudo` ("No additional sudoers file is needed"), while `14-acorn-installation-config.ts:84` names `doas` and its rule; the one intended difference is spelled out on one side only.
- [parity] U034 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/05-installation-config.ts:7-8` LevitateOS's config and boot intros (`06-installation-boot.ts:7-8`) omit the "installation steps 10-14 / 15-18" prefix that the AcornOS copies (`14-acorn-installation-config.ts:8`, `15-acorn-installation-boot.ts:8`) and every other page have.
- [parity] U035 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/07-post-installation.ts:89` LevitateOS's next steps say "Install a desktop environment (Sway, GNOME, KDE, etc.)" and `16-acorn-post-installation.ts:69` says "vim ships with the system"; neither mentions the shipped desktop (Plasma / LXQt per `docs/desktop.md`), and the two lists differ.
- [parity] U050 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/presentation/pages.tsx:159` hard-codes the welcome title "Prepare a disk for LevitateOS"; the same app runs on AcornOS's install screen (`distro/variants/acorn/ring2/products.toml:11`), so AcornOS users are told they are installing LevitateOS (regression of the previous untangle's C004).
- [parity] U051 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/docs.ts:23-27` resolves `DOCS.chooseDisk` against the LevitateOS page `installation-disk` and shows its title ("Disk Preparation") on every distro; on AcornOS the link opens `acorn-installation-disk` (titled "AcornOS Disk Preparation") through install-docs' counterpart rule, so the label and the page differ, and the build-time check (`docs.ts:8-18`) never verifies the AcornOS counterpart or its section exist.
- [parity] U068 `tui/kit/install-state/src/index.ts:10,12` names the state `/run/levitate-install/state.json` and `LEVITATE_INSTALL_STATE`, and `install-hub/src/presentation/hub-screen.tsx:195` / `disk-plan/src/presentation/pages.tsx:895` show that path to the user, so AcornOS's install screen says "levitate".
- [parity] U079 `docs/content/src/tool-help.ts:8` (generated from `recstrap --help`, embedded in the shared recstrap page) reads "Extracts the LevitateOS EROFS rootfs image … the pacstrap equivalent for LevitateOS"; the same binary and page are used on AcornOS, so its own help names the other distro.
- [parity] U094 `docs/website/src/pages/index.astro:189-194`, `src/pages/download.astro:112-114` and `src/components/layout/Footer.astro:9-10` link only `/docs/installation/` and `/docs/post-installation/`; the site has no entry point to AcornOS's install guide, and `src/pages/docs/index.astro:2` redirects `/docs` to LevitateOS's Getting Started.
- [parity] U096 `docs/website/src/components/layout/DocsSidebar.astro:22-45` renders `docsNav` unfiltered, so the website's "Getting Started" section lists LevitateOS's eight pages under generic titles ("Installation", "Disk Preparation") followed by AcornOS's eight prefixed ones ("AcornOS Disk Preparation"); LevitateOS reads as the default and AcornOS as an appendix.
- [parity] UD2019 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/layout/page.ts:447-454` lays out `interactive` block steps with `commandLines(...)` and no anchor, so their commands cannot be selected or copied. `command` blocks (`:380-392`) pass an anchor and can be copied. The same kind of content behaves two ways depending on block type, and `interactive` is used by `03-installation-disk.ts` and `05-rec-tooling/02-installation-tools-overview.ts`.
- [parity] UD2097 `docs/content/src/types.ts:76` `TableBlock.monospaceCol` (set on many helper and package-manager pages) is honoured by the website (`docs/website/src/components/docs/TableBlock.astro:25`) and ignored by install-docs' table layout (`tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/layout/page.ts`). The shared content model has a field only one renderer reads.
- [special] U012 `docs/content/scripts/build.ts:121-159` `inferMetaFromSection` defaults every `getting-started`, `rec-tooling`, `architecture` and unknown-section page to `product: "levitate"`; a new page is silently LevitateOS-only (hidden from AcornOS's install-docs) unless it declares `meta.product`, so the AcornOS pages and every shared page must each override it.
- [special] U042 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/docs.ts:18-19` hard-codes the distro list `"levitate" | "acorn"` (and `docs/content/src/types.ts:8`, `docs/content/scripts/build.ts:116` repeat it with `"shared"`); a third variant needs edits in three files and the viewer, while the variants already exist as `distro/variants/*`.
- [special] U043 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/docs.ts:22-30` `detectProduct` maps `/etc/os-release` `ID=acornos|acorn` to AcornOS and everything else (including a read error, swallowed at `:26-27`) to LevitateOS; the ID strings are a copy of what the variant writes into os-release.
- [special] U045 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/docs.ts:66-72` `pageIndex` maps a slug to its counterpart by stripping/adding the literal prefix `acorn-` (`slug.replace(/^acorn-/, "")`, `${product}-${bare}`); the LevitateOS pages have no prefix, so the rule is asymmetric and works only for exactly these two products and only while file names follow the convention.
- [special] U049 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/main.tsx:13,15` falls back to the title "System install" when os-release is missing or unreadable (swallowed `catch`), so a dev-checkout run or story shows the host's distro name or "System".
- [special] U084 `docs/content/src/discovery.ts:47-50` and `scripts/build.ts:100-103` give files in section `helpers` a `helpers-` slug prefix and no other section; AcornOS pages instead carry their `acorn-` prefix in the file name, so there are two unrelated slug-prefix conventions.
- [special] U090 `docs/content/CLAUDE.md:22-27` "Docs Are the Spec: if the ISO doesn't match docs, fix the ISO" makes every per-distro sentence in the AcornOS/LevitateOS copies a requirement on that distro's ISO, so each divergent copy (U020-U036) silently specifies a different product.
- [special] U095 `docs/website/src/components/docs/DocsPage.astro:34` gives the hero frame only to slug `getting-started`; AcornOS's `acorn-getting-started` renders as an ordinary page.
- [special] UD1024 `docs/content/scripts/build.ts:161-187` `normalizeEnum`/`normalizeEnumArray` silently replace an invalid `product`/`scopes`/`audience`/`stability` value with the inferred default, so a typo in a page's `meta.product` (e.g. `"acornos"`) quietly makes it a LevitateOS page instead of failing the build.
- [special] UD1025 `docs/content/scripts/build.ts:251-254` a content file with no DocsContent-shaped export only logs a warning and is skipped, so a broken page vanishes from the ISO's generated index instead of failing the build.
- [special] UD1059 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/presentation/ink/screens/install-viewer.tsx:105-108` (via `src/docs.ts:81-89`) any link whose href is not a docs slug (e.g. `/download` in `01-getting-started.ts:33`, GitHub links) is reported as "invalid docs link target" on the live ISO; docs pages are written for the website's routes and the TUI handles only one kind.
- [special] UD1130 `docs/website/src/components/docs/InlineContent.astro:84` an unknown inline node renders as an empty string, and `RenderBlock.astro:20-60` renders an unknown block type as nothing, while install-docs' `layoutBlock` switch (`page.ts:372-477`) is type-checked; a new block or inline type silently disappears on the website.
- [special] UD2018 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/layout/page.ts:340` maps every note variant except `info` to the `warning` text intent (`block.variant === "info" ? "info" : "warning"`). The theme gives `danger` its own label and background (`src/presentation/ink/theme/index.ts:102-107`) and the kit has an `error` intent (`tui/kit/core/src/theme/tokens.ts:75`), but text inside a danger note is coloured as a warning.
- [special] UD2106 `docs/website/src/components/docs/DocsPage.astro:17-28` builds the meta description by keeping strings and nodes with `type === "text"`, a type `InlineNode` does not have (`docs/content/src/rich-text.ts:33`). Bold, code and link text therefore drop out of every rich intro's description.
- [special] UD3014 `docs/content/scripts/build.ts:100-103` `generateSlug` prefixes only the `helpers` section, and every other section's slug is the bare file name. `buildContentMap` (`:326`) assigns `contentBySlug[entry.slug]` with no duplicate check, so two sections with the same page file name overwrite each other silently.
- [special] UD3015 `docs/content/scripts/build.ts:238` a content file whose path does not match `parseContentPath`'s `NN-section/NN-page.ts` pattern is skipped with `continue` and no message. The build succeeds and the page is missing from both renderers.
- [special] UD3025 `docs/content/scripts/syntax.ts:59-61` silently drops the alpha channel of 8-digit theme colours. `normalizeTokenLines` (`:134-136`) turns a token whose `content` is not a string into empty text instead of failing.
- [hidden] TL093 `tools/recab/src/lib.rs:1` describes recab as for "systemd-boot systems" (and `Cargo.toml:8` as for "LevitateOS-style" systems, though it serves AcornOS too). The backend type `SystemdBoot` is used concretely everywhere (`lib.rs:152`, `stage.rs:16`), so the boot loader of both distros is fixed inside recab with no seam, while the init-system difference has one.
- [hidden] TO1139 `tools/recuki/README.md:40-44` documents `UkiConfig::new(...).with_os_release(...)`, which `tools/recuki/src/lib.rs` does not have (it is a public `os_release` field, `:20`); a doc restating an API that changed.
- [hidden] TO2053 `tools/recab/README.md:186-188` says stage uses `chroot`; no code in `tools/recab/src` runs chroot (stale external-tool list).
- [hidden] TO2054 `tools/recab/README.md:26` the update example names ISO-internal paths (`/mnt/live/filesystem.erofs`, `/mnt/boot/initramfs-installed.img`) owned by reciso and the release builder.
- [hidden] TO2056 `tools/recab/README.md:266-283` the environment-override list is a hand copy of the env reads spread over four source files.
- [hidden] TO2057 `tools/recab/README.md:9` ("the slot state machine for LevitateOS's A/B layout") and `tools/recab/Cargo.toml:8` ("for LevitateOS-style immutable systems") describe the shared tool as LevitateOS's although AcornOS uses it identically.
- [hidden] TO2088 `tools/recpart/README.md:91` "a shrink keeps the used space plus 10% and 64 MiB" restates `min_shrink_bytes` (`tools/recpart/src/inspect.rs:408-410`).
- [hidden] TO2098 `tools/recpart/docs/use-cases.md:28` documents running `target/debug/deps/loop_disk-<hash>`, a cargo-internal path that ignores `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`; `:38` hard-codes `../../../../../target/debug/recpart`.
- [hidden] TO2099 `tools/recpart/docs/use-cases.md:9,12` restates the template partition count ("4 AB partitions", "EFI + ROOT only") owned by `policy.rs`.
- [hidden] TO2106 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recstrap/guards.rs:187-189` comment restates recpart's mount layout ("recpart (or the guide) mounts at /mnt, /mnt/boot and /mnt/var").
- [hidden] TO2156 `tools/recinit/README.md:120` documents a `{{BOOT_MODULES}}` placeholder the template does not have (it uses `BOOT_MODULE_PATHS`/`KERNEL_RELEASE`); `:126-139` lists `elf.rs` and `systemd.rs`, which do not exist, and omits `live.rs`/`payload.rs`; `:25` points at a standalone `github.com/LevitateOS/recinit` dependency.
- [hidden] TO2168 `tools/reciso/README.md:57` documents `--checksum` "Generate SHA256 checksum"; the code writes SHA-512 by default with `--no-checksum` to skip (`tools/reciso/src/main.rs:86-88`, `tools/reciso/src/iso.rs:8`); `:63-70` shows `with_os_release`/`with_uki` builders and a one-argument `create_iso` that do not exist; `--live-payload-layout`, `--systemd-boot` and `--quiet` are undocumented.
- [hidden] TO2173 `tools/recstrap/README.md:72-88` the safety-check table restates `GUARDS` (`tools/recstrap/src/bin/recstrap/guards.rs:84-97`) in a different order and with different checks (root check first and EROFS support second, where the code runs the protected-path and host-filesystem checks first and EROFS support last; "Rootfs readable", "Target exists" are not guards), contradicting `:94` of the same README and `guards.rs:1`'s "in the order they run"; `:85,112` restate the 2 GB floor.
- [hidden] TO2175 `tools/recstrap/docs/recchroot.md:28` says recchroot bind-mounts `/sys/firmware/efi/efivars` separately; `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recchroot.rs:32-34` binds only `/proc`, `/sys` (recursively), `/dev`, `/run`.
- [hidden] TO3008 `tools/recuki/README.md:17-20` brands the shared library's examples `--os-name 'LevitateOS' --os-id levitateos` / `levitateos.efi`, while the same library builds AcornOS's UKIs. The crate's docs speak for one sibling.
- [hidden] TO3034 `tools/reciso/README.md:9` ("Works for UEFI boot with EROFS rootfs") and `tools/reciso/src/main.rs:53` ("Path to rootfs image (EROFS)") restate a requirement reciso never checks. It copies the rootfs as an opaque file; the EROFS requirement belongs to the live init (`tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:5`).
- [hidden] TO3153 `tools/recstrap/src/bin/recstrap/main.rs:1-14` says "User does EVERYTHING else manually (partitioning, formatting, fstab, bootloader)" and "This is NOT archinstall". The same crate ships recsetup, which does locale, users, bootloader and boot entries, and recfstab, which writes the fstab.
- [hidden] TO3189 `tools/recinit/LICENSE-APACHE` and `LICENSE-MIT` differ from the byte-identical copies in recab, recpart, recstrap, reciso, recuki, recqemu and recshuttle. One of the sibling crates has drifted license files.
- [hidden] U001 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/01-getting-started.ts:4` and `10-acorn-getting-started.ts:4` are two copies of the same page (download, USB, Rufus, QEMU, next steps) differing only in the ISO name and a few values; a change to the media steps must be made twice and nothing ties them.
- [hidden] U002 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/02-installation.ts:4` and `11-acorn-installation.ts:4` duplicate the install overview (overview text, A/B note verbatim, "What You'll Do", step list with the same step-number ranges).
- [hidden] U003 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/03-installation-disk.ts:4` and `12-acorn-installation-disk.ts:4` are a ~250-line copy pair: after replacing the distro name the diff is about 15 hunks; the disk steps, recpart commands, layout table, A/B note and dry-run/confirm text are byte-identical in both.
- [hidden] U004 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/04-installation-base.ts:4` and `13-acorn-installation-base.ts:4` are a copy pair differing in three paragraphs (contents list, fstab wording, recsetup inspect sentence).
- [hidden] U005 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/05-installation-config.ts:4` and `14-acorn-installation-config.ts:4` are a copy pair differing in the intro and two sentences (musl locale note, doas rule); every recsetup command line is duplicated.
- [hidden] U006 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/06-installation-boot.ts:4` and `15-acorn-installation-boot.ts:4` are a copy pair differing in the explanation of what recsetup does, the services paragraph and an AcornOS-only WiFi block.
- [hidden] U007 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/07-post-installation.ts:4` and `16-acorn-post-installation.ts:4` copy the "System Updates" section (mount ISO, recab stage, set-next, reboot, carried-over list) with `sudo` vs `doas` and the `--initrd` flag as the only intended differences.
- [hidden] U008 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/08-troubleshooting.ts:4` and `17-acorn-troubleshooting.ts:4` copy the whole "Boot Issues" section (three Q&As, mount/ls/blkid/recsetup commands) with only the boot-directory name and initramfs presence changed.
- [hidden] U009 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/12-acorn-installation-disk.ts:7` (and every acorn page's intro/links) hard-codes `/docs/acorn-*` hrefs while `03-installation-disk.ts:7` hard-codes `/docs/installation*`; the slug pairing that install-docs relies on (`acorn-` + Levitate slug) is kept only by file naming, and nothing checks that every Levitate page has an AcornOS counterpart.
- [hidden] U044 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/docs.ts:22-33` and `distro/variants/acorn/ring2/products.toml:9` (`args = ["--product", "acorn"]`) are two ways to choose the same thing: the launcher passes `--product acorn` and the app also sniffs os-release; LevitateOS's `products.toml:9` passes no `--product` and relies on the sniff falling through to the default.
- [hidden] U047 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/CLAUDE.md:5` ("the app holds no distro tables") and `:28` ("no scoping by product or distro") contradict `src/docs.ts:18-72`, which now filters by product and maps per-distro slugs; this is a regression of the previous untangle's F13 ("`rg -i levitate tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src` finds only package names").
- [hidden] U048 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/main.tsx:9-17` reads `NAME` from `/etc/os-release` for the title, and `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/docs.ts:22-30` reads `ID` from the same file; two apps parse os-release with their own regexes, while the builder already passes the OS name to install-docs as `--title "<os> install guide"` (`distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:86-90`) but passes nothing to the hub (`install_ux.rs:101-104`).
- [hidden] U052 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/presentation/pages.tsx:95` prints `install-docs --slug <slug>` as the way to open a page; it depends on install-docs' flag name, which the builder reads from install-docs' manifest (`inspect.pages.arg`, `distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:92-98`) but disk-plan hard-codes.
- [hidden] U078 Shared pages link LevitateOS slugs: `04-architecture/03-atomic-updates.ts:49` (`/docs/post-installation`), `04-architecture/04-variants.ts:63` (`/docs/getting-started`), `05-rec-tooling/02-installation-tools-overview.ts:49,140` (`/docs/installation`), `05-rec-tooling/14-recsetup.ts:245-246` (`/docs/installation-config`, `/docs/installation-boot`, with LevitateOS's titles "Configuration", "Bootloader & Finish"); they reach AcornOS's pages only through install-docs' prefix rule, and on the website they always open LevitateOS's.
- [hidden] U083 `docs/content/src/discovery.ts:33-63` (Vite `import.meta.glob`, used by the website through `@levitate/docs-content/live`) and `docs/content/scripts/build.ts:66-103,279-321` (generated index, used by install-docs) each implement page discovery, the `NN-section/NN-page.ts` path regex, the `helpers-` slug prefix, section titles and nav building; the website and the install screen can disagree on slugs or order, and only the build path has `metaBySlug`.
- [hidden] U088 `docs/content/scripts/build.ts:125` infers `scopes: ["install","post_install"]` only for a page literally named `post-installation`; `acorn-post-installation` would miss it (it sets meta itself), another name-keyed rule.
- [hidden] U091 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/snapshots/` holds snapshots only of LevitateOS and shared pages (`installation`, `installation-disk`, `faq`, `cli-reference`, `helpers-*`); no AcornOS page is snapshotted, so a broken AcornOS page layout is caught only by the per-variant render in `cargo xtask check tui`.
- [hidden] U097 `docs/website/src/pages/index.astro:48` copies the update example "from tools/recab/README.md" with `--initrd /mnt/boot/initramfs.img`, while the docs say `initramfs-installed.img` (`07-post-installation.ts:69`, `03-recab.ts:30`); the copies already disagree on the file name.
- [hidden] U118 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/README.md:3,11` says the launcher passes `--title` and `--slug`; the launcher passes the manifest's page flag and, for AcornOS only, `--product` (`distro/builder/src/install_ux.rs:86-98`, products.toml:9).
- [hidden] UD1019 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/12-acorn-installation-disk.ts:1` (and the other `1[0-7]-acorn-*.ts` pages) end statements with semicolons, while the LevitateOS pages follow `docs/content/biome.json:37` (`semicolons: asNeeded`); the two halves of each page pair are formatted under different rules (oxfmt default vs Biome), so nothing keeps the pairs in one shape.

The nav items carry only an `href` built as `/docs/${slug}` (`docs/content/scripts/build.ts:318`, `src/discovery.ts:108`), so each consumer re-derives the slug from that URL convention.

### G24 TUI apps do IO through the kit, with one definition per helper and keymap-driven hints

Solves **40** problems (14 special, 25 hidden, 1 surplus).

- **Design:** Apps do IO through the kit's runner and lifecycle, which reports every failure; one definition per helper and per page fact; keys and hints come from the keymap, and one key means one thing across the install screen.
- **Done when:** No app spawns or reads files outside the kit runner; no duplicated helpers across apps (grep); hints are generated from bindings.
- **Depends on:** G22
- **Merges area designs:** UD-08 (tui-docs), UD-10 (tui-docs), UD-11 (tui-docs) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [special] U067 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/package.json:35,51` the stories point `LEVITATE_INSTALL_STATE` at `fixtures/no-state.json`, a file that does not exist, relying on `readState`'s ENOENT-means-empty rule (`kit/install-state/src/index.ts:61-62`); and the hub has no stand-ins (`inspect.path`), so the "fresh install" story's preflight checks read the build host's `/sys/firmware/efi` and `/run/live-rootfs.erofs` (`handoff.ts:25-27`).
- [special] U071 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/app/handoff.ts:58-66` waits for Enter through a separate `/bin/sh -c "printf …; read _"`; and `:11-13` keeps the pending action and the last notice in module globals that `main.tsx:27-31` (`afterExit`) and `hub-screen.tsx:39,49-52` must call in the right order (handOff → exit → runPending → remount → takeNotice).
- [special] U123 `tui/kit/core/src/app/run-app.tsx:39-44,162` `afterExit` exists for one caller (install-hub, `main.tsx:27-31`); it takes no argument, so the hub passes the action to run through a module global (`install-hub/src/app/handoff.ts:11,15-17`) set during render and read after unmount.
- [special] UD1098 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/flow.ts:263` the `destructive-confirm` page is marked done on the rail even when its gate does not hold; one page id is exempt from the rule every other page follows.
- [special] UD1100 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/app/plan-runner.ts:100-107` `resolveDiskPath` swallows a failed `realpath` and returns the input, so a mistyped `--disk` is passed on unresolved.
- [special] UD1101 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/app/plan-runner.ts:55-77` the layout/plan temp directory is created once per process behind the `layoutDirMade` module flag and removed in a `process.on("exit")` hook; files are left behind when the process dies by a signal, and the flag is hidden module state.
- [special] UD1106 `tui/apps/runtime/recab-tui/src/presentation/use-recab.ts:75` after a run the status is re-read except when the action is `reboot`; one action id is special-cased in the generic run loop.
- [special] UD1111 `tui/kit/core/src/app/command.ts:57-68` a spawn failure is caught and turned into a result with `exitCode: null` and the error text in `stderr`; every caller (disk-plan `toRun`, recab-tui `recabError` at `recab.ts:152-154`) must know that null means "did not start".
- [special] UD2027 `tui/kit/core/src/app/run-app.tsx:160-167` treats a rejected `waitUntilExit()` as a clean exit (`() => process.exit()`). An Ink failure that bypasses `RecordCrash` therefore exits 0.
- [special] UD2059 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/domain/layout.ts:256-272` `parseSize` treats a bare number as GiB and folds `gb`/`gib`, `mb`/`mib`, `tb`/`tib` onto one 1024-based multiplier by first letter, so a typed `20GB` means 20 GiB. recpart's layout takes MiB, and this unit policy exists only in the editor.
- [special] UD2068 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/presentation/disk-plan-screen.tsx:173,181-184` writes the layout and plan files to disk from inside `useMemo` during render (`writeLayout`, `writeDocument`). This side effect in render is correct only as long as memo identity and the temp-dir module flag in `src/app/plan-runner.ts` hold.
- [special] UD2087 `tui/apps/runtime/recab-tui/src/app/recab-runner.ts:35-42` turns every `statSync` error (EACCES, ELOOP, …) into "doesn't exist", and `:51-54`, `:61-65` swallow `readdirSync`/`statSync` errors during path completion.
- [special] UD3055 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/app/handoff.ts:73-76` ignores the exit status of a step app (`disk-plan`, `base-system`). Only a spawn error sets a notice, so an app that crashes or exits non-zero returns to the hub with no message, while `run` steps (`:94-104`) do report their status.
- [special] UD3067 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/presentation/hub-screen.tsx:102` passes `focus="navigation"` whenever both panes fit, but `focus` (`keymap.ts:8`, Tab "switch pane") is still bound and shown in the footer. On a wide screen Tab toggles a focus plane that changes nothing.
- [hidden] U069 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/app/handoff.ts:49-51` starts `$SHELL` (default `/bin/sh`) for the shell step, while the install shell is declared per variant as `[install_ux] shell` (`/bin/bash` vs `/bin/sh`, products.toml:8) and reaches the hub only because the launcher exports `SHELL=@SHELL@` (`distro/builder/guest/install-ux/install-ux.sh:5`), an environment contract nothing in the hub declares; the hub's `run` steps always use `/bin/sh -c` (`handoff.ts:100`) regardless.
- [hidden] U072 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/presentation/keymap.ts:6` binds `m` to "mark this step done" while install-docs binds `m` to "sidebar mode" (`install-docs/src/presentation/ink/keymap.ts:7`) and quits on `escape` too; the two panes of one screen give the same key different meanings, and their quit keys differ.
- [hidden] U099 `docs/website/src/pages/download.astro:112` restates the install screen's keys (`Ctrl-]` switch, `j`/`k` scroll, `Ctrl-q` leave), which `distro/builder/install-split-pane/src/main.rs:43-47` and the TUIs' keymaps own; `index.astro:190` restates `Ctrl-]` again, and the disk pages (`03-installation-disk.ts:14`) a third time.
- [hidden] U115 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/presentation/ink/theme/index.ts:11-21` gives install-docs its own palette while install-hub and disk-plan use the kit's default theme (`tui/kit/core/src/theme/palette.ts`), so the two panes of the one install screen use different colours and nothing keeps them aligned.
- [hidden] U116 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/presentation/ink/keymap.ts:39` `BACKWARD_KEYS` restates by hand which keys of `NAV_KEYS`/`CONTENT_KEYS` go backwards (previous untangle "Leave as is" noted three copies; still present).
- [hidden] UD1067 `tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/layout/page.ts:230-232` the copy hint hard-codes "(c to copy)" / "(c)" while the copy keys are declared in `presentation/ink/keymap.ts:27` (`["c","y"]`); rebinding copy leaves a wrong hint on every command.
- [hidden] UD1080 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/app/handoff.ts:6,75,92,100` the hub runs its steps with `spawnSync` directly, bypassing the kit's `startCommand`/`runCommand`, which `tui/kit/core/src/app/command.ts:42-43` and `tui/kit/core/README.md:16` call "the one way" the apps run their backend tools.
- [hidden] UD1082 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/app/handoff.ts:97-99` builds the step script by single-quote-escaping each line itself, while `src/domain/steps.ts:50-52` has a separate `shellQuote`; two quoting rules for the same command strings.
- [hidden] UD1099 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/flow.ts:253-258` `failedAt` maps run steps (`plan`, `dry-run`, `preflight`, `apply`) to page ids by string, a second mapping beside each page's own `gate` in `PAGES`; adding or renaming a page needs both edited.
- [hidden] UD2026 `tui/kit/core/src/components/page/blocks.tsx:19-21` keeps a private intent-colour hook that the kit does not export (`resolveIntentColor(useTuiTheme(), intent, useTuiColors())`). `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/presentation/partition-editor.tsx:25-27` and `tui/apps/runtime/recab-tui/src/presentation/pages.tsx:35-37` re-implement it. `disk-plan-screen.tsx:439`, `install-hub/src/presentation/hub-screen.tsx:124`, `base-system/src/presentation/base-screen.tsx:202`, `recab-tui/src/presentation/recab-screen.tsx:197` and the kit's own `components/navigation/nav-list.tsx:80` each write their own `color = (intent) => resolveIntentColor(theme, intent, colors)`.
- [hidden] UD2029 `tui/kit/core/src/app/command.ts:41-43` runs backend tools "with the app's own environment" ("The one way the apps run their backend tools"). Stand-in selectors (`RECPART_FIXTURE_DISKS`, `RECPART_FIXTURE_APPLY`, `RECSTRAP_FIXTURE`, `RECFSTAB_FIXTURE`, `RECAB_FIXTURE`, `LEVITATE_REAL_TOOLS`) and `LEVITATE_INSTALL_STATE` reach the child tools only through the inherited ambient environment, with nothing declaring which variables pass through.
- [hidden] UD2030 `tui/kit/core/src/utils/strings.ts:48` has `errorMessage`, but `src/index.ts` does not export it. `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/app/install-state.ts:34`, `tui/apps/install/base-system/src/app/runner.ts:61,78` and `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/app/handoff.ts:35,45` each re-type `error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)`.
- [hidden] UD2031 every app works out a binding's direction from the key name itself, because the kit's keymap entries carry no direction. Examples: `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/presentation/keymap.ts:49` `PICK_FIRST` and `:80` `MORE_ROOM`, `disk-plan-screen.tsx:251` (`["up","k","left","h"].includes(key)`) and `:266-276` (inline up/down/left/right lists), `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/presentation/hub-screen.tsx:61` (`key === "up" || key === "k"`), and the kit's own `components/page/page-scroll.ts:20` `SCROLL_UP`. Each list must stay in step with its keymap entry's `keys` by hand.
- [hidden] UD2049 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/presentation/hub-screen.tsx:189` ("Then press m here to mark the step done") and `src/app/handoff.ts:87` ("mark the step done with m") hard-code the `m` key that `HUB_KEYS` owns (`src/presentation/keymap.ts:6`). `handoff.ts:87,90` also hard-code "Type exit to go back".

- [hidden] UD2061 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/presentation/pages.tsx:194,791`, `src/flow.ts:202` and `tui/apps/install/base-system/src/presentation/pages.tsx:150,185,220,281,300,438,464` say "press Enter" / "Enter starts …" in prose.
