This sits beside the one table `partition-editor.tsx:29` `MODE_NAME` and the generated `INSTALL_MODE_HELP`.
- [hidden] UD2066 `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/presentation/disk-plan-screen.tsx:443` writes the welcome page's rail label "Overview" by hand instead of using the page's own title (`src/flow.ts:127`).
- [hidden] UD2071 `/dev/` prefix stripping is re-implemented inline at `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/src/domain/setup.ts:126`, `src/presentation/pages.tsx:450,580` and `src/presentation/use-partition-editor.ts:238,243`, while `partition-editor.tsx:31-33` already has a `short()` helper for it.
- [hidden] UD2133 `tui/kit/core/src/components/page/page-scroll.ts:11-20` `PAGE_SCROLL_KEYS` (`C-u`, `pageup`, `space`, `C-d`, `pagedown`, hint "space/PgUp") is what disk-plan, install-hub, base-system and recab-tui scroll with. install-docs binds its own `pageScroll` with `b` added and hint "space/b" (`tui/apps/live-tools/install-docs/src/presentation/ink/keymap.ts:15-20,28-33`). `tui/apps/install/disk-plan/README.md:17` says disk-plan pages scroll "with space/b", which only install-docs does.
- [hidden] UD3073 `tui/apps/install/base-system/src/presentation/base-screen.tsx:231-233` hand-writes the rail legend ("✓ done ◐ running", "● ready ○ waiting", "✗ failed") beside the `MARK` table (`:174-180`) that it describes, so the glyphs are stated twice.
- [hidden] UD3074 `tui/apps/install/base-system/src/presentation/base-screen.tsx:182-188` `RAIL_LABEL` (with hand-numbered "1 Check", "2 Copy", "3 fstab") and `src/domain/base.ts:71-77` `PAGE_TITLE` are two per-page name tables for one set of page ids.
- [hidden] UD3080 `tui/apps/install/base-system/src/domain/base.ts:150` and `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:103` each format the copied-file count with `toLocaleString("en-US")`, and the kit has no shared number formatter.
- [hidden] UD3101 `tui/apps/runtime/recab-tui/src/presentation/keymap.ts:19` `FOOTER_ACTIONS` names `MAIN_KEYS` actions in a `ReadonlySet<string>`, so a renamed action silently drops out of the footer.
- [surplus] UD1109 `tui/kit/core/src/app/app-provider.tsx:32` `useAppName` and `tui/kit/core/src/components/feedback/status-line.tsx:50` `scopeStatusSegment` are exported from `src/index.ts:2,21`, which says it is "exactly what the apps import", but no app imports either.

### G25 install-tests is the only scenario runner and VM launcher

Solves **103** problems (3 parity, 37 special, 41 hidden, 5 needless, 17 surplus).

- **Design:** install-tests owns the whole scenario run (inputs, cache, results); xtask passes typed options and renders the result. One VM launcher with explicit inputs (injection, SSH key and user, size, URI) serves both `test` and `run`; `run` opens that VM on your terminal. Host libvirt's QEMU, OVMF and qemu-img are the only VM tools, resolved by recqemu, which reports failures; the QEMU/OVMF producer moves to the harness or goes. Missing prerequisites are a typed value that only xtask renders. No env-var handshakes between crates.
- **Done when:** xtask has no VM, SSH or serial code; no `unsafe set_var`; `cargo xtask env` and the repo QEMU download are gone; `run` and `test` VMs use the same naming and firmware.
- **Depends on:** G05, G06, G09
- **Decisions:** Drop `cargo xtask env` and `setup`'s QEMU download (default yes). Rename `run` VMs to `<distro>-<scenario>` (default yes). Injection via fw_cfg only (default yes, after confirming both live initramfs images carry `qemu_fw_cfg`).
- **Merges area designs:** XT-03 (xtask), XT-04 (xtask), TV-10 (testing-vm), XT-08 (xtask), XT-10 (xtask), RG-12 (builder-recipes-guest) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] TI074 `testing/install-tests/src/qemu/session.rs:92-95` attaches the boot injection (fw_cfg plus an extra CD) to *every* scenario VM, installed-system boots included. `xtask run installed-boot` refuses injection because "the installed system does not read it" (`xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:47-50`). The two VM paths disagree about which VMs get injection.
- [parity] XT1165 `xtask/tests/cli.rs:24-61` checks stale-status reporting only for `levitate`, and `cli.rs:63-78` checks corrupt-state handling only for `acorn`. Neither behaviour is checked for the other distro.
- [parity] XT2165 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:282` (serial wait 120 s) and `xtask/src/cli/types.rs:315-316` (`--ssh-timeout` 90 s) give both distros the same fixed live-boot budget for `run`, while install-tests allows AcornOS a longer boot (`testing/install-tests/src/distro/acorn.rs:17-19` 180 s stall timeout vs the 60 s default in `distro/mod.rs:23-25`). The allowance for the slower sibling exists only in install-tests, so `run --no-shell acorn` can fail a boot that `test live-boot acorn` would accept.
- [special] BR110 `distro/builder/recipes/qemu-tools.rhai:81` excludes `qemu-bridge-helper` from extraction with a cpio `--nonmatching` pattern because scenarios use user networking, a carve-out for one consumer inside the producer.
- [special] RG2139 `distro/builder/recipes/qemu-tools.rhai:59-65` `is_installed` throws on a QEMU version mismatch instead of returning `needs_work`, while the kernel recipes return `needs_work` for a stale pin. A new pin therefore fails with an error instead of reinstalling.
- [special] TI050 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:577` `remote.power_off()` sends `poweroff -f` and discards the result (`tools/recshuttle/src/lib.rs:113-115`). The domain is then destroyed by `drop(vm)`. Cleanliness of the install disk relies on the earlier `sync` and `umount -R` verify steps (`:568-569`), which are listed as "verification".
- [special] TI055 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:304,309` `reboot`/`poweroff` send the command with a 2s exec timeout and discard the result (`let _ =`). Success is judged only by the domain disappearing within `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT` (180s).
- [special] TI076 `testing/install-tests/src/boot_injection.rs:115-135` tries `xorriso`, `genisoimage` and `mkisofs` in turn to build the injection ISO, a fallback chain that also duplicates the fw_cfg channel, which already carries the same payload (`attach`, `:39-44`).
- [special] TI077 `testing/install-tests/src/boot_injection.rs:40` `attach` falls back to the relative path when `canonicalize` fails (`unwrap_or_else`), so a missing file reaches libvirt as a relative path instead of an error. `session.rs:78` canonicalizes the other paths with `?`.
- [special] TI078 `testing/install-tests/src/qemu/session.rs:128-135` `allocate_local_port` binds port 0 and releases it before passt binds it (a documented race). `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:672-683` `ensure_ssh_port_available` is a second bind-probe for the same purpose.
- [special] TI082 `tools/recqemu/src/libvirt.rs:22-24` fixes memory at 4096 MiB and 4 vCPUs for every domain; scenarios have no way to size a VM (for example a desktop ISO).
- [special] TI083 `tools/recqemu/src/libvirt.rs:204-216` `create_paused` destroys any running domain of the same name (`let _ = virsh destroy`). Two concurrent runs of one scenario (or ab-update's repeated boots, TI062) kill each other silently, and the runtime-dir socket and log names collide the same way.
- [special] TV1121 `testing/install-tests/src/qemu/session.rs:69` `let _ = fs::remove_file(&serial_log)` swallows the error; `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:574` repeats it.
- [special] TV1137 `tools/recqemu/src/libvirt.rs:205-207` a `virsh dominfo` failure is treated as "not persistent" via `unwrap_or(false)` and the domain is then destroyed; `:214`, `:219`, `:227`, `:284`, `:311` swallow `virsh destroy` / `remove_file` results.
- [special] TV1143 `tools/recqemu/src/lib.rs:81-82` `find_ovmf_vars` defaults `parent()` to `/` and `file_name()` to `""` instead of failing.
- [special] TV2127 `tools/recqemu/src/serial/console.rs:61` the reader thread's `while let Ok(n) = output.read(..)` ends on the first read error exactly as on EOF, so an I/O error on the serial socket looks to every caller like the guest closing the port.
- [special] TV2130 `tools/recqemu/src/lib.rs:21-34` `disk_image_format` returns `"raw"` when the file cannot be opened or read, so an unreadable qcow2 is handed to libvirt as a raw disk instead of failing.
- [special] TV3115 `testing/install-tests/src/boot_injection.rs:28-35` `Drop` removes the payload file and the injection ISO with `let _ = remove_file`, which swallows cleanup failures. Leftover `levitate-boot-injection-*` files are never reported.
- [special] TV3122 `tools/recqemu/src/libvirt.rs:316-323` `domain_exists` treats any `virsh domstate` failure (libvirt unreachable, wrong URI, spawn error) as "no such domain". `is_running` (`:266-268`) then reports false, and `wait_for_shutdown` (`:271-280`) returns `Ok` at once when virsh is broken. The A/B scenario reads that as "the guest rebooted".
- [special] TV3138 `tools/recqemu/src/serial/ansi.rs:29` tests CSI final bytes with `next as u8`, which truncates a `char` to its low byte. A non-ASCII char whose low byte falls in 0x70-0x7E ends an escape sequence early, and the following output is stripped or kept incorrectly.
- [special] X008 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:649-661` the `run --ssh` debug probe types OpenRC commands (`rc-service networking/dhcpcd status`) and systemd commands (`journalctl -u sshd.service`) into every guest, all `|| true`; each distro gets half the probe failing silently, and AcornOS gets no sshd log at all (nothing reads OpenRC's log).
- [special] X039 `xtask/src/cli/types.rs:312` and `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:672-683` `run --ssh` uses a fixed default port 2222 with a bind-then-drop check (a race), while install-tests allocates a free port (`session.rs:128-135`); two policies for the same forward.
- [special] X042 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:283-286` an undocumented env var `LEVITATE_LIVE_BOOT_SERIAL_TIMEOUT` overrides the 120 s timeout, and a value that does not parse is silently ignored.
- [special] X043 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:311,429` polling loops with `sleep(1s)`; `:663` sleeps 2 s after typing the debug probe and hopes the output has landed.
- [special] X044 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:416` `let _ = collect_guest_ssh_debug(vm)` drops the probe's error, `emit_new_log_lines` (`:434-437`) turns a failed log read into `Ok(())`, and five hand-placed `let _ = fs::remove_file(&known_hosts)` (`:382,387,405,410,418`) sit on each exit path (cleanup by convention, not by a guard).
- [special] X048 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:40-52` `installed-boot` rejects `--ssh`, `--no-shell` and every boot-injection flag one by one; the `RunArgs` struct offers options that one variant of the enum can never take.
- [special] X077 `xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:76-82` `gap()` turns any non-prerequisite error into the fix "cargo xtask setup", even for errors setup cannot fix (contract load failures, a kernel verify error).
- [special] XT1070 `xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:66-71` doctor's exit error carries only the first failing required check's fix (`missing.first()`), and `unwrap_or_default()` makes it an empty fix when that check has none.
- [special] XT1072 `xtask/src/util/repo.rs:38` treats every canonicalize error except PermissionDenied (a dangling `current` symlink, ELOOP, ENOTDIR) as "tools not installed" (`Err(_) => Ok(None)`), which silently falls back to the host QEMU and OVMF.
- [special] XT1075 `xtask/src/util/tools_env.rs:24` sets `LD_LIBRARY_PATH` to the repo tools' `usr/lib64` instead of prepending it, so any host value is dropped for every child of `build` and `test` (and in `cargo xtask env`'s export, `:59`).
- [special] XT1082 `xtask/src/util/ssh.rs:20-21` `let _ = remove_file` on both key halves before regenerating swallows errors.
- [special] XT1099 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:651,660` the SSH debug probe prints `___SSH_DEBUG_BEGIN___` / `___SSH_DEBUG_END___` markers that nothing in the repo parses.
- [special] XT1104 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:59-69,76` `--ssh-key` alone (without `--ssh`) still resolves the key and injects `SSH_AUTHORIZED_KEY`, and then `:76` ignores the key unless `--ssh`. The serial install UX is injected only when neither `--ssh` nor `--no-shell` is given, three flag combinations that each behave differently.
- [special] XT1107 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:178-180` `test` always resolves and injects the SSH key, even for scenarios that boot nothing such as `build-preflight`, and `.context("the payload always holds the SSH key")` guards a `None` that cannot happen.
- [special] XT1166 `xtask/tests/cli.rs:118-122` the doctor test accepts exit 0 or 3, so it passes whether or not the host is set up. It checks only that failed checks carry a `fix`.
- [special] XT2009 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:273-278` `LiveBootPatterns::first_in` turns a serial-log read error into "no match" (`read_to_string(..).ok()?`), so a missing or unreadable log is never reported and the loop just runs until the timeout. Each loop iteration reads the whole log twice, once for fatal and once for success patterns.
- [special] XT2015 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:363-431` vs `:281-313` the two run monitors are separate loops with different behaviour: `monitor_live_iso_ssh` echoes the serial log to stdout while it waits (`:379`, `emit_new_log_lines`), while `monitor_live_iso_serial` (`--no-shell`) prints nothing until it succeeds or fails. The two run modes report boot progress differently.
- [special] XT2073 `xtask/src/util/ssh.rs:45` builds the public-key path by appending `.pub` to the string (`format!("{}.pub", ..)`), while `ensure_repo_key` (`:15`, `:21`) derives it with `with_extension("pub")`. These are two rules for the same file name, and they disagree for a key whose name already has an extension.
- [special] XT2074 `xtask/src/util/ssh.rs:46-48` a `--ssh-key` path that does not exist is a plain `bail!` (exit 1), not a usage error (2) or a missing prerequisite (3), while a missing `ssh-keygen` in the same module is a prerequisite (exit 3, `:26-31`).
- [special] XT3017 `xtask/src/util/repo.rs:46` `ovmf_path` returns `<tools>/usr/share/edk2/ovmf/OVMF_CODE.fd` without checking that the file exists when the repo tools are present, while the host branch (`recqemu::find_ovmf`) does check it. `repo.rs:48` then replaces the host lookup's own error with a fixed message (`map_err(|_| ...)`).
- [hidden] BR107 `distro/builder/recipes/qemu-tools.rhai:52` and `distro/builder/src/recipe/qemu.rs:20` each list the four required QEMU/OVMF files. `src/recipe/qemu.rs:15` grants `dl.rockylinux.org:443` separately from the URLs in the recipe (`:12-28`).
- [hidden] RG1131 `distro/builder/recipes/qemu-tools.rhai:3,7,12-27`: the QEMU version `10.0.0` appears both in `version` (checked against `--version` at `:59`) and inside the qemu RPM URLs. Rocky `10.1` appears in the comment and in every URL. A bump means editing all of them by hand.
- [hidden] TI062 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:208-209,218-219` every boot of the scenario reuses the domain name `<distro>-ab-update`. `Domain::create_paused` destroys a same-named running domain (`libvirt.rs:204-216`), so the scenario relies on the previous boot having fully ended.
- [hidden] TI072 `testing/install-tests/src/qemu/session.rs:92` `spawn` reads the boot injection from process env (`LEVITATE_BOOT_INJECTION_FILE`), which `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:183-186` sets with `unsafe { set_var }`. The login key reaches `recshuttle` the same way (`LEVITATE_SSH_PRIVATE_KEY`, `tools/recshuttle/src/lib.rs:23`). This is a global handshake between crates with no parameter showing it.
- [hidden] TI073 `testing/install-tests/src/qemu/session.rs:64-107` and `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:562-617` `start_vm` are two parallel builders of a `DomainSpec` (socket and log naming in `runtime_dir`, fresh vs existing UEFI vars, find_ovmf, attach injection, `create_paused`, `Console::connect(.., 20s)`, `resume`). Domain names follow two conventions (`<distro>-<scenario>` vs `<distro>-run-<scenario>`).
- [hidden] TI081 `tools/recqemu/src/libvirt.rs:138-142` attaches the boot ISO twice, as a CD and as a read-only SCSI disk, "so the live init sees the ISO's partitions". This knowledge belongs to recinit's partition probe (`BOOT_DEVICE_PROBE_ORDER`, `ROOTFS_PAYLOAD_PARTITION`), and the SCSI letters it creates (`sda`, `sdb`, ...) must line up with `recinit::live::BOOT_INJECTION_DEVICES` (`/dev/sr1`, `/dev/sdb`, ...).
- [hidden] TI087 `tools/recqemu/src/libvirt.rs:26-29` `LEVITATE_LIBVIRT_URI` env override; `lib.rs:59-98` `OVMF_PATH`/`OVMF_VARS_PATH`. Behaviour depends on host env read deep in the library.
- [hidden] TI142 `tools/recshuttle/src/lib.rs:52-62` uses `StrictHostKeyChecking=no` plus `UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null`, `ConnectTimeout=5`, and no `IdentitiesOnly`. `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:503-518` uses `accept-new`, a temp known_hosts file, `ConnectTimeout=10` and `IdentitiesOnly=yes` against the same guests. The two SSH configurations are kept separately.
- [hidden] TI143 `tools/recshuttle/src/lib.rs:22-28` defaults the key to `~/.ssh/id_ed25519` (or `$HOME=/root`), while the key xtask injects into the guest is the repo key from `xtask/src/util/ssh.rs:40-44`. The harness works only because xtask also exports `LEVITATE_SSH_PRIVATE_KEY` (TI072). The default never matches the key that was injected.
- [hidden] TI149 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:273-313,368-431` `cargo xtask run --no-shell/--ssh` re-implements boot detection by re-reading the whole serial log file every second and `contains`-matching it, instead of `Console::wait_for_boot`. It also has its own 120s timeout (`LEVITATE_LIVE_BOOT_SERIAL_TIMEOUT`) and no stall logic, so it judges a boot differently from `cargo xtask test live-boot`.
- [hidden] TV2095 `testing/install-tests/src/boot_injection.rs:17-18,105`, `src/scenarios/state.rs:107`, `tools/recshuttle/src/lib.rs:23`, `tools/recqemu/src/libvirt.rs:28,349` the env and temp names `LEVITATE_BOOT_INJECTION_*`, `LEVITATE_SCENARIO_STATE_DIR`, `LEVITATE_SSH_PRIVATE_KEY`, `LEVITATE_LIBVIRT_URI`, `levitate-boot-injection-*` and `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/levitate-vm` carry one sibling's name although they drive AcornOS runs too.
- [hidden] TV2117 `tools/recshuttle/src/lib.rs:63` the SSH transport always connects as `root@{HOSTFWD_ADDRESS}`; the guest user is fixed inside the transport crate, with no way for a caller to log in as the contract's `default_username`.
- [hidden] TV2131 `tools/recqemu/src/lib.rs:82-87` with `code_name` defaulting to `""` every `OVMF_FILES` row matches `ends_with`, and the fallback chain appends `OVMF_VARS.fd`/`OVMF_VARS_4M.fd`, names already listed in `OVMF_FILES` (`:38-44`).
- [hidden] TV3124 `tools/recqemu/src/libvirt.rs:45-46` documents `UefiVars::Fresh` as "removed when the domain ends", but the file is removed only in `Drop for Domain` (`:304-313`), which `keep` skips. When the guest reboots or powers off, the domain ends and the file stays until the handle drops. The doc describes a lifetime the code does not have.
- [hidden] X009 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:654-659` the probe hard-codes per-distro service names (`networking`, `dhcpcd`, `sshd.service`) that the variant contracts also own; renaming a service in a variant leaves the probe stale.
- [hidden] X018 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:139-146` and `:129-136` the "needs a disk from the install scenario" prerequisite is written twice with near-identical text, and both swallow `resolve_latest_install_runtime`'s real error (`map_err(|_| ...)`).
- [hidden] X020 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:148` vs `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:159-170` the ISO is resolved and checked twice per scenario, once by xtask (`require_iso`) and again by install-tests (`resolve_iso_artifact_for_scenario` + `require_preflight`), by different code.
- [hidden] X023 `xtask/tests/cli.rs:9,28-34,66-67` the CLI test writes install-tests' private state-file JSON shape (`input_fingerprints`, `results.{passed,timestamp,evidence}`) and its env var `LEVITATE_SCENARIO_STATE_DIR` (`testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/state.rs:107`) by literal; a change to install-tests' state format breaks xtask's test with nothing pointing there.
- [hidden] X026 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:183-186` `test` hands the boot injection and the SSH key to install-tests by `unsafe set_var` of process-global env vars; install-tests reads one in `boot_injection_from_env` (`testing/install-tests/src/boot_injection.rs:89-97`) and recshuttle reads the other (`tools/recshuttle/src/lib.rs:22-28`). The call contract is invisible in the function signatures.
- [hidden] X027 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:185` `"LEVITATE_SSH_PRIVATE_KEY"` is a string literal repeated in `tools/recshuttle/src/lib.rs:23,48` with no shared constant, unlike `ENV_INJECT_FILE`, which is a const.
- [hidden] X029 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:177` `test` applies the repo tools env to the whole process (`tools_env::apply_to_process`, `OVMF_PATH`/`OVMF_VARS_PATH`/`PATH`/`LD_LIBRARY_PATH`), but `run` never does; `recqemu::find_ovmf` reads `OVMF_PATH`, so `test` VMs use the repo OVMF and `run` VMs use the host's, for the same libvirt QEMU. `start_vm`'s comment (`scenarios.rs:590`) says the host's OVMF must match libvirt's QEMU, which `test` breaks.
- [hidden] X030 `xtask/src/util/tools_env.rs:12-28` and `:49-67` the env is built twice, once as a `Vec` and once as a hand-formatted `export` string, so a new variable must be added in both.
- [hidden] X031 `xtask/src/util/tools_env.rs:17` and `:66` derive the VARS file by `with_file_name("OVMF_VARS.fd")`, duplicating recqemu's CODE→VARS pairing table (`tools/recqemu/src/lib.rs:38-44`, which also has `_4M` pairs).
- [hidden] X032 `xtask/src/util/repo.rs:22,46` hard-code the layout of distro-builder's QEMU tools output (`.artifacts/tools/current`, `usr/share/edk2/ovmf/OVMF_CODE.fd`, `usr/bin`, `usr/libexec`, `usr/lib64` in `tools_env.rs:19,24`); distro-builder's `prepare_qemu_tools` owns that tree.
- [hidden] X034 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:562-617` `start_vm` duplicates `testing/install-tests/src/qemu/session.rs:64-107` `spawn` (runtime_dir socket/log names, removing the old log, Fresh/Existing UEFI vars, canonicalize, `DomainSpec` fields, create_paused → Console::connect(20s) → resume). A `DomainSpec` change has to be made in both.
- [hidden] X035 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:571` names run domains `<distro>-run-<scenario>` and titles them `"<Pretty> <scenario> (cargo xtask run)"`, while install-tests uses `<distro>-<scenario>` / `"Scenario VM <name>"` (`session.rs:22,81`); `AGENTS.md` states `<distro>-<scenario>` for both. Two conventions plus a doc.
- [hidden] X040 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:93-121` `injection_payload` parses `--inject` comma pairs itself, while install-tests has `BootInjection::from_pairs` for the same syntax, with validation (`boot_injection.rs:58-85`); xtask's copy accepts entries without `=`.
- [hidden] X047 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:351-360` `boot_live_iso_ssh` appends the log tail (`maybe_append_log_fault`) to errors that `bail_with_tail` already ended with the same 120-line tail, so failures print it twice; `dump_log_tail` / `bail_with_tail` / `maybe_append_log_fault` (`:693-734`) are three overlapping helpers.
- [hidden] X075 `xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:127-133` doctor's OVMF row calls `recqemu::find_ovmf()` without the tools env, so it reports the host's firmware even when the repo tools (`util/repo.rs:44-54` `ovmf_path`) would be used by `test`; `ovmf_path` and `find_ovmf` are two resolvers.
- [hidden] XT1030 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:189-200` `test all` runs its scenarios in a fixed order and `break`s at the first failure, which `xtask/README.md:29` documents as "in order, stop at first failure". `AGENTS.md` says scenarios run independently with no ordered test gates and `architecture.md` says there is no ordered ladder, so the front door holds an ordered gate the repo rules forbid.
- [hidden] XT1076 `xtask/src/util/tools_env.rs:38-46` `apply_to_process` mutates the process env with `unsafe set_var` and relies on the doc rule "Call before starting threads" (caller `scenarios.rs:177`). Nothing enforces it.
- [hidden] XT1103 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:68` writes `{SERIAL_KEY}=1`, and `distro/builder/guest/install-ux/install-ux.sh:23` greps for exactly `@SERIAL_KEY@=1`. The key is a shared constant, but the `=1` value is written separately in xtask and in the guest script.
- [hidden] XT1164 `xtask/tests/cli.rs:124` asserts that a doctor check is labelled exactly `"OVMF firmware"` (`setup.rs:128`), and `cli.rs:109-110` pins the "needs a terminal" / "instead: cargo xtask" wording of `xtask/src/util/exit.rs:64`. Rewording a display string breaks the test.
- [hidden] XT2004 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:520-539,585` `run installed-boot` boots the install scenario's own disk and its VARS file in place (`UefiVars::Existing`, no copy or overlay), using the host's OVMF code (`:590-591`) with a VARS file the install scenario created under `test`'s repo-tools firmware. An interactive session changes the disk that the cached `installed-boot`/`automated-login`/`runtime` results were computed on, and the install-run fingerprint does not notice (the xtask counterpart of TI025).
- [hidden] XT2167 `xtask/README.md:47` promises that a prerequisite error exits 3 and ends with `fix: <command>`, but prerequisite errors raised inside install-tests are plain `anyhow` errors (`testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:724-727`, "Build it first: cargo xtask build ..."), so `xtask/src/util/exit.rs:45-52` `code_for` gives them exit 1 and no `fix:` line. The same goes for distro-builder's remediation errors (CL1048). Only the checks xtask repeats up front (X019, X056) get exit 3.
- [hidden] XT3009 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:356` and `:358` re-wrap the `run --ssh` monitor error as a new string error (`bail!("{err}\n{report}")` / `bail!("{:#}", err)`). The original error chain is lost, so any typed `MissingPrereq`/`Usage` in it would no longer reach `exit::code_for` (`xtask/src/util/exit.rs:45`), and the `{err}` branch (taken whenever a log fault report exists) prints only the outermost message without its context chain. TI037 records the same flattening in install-tests.
- [hidden] XT3020 `xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:121-125` doctor looks for `qemu-img` with `which` on xtask's own PATH. It never applies the tool environment (only `test` does, `scenarios.rs:177`), so after `cargo xtask setup` puts `qemu-img` under `.artifacts/tools/current/usr/bin`, doctor still reports `[FAIL] qemu-img` and offers `cargo xtask setup` as the fix.
- [hidden] XT3026 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:61` and `:179` spell the guest injection key `SSH_AUTHORIZED_KEY` as a literal, and the live init matches it with its own literal (`tools/recinit/templates/init_tiny.template:353`). The other injection names come from recinit constants (`scenarios.rs:2` `BOOT_INJECTION_DIR`/`BOOT_INJECTION_ENV`), but this key the init reads has no shared definition.
- [hidden] XT3028 `xtask/README.md:50` says xtask always injects "the SSH key" itself, but `run` injects it only with `--ssh` or `--ssh-key` (`scenarios.rs:59`). A plain `run live-tools` boots without the repo key.
- [hidden] XT3029 `xtask/README.md:49` promises "Nothing reads `~/.ssh`", but `common_ssh_args` (`xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:503-518`) passes no `-F /dev/null`. The user's `~/.ssh/config` (User, ProxyJump, `Host *` options) still applies to `run --ssh`'s readiness probe and interactive session. X028 records a different leak of the same promise, recshuttle's `~/.ssh/id_ed25519` fallback.
- [hidden] XT3040 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:207` `test --json` prints its JSON summary on stdout after install-tests has already printed its progress there (`testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:175-202` and the crate's other `println!` calls). The advertised JSON output (`xtask/README.md:29`) cannot be parsed from stdout, while `status --json` and `doctor --json` keep stdout clean.
- [needless] RG1134 `distro/builder/recipes/qemu-tools.rhai:1-2`: a host-side test-tool producer sits among the distro product recipes in `distro/builder/recipes`, and `distro/builder/src/recipe/qemu.rs:6` reaches into that folder for it. Distro recipe changes and VM-harness tool changes share one directory and one `--recipes-path`.
- [needless] X022 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:215-228` status output is split by format: install-tests prints the text table (`print_status`), xtask builds the JSON. The same goes for `test --json` (`:202-208`) while install-tests prints the per-scenario lines. Each format changes in a different crate.
- [needless] X037 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:259-271` `LiveBootPatterns::load` assembles the pattern set from three crates' internals: `install_tests::distro::load_scenarios`, `install_tests::qemu::serial::live_fatal` and `recqemu::serial::protocol::SHELL_READY_MARKER`.
- [needless] X041 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:68` reaches into `distro_builder::install_ux::SERIAL_KEY` to turn on the serial install UX, and `:2,657-658` into `recinit::live::{BOOT_INJECTION_DIR, BOOT_INJECTION_ENV}` for the debug probe: the front door depends on builder and init internals.
- [needless] XT3008 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1178`, `:1190`, `:1408` and `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/state.rs:45` write xtask command lines ("Run: cargo xtask test install", "Re-run install: cargo xtask test install {} --force", "Rebuild live-boot ISO: cargo xtask build live-boot", "Reset it with: cargo xtask reset") into the test crate's own error text, and `xtask/tests/cli.rs:72` pins one of them. Renaming an xtask subcommand or flag (`xtask/src/cli/types.rs`) silently makes these remediation messages wrong. This is the same pattern BP117 (`distro/builder/src/pipeline/kernel.rs:97`) and TI135 (`preflight.rs:761`) record for other sites.

README:20-21 documents both channels.
- [surplus] TI079 `testing/install-tests/src/qemu/session.rs:46` `Vm.serial_log` is stored and never read.

- [surplus] TI145 `tools/recshuttle/src/lib.rs:113-115` `power_off` (`let _ = exec("poweroff -f")`) is a scenario policy (forced, unclean) living in a transport crate whose doc says "what to run is the caller's".
- [surplus] TV2092 `testing/install-tests/README.md:23-25` says `cargo xtask test` and `cargo xtask run` set both `LEVITATE_BOOT_INJECTION_FILE` and `LEVITATE_BOOT_INJECTION_KV`; xtask only ever sets the file variable, so the README documents the unused KV channel as live.
- [surplus] TV2096 `testing/install-tests/src/qemu/session.rs:44,51-53` `Vm::ssh_port` is both a pub `Option<u16>` field and a method of the same name that unwraps it; callers can reach the port two ways.
- [surplus] X019 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:176` and `:190` `require_inputs` runs for the first scenario before the loop and again inside it.
- [surplus] X021 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:230-232` `reset` passes straight through to `install_tests::scenarios::reset_state`; `status` without `--json` (`:216-218`) is also a pass-through to `print_status`.
- [surplus] X024 `xtask/tests/cli.rs:86` the "bad usage" case passes `--up-to-scenario`, a flag from the removed ordered-ladder design; it only proves clap rejects an unknown flag.
- [surplus] X033 `xtask/src/tasks/setup.rs:11-12` and `xtask/README.md:51` `setup` installs a repo QEMU, and README says "QEMU: the repo tools when installed", but every VM is a libvirt domain run by libvirt's own QEMU (`tools/recqemu/src/libvirt.rs`); the repo `qemu-system` is never the emulator. Only `qemu-img`/OVMF from it can matter.
- [surplus] X045 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:444-446` `for line in lines.drain(..cursor) { let _ = line; }` does nothing useful; `emit_new_log_lines` re-reads the whole log every second.
- [surplus] X046 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:685-691` `temp_file_path` builds timestamp-named temp files by hand while the same file uses `tempfile` (`:114`); install-tests has another copy (`boot_injection.rs:99`).
- [surplus] XT1097 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:419` `format!("No successful SSH handshake observed.")` has no arguments, and `:421` overwrites the value when no hook was seen.
- [surplus] XT1139 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/inspect.rs:270-275` `unix_timestamp` and `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:685-691` `temp_file_path` each read `SystemTime::now()` since the epoch to name files, with different error handling.
- [surplus] XT2020 `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:668-670` `detect_boot_regression` is a one-line forwarder to `LiveBootPatterns::first_in(&patterns.fatal, ..)`. It returns `Result` but has no error path, and both callers (`:294`, `:386`) apply `?` to it.

### G26 Scenarios judge both distros by the same oracle

Solves **116** problems (9 parity, 52 special, 41 hidden, 2 needless, 12 surplus).

- **Design:** A scenario checks a capability (sshd runs, the privilege tool works, a failed trial comes back) the same way on both distros; only declared facts and one small systemd/OpenRC service backend differ. One `Guest` interface over serial and SSH with honest outcomes; waits end on structured signals under one time budget; evidence scripts are one portable library that records every failure; preflight checks the contract's declared artifacts once; each producer verifies its own output when it writes it. Both distros × both install modes (A/B and mutable) plus ab-update are the proof.
- **Done when:** No per-distro branch in scenarios or test scripts; no fixed sleep or console-string readiness check; the same scenarios pass on both distros.
- **Depends on:** G04, G05, G07, G17, G25
- **Decisions:** installed-boot fails on any failed service (default yes). **Designers disagree on the mutable install path:** TV proposed dropping it, TO keeps it and tests it. Default here: keep it and test it on both.
- **Merges area designs:** TV-02 (testing-vm), TV-04 (testing-vm), TV-05 (testing-vm), TV-11 (testing-vm), TV-13 (testing-vm), PA-12 (parity-all), TS-04 (testing-static), TO-16 (tools) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] TF068 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/install_initramfs.rs:1-226` checks only the recinit systemd install initramfs. AcornOS has no `initramfs_installed` transform (`distro/variants/acorn/ring1/transforms.toml` vs levitate `:21-25`), and `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:383` gates the call on `distro_id == "levitate"`. AcornOS's installed boot path has no static check at all.
- [parity] TI051 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/install.sh:29-32` the install evidence only checks `$TARGET/bin` and `BOOTX64.EFI`. The A/B-specific checks live in Rust (`mod.rs:549-566`), so what "install passed" means is split between the script and the harness.
- [parity] TI111 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:605-645` installed-boot never runs its contract evidence `installed-boot.sh` (`scenarios.toml:130-132`), so the "Installed System Separation" checks (`test-scripts/installed-boot.sh:31-38`: no live helper, no live profiles, no serial autologin, no empty root password) are declared and never executed, for either distro.
- [parity] TI113 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/live-boot.sh:39-58` the systemd branch checks `inst.sshd=0`, sshd enabled/active and the tmpfiles policy but *not* networking or a default route. The OpenRC branch checks networking, dhcpcd and a default route but not `inst.sshd=0` or tmpfiles. Each distro's live boot is held to a different checklist.
- [parity] TL110 `tools/recab/tests/lifecycle.rs:134` every lifecycle test forces `RECAB_INIT_SYSTEM=systemd`; only two tests (`:766`, `:817`) cover OpenRC (trial and install-unit), so `init`, `stage`'s OpenRC carry list and `reboot` choice are exercised only for systemd.
- [parity] TL111 `tools/recab/README.md:220-236` the A/B end-to-end run "has not run on a booted LevitateOS yet", AcornOS's live-tools build "has not been tried", and OpenRC's state directory and `rc-status --crashed` are unseen on a real AcornOS boot; the OpenRC trial logic (TL083/TL084) is built on assumptions.
- [parity] TO2095 `tools/recpart/tests/loop_disk.rs:59-116` covers only `--mode ab` on the build host's glibc userland with udevadm; mutable mode, manual layouts, and the AcornOS (busybox/mdev, no udevadm) apply path are never exercised on a block device.
- [parity] TO2134 `tools/recstrap/tests/recsetup.rs:9-22` the only fake target is a systemd one with bootctl and `ID=levitateos`; the OpenRC/Copy-bootloader/no-initramfs plan that AcornOS takes has no test.
- [parity] TV3118 `distro/variants/acorn/scenarios/scenarios.toml:29` makes OpenRC's `"ERROR: cannot start"` fatal during live boot. The same file lists it at `:113` only as a tolerated `service_failure_patterns` entry for installed boot, so one console message fails one AcornOS scenario and passes another.
- [special] TF074 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/iso.rs:318-324` treats a missing `fdisk` or any fdisk error as "not partitioned" (swallowed error). `appended-partitions` is reciso's default layout (`tools/reciso/src/main.rs:93`), so without `fdisk` on the host, preflight reports `live/filesystem.erofs` "Missing (CRITICAL: no system to boot into)" instead of a tool error. `fdisk` is not in `required_build_tools` (`distro/variants/*/build-host/build-host.toml:4-18`).
- [special] TF075 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/iso.rs:327-329` requires both partitions 2 and 3, but reciso appends partition 3 only `if let Some(ref overlay_image)` (`tools/reciso/src/lib.rs:182-188`). An ISO without an overlay fails the rootfs check through the same misleading message.
- [special] TF077 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/iso.rs:268-271` always adds a passing "loader.conf editor policy" result without inspecting anything. It is a hard-coded pass.
- [special] TF085 `testing/fsdbg/src/iso.rs:49` `get_volume_id(path).ok()` swallows the `isoinfo` error, so the ISO checklist reports "No volume ID set" (`checklist/iso.rs:305-310`) instead of the read failure.
- [special] TI001 `testing/install-tests/src/distro/acorn.rs:16` AcornOS overrides `live_boot_stall_timeout_secs` to 180s ("OpenRC's early boot can stay quiet for longer"); LevitateOS gets the 60s trait default (`distro/mod.rs:23`). It is a timeout raised for one distro, which contradicts `docs/FAIL_FAST_POLICY.md:15-30` in the same crate.
- [special] TI005 `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:383` `if distro_id == "levitate"` gates the install-initramfs checklist by distro name, not by whether the contract declares `transforms.initramfs_installed` (which `canonical_runtime_artifact_names_for_distro` already reads at line 465).
- [special] TI049 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:514` `remote.wait_ready` reuses `ctx.live_boot_stall_timeout_secs()`, a *serial silence* budget, as the SSH readiness timeout, while `verify_live_boot_ssh_login` (`:838`) uses a hard-coded 60s for the same wait in the other two live scenarios.
- [special] TI056 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:28-34` five separate fixed timeouts (`STAGE_TIMEOUT` 900s, `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT` 180s, `SHELL_TIMEOUT` 60s, `TRIAL_REBOOT_TIMEOUT` 300s, `COMMIT_WAIT` 200s), none tied to the per-distro stall timeout used by the live scenarios.
- [special] TI057 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/ab_update.rs:325-343` `failing_service` writes a systemd unit plus a wants-symlink, or an OpenRC script plus a runlevel symlink, by matching on `InitSystem`. The test owns the init systems' on-disk enablement layout, which recab already models for its own trial service (`trial_service_link()`).
- [special] TI067 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:371-378` live-boot builds a `ScriptEvidence` for `live-boot-ssh-preflight.sh` with a hard-coded pass marker `"OPENSSH PREFLIGHT PASSED"`. Every other evidence script and marker comes from the contract.
- [special] TI069 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:885-886` diagnoses `anaconda-sshd.service` on every distro, and `test-scripts/live-boot-ssh-preflight.sh:25` requires `inst.sshd=0` (a Fedora installer knob) on both distros, including AcornOS, which has no anaconda (the requirement is `distro/contract/src/schema.rs:9` `BOOT_REQUIRED_KERNEL_CMDLINE_BASE`).
- [special] TI085 `tools/recqemu/src/libvirt.rs:79,106,352-354` the domain type and CPU mode switch on `/dev/kvm` existing, so without it everything silently runs under TCG and the fixed scenario timeouts (TI056, TI102) are tuned for KVM speeds.
- [special] TI089 `tools/recqemu/src/serial/boot.rs:53-64` success, fatal and service-failure detection are plain `line.contains(pattern)`. With the contract's lists (e.g. `distro/variants/acorn/scenarios/scenarios.toml:20,30,87` `"File not found"`, `"ERROR: "`, `"login:"`) a pattern matches anywhere in any console line seen during the boot wait, including unrelated kernel or service messages.
- [special] TI091 `tools/recqemu/src/serial/exec.rs:32,44-49` an exit status that does not parse becomes `-1`, the same value as "did not complete". A timed-out command keeps running in the guest, so its late output and DONE marker interleave with the next `exec` (the markers are per-id, but `collecting` output is not filtered).
- [special] TI092 `tools/recqemu/src/serial/exec.rs:40` `exec` appends the raw `line` to `output` while matching markers on the ANSI-stripped `clean`, so callers' `output.contains(...)` checks (`scenarios/mod.rs:662,972`) see escape codes.
- [special] TI096 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:951-962` `run_evidence` transfers scripts in 200-char `printf '%s' >> file` chunks "below the terminal's canonical line bound". The bound is for the serial path, but the same chunking is used over SSH (`evidence_over_ssh`), so each chunk costs an ssh process (a 3 KB script takes ~20 connections). The 200 also ignores quote-expansion and the marker wrapper that `exec` adds (`protocol.rs:57-63`).
- [special] TI098 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:972-981` evidence passes only when the exit code is 0 *and* the output contains the pass marker. The marker is the contract's (`SCENARIO_PASS_MARKER` env), but `report_results` in `common.sh:186` falls back to a marker derived from the label when the env is unset, a second way to spell the same marker.
- [special] TI101 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1295` `INSTALLED_BOOT_STALL` is 90s for both distros, while live boot uses the per-distro value (60s/180s, TI001). The AcornOS OpenRC rationale is applied to one boot and not the other.
- [special] TI102 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:1296-1298` `LOGIN_TIMEOUT` 15s, `COMMAND_TIMEOUT` 5s (used for tool checks that run `--version` on the serial console), `EVIDENCE_TIMEOUT` 120s. `:901` adds another 15s for diagnostics.
- [special] TI103 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:838` 60s SSH readiness after shell-ready, hard-coded.
- [special] TI104 `testing/install-tests/src/qemu/session.rs:17` `SERIAL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT` 20s; `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:608` repeats `Duration::from_secs(20)`.
- [special] TI105 `tools/recshuttle/src/lib.rs:59,101` `ConnectTimeout=5` per ssh call and a 1s sleep between `wait_ready` attempts.
- [special] TI115 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/live-boot-ssh-preflight.sh:27` "Root authorized_keys is injectable" runs `test -d /root/.ssh || mkdir -p /root/.ssh`, a check that changes the guest so that it passes.
- [special] TI116 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/live-boot-ssh-preflight.sh` is called a "preflight" but runs after SSH login has already succeeded (`mod.rs:368-378`), so it can only confirm what the login proved.
- [special] TI122 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/installed-boot.sh:29` "Init system running" is `ps aux | grep -v grep | grep -q init`, a substring match that any process with "init" in its command line satisfies.
- [special] TI134 `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:220-250` when no ISO path is given, the evidence script resolves the latest successful **base-rootfs** run regardless of which product is under test, and `resolve_latest_successful_run_dir` (`:270-295`) re-implements `run_history::latest_successful_run_id` with an extra "ISO still on disk" filter.
- [special] TI135 `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:759-764` the failure hint always says "Rebuild the ISO: cargo xtask build base-rootfs", even when the failing artifact is a live-boot or live-tools ISO.
- [special] TI136 `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:351-354,589-603` when the named ISO is missing, it falls back to "the first `.iso` found" in the directory (`find_iso_file`, read_dir order). The run manifest already names the ISO.
- [special] TI139 `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:161-170` a passing conformance check reports a hard-coded `total_checks: 2, passed_checks: 2`, whatever was validated.
- [special] TI144 `tools/recshuttle/src/lib.rs:67` maps a signal-killed ssh (no exit code) to 255, the same value ssh uses for its own connection failures.
- [special] TO2063 `tools/recab/tests/lifecycle.rs:186-188` `Tree::read` returns `""` for an unreadable file (`unwrap_or_default`), so asserts like `!entry.contains("rd.emergency=reboot")` (`:782-785`) pass when the entry does not exist.
- [special] TO2064 `tools/recab/tests/lifecycle.rs:458-462,473-494` no test runs a successful `recab stage`: the scratch tree has no PARTUUID partition so stage stops at its partition check; formatting, recstrap extraction, `carry_configuration`, `merge_accounts` and `write_fstab` (`tools/recab/src/stage.rs:198-259`) are verified only by a manual loop-device run named in the README.
- [special] TO3166 `tools/recstrap/tests/recsetup.rs:36-56` the only recsetup tests check that `plan` refuses an unmounted root. Plan success, `apply`, the locale/user/boot steps and `inspect` are untested.
- [special] TS1095 `testing/fsdbg/src/cpio.rs:331-371` resolves a symlink target one level only and does not follow intermediate symlinks (`lib` -> `usr/lib`), so links through the merged-usr symlinks are reported missing.
- [special] TS1109 `testing/fsdbg/src/iso.rs:133,318-323` default unparsable sizes to 0 and block size to 2048 with `unwrap_or`.
- [special] TS1117 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/live_initramfs.rs:141-155` records a missing `modules.dep` as a failure while its message says insmod still works; the severity and the message disagree.
- [special] TS2071 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/install_initramfs.rs:379-385` and `rootfs.rs:468` skip already-checked symlinks by comparing the raw `entry.path` (stored as written in the archive, `cpio.rs:266`) with list entries, while every lookup normalises the path first (`cpio.rs:298-306,325-328` strip `./` and `/`). An archive written with `./`-prefixed names would re-check those links.
- [special] TS3079 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/iso.rs:159-164` records a missing `live/filesystem.erofs`/`overlayfs.erofs` as PASS "(partitioned payload)" whenever the partition heuristic is true; the payload partitions' contents are never checked, so an absent artifact is reported as a pass.
- [special] TS3121 `testing/fsdbg/src/cpio.rs:189-193` treats `UnexpectedEof` while reading a header as end of archive, so a truncated archive without `TRAILER!!!` parses as complete, and entry names and link targets are converted with `from_utf8_lossy` (`cpio.rs:231,260`), so non-UTF-8 names silently change.
- [special] TV1057 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/lib/common.sh:83-101` `test_tool` (and the other `test_*` helpers) return 1 on failure, and every script runs under `set -euo pipefail` (e.g. `live-tools.sh:13`) and calls them at top level, so the first failing check aborts the script; `report_results`' missing/broken summary and fail marker are never reached and the harness only sees a missing pass marker.
- [special] TV1070 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/installed-boot.sh:37` "Installed consoles authenticate" picks busybox-init vs systemd autologin checks by whether `/etc/inittab` exists, an init-system guess inside a shared script.
- [special] TV1104 `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:207`, `:223`, `:231`, `:243`, `:266` hand-write violation tags `[RecipeKernelOrchestrationRequired]` / `[InvalidEvidenceDeclaration]` as strings, imitating `distro_contract::ViolationCode` variants (`distro/contract/src/error.rs:55-56`) without using them.
- [special] TV1107 `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:561`, `:668` `metadata(..).map(len).unwrap_or(0)` prints a 0 MB size on a metadata error.
- [special] TV1130 `tools/recqemu/src/serial/boot.rs:62` the generic boot waiter always accepts `SHELL_READY_MARKER` as success, including installed-system boots (`testing/install-tests/src/qemu/serial/mod.rs:82-96`) whose success patterns come from the contract.
- [special] TV1148 `tools/recshuttle/src/lib.rs:45-73` `exec` has no command timeout (only `ConnectTimeout=5`), so a hung `recstrap`/`recpart` over SSH blocks the scenario indefinitely, unlike serial `exec`, which takes a timeout.
- [special] TV2015 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:619-631` installed-boot reports PASS when `wait_for_installed_boot_with_context` returns a non-empty `failed_services` list; the failures are only appended to the evidence text ("failed services seen: ..."), so the contract's `service_failure_patterns` never fail a scenario.
- [special] TV2066 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/lib/common.sh:197,219,225` `report_results` labels every failure "Missing (not in PATH)" and prints "All tools are present and functional" / "Check package installation" for every script, including install, boot and login checks recorded through `test_command`/`test_file_exists`; tool-check wording on the shared reporter for all scenarios.
- [special] TV2069 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/live-tools.sh:14,106-109` measures elapsed seconds and prints "Live-tools smoke duration"; nothing in the harness or xtask reads that line.
- [special] TV2074 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/live-boot-ssh-preflight.sh:31-32` the systemd branch checks Fedora's packaging paths `/usr/lib/systemd/system/sshd.service` and `sshd-keygen@.service` as product facts; a base-package rename breaks the evidence with nothing in the variant tying them.
- [special] TV2098 `testing/install-tests/src/qemu/serial/mod.rs:58-59` (and `:78-79`) `wait_for_live_boot_with_context` / `wait_for_live_tools_with_context` discard the failed-service list `wait_for_boot` returns (`?; Ok(())`), while installed-boot keeps and reports it; the same boot waiter's output is used per caller.
- [special] TV2103 `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:393-398` a missing install initramfs prints `SKIP` and does not fail preflight, while `scenarios/install.rs:86-88` says the scenario names the installed initramfs "so it fails loudly if the ISO lacks it"; the missing artifact is only caught later, inside the VM.
- [special] TV3098 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/installed-boot.sh:27` "EFI partition mounted" is `test_file_exists "/boot/EFI"`, which only checks that the directory exists. It passes whether or not the ESP is mounted there, so the check is not the property its name gives.
- [hidden] P076 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/live-boot.sh:39-58`, `live-boot-ssh-preflight.sh:28-53` and `installed-tools.sh:27-50` each branch on `systemctl`/`rc-service` or `ID=levitateos|acornos` with different checks per branch (P034), so the two distros run different evidence under one scenario name.
- [hidden] PA2069 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:139` the hub's boot step runs `recsetup apply --root T <bootArgs> --machine-id` with no `--kernel` and no `--options`, while the install scenario (`testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/install.rs:80-85`) runs recsetup with `--kernel .../boot/vmlinuz --options "console=tty0 console=ttyS0,115200n8"` (plus `--hostname` and `--initrd`). The boot entry the test proves is not the one the hub writes, and nothing ties the two command lines together.

Nothing ties the pattern to the hostname the harness picks.
- [hidden] TV3030 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:529-569` the post-install verify commands interpolate `target` unquoted (`test -f {target}/etc/os-release`, `umount -R {target}`, ...), while `install.rs:67-68` shell-quotes the same recpart-reported value (`let t = shell_quote(&target)`). One value has two quoting policies in one flow.
- [hidden] TV3037 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/mod.rs:929-931` `run_evidence` checks the evidence script name itself (one path component, ends in `.sh`), while the contract validator checks the same name with a different rule (prefix plus `.sh`, `distro/contract/src/validate.rs:517`). Two validators for one field, each with its own rule.
- [hidden] TV3082 `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:368-440` `run_preflight` prints the full report and `print_summary` to stdout. `require_preflight` (`preflight.rs:735-764`) then gathers the same `details` again into its `bail!` text, and the scenario's `print_failure` prints that error, so each preflight failure is reported twice by two code paths.
- [hidden] TV3091 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/live-boot.sh:48-52` and `live-boot-ssh-preflight.sh:43-49` hard-code OpenRC runlevel placement (`/etc/runlevels/boot/networking`, `default/dhcpcd`, `default/sshd`). The contract gives only service names (`required_live_services`), the builder decides the runlevels, and `distro/contract/src/runtime.rs:784-864` checks the same placement a further time. Scripts, contract runtime check and builder have to agree by hand.
- [hidden] TV3102 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/lib/common.sh:77-81,132-136` `test_tool` and the other probe helper wrap the probe in `set +e` ... `set -e`. They turn errexit back on whatever the caller had set, so they work only for callers that run under `set -e`, which every script happens to do (`set -euo pipefail`).
- [needless] TI148 `testing/install-tests/src/qemu/serial/mod.rs:13-35` `SerialExecutorExt` is an extension trait on `recqemu::Console` whose three methods each re-load the variant contract and pick pattern fields. The trait exists to attach install-tests knowledge (the contract) to a recqemu type, and xtask calls into it (`install_tests::qemu::serial::live_fatal`, `xtask/src/tasks/scenarios.rs:267`) to rebuild live-boot patterns, adding `SHELL_READY_MARKER` by hand (`:269`), which `wait_for_boot` already treats as success (`recqemu/src/serial/boot.rs:62`).
- [needless] TS1131 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/live_initramfs.rs:8-18` and `checklist/iso.rs:29-40` narrate the live boot flow (probe devices, mount EROFS, overlay, switch_root) owned by recinit's live init; the narration changes whenever recinit does.
- [surplus] PA2114 `distro/variants/acorn/scenarios/scenarios.toml:87` the installed-boot success pattern `"Welcome to AcornOS"` is printed by nothing: the only occurrence in the repo is this line, the installed `/etc/issue` is `Linux \l` (`distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:103`), and the shipped motd says "Welcome to Alpine!". The pattern is dead. What still matches is the bare `"login:"` (already logged), so AcornOS's installed-boot oracle is one dead pattern plus one that matches any getty prompt.
- [surplus] TF078 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/iso.rs:7-27,83` doc comments are stale: `boot/initramfs.img` is listed twice (`:12-13`), the comment on `INITRAMFS_LIVE_ISO_PATH` says `"boot/initramfs-live.img"` while recinit defines `boot/initramfs.img` (`tools/recinit/src/live.rs:45`), and `:1` still says "Stage 00".
- [surplus] TI048 `testing/install-tests/src/scenarios/install.rs:27` `shell_quote` is defined in `install` and imported by `scenarios/mod.rs` (6 uses) and `ab_update.rs`. It is a general helper living in one scenario's module.
- [surplus] TI118 every script in `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/*.sh` (e.g. `live-boot.sh:12-19`) falls back to `/usr/local/lib/scenario-tests/common.sh`, a location the harness no longer uses (it always writes `lib/common.sh` beside the script).
- [surplus] TI120 `testing/install-tests/test-scripts/lib/common.sh:7-24,171-175,254-276` colour handling (never a tty under the harness), `default_fail_marker`/`SCENARIO_FAIL_MARKER` (never set or read by the harness), and `warn`/`success` helpers (unused by any script).
- [surplus] TI131 `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:509-531` `resolve_required_file`/`resolve_required_dir` return `artifact_dir.join(canonical)` on both branches of their `if`, and `resolve_optional_file` is one `is_file` check. They wrap `Path::join` and return a `Result` that is never `Err`.
- [surplus] TI133 `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:612-659` `verify_artifact` handles `ChecklistType::Rootfs`, `Iso`, `AuthAudit` and `Qcow2`, but it is only ever called with `LiveInitramfs` and `InstallInitramfs`. ISOs go through the near-duplicate `verify_iso_distro` (`:534-584`), which repeats the ISO arm and its size-printing code.
- [surplus] TI137 `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:47-65` `PreflightResult`/`PreflightCheck` are public with counters (`total_checks`, `passed_checks`, `failures`) that only feed printing. `run_preflight` is public with no external callers, and `require_preflight` re-collects the details by hand from four `Option` fields.
- [surplus] TS1120 `testing/fsdbg/src/checklist/live_initramfs.rs:41` `INIT_PERMS` is used only in an error message; any executable bit passes.
- [surplus] TS2044 `testing/fsdbg/src/cpio.rs:49-56` `uid`, `gid`, `nlink`, `mtime`, `dev_major`, `dev_minor`, `rdev_major`, `rdev_minor` are parsed into every `CpioEntry` and read by nothing outside the parser, and `CpioStats.other` (`cpio.rs:386,399`) is counted and never read.

- [surplus] TV1098 `testing/install-tests/src/preflight.rs:370-381` a missing live initramfs prints `SKIP` and the preflight carries on; because `resolve_required_file` (`:509`, used at `:315`) never fails, the "required" live initramfs is never enforced.

### G27 One check sweep, run the same locally and in CI for both distros

Solves **72** problems (7 parity, 16 special, 43 hidden, 1 needless, 5 surplus).

- **Design:** `cargo xtask check [tui]` is the one definition of the quick checks; CI runs exactly it plus the scenarios as a distro matrix; every static check, tool test and CI job runs for both distros or is deleted; reusable workflows are versioned with declared inputs; the workspace root owns shared package metadata, dependency versions and JS tool config.
- **Done when:** CI has no repo facts of its own and runs AcornOS too; no per-crate Cargo.lock or duplicated metadata.
- **Depends on:** G05, G25, G26
- **Decisions:** CI runs both distros on every push (about double the CI time; default yes).
- **Merges area designs:** XT-12 (xtask), XT-14 (xtask), XT-15 (xtask), XT-16 (xtask), PA-13 (parity-all), TV-15 (testing-vm) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] PA1115 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/instance.toml:28` AcornOS's Ginux instance runs with `[network] backend = "none"` and no `net0` device, while LevitateOS's (`levitate/instance.toml:29-32`) has `net0` and `backend = "shared"`; the two Ginux boots exercise different capabilities, and AcornOS's `expect:6` records dhcpcd crashing because "the instance has no network".
- [parity] TI150 `testing/rootfs-tests/src/main.rs:107` the default input is `.artifacts/out/levitate/levitateos-base.tar.xz`, a LevitateOS-only path that the build no longer produces (`.artifacts/out/levitate/` holds only `releases/`). There is no AcornOS input and no distro parameter.
- [parity] TI151 `testing/rootfs-tests/src/tests/admin.rs:132-147,165-181,198-213,265-276` the "daily driver" checks require `sudo` with `Sudo version` and a `%wheel` sudoers line, util-linux `su`, `systemctl` and `journalctl` output containing "systemd". `network.rs:61-79,176-188` require iputils `ping` and iproute2 `ss`. Every check encodes LevitateOS's userland, so the suite cannot express AcornOS (doas, busybox, OpenRC).
- [parity] TI152 `testing/rootfs-tests/src/container.rs:78-95` every command runs as `/bin/bash -c` inside `systemd-nspawn`, which ties the suite to a bash-bearing systemd rootfs.
- [parity] TV1177 `.github/workflows/ci.yml:60-109` CI builds, runs `test all` and runs rootfs-tests for `levitate` only; AcornOS's products, scenarios and rootfs never run in CI.
- [parity] XT1001 `.github/workflows/ci.yml:63-117` the only product/integration job acquires the kernel, builds live-boot/live-tools, runs `test all`, checks the composed rootfs and uploads the ISO for `levitate` only. AcornOS has no CI build, boot, install or rootfs check, so an AcornOS regression never fails CI.
- [parity] XT1116 `xtask/src/tasks/check.rs:64` checks the guest scripts with the build host's `sh -n`. Every script under `distro/builder/guest/` is `#!/bin/sh`, which is bash on LevitateOS and busybox ash on AcornOS, so a bashism that breaks only AcornOS passes this check.
- [special] TI157 `testing/rootfs-tests/src/main.rs:132-145` fail-fast stops at the first failed check, so one run reports at most one failure.
- [special] TI158 `testing/rootfs-tests/tests/cli.rs` is a cargo-test integration target (asserts on CLI exit codes). The README presents it as the way to run checks (`cargo test -p rootfs-tests --test cli`), although workspace rules allow only focused CLI checks and ban unit tests.
- [special] TV1160 `testing/rootfs-tests/src/tests/admin.rs:32`, `:64`, `:100` each test pre-cleans with `userdel -r … 2>/dev/null || true`.
- [special] TV1163 `testing/rootfs-tests/src/main.rs:109` `extracted.as_ref().unwrap()` relies on the `rootfs.is_none()` branch at `:93-101` having created the tempdir.

It exists only in each caller's `workflow_dispatch` (`distro/elf/.github/workflows/ci.yml:10`, same in recipe and hardware-compat). It is a naming convention the callers just have to know, and any other name silently means `patch`.

[skip ci]`, and every caller's release job guards on `!startsWith(head_commit.message, 'chore: Bump version')` (`distro/elf/.github/workflows/ci.yml:37`, same in recipe and hardware-compat). The commit-message prefix is a contract copied into the reusable workflow and into each caller.

- [hidden] XT1132 `xtask/src/tasks/tui/check.rs:112-125` `bun_run` duplicates `xtask/src/tasks/check.rs:82-95` `command` (run, collect stdout+stderr, bail with a tail) with a different tail length.

- [hidden] XT1150 `turbo.json:8-11` declares build outputs `dist/**` and `.output/**`, but `docs/content`'s build writes `src/generated/index.ts` (`docs/content/scripts/build.ts:28,395`), which is not a declared output. A turbo cache hit would skip regenerating the docs content.

- [hidden] XT3071 `xtask/src/tasks/check.rs:52-53` states that every script the builder writes into the guest is a file under `distro/builder/guest/`. But `distro/builder/recipes/fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai:232` builds a `#!/bin/sh` script (`gnu-smoke`) inline as a Rhai string, and the `sh -n` step never parses it.
- [hidden] XT3087 `Cargo.toml:28` declares `license = "MIT OR Apache-2.0"` in `[workspace.package]` and `README.md:80` states it for all of LevitateOS's own code. Yet `xtask/Cargo.toml`, `distro/builder/install-split-pane/Cargo.toml`, `testing/rootfs-tests/Cargo.toml` and `testing/hardware-compat/Cargo.toml` declare no license and do not inherit it, while `distro/builder`, `distro/contract` and `testing/install-tests` each repeat it by hand.
- [hidden] XT3088 the root `Cargo.toml` has no `[workspace.dependencies]`, so each member pins shared crates itself and the pins drift. `which = "5.0"` (`distro/builder/Cargo.toml:28`) against `which = "7"` (`xtask/Cargo.toml:15`) puts two major versions in `Cargo.lock:2665` and `:2678`; `clap` is required as `4`, `4.4` or `4.5` and `tempfile` as `3` or `3.10` across members. `deny.toml:26` `multiple-versions = "warn"` keeps the duplication from failing anything.
- [hidden] XT3089 `xtask/Cargo.toml:22` (`vt100 = "0.15"`) and `:24` (`portable-pty = "0.8"`) pin the same terminal-emulation stack as `distro/builder/install-split-pane/Cargo.toml:17-19`, each on its own. The headless TUI capture and the shipped split pane must emulate the same terminal, but nothing ties their versions together.

CI can resolve and use dependencies that differ from `bun.lock` without failing, which `cargo xtask check` would reject.

Any push to the super repo's master changes the CI of all six repos, with no version pin.

Its overlap with the install scenarios' `installed_tools` checks has no shared definition.
