### G28 Both siblings use NetworkManager, wired and WiFi, live and installed

Solves **9** problems (7 parity, 2 hidden).

- **Design:** One network stack on both: NetworkManager plus wpa_supplicant, configured the same way live and installed, carried across A/B, documented with one procedure. The OpenRC network branch, `etc/network/interfaces` and the `eth0` assumption go.
- **Done when:** Both live and installed systems bring up any NIC and WiFi with `nmcli`; one network section in the guide.
- **Depends on:** G07, G13, G15, G19
- **Decisions:** **User-visible.** NetworkManager on both (default; AcornOS drops ifupdown/dhcpcd). Carry live WiFi into the install (default yes).
- **Merges area designs:** PA-03 (parity-all), UD-15 (tui-docs) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] U020 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/11-acorn-installation.ts:82` and `03-installation-disk.ts:50-64` vs `12-acorn-installation-disk.ts:50-72`: LevitateOS uses NetworkManager/iwd (`iwctl`), AcornOS uses dhcpcd + wpa_supplicant (`iw dev`, `wpa_passphrase`, `rc-service wpa_supplicant start`); the WiFi step is a different procedure on each, and AcornOS's says WiFi cards needing firmware "do not work yet: the ISO ships no firmware" (capability gap).
- [parity] U029 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/06-installation-boot.ts:40` says only that NetworkManager is enabled and that live-session SSH stays in the live overlay, while `15-acorn-installation-boot.ts:39` says the installed AcornOS gets `networking`, `dhcpcd` and `sshd`; the install scenarios require `sshd` and `auditd` on LevitateOS (`distro/variants/levitate/scenarios/scenarios.toml:87`) and `networking`/`dhcpcd`/`sshd` on AcornOS (`distro/variants/acorn/scenarios/scenarios.toml:80`): the two guides describe the installed services differently, LevitateOS's reads as if sshd is absent, and `auditd` exists on one sibling only.
- [parity] U030 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/15-acorn-installation-boot.ts:49-58` only AcornOS's guide carries the live session's WiFi config into the installed system (`cp wpa_supplicant.conf`, `--service wpa_supplicant`); LevitateOS's has no step to keep the live network, and `07-post-installation.ts:79` says "network connections" carry across A/B updates while `16-acorn-post-installation.ts:59` omits them.
- [parity] U036 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/08-troubleshooting.ts:98-117` LevitateOS's "No network" answer is `systemctl`/`nmcli`; `17-acorn-troubleshooting.ts:124-153` is `rc-service dhcpcd`/`iw dev` and gives no WiFi connect command; the network capability after install differs (NetworkManager with nmcli vs bare dhcpcd).
- [parity] U102 `docs/install-tui-todo.md:14,183-187` records that LevitateOS ships no iwd, so its guide's `iwctl` WiFi steps (`03-installation-disk.ts:53-64`) fail, while AcornOS ships wpa_supplicant and iw; the LevitateOS install guide documents a capability its ISO lacks and AcornOS has.
- [parity] UD1005 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/12-acorn-installation-disk.ts:72` AcornOS's guide warns that WiFi cards needing firmware "do not work yet: the ISO ships no firmware"; LevitateOS's WiFi steps (`03-installation-disk.ts:50-64`) carry no such warning although neither variant ships linux-firmware (P096), so the same limitation is documented for one sibling only.
- [parity] UD3118 `docs/install-tui-todo.md:186-188` records that AcornOS's rootfs has no `/lib/modules`, so no modular driver (WiFi's among them) can load on AcornOS. LevitateOS has no such limitation.
- [hidden] PA1110 `testing/fsdbg/src/oracle/bins.rs:156,380` and `oracle/units.rs:114` expect `wpa_supplicant`/NetworkManager-wifi on LevitateOS ("iwctl (iwd) is not in the base repos; WiFi uses NetworkManager-wifi"), the LevitateOS docs (`03-installation-disk.ts:57-63`) tell users to run `iwctl`, and the locked RPM set ships neither; three places disagree on LevitateOS's WiFi tool.
- [hidden] U109 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/15-acorn-installation-boot.ts:39` promises the installed AcornOS `networking`, `dhcpcd` and `sshd`, a hand-written list tied neither to `distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:111-113` (which sets up the installed runlevels) nor to the scenario (`distro/variants/acorn/scenarios/scenarios.toml:80`); the same three names are also the live overlay's OpenRC allowlist (`live-overlay.rhai:85`, noted in `docs/desktop.md:92`), so the three lists match only by coincidence.

### G29 One update story on both: Recipe is the package manager, A/B images update the system

Solves **15** problems (12 parity, 1 special, 2 hidden).

- **Design:** Recipe is the package manager on both installed systems (user decision, 2026-11-07: "it's the heart of the OS"): both images ship the `recipe` binary and its state/config so the user installs software with it, and the docs describe it once for both. The base system itself updates by A/B image through recab, with the same trial and tools on both. AcornOS stops shipping `apk-tools`/`alpine-conf` and the build-host `/etc/apk/repositories`. `tools/recipe` stays out of scope: this fix packages and configures Recipe, it does not change it.
- **Done when:** `recipe` runs on both installed systems (checked by a scenario on both); no `/etc/apk/repositories` pointing at a build-host path; the docs describe one package procedure and one update procedure.
- **Depends on:** G08, G15, G19
- **Decisions:** **Decided:** Recipe on both. Open: whether Recipe builds as a static musl binary for both (else it needs each base's libc); recab-tui on the installed system: neither for now (default).
- **Merges area designs:** PA-09 (parity-all), RG-06 (builder-recipes-guest), UD-16 (tui-docs) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] P010 `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:58` installs `alpine-base`, which brings `apk-tools` and `alpine-conf` (`setup-alpine`, `setup-disk`, `lbu`) into AcornOS's live and installed system: an Alpine installer and package manager the user can run beside recpart/recstrap; LevitateOS has neither `rpm` nor `dnf`.
- [parity] P012 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/07-post-installation.ts:15-48` documents `recipe install` as LevitateOS's package manager, but no product installs the `recipe` binary into the rootfs (no `usr/bin/recipe` producer anywhere in `distro/`); AcornOS's page (`16-acorn-post-installation.ts:15`) documents services instead and no package management at all.
- [parity] U024 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/03-installation-disk.ts:129` and `04-installation-base.ts:75` say user homes live under `/var/home`; the AcornOS copies (`12-acorn-installation-disk.ts:139`, `13-acorn-installation-base.ts:71`) drop that clause, so either the layouts differ or one copy is stale.
- [parity] U026 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/04-installation-base.ts:40` lists "the `recipe` package manager" in LevitateOS's base system, while `13-acorn-installation-base.ts:40` lists OpenRC, busybox, dhcpcd, OpenSSH, doas and recab with no `recipe`; the two guides promise different package management, and neither product actually installs `recipe` (P012).
- [parity] U027 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/07-post-installation.ts:14-50` LevitateOS's post-install has a "Package Management" section (`recipe list/info/install`); `16-acorn-post-installation.ts` has none, and instead has a "Services" section (`rc-status`, `rc-update`) that LevitateOS lacks (no `systemctl` equivalent shown).
- [parity] U031 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/06-installation-boot.ts:26` vs `15-acorn-installation-boot.ts:26`: LevitateOS boots kernel + installed initramfs via `bootctl install`, AcornOS boots kernel-only (no initramfs) with systemd-boot copied by hand; the boot chain differs in kind, and every later page (update, troubleshooting) forks on it (`07-post-installation.ts:69` `--initrd`, `08-troubleshooting.ts:40` `initramfs.img`).
- [parity] U032 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/14-acorn-installation-config.ts:38` AcornOS sets only `LANG` ("musl compiles no locales") and loads the keymap through busybox `loadkmap`; LevitateOS (`05-installation-config.ts:38`) has real locales; the configuration step gives a weaker locale capability on AcornOS.
- [parity] U076 `docs/content/src/content/05-rec-tooling/03-recab.ts:30` (product `shared`, shown on AcornOS) gives the update example with `--initrd /mnt/boot/initramfs-installed.img`, which is LevitateOS-only; AcornOS boots without an initramfs (`16-acorn-post-installation.ts:49`).
- [parity] U077 `docs/content/src/content/04-architecture/03-atomic-updates.ts:24,36` (product `shared`) says the EFI partition holds "each slot's kernel and initramfs" and gives `recab stage ... --initrd <initramfs>`; both are false on AcornOS.
- [parity] U111 `tui/README.md:6` and `docs/install-tui-todo.md:237-238`: recab-tui ships on neither distro, so after install neither sibling has an A/B UI; the guides send users to the `recab` CLI with `sudo`/`doas` (`07-post-installation.ts:75`, `16-acorn-post-installation.ts:55`).
- [parity] UD1028 `docs/content/src/content/04-architecture/03-atomic-updates.ts:37` the shared (`product: "shared"`, `:6`) A/B page says `recab-trial.service` commits the trial; on AcornOS the trial runs as the OpenRC service `recab-trial` in the boot runlevel (`tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:92`), so the shared page names one init system's unit.
- [parity] UD3131 `docs/website/public/llms-full.txt:99` gives the update command as `recab stage --rootfs next.erofs --kernel vmlinuz --initrd initramfs.img` with no per-distro note, but AcornOS stages without an initramfs (`docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/16-acorn-post-installation.ts:49`).
- [special] BR011 `distro/builder/recipes/alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai:233` writes the build host's `iso-contents/apks` path as the first line of the shipped rootfs's `/etc/apk/repositories`. No later step in this area (or `guest/installed/system-setup.sh`) rewrites it, so the installed AcornOS points `apk` at a directory that only existed on the build machine.
- [hidden] U100 `docs/website/src/pages/index.astro:144-145` presents `recipe` as LevitateOS's package manager "instead of apt or dnf", another copy of the docs' package-manager claim (`07-post-installation.ts:15-48`), while no product installs the `recipe` binary (P012) and AcornOS's pages document no package manager (U026).
- [hidden] UD1009 `docs/content/src/content/01-getting-started/03-installation-disk.ts:129`, `04-installation-base.ts:75` and the shared `04-architecture/03-atomic-updates.ts:23` state that user homes live under `/var/home` (shared by both slots); no code in `distro/`, `tools/` or `testing/` puts them there (`distro/builder/src/build/filesystem.rs:41` creates a plain `home` directory in the rootfs), so the docs assert a layout fact that nothing owns.

### G30 The desktop is a product layer over the same base on both

Solves **6** problems (5 parity, 1 special).

- **Design:** The desktop ISOs (Plasma, LXQt) are a `live-desktop` product layered on the same base, with the same network stack, lock mechanism, health checks and install path.
- **Done when:** Both desktop products build through the registry and pass the same desktop scenario.
- **Depends on:** G05, G14, G28, G29
- **Decisions:** Display server: Wayland on both was proposed, but Alpine 3.24 has no `lxqt-wayland-session` (`docs/desktop.md`). Default: accept X11 on AcornOS until Alpine ships it.
- **Merges area designs:** PA-16 (parity-all), UD-17 (tui-docs) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] P059 `docs/desktop.md:93-94` `recab stage` does not check the flavour, so staging a base image onto a desktop install reverts it; this applies to both distros, but only becomes visible once a desktop exists (see Desktop).
- [parity] P086 `docs/desktop.md:17,20` LevitateOS's Plasma prototype runs on Wayland with no failed units; AcornOS's LXQt runs on X11 and OpenRC marks `sddm` failed (`:73-76`). Neither desktop is in the pipeline; the planned products already differ in display server and health.
- [parity] P087 `docs/desktop.md:84-85` LevitateOS's desktop lock is to be solved with dnf from `xtask base lock`, AcornOS "needs a lock in the same shape (it pins `extra_apks` by hand today)"; the desktop multiplies P002.
- [parity] P088 `docs/desktop.md:69-70` AcornOS's desktop switches the network stack from dhcpcd/networking to NetworkManager, so AcornOS base and AcornOS desktop would differ in networking, while LevitateOS keeps NetworkManager in both.
- [parity] U108 `docs/desktop.md:90-91` on the desktop ISOs "the install screen stays on ttyS0 and in a terminal"; neither install guide, nor install-hub, nor any docs page covers installing from the desktop ISO on either distro.
- [special] U110 `docs/desktop.md:73-76` AcornOS's SDDM init script needs its own `start_post` for autologin or OpenRC marks sddm failed; a one-distro workaround the LevitateOS side does not need.

### G31 ginux-boot stages both OSes the same way and judges boot health through recab

Solves **49** problems (4 parity, 21 special, 20 hidden, 2 needless, 2 surplus).

- **Design:** One probe for both OSes runs `recab health --json`; one shared Ginux root preparation and one `instance.toml`; the driver owns the Ginux process (probe powers off, driver waits with a timeout); `expect` is the only list of known gaps and a stale gap fails the run.
- **Done when:** No `if acorn` branches in ginux-boot; no `pkill` or poll loop; README copies no gaps.
- **Depends on:** G19
- **Merges area designs:** TS-05 (testing-static), TS-06 (testing-static), TS-07 (testing-static), TS-08 (testing-static) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] TS1002 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:85` vs `:90` AcornOS counts every service whose state is not `started` as not working, while LevitateOS counts only units in state `failed` (inactive/dead units pass); the two siblings are judged by different strictness.
- [parity] TS1003 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:97` vs `:99` the success report prints `started:` (all services) for AcornOS but `running:` (only `.service` units with sub-state running) for LevitateOS; the same check yields differently shaped output per distro.
- [parity] TS1023 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/instance.toml:24-28` has no `net0` and `backend = "none"` while `levitate/instance.toml:28-32` gets `net0` and `backend = "shared"`; AcornOS's `dhcpcd` gap (`acorn/expect:6`) follows from the test giving it no network, not from the OS.
- [parity] TS1024 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/instance.toml:7-8` passes no `args`/`environment` while LevitateOS's passes `--system --unit=multi-user.target` and `container=ginux`; the container hint exists for only one sibling.
- [special] TS1001 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:82` and `:96` branch twice on `if [ "$os" = acorn ]` to choose the init-done marker, the not-working/working queries and the report line; the per-OS judging rules live in the generic driver instead of next to `acorn/` and `levitate/`, so adding or changing an OS edits the driver.
- [special] TS1008 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:48,67,68` swallow `pkill` and `wait` failures with `|| true` / `2>/dev/null`; a ginux instance that survives is never reported.
- [special] TS1009 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:63-65` polls `sudo test -e` once per second (`sleep 1`) for the probe file instead of being told when the guest is done.
- [special] TS1010 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:21` default `timeout=300` and `levitate/probe.sh:5` inner `timeout 120` for `is-system-running --wait` are independent magic timeouts; AcornOS's probe has no equivalent wait, so the two OSes settle differently.
- [special] TS1011 `testing/ginux-boot/levitate/probe.sh:5,9` and `acorn/probe.sh:5,7` discard stderr (`2>/dev/null`) of `systemctl`/`rc-status`/`cat softlevel`; a broken probe produces an empty list that the judge reads as "nothing failed".
- [special] TS1016 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/stage.sh:52-54` force-enables `networking`, `sshd` and `dhcpcd` in runlevels itself; the test, not the product, decides which services AcornOS boots (installed-system enables no `sshd`), and the three known gaps in `acorn/expect` are exactly these test-added links.
- [special] TS1017 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/stage.sh:26-27` writes a hand-picked `/proc/filesystems` "OpenRC reads /proc/filesystems; Gate 9's staging never needed it" — a fixture for one consumer.
- [special] TS1018 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/stage.sh:31,55` hard-code hostname `acorn` while Ginux's helper writes `ginux`; the identity the test boots with is unrelated to the variant's configured hostname.
- [special] TS1019 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/stage.sh:62` and `:60-61` `chmod 600 /etc/shadow` after a blanket `chown -R 65534` / `chmod u+w` to undo its own side effects; staging encodes Ginux's nobody-identity workaround.
- [special] TS1033 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:72,114` flip `keep=1` on failure from inside the flow so the EXIT trap at `:47-51` keeps the work dir; cleanup behaviour depends on a variable mutated far from the trap.
- [special] TS2006 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:60` builds the `script -qefc "sudo timeout $timeout $ginux native $here/$os/instance.toml $root 2>$work/audit.log"` command line by string interpolation: `$timeout`, `$ginux`, `$here`, `$root` and `$work` are unquoted inside the string that `script(1)` hands to `sh -c`, and `--timeout` (`:26`) is never checked to be a number. A path with spaces or shell characters splits or is interpreted, so the quoting convention between the driver and `script`'s shell is implicit.
- [special] TS2012 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/probe.sh:7-8` strips the brackets and takes `$NF` of each `rc-status` line as the service status; OpenRC prints supervised services as `[ started <uptime> (<respawns>) ]`, whose last field is `(0)`, so `ginux-boot.sh:85` would judge a running supervise-daemon service as not working. The judge depends on a column heuristic over another tool's display output.
- [special] TS2013 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/probe.sh:7` runs `rc-status -a` (every runlevel, including `shutdown`), and `ginux-boot.sh:85` fails every listed service that is not `started`. Services that are correctly stopped during boot, such as the installed AcornOS shutdown runlevel's `mount-ro`, `killprocs`, `savecache` (`distro/builder/recipes/installed-system.rhai:112`), would be judged failures unless listed as gaps, unlike recab's boot-runlevels-only rule (`tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:329`).
- [special] TS2015 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:101-112` only judges names that appear in the probe output. A `gap` line for a service or unit that is no longer present at all (renamed, dropped from the package set, not loaded) is neither working nor not working, so stale gaps never surface. The README's "the list only shrinks" (`README.md:12-13`) is not enforced, and a fixed gap (`:109-111`) only prints a NOTE, never a failure.
- [special] TS2017 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:113` reports `$(echo "$gaps" | wc -l)` known gaps, which prints 1 when the expect file has no `gap` lines (`echo` of an empty string is one line).
- [special] TS2019 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:35` also accepts `-L "$rootfs/sbin/init"`, so a dangling `/sbin/init` symlink passes the precheck (`-x` on the absolute symlink would test the host's target path instead). `:37-39` checks only `sudo script cargo`, while the run also needs `git` (`:41`, whose failure inside `$(...)` just prints an empty revision), `timeout`, `pkill`, `awk`, `mknod`, the host's `/usr/share/zoneinfo/UTC` (`acorn/stage.sh:38`) and a Rocky 10 host (`README.md:31`). None of these is checked.
- [special] TS2022 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/stage.sh:22` writes a fixed `/proc/meminfo` of `MemTotal: 1048576 kB` (1 GiB) that must agree by hand with `acorn/instance.toml:12` (`memory_mib = 1024`); change the instance's memory and the guest still sees 1 GiB.
- [special] TS2023 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/stage.sh:38` copies the build host's `/usr/share/zoneinfo/UTC` over the product's `/etc/localtime` (`cp --remove-destination`). A host-dependent input overrides product config, and only for AcornOS.
- [special] TS2027 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/stage.sh:39-42` skips `mknod` when `/dev/$1` already exists (skip-if-exists), so a stale or wrong device node copied from the rootfs by `ginux-boot.sh:54` (`cp -a`) is kept. LevitateOS's device nodes come from Ginux's `prepare_root` (`levitate/stage.sh:15`) instead.
- [special] TS2034 `testing/ginux-boot/README.md:34-38` records that the published results came from roots built by hand on the host ("unpacking the locked Fedora 45 RPMs with the recipe's steps", AcornOS via `apk.static`), not from the recipe outputs that `:25-28` tells users to pass. The recorded pass is for a third root that the product build did not produce.
- [special] TS3038 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/stage.sh:58-60` chowns the whole staged root to `65534:65534` (Ginux's nobody identity), and the AcornOS `sshd` gap recorded as an OS gap (`acorn/expect:5`, "/var/empty must be owned by root"; `README.md:84`) follows from that ownership; `levitate/expect:30` attributes LevitateOS's `sshd` failure to the same cause. The known-gap lists record test-staging effects as product gaps.
- [hidden] TS1004 `testing/ginux-boot/levitate/probe.sh:7` writes a `state <is-system-running>` line that `ginux-boot.sh` never reads; `degraded` is silently accepted (README:57 records "degraded"), so the probe and the judge disagree about what the probe is for.
- [hidden] TS1005 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:63,69,75`, `acorn/probe.sh:9-10`, `levitate/probe.sh:11-12` and `README.md:48` all hard-code `/ginux-probe.out` (and the `.tmp` rename protocol); the host poll loop depends on the guest scripts' atomic-rename convention with nothing tying them together.
- [hidden] TS1006 `/usr/local/sbin/ginux-probe` is spelled in `acorn/stage.sh:48,56`, `levitate/stage.sh:10` and `levitate/ginux-probe.service:8`; the install path and the inittab/unit that run it must change together.
- [hidden] TS1007 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:48` and `:67` repeat the `pkill -f "^$ginux native $here/$os/instance.toml $root"` pattern, which must match the exact command line built at `:60`; changing the invocation silently stops the kill.
- [hidden] TS1012 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/stage.sh:12-37` re-implements Ginux's `prepare_root` (`tools/ginux` `scripts/ginux-host-instance-helper.sh:146-206` on `levitate/boot`) line for line (fake `/proc/stat`, `/proc/meminfo`, `boot_id`, `cap_last_cap`, cgroup files, `6.12.0-ginux-abi1`), while `levitate/stage.sh:15` calls the original; a Ginux staging change must be copied by hand into the AcornOS path.
- [hidden] TS1015 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/stage.sh:44-51` writes a third copy of AcornOS's inittab (OpenRC sysinit/boot/default/ctrlaltdel/shutdown lines) besides `distro/builder/recipes/live-overlay.rhai:24-32` and `installed-system.rhai:96-101`; the comment at `acorn/stage.sh:6` claims it is the inittab `distro/builder/src/pipeline/overlay.rs` writes, but `overlay.rs` contains no inittab.
- [hidden] TS1020 `testing/ginux-boot/levitate/instance.toml` is a copy of Ginux's `config/instance-gate9.toml` (only `id` differs) and `acorn/instance.toml` is a copy of that copy; Gate 9 config changes must be mirrored by hand into both.
- [hidden] TS1025 `testing/ginux-boot/levitate/instance.toml:9` `args` duplicate the `/sbin/init --system --unit=multi-user.target` that Ginux's `prepare_root` writes into `/proc/1/cmdline`; `acorn/stage.sh:17` writes only `/sbin/init`.
- [hidden] TS1027 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:8` and `README.md:22-24` require an external Ginux checkout on branch `levitate/boot`, while the superproject's `tools/ginux` submodule is pinned detached at `2eac10bf`, which has neither `config/instance-gate9.toml` nor `scripts/ginux-host-instance-helper.sh`; the comment references at `levitate/instance.toml:3` and `acorn/stage.sh:4` point to files that do not exist at the pinned submodule.
- [hidden] TS1028 `testing/ginux-boot/README.md:81-97` restates every known gap and its cause already in `acorn/expect` and `levitate/expect`; the script tells you to edit `expect` but the README copy goes stale.
- [hidden] TS1029 `testing/ginux-boot/README.md:52-60` records a results table pinned to Ginux `5cd55224` and specific Alpine/Fedora/systemd/OpenRC versions; facts owned by the variant locks and the Ginux branch.
- [hidden] TS1031 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:79` parses `expect` with `awk '$1 == "gap" { print $2 }'`; the `gap NAME reason` line format is a convention known only to the awk and the comments in the two `expect` files.
- [hidden] TS2010 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:83-86` + `acorn/probe.sh:5-8` decide "has OpenRC finished booting and which services failed" (softlevel string `default` plus the status column of `rc-status -a`) separately from `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:281-360` (`openrc_startup`/`openrc_failed`: softlevel `default` + empty `starting/` + no `openrc` process, only `BOOT_RUNLEVELS = ["sysinit", "boot", "default"]` at `:329`, plus `rc-status --crashed`). The two boot judges in the repo disagree on which services count and what state is healthy.
- [hidden] TS2011 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:88-91` + `levitate/probe.sh:5-10` decide systemd boot health (`unit multi-user.target active active`, units with ACTIVE `failed`) separately from `tools/recab/src/init_system.rs:215-269` (`systemctl is-system-running --wait`, then `list-units --failed`); the target name and the failed-state rule are typed in both judges.
- [hidden] TS2028 `testing/ginux-boot/levitate/stage.sh:12-14` hand-creates the `multi-user.target.wants/ginux-probe.service` link, while `levitate/ginux-probe.service:10-11` also declares `[Install] WantedBy=multi-user.target`. The enablement is stated twice, only the hand link takes effect (nothing runs `systemctl enable` or a preset), and the two must be kept in agreement by hand.
- [hidden] TS2032 `testing/ginux-boot/README.md:62-75` restates the commit list of the external Ginux `levitate/boot` branch, with upstream PR numbers (#242, #244-#248) and their status. That branch and GitHub own these facts, so the table drifts as soon as the branch is rebased or a PR merges.
- [hidden] TS3003 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:83-91` parses the probe output by line keyword and field position (`softlevel default`, `service NAME STATUS`, `unit NAME ACTIVE SUB`), a record format that `acorn/probe.sh:5-8` and `levitate/probe.sh:7-10` each write on their own; the host check retypes the writers' literals (`grep -qx 'softlevel default'`, `'unit multi-user.target active active'`) and nothing defines the format in one place.
- [hidden] TS3006 `testing/ginux-boot/ginux-boot.sh:60` sends Ginux's stderr to `$work/audit.log` and README calls that file the audit log, which holds only because both `acorn/instance.toml:30-31` and `levitate/instance.toml:34-35` set `[audit] mode = "stderr"`; change the audit mode and `audit.log` silently goes empty.
- [hidden] TS3013 `testing/ginux-boot/levitate/probe.sh:9-10` takes `$3` as ACTIVE and `$4` as SUB from `systemctl list-units --all --no-legend --plain` display output, and `ginux-boot.sh:88-91,99` judges on those positions; the LevitateOS judge depends on systemctl's human column layout (the AcornOS counterpart over `rc-status` is TS2012).
- [hidden] Z031 `testing/ginux-boot/README.md:24-27` names the producing recipes `alpine-live-source-rootfs.rhai` / `fedora-rpm-source-rootfs.rhai` by file; `acorn/stage.sh` (62 lines) vs `levitate/stage.sh` (15) prepare the trees differently.
- [needless] TS1014 `testing/ginux-boot/levitate/stage.sh:15` runs `$GINUX_DIR/scripts/ginux-host-instance-helper.sh prepare-root`, a Ginux-internal dev helper whose CLI and behaviour change for Ginux's own gate scripts.
- [needless] TS3014 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/probe.sh:5` reads OpenRC's runtime state file `/run/openrc/softlevel` directly rather than asking OpenRC, so the AcornOS readiness check depends on OpenRC's private runtime layout.
- [surplus] TS1021 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/instance.toml:5` and `levitate/instance.toml:7` carry a pinned Rocky OCI digest in `root` that `ginux native CONFIG ROOT` ignores (the comment at `levitate/instance.toml:4` says so).
- [surplus] TS1036 `testing/ginux-boot/acorn/stage.sh:2` documents `stage.sh ROOT GINUX_DIR` and `ginux-boot.sh:55` passes `$ginux_dir`, but the AcornOS script never reads `$2`; a parameter only the LevitateOS stage uses.

### G32 Docs point at the code that owns each fact

Solves **56** problems (2 parity, 4 special, 43 hidden, 7 surplus).

- **Design:** READMEs, architecture and plan docs record decisions and point at owners; `cargo xtask --help` is the command reference; stale docs describing removed mechanisms are deleted.
- **Done when:** No doc restates a scenario list, exit code, command walkthrough or layout value (grep against the owners).
- **Depends on:** G05, G23
- **Merges area designs:** XT-17 (xtask), CL-15 (builder-cli), UD-14 (tui-docs), TV-16 (testing-vm) — detail in `.artifacts/untangle-repo/redesign/area-<area>.md`

- [parity] U060 `tui/apps/install/install-hub/src/domain/steps.ts:112-126` Configuration is the only step that is a shell to type in (`kind: "shell"`, marked done by hand with `m`), so `docs/install-tui-todo.md:92`'s checked "Done when: you can install either variant from boot to reboot without typing a command" does not hold on either distro.
- [parity] X111 `README.md:14-18` the Variants table presents the two distros as different products ("Best for: Maximum compatibility" vs "A smaller, simpler base") and never states the three intended differences (sudo/doas, Plasma/LXQt, LTS/latest kernel); `architecture.md:16` names only the privilege tool.
- [special] CL2115 `distro/builder/README.md:89-95` the recipe-output table lists "Fedora ISO preseed: `iso` file", "Alpine asset preseed: `iso` and `apk_tools`" and a "Legacy Alpine facade". The contracts now declare `rpms` (directory) for LevitateOS and `iso`, `apk_tools`, `extra_apks` (directory) for AcornOS (`distro/variants/*/ring3/sources.toml:13` `preseed_outputs`).
- [special] CL2122 `distro/builder/docs/00_MIGRATION_INDEX.md:67-68` the "Still open" item says `distro-contract` validation/runtime/error surfaces "still center `CheckpointId`", but `distro/contract/src` has no `CheckpointId`. `03_MIGRATION_STAGELESS.md:3-5` calls itself a "Historical migration record" and "Status: ready" at once, and the index keeps tracks 04-06 `in_progress` (`:16`, `:20`, `:24`) while also calling them "materially landed" (`:50`, `:56`, `:61`).
- [special] CL2123 `distro/builder/docs/06_MIGRATION_VARIANT_LAYOUT.md:84`, `:110`, `:164-166` and `:256` say variant live overlay material lives under `ring2/overlays/live/` (and `:157-160` `ring3/assets/rootfs-source/`), but neither variant has `ring2/overlays/` (each `ring2/` holds only `products.toml`), so the doc describes a layout the code no longer uses.
- [special] TI159 `testing/rootfs-tests/Cargo.toml:4` `edition = "2024"` and its own version, while the workspace sets 2021. `testing/install-tests/Cargo.toml:3-4` likewise pins its own `0.1.0`/`2021`, unlike `tools/recqemu` and `recshuttle`, which use `workspace = true`.

The parts that actually drive builds (`bin/`, `pipeline/`, `recipe/`, `run_history`) are missing from it.

The code uses `identity/identity.toml` (`parse.rs:199`) and `ring0_hook_path` (`release_hook.rs:62`), so the doc states facts the code contradicts.

Those facts belong to tools/ginux, the recipes and the pipeline, and the README drifts when they change.

None of these exist: patterns come from the contract, `exec` checks no fatal patterns, and VMs are libvirt domains. The policy and the code have drifted apart.
