Any failure reboots (`busybox reboot -f`) when the command line has `rd.emergency=reboot` (`FAIL_REBOOT_OPTION`, now also a template var), else a shell. `blkid` and `e2fsck` are copied from `--rootfs` into `usr/sbin` with the ELF interpreter (readelf -l) and every needed library at its rootfs path, so glibc and musl rootfs both work; merged-usr links in the archive. Deleted: `payload.rs` (systemd tables, unit patches, hand-written passwd/group/shadow, `PRIVATE_LIB_SUBDIRS`, glibc-x86_64 `LIB_DIR`/`LOADERS`, firmware copy, `EMERGENCY_REBOOT_OPTION` duplicate), `--no-firmware`, `MOUNT_DIRS`, the systemd candidate dirs in `elf::find_library`, `copy_library_to`/`copy_dir_recursive`; `INSTALL_MODULES` lost hid/usbhid/dm-mod/dm-crypt/xfs/btrfs. `tiny.rs` and `install.rs` share `modules::install_boot_modules` and `tiny::write_init`. fsdbg: deleted the `install_initramfs` checklist (it imported the payload tables; TS-03/04 deletes it anyway) and the `ChecklistType::InstallInitramfs` variant.
  - **Verified:** `cargo check -p recinit -p fsdbg --all-targets`; built an installed initramfs from the host as rootfs (`recinit build-install --rootfs / --modules-dir /lib/modules/<rel>` with `.artifacts/tools/busybox-static`): 4.7 MB, contains `usr/sbin/{blkid,e2fsck}` with libs + loader, rendered `init` passes `sh -n`. **Not run:** no boot (quiet hours); an Alpine/musl rootfs build; busybox `reboot -f` as PID 1.
  - **Behaviour changes:** LevitateOS's installed early boot moves from systemd-in-initramfs to this init (journal no longer holds initramfs logs; no udev in the initramfs, root found by `blkid`); AcornOS gains an initramfs (root fsck, modular storage drivers, trial failure -> reboot). The initramfs no longer carries firmware (storage needs none; the rootfs carries it). Root is found by waiting at most 30 s (was systemd's device timeout).
  - **Decisions taken / not taken:** (1) LUKS/LVM is NOT implemented: neither rootfs has cryptsetup/lvm and no installer creates them (TO-07: later product decision); the modules were dropped (TL057). (2) Kept separate live and installed templates and the `initramfs-installed.img` name: one shared image would mean changing the live init's cmdline dispatch and reciso/recstrap/recab names (other agents' paths); recinit builds both from the same busybox and module code, so merging later is a template change. (3) Only ext2/3/4 get fsck; recfstab (`boot_fsck_supported`, TO3159) still gives xfs/f2fs pass 2 - recfstab owner should limit it to ext*.
  - **For rootfs recipes (agent E):** (a) AcornOS rootfs must ship real `e2fsck` (`e2fsprogs`) and util-linux `blkid` (apk `blkid`), not busybox applets (recinit refuses a busybox-symlink blkid/e2fsck with a clear error); LevitateOS already has both. (b) Both rootfs trees must contain `usr/lib/modules/<release>` with a depmod'ed tree (`modules.dep`, `modules.builtin`; the compressed `.ko.xz/.gz/.zst` are decompressed by recinit with host xz/gzip/zstd) plus the curated firmware under `usr/lib/firmware` (iwlwifi/ath9k set, common Ethernet, GPU) and microcode; `readelf` and these hosts tools are build-host requirements. (c) the `modules_dir` handed to recinit should be that rootfs path.
  - **For the builder / release (G16 agent):** replace `build-release.sh`'s `usr/lib/systemd/systemd` probe: always build the installed initramfs, for every variant: `recinit build-install --rootfs <rootfs> --modules-dir <rootfs>/usr/lib/modules/<release> --busybox <busybox> --output <initramfs-installed.img>` (flags unchanged except `--no-firmware` is gone; the release hook passed none). With acorn `installed.initramfs = true`, `artifacts.initramfs_installed` is Some for both, so `release_hook.rs:80`, `release.rs:147` and install-tests need no change; the `[installed] initramfs` field and `ArtifactNames::derive(.., bool)` (`distro/contract/src/{schema,variant}.rs`, uncommitted edits by others) can then be deleted outright, with `Option<String>` becoming `String` in `release.rs`, `preflight.rs:37,171,238`, `scenarios/{install:87,ab_update:57,mod:483}.rs`, `xtask/src/tasks/generate.rs:110`. I did not touch the contract because it is mid-edit elsewhere.
  - **For recab / recstrap / install-tests:** recab's OpenRC trial options (`systemd_boot.rs:55`) already take `FAIL_REBOOT_OPTION` whenever `initrd` is set; recsetup `plan.rs:436` must pass the installed initramfs for OpenRC targets too (drop the systemd condition); install-tests `uses_initramfs()` and `preflight.rs:383` `distro_id == "levitate"` become "always"; docs post-installation pages: one boot chain.
  - **Problems solved:** TL001, TL002, TL003 (recinit side), TL005 (recinit side), TL055, TL056, TL057, TL058 (recinit half), TL059 (payload tables gone), TO2154, TO3062, C096 (once acorn's field is committed), P096 (recinit's missing-firmware copy gone; firmware itself is rootfs work). **Left:** C097/P047/TO3188/TO1103 (release hook probe, per-distro `uses_initramfs`, name sharing) - G16 and the recab/recstrap/install-tests owners above; TO3159 (recfstab); P096 firmware content and kernel modules in the rootfs - rootfs recipes.
- [x] G28 (recipe and docs half) — NetworkManager + wpa_supplicant on both distros. builder `installed-system.rhai`, docs/content `src/pages.ts` + `06-installation-boot.ts`, superproject pointers.
  - **Changed:** OpenRC `network_services` is now `["networkmanager"]` (was `networking`, `dhcpcd`); `networking` dropped from the runlevel table; dbus already comes from `capability_services`. `live-overlay.rhai` and `guest/**` needed no change (G13 left no network files). The docs' `network(d)` helper (in `pages.ts`, not under `content/`) is now one procedure: `nmcli device wifi list/connect`, `cp -a` of `system-connections` into the target (the Review Services step is unconditional), daemon `NetworkManager` (systemd) / `networkmanager` (OpenRC). Removed the AcornOS `iw`/`wpa_passphrase`/`dhcpcd` text and the unused `reconnect`/`installedServices`; the services sentence names only the network daemon (sshd is not enabled by the recipe).
  - **Verified:** `bun run typecheck` in docs/content passes. No recipe or ISO run.
  - **Behaviour change:** AcornOS installed boots NetworkManager instead of networking+dhcpcd; the guide's AcornOS WiFi step is now nmcli.
  - **Left:** (1) agent E: rootfs packages `networkmanager`, `networkmanager-wifi`, `networkmanager-openrc`, `wpa_supplicant`, drop `dhcpcd`/`iw`/`ifupdown` and `etc/network/interfaces` (in the Alpine rootfs producer; not found in my paths); (2) the carry is a manual `cp -a` step in the docs, not a recsetup option; (3) `pages.ts` also holds another agent's `RECIPE` hunk, committed with mine; (4) 08-troubleshooting is the other agent's file but now reads correctly through `network(d)`; (5) fsdbg WiFi expectation (PA1110), scenarios requiring `networking`/`dhcpcd` on AcornOS (`distro/variants/acorn/scenarios/scenarios.toml:80`), and `live-boot.sh` route check (G26) need updating.
